(101- 200)



uriyāciriyar
oḷavi turicāmi



patippāciriyarkaḷ
muṉivar oḷavi naṭarācaṉ

ceṉṉi - 600 004.


nūṟ kuṟippu

nūṟpeyar	:	urivēntar 						   tamiḻttoki - 15
uriyāciriyar	:	oḷavi turicāmi
patippāḷar	:	i. tamiḻamutu
patippu	:	2009
tāḷ	:	16 ki veḷḷittāḷ
aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	8 + 496 = 504
nūl kaṭṭamippu	:	iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vili	:	urupā. 315/-
paṭikaḷ	:	1000
nūlākkam	:	pāvāṇar kaṇiṉi
		ti.nakar, ceṉṉi - 17.  
aṭṭi ōviyam	:	ōviyar marutu  
aṭṭi vaṭivamippu	:	va. malar

		āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu
		irāyappēṭṭi, ceṉṉi - 14.
veḷiyīṭu
iṉiyamutu patippakam
pi.11 kulmokar kuṭiyiruppu, 
35 cevāliya civāci kaṇēcaṉ cāli, 
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017. 
to.pē. 2433 9030
patippuri
oḷavi cu.turicāmip piḷḷi
tamatu ōyvaṟiyā uḻippāl  tamiḻ āyvuk kaḷattil uyarntu niṉṟavar. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku puttuyir ūṭṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷuḷ muṉ variciyil niṟpavar. naṟṟiṇi, puṟanāṉūṟu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu ākiya caṅka nūṟ celvaṅkaḷukku urivaḷam kaṇṭavar. civa peruṅkaṭalil mūḻkit tiḷittavar. urivēntar eṉṟu tamiḻulakam pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭṭa oḷavi cu.turicāmip piḷḷi 1903il piṟantu 1981il maṟintār. 
 vāḻnta āṇṭukaḷ 78. eḻutiya nūlkaḷ 38. itaṉi  poruḷ vaḻip pirittu “urivēntar tamiḻttoki” eṉum talippil 28 tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.
illaṟa ēntalākavum, urinayam kaṇṭa uravōrākavum , naṟṟamiḻ nāvalarākavum, civa cittāntac cemmalākavum , niṟipukaḻ eytiya urivēntarā kavum, pulamiyilum perum pulamipeṟṟavarākavum tikaḻntu viḷaṅkiya ipperuntamiḻācāṉiṉ nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oru cēra veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟōm. ivaruṭiya nūlkaḷil  em kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷ 5. maṟṟum ivar eḻutiya tiruvaruṭpā  nūlkaḷum it tokutikaḷil iṭam peṟavilli. 
  “ palvēṟu kālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, urikaḷ, varalāṟu, kalveṭṭu, camayaṅkaḷ eṉṟiṉṉa tuṟip palavaṟṟil niṟipulami peṟṟavar oḷavi cu .turicāmi avarkaḷ” eṉṟu mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam avarkaḷālum,
  “iravupakal tāṉaṟiyāṉ iṉtamiḻi vittu
	 varavu celavaṟiyāṉ vāḻvil - uramuṭiyāṉ
	 taṉkaṭaṉ tāynāṭṭu makkaṭ kuḻippatilē
	 muṉkaṭaṉ eṉṟurikkum ēṟu” 
eṉṟu pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷālum pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭa ipperuntakiyiṉ nūlkaḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷākak kōrttu, muttumāliyākak  koṭuttuḷḷōm.   
 avar kālattil vāḻnta camakāla aṟiñarkaḷāl pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭavar.  civa ulakil taṉakkeṉat taṉiyiṭattip peṟṟavar.  ivar eḻutiya aṉittu nūlkaḷ maṟṟum malarkaḷ, itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta kaṭṭurikaḷiyellām tēṭit tēṭi eṭuttu orē vīccil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. 
ittokutikaḷ aṉittum mikacciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku muḻuottuḻippum utaviyum nalkiyavarkaḷ avaruṭiya tirumakaṉ oḷavi tu.naṭarācaṉ, marukar irā.kumaravēlaṉ, makaḷ vayiṟṟup peyartti tirumati vēṉilā sṭāliṉ ākiyōr āvar. ivarkaḷ it tamiḻttokikku takka matippuriyum aḷittu eṅkaḷukkup perumic cērttu uḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷukku neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi
taṉ matippu iyakkattil pērīṭupāṭu koṇṭu  uḻitta ivvaruntamiḻaṟiñar tamiḻp pakivarit tam pakivarākak koṇṭa uyar maṉattiṉarāka vāḻntavar eṉpati niṉivil koṇṭu it toki  nūlkaḷi  ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñariṉ 
107  ām āṇṭu niṉivāka ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil  veḷiyiṭuvatil perumakiḻvu koḷkiṟōm. tamiḻ nūl patippil eṅkaḷiṉ inta ariya muyaṟcikkut tōḷ tantu utavuṅkaḷ.

naṟṟiṇi
mūlamum viḷakkavuriyum
101. veḷiyantiṇṇaṉār
iccāṉṟōr veḷiyaṉ eṉpāṟku makaṉāvar. veḷiyaṉ, veḷimāṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭiya makkaḷ palar paṇṭi nāḷil iruntuḷḷaṉar. tiṇṇaṉ eṉpatu ivaratu iyaṟpeyar. ivarip paṟṟi vēṟu varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ kiṭittila. ivar peyar tiṇṇaṉār eṉṟum, veḷḷiyan tiṇṇaṉār eṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
talimakaṭkut taṉpāl kātaluṇmi tuṇinta talimakaṉ akkātal muṟukip perukum vaṇṇam kaḷavoḻukkatti mēṟkoḷḷa virumpiṉāṉ. ataṟku avaḷuṭiya uyirt tōḻiyiṉ tuṇimi iṉṟiyamiyāteṉa uṇarntu avaḷatu tuṇipeṟal vēṇṭit taṉakku avaḷāl tīrat takuvatoru kuṟiyuṭiyavaṉ pōlac collāṭaluṟṟāṉ. talimakaṭkum avaṉukkum taṉimiyil tōṉṟip poliyum kātaluṟavit tōḻi aṟiyāmiyāl toṭakkattil avaṉi aṇukaviṭātu pēcaluṟṟāḷ. avaṉ paṉmuṟiyum toṭarntu taṉkuṟi tōṉṟa uriyāṭiyatu tōḻiyiṉ uḷḷattil ārāycci piṟappittatu. talimakaḷuṭiya nōkkamum, avaṉ varavāl avaḷpāl viḷaṅkum kiḷarcciyum avaḷatu aṟivi naṉku paṇikoḷḷat toṭaṅkiṉa. itukāṟum ayalāṉ pōlat talivaṉik karutiya avaḷatu uḷḷam, aṉpu ceytaṟ kuriyāṉ eṉak karuti nōkkum aḷaviṟku nekiḻvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉik kuṟippāl uṇarnta talimakaṉ, taṉ maṉak kātalic cila coṟkaḷāl tōḻi aṟiya urikkaluṟṟu, "paratavar makaḷākiya talimakaḷatu māṉ pōṉṟa nōkkam eṉakku vāyppataṉ muṉpellām ikkaṭaṟṟuṟi yiṭattiluḷḷa pākkamum ataṉic cārnta kāṉaṟ cōliyum yāṉ uṟitaṟku mikka iṉpamāka iruntaṉa; ippōtu avi mikavum iraṅkattakka niliyip payantuḷḷaṉa. itaṟku eṉ ceyvēṉ" eṉa avaḷ cevippaṭa moḻintāṉ. taṉ mukatti uyarttik kaṇ pārviyi virittut talimakaṉi viyappōṭu nōkkiṉāḷ tōḻi. innikaḻcciyit tiṇṇaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
muṟṟā mañcaṭ pacumpuṟam kaṭuppac
cuṟṟiya piṇara cūḻkaḻi iṟaviṉ
kaṇaṅkoḷ kuppi uṇaṅkutiṟaṉ nōkkip
puṉṉiyaṅ koḻuniḻal muṉṉuyttup parappum
1tuṟinaṇi irunta pākkamum uṟinaṉi
iṉitumaṉ aḷitō tāṉē 2muṉiviṉṟu
akaṉṟa alkul aitami nucuppiṉ
mīṉeṟi paratavar maṭamakaḷ māṉamar nōkkam kāṇā vūṅkē.
itu, piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṉ tōḻi kēṭpac colliyatu.
uri
muṟṟā mañcaḷ pacumpuṟam kaṭuppa - muṟṟāta mañcaṭ kiḻaṅkiṉ paciya puṟattip pōla; cuṟṟiya piṇara - uṭalic cuṟṟip piṇar poruntiya; cūḻkaḻi iṟaviṉ - kaṭaṟkariyic cūḻavuḷḷa kaḻiyil vāḻum iṟāmīṉkaḷi; kaṇaṅ koḷ kuppi - kūṭṭamākak kuvitta kuviyal; uṇaṅkutiṟaṉ nōkki - veyilil ularum vakiyi eṇṇi; puṉṉiyaṅ koḻu niḻal muṉṉuyttup parappum - puṉṉi maraṅkaḷiṉ koḻuviya niḻaṟkaṇ muṉṉark koṇṭu ceṉṟu piṉṉar veyil vantavuṭaṉ parappi ularttum; tuṟinaṇi irunta pākkamum - kaṭaṟṟuṟikku aṇittāka irukkum pākkamum; uṟinaṉi iṉitumaṉ - uṟivataṟku mikka iṉitāka iruntatu; tāṉ muṉiviṉṟu aḷitu - atutāṉum ippōtu veṟukkappaṭuva taṉṟāyiṉum iraṅkat takka tāyiṟṟu. kāṇ; akaṉṟa alkul aitu ami nucuppiṉ - akaṉṟa alkuliyum mellitāy aminta iṭiyiṉiyu muṭiya; mīṉ eṟi paratavar maṭa makaḷ - mīṉkaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭip paṭukkum paratavaruṭiya iḷiya makaḷuṭiya; māṉ amar nōkkam kāṇavūṅku - māṉpōṉṟu amartta kaṇkaḷik kāṇāmuṉpu. e-ṟu.
maṭamakaḷ nōkkam kāṇā vūṅku, pākkam uṟinaṉi iṉi tumaṉ aḷitu eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. muṟṟiya mañcaṭ kiḻaṅkiṉ puṟam niṟam ovvāmiyiṉ, muṟṟā mañcaḷ eṉa eṭuttōtiṉār. piṇar, caruccari, kaṭaṟkāṉalic cūḻntu kiṭappatu paṟṟik kaḻi. cūḻkaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu.  iṟavu, iṟāmīṉ, kaṇaṅkoḷ kuppi eṉṟatu, mikutipaṟṟi ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir nuḻiyāvāṟu ceṟiyat taḻittirukkum puṉṉiyiṉ nīḻalik koḻuniḻal eṉṟār. "muṭamutir palaviṉ koḻuniḻal vatiyum1" eṉpa piṟarum. kaṭaṟṟuṟiyiṉ aṇimiyil irukkum tiṟam tōṉṟa tuṟinaṇi yirunta pākkam eṉṟār. pākkam neytal nilattu ūr, uṟi, mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyar. iṉitumaṉ eṉṟatu. ippōtu iṉitaṉṟu eṉpatupaṭa niṟṟaliṉ, 'maṉ', kaḻiviṉkaṇ vantatām.  taṉ uḷḷattiṉ citivu piṟarkkup pulaṉākāvāṟu maṟittu nōkkutaliṉ, kātaṟ putunōy tīṇṭiya paratavar makaḷatu pārviyi māṉamar nōkkam eṉṟār. kaḻittuṟiyum kāṉaṟ cōliyum taḻīi niṟṟaliṉ pākkamum eṉṟaviṭattu ummiyi eccamākak kōṭalu moṉṟu, piṇara eṉṉum peyareccam iṟaviṉ eṉṉum peyar koṇṭu muṭintatu.
piṉṉiṉṟu uḻalum taṉ kātaṉmiyum varuttamum nōkkātu kaṭaṟṟuṟiyiyum pākkattiṉ ciṟappiyum nōkkic cemmāntu iyalum tōḻiyi matiyuṭampaṭukkum kuṟippāl nāṭōṟum vantu piṉṉiṉṟu varuntukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, taṉ varuttamikuti pulappaṭa, tuṟinaṇi yirunta pākkamum uṟinaṉi iṉitu maṉ eṉṟāṉ. tāṉ koṇṭu varuntum kātaṟperukkit talimakaḷaṟintu taṉpāl aruḷum kuṟippiṉaḷāyiṉaḷ; ākavē avaḷpāl eṉ kuṟiyi urittal naṉṟu eṉpāṉ, mīṉeṟi paratavar maṭamakaḷ māṉamar nōkkam eṉṟum, talimakaḷ pārvik kaṇ viḷaṅkum maruṭci niṉṉi matiyuṭampaṭukkum kuṟip piṟṟu eṉpatu tōṉṟa, māṉamar nōkkam eṉṟum ciṟap pittuk kūṟiṉāṉ. nōkkam peṟṟapiṉ, avaḷ kūṭṭam nalkāmiyiṉ tuṟiyum pākkamum uṟivu iṉiya alla vāyiṉa eṉpatu kuṟippu. iṟaviṉ kuppiyatu uṇaṅkutiṟaṉ nōkkip pākkattu uṟiyum paratavar ataṉip puṉṉi niḻalil muṉṉuyttup parappuvar eṉṟatu, talimakaḷatu māṉamar nōkkattāl varunti uṇaṅkum taṉtiṟam nōkkit talimakaḷip puṉṉi nīḻaṟkaṇ koṇṭuyttal vēṇṭum eṉat talimakaṉ taṉkaruttik kuṟippāyt tōḻikkuc coṟṟavāṟu, mīṉeṟiyum paratavar makaḷ eṉṟatu, taṉ nōkkattāl eṉ valiyi eṟintu eṉṉi varuntac ceytal talimakaṭku iyalpāyiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟu. alkul, aitami nucuppu eṉṟu eṭuttu moḻintatu; talimakaḷatu māṇpit tōḻikku varintu kāṭṭiyavāṟu.
"ēṉōr pāṅkiṉum eṇṇuṅ kāli, āṉā vakiya tiṇinilip peyarē1" eṉṟa nūṟpā vuriyil itaṉik kāṭṭi, "itu varitaṟ poruṭṭut talivi vēṟupāṭṭiṟku āṟṟāta tōḻi ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu"eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.

102. cempiyaṉār
cempiyaṉ eṉpatu cōḻarkuṭik kuriya peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. tūṅkeyil eṟinta toṭittōṭ cempiyaṉ eṉṟoru cōḻaṉ paṇṭināḷil vāḻntami nāṭaṟintatu. cōḻaṉ, vaḷavaṉ, ceṉṉi, eṉpaṉa pōlac 'cempiyaṉ' eṉpatum payila vaḻaṅku vatu.  ivvāṟē cempiyaṉ eṉa makkaṭkuc ciṟappākap peya riṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatum tolvaḻakku. tañci māvaṭṭattil cempiyaṉ iruppu, cempiyaṉ kāṭṭūr2 cempiyaṉ nallūr3 cempiyaṉ maṇali4 eṉṟa ūrkaḷum cempiyaṉ eṉṟa peyaric  ciṟappākak koṇṭu viḷaṅkutali nōkkiṉ, ivi orukāl iccāṉṟōr peyarāl tōṟṟiṉavō eṉavum niṉittaṟku iṭamuṇṭākiṟatu. ivar pāṭiyatāka ippāṭṭu oṉṟutāṉ ittoki nūlil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
kaḷavoḻukkam mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukum talimakkaḷiṭiyē kātaluṟavu peruki oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāta  aḷaviṟ ciṟantu niṟpavum, talimakaṉ varintu kōṭali niṉiyātu kaḷavē virumpi oḻukiṉāṉ. ataṉāl tali makaṭkuk kātaluṇarvu perukik kiyaṟavu tarukuvatāyiṟṟu. anniliyil uṟuppuṭiyatu pōlavum uṇarvuṭiyatu pōlavum karutikkoṇṭu neñcoṭu pēcutalum, kēṭṭal urittal mutaliya ceyal illāta aḵṟiṇip poruḷkaḷi avi uṭiyaṉa pōlak karutik kēṭkumāṟum, urikku māṟum, urinikaḻttutalum; kāmak kātalāl kiyaṟavu paṭṭār pāl nikaḻvatu iyalpu. ataṉāl talimakaḷ maṟupaṭiyum tāṉ tiṉippuṉam kāttaṟku vantapōtu puṉattiṟpaṭiyum kiḷi kaḷi nōkki, "kiḷikaḷē, ippuṉattiṟku pōntu nuṅkaṭku vēṇṭuvaṉavākiya uṇavup poruḷip peṟṟuk koṇṭu pōvatil eṉporuṭṭu nīvir iṉi añca vēṇṭā; āṉāl oṉṟu; nīvir tirumpic cellum pōtu eṉ poruṭṭu oṉṟu ceyyumāṟu nummik kitoḻutu vēṇṭuvēṉ; aḵtu yāteṉiṉ, palāmaraṅkaḷ niṟinta cāraliyuṭiya, atō tōṉṟum malippakkam celkuvīrāyiṉ, aṅkē uṟiyum eṉ kātalarik kaṇṭu, avarpāl yāṉ immalic cāraṟ puṉattil tiṉikkāval pūṇṭuḷḷēṉ eṉṟa ceytiyi aṉpu kūrntu terivippīrkaḷāka," eṉa urikkaluṟṟāḷ.
paṇṭu tiṉikkāval mēṟkoṇṭa pōtu kātaṟṟoṭarpuṟṟuk kaḷavoḻukkatti mēṟkoṇṭa kātalaṉ kaṭitiṉ varivu mēṟkoḷḷāmikkuc ciṟitum veṟuppuṟātu, avaṉit talippeytu peṟum kātaṟkūṭṭa iṉpattiyē mēlum viḻintu aṟantalip piriyātu oḻukum nalloḻukkam, avaḷatu ivvurikkaṇ veḷippaṭuvatu kaṇṭa cempiyaṉār, ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉi aḻakuṟat toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
koṭuṅkural kuṟitta cevvāyp1 pacuṅkiḷi
añcal ōmpi ārpatam koṇṭu
niṉkuṟi muṭitta piṉṟi eṉkuṟic
2cellal vēṇṭumāl kitoḻu tirappal
palkōṭ palaviṉ cāral avarnāṭṭu
niṉkiḷi 3maruṅkiṉiṟ cēṟi yāyiṉ
ammali kiḻavōṟ kurimati immalik
kāṉak kuṟavar maṭamakaḷ
ēṉal kāva lāyiṉaḷ eṉavē
itu kāmam mikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷavi.
uri
koṭuṅkural kuṟitta cevvāyp pacuṅkiḷi - muṟṟit talivaḷintu niṟkum tiṉikkatirkaḷi uṇṭa civanta vāyiyum pacumiyāṉa niṟattiyu muṭiya kiḷiyē; añcal ōmpi - accamiṉṟi; ārpatam koṇṭu - vēṇṭum uṇavup poruḷi nirampak koṇṭuceṉṟu; niṉkuṟi muṭitta piṉṟi - niṉakku iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇiyic ceytu 
muṭittapiṉpu; eṉkuṟi cellal vēṇṭumāl - eṉatu kuṟiyoṉṟiyum muṭittut taral vēṇṭic cellavēṇṭuvēṉ; kitoḻutu irappal - atu kuṟittu yāṉ niṉṉik kitoḻutu kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ; avar nāṭṭu palkōḷ palaviṉ cāral - avaratu nāṭṭiṉ palavākiya kulikaḷiyuṭiya palāmaraṅkaḷ niṟinta cāralil; niṉkiḷi maruṅkiṉil cēṟiyāyiṉ - niṉṉuṭiya cuṟṟamākiya kiḷikaḷ vāḻum iṭattiṟkuc celkiṉṟāyākaliṉ; ammali kiḻavōṟku - anta malikku uriyaṉākiya eṉ kāta
laṟku; urimati - eṭuttuc colluvāyāka; immalik kāṉak kuṟavar maṭamakaḷ - immaliyiṭattē vāḻum kāṉavēṭṭuvaruṭiya iḷamakaḷ; ēṉal kāval āyiṉaḷ 
eṉa - mīṭṭum tiṉippuṉaṅ kāvali mēṟkoṇṭāḷ eṉṟu e-ṟu.
pacuṅkiḷi, añcal ōmpi, ārpataṅ koṇṭu, niṉkuṟi muṭitta piṉṟi, avarnāṭṭuc cāral cēṟiyāyiṉ, ammali kiḻavōṟku immali maṭamakaḷ ēṉal kāval āyiṉaḷ 
eṉa urimati; eṉkuṟi itaṉic ceytaṟkuc cellal vēṇṭum; ataṟkāka niṉṉik kitoḻutu irappal eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. muṟṟiyavaḻit tiṉimaṇikaḷiṉ poṟi āṟṟātu katirkaḷ tali cāyntu viṭuvatupaṟṟi, koṭuṅkural eṉṟum, akkatirkaḷik koytu koṇṭu cellum iyalpiṉa vākaliṉ kuṟitta eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. koṭuṅkuralik kuṟikkum vāy cevvitākal vēṇṭum eṉṟa nayam tōṉṟak koṭuṅkural kuṟinta cevvāyp pacuṅkiḷi eṉṟār. kiḷiyiṉ vāy civantiruttal iyalpu. piṟar viḷitta katirit tāṉ koytu cēṟal kuṟṟamātali uṇarātu puṉattiṟ paṭitalāl, tiṉikavarum kiḷikaḷi accuṟutti ōppuvarākaliṉ kiḷikaṭku accam iyalpu āyiṟṟu. ārpatam, uṇṇum cevviyuṭiya uṇavupporuḷ, katir koytu ceṉṟu peṭikkum pārppukaṭkum nalkum iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇiyātaliṉ kiḷiyiṉ ceyalik kuṟi eṉṟār. kuṟi - iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷ; taṉkuṟi muṭikkāmal piṟar kuṟi muṭikkak karututal naṉ
ṟaṉṟu eṉpatu paṟṟiyum, eṉkuṟiyi muṭittapiṉ niṉkuṟiyi muṭikka eṉṟal kuṟiyirappōrkku muṟiyaṉṟu eṉpatu paṟṟiyum; niṉkuṟi muṭitta piṉṟi eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu; kōḷ, kuli; kiḷiyumām; paṉmuṟiyum kāyum kaṉiyum koḷḷappaṭuvatupaṟṟi palkōṭ palavu eṉappaṭṭatu eṉṟumām; "palkōḷ nelli1" eṉṟāṟ
pōla.
kātalvēṭki mītūrtalāl kaḻipaṭar uṟṟuc ceyvaki aṟiyātu kiḷiyik kātalaṉpāl tūtuviṭak karutukiṉṟāḷ. ākaliṉ uyarcoṟ kiḷaviyāl koṭuṅkural kuṟitta cevvāyp pacuṅkiḷi eṉṟāḷ. taṉṉik kāṇiṉum taṉmoḻi kēṭpiṉum palavāyp pōtarum kiḷiyiṉam, taṉ kikkavaṇukkum taṭṭikkum añci nīṅkutaliṉ, añca vēṇṭā eṉat tēṟṟuvāḷ, añcal ōmpi eṉṟum accamikutiyāl, vēṇṭuvaṉa kuṟiyak koḷḷātu nirampak koṇṭu celka eṉpāḷ. ārpatam koṇṭu eṉṟum, piṟar viḷitta poruḷit tāṉ kāvalikantu kavarntēkutal muṟiyaṉ ṟāyiṉum, atu niṉakku iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpatu paṟṟic ceykiṉṟaṉi yākaliṉ, ataṉi virumpiya vaṇṇamē, ceyka eṉpāḷ, niṉkuṟi muṭitta piṉṟi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. tamakkuṟṟa kuṟiyoṉṟi muṭikkuñ ceyalil īṭupaṭṭōr ataṉi muṭittallatu piṟa etaṉiyum karuttiṟ koḷḷāmi iyaṟki yātaliṉ niṉkuṟiyi mutaṟkaṇ muṭikka eṉpāḷ niṉkuṟi muṭitta piṉṟi eṉṟum, niṉkuṟi muṭitaṟkuyāṉ utavuvatu pōla eṉkuṟi yoṉṟu uḷatu, ataṉi nī muṭittut tarutal vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ; eṉkuṟi cellal vēṇṭumāl eṉṟum, aḵtu niṉakku ariyatoṉ ṟaṉṟu; niṉkuṟi muṭintapiṉ, kiḷiyākiya kiḷḷiyiṉattik kāṇac celkuvi yākaliṉ, appoḻutu eṉporuṭṭu eṉ kātalaṉpāl cellutal vēṇṭum; ituvē eṉakku nī ceyyak kūṭiya utavi eṉpāḷ. niṉkiḷimaruṅkiṉil cēṟiyāyiṉ eṉṟum, cellal vēṇṭumāl eṉṟum, itaṉip puṟakkaṇiyātu ceytal vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, kitoḻutu irappal eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. eṉ kātalaṉum mikka cēymiyi luḷḷavaṉallaṉ; atō aṇmiyil tōṉṟum malikkuriyaṉ eṉpāḷ ammalikiḻavōṉ eṉṟum, avaṉpāl ceṉṟaviṭattu, yāṉ paṇṭupōl ēṉal kāvali mēviṉēṉ eṉpataṉi māttiram urittal vēṇṭum eṉṟaṟku ammali kiḻavōṟku urimati immalik kuṟavar maṭamakaḷ ēṉal kāval āyiṉaḷ eṉa eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. paṇṭu ēṉal kāval mēviya pōtu iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻntami niṉippittaṟkut tiṉikkāvali eṭuttu moḻintāḷ, immali, muṟṟiya tiṉimaṇikaḷi yuṭittātal pōla, ammali palkōṭ palaviṉi yuṭittu; ākavē āṇṭuc celvatāl nīyum payaṉpeṟukuvi eṉṟaṟkup pal kōṭ palaviṉ cāral eṉac ciṟappittāḷ.
"maṟintavaṟ kāṇṭal"1 eṉat toṭaṅkum nūṟpā vuriyil "aṉṉapiṟa" eṉṟataṉāl "iṉṉum talivi kūṟṟāka ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉāṉ amikka" eṉṟu kūṟi, ippāṭṭik kāṭṭi, "itu pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ nīṭa āṟṟātu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu" eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
103. marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār
illiruntu nallaṟam purintuvarum putumaṇa vāḻvil talimakaṉ poruḷ kuṟittup piriyavēṇṭiya toru kaṭami yuṭiyaṉāṉāṉ. kaṭamikkum kātalukkum kaṭumpōr nikaḻumiṭam iḷamic cevviyākaliṉ, appōrkku iṭamāṉa avaṉatu neñciṉkaṇ kaṭami veṉṟatu; kātalipāl aritiṉ viṭipeṟṟa avaṉ neṭuvaḻi kaṭantu celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. vaḻiyil curattiṉ arumiyum vemmiyum orupāl varut
tiṉum, orupāl āṅkāṅku vāḻum vilaṅkum, puḷḷum ākiya uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṭiyē nikaḻum kātaliṉpak kāṭcikaḷ talimakaṉ maṉattik kalakkik kavalvittaṉa. vemmi mikutiyāl nīrppaciyaṟṟut tōṉṟiya curattiṉkaṇ, orupāl kaḷiṟṟu yāṉi oṉṟu ciṟunīr kaḻittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟatāka; avvaḻiyē pōnta cennāy oṉṟu ciṟunīr īrattik kaṇṭu ataṉkaṭ kiṭantu taṉ vemmiyit taṇittuk koḷḷa muyalavum; ataṉik kūṭi iṉpuṟṟup pirinta āṇnāy taṉ piṇaviṉ poruṭṭu vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟatu. peṇṇāy ataṉ iṉpavaravi niṉintu varuntik koṇṭu kiṭantatu. ikkāṭci talimakaṉ uḷḷattil avaṉuṭiya kātaliyi niṉippittuk kātalvēṭkiyik kiḷarac ceytu varuttat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. varutta mikutiyāl āṟṟāṉākiya talimakaṉ taṉ neñci nōkki, "inta neṭuñcurattut taṉittup pōnta neñcamē! mēṟkoṇṭu pōnta viṉi kuṟittu yām toṭarntu celvatā? aṉṟi iṭiyil maṭaṅki mīḷat tirumpi nam kātalipāl celvatā? ceyyat takkataṉi oru taliyākat tuṇintu kūṟuka" eṉṟu viṉavaluṟṟāṉ.
kātal viḷikkum varuttattiṉum kaṭamiyi ikaḻntāṟ piṟakkum iḷivaravukku añcik kaṭami vaḻiyoḻukip pukaḻpeṟuvatu talimakkaḷiṉ talimic ceyal; ataṉāl talimakaṉ mīḷuvatiṉṟi mēlum toṭarntu ceṉṟu kaṭami muṭivil
tāṉ mīḷkiṉṟāṉ. āyiṉum iṭiyil avaṉ taṉ taṉmikku māṟākak kaṭamiyi nekiḻntu kātaṟkāma vayappaṭṭuk kātali yirukkum maṉinōkki varat tuṇintāṉ pōla nikaḻum avaṉuṭiya uriyiṉkaṇ avaṉatu talimi māṇpu takkāṅku veḷippaṭuvatu kaṇṭa marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
oṉṟuterin turitticiṉ neñcē! puṉkāl
ciṟiyili vēliṉ 1periya koṉṟu
kaṭāam cerukkiya kaṭuñciṉa muṉpiṉ
kaḷiṟuniṉ ṟiṟanta nīral līrattup
pālvī tōṉmuli akaṭunīlañ cērttip
paciyaṭa muṭaṅkiya piṅkaṭ cennāy
2māyā vēṭṭam pōkiya kaṇavaṉ
poyyā marapiṟ puṇarvu 3niṉin tiraṅkum
viruntiṉ veṅkāṭṭu varuntutum yāmē.
āḷviṉik kakalvām eṉiṉum
mīḷvām eṉiṉum nītuṇin tatuvē.
itu, poruḷvayiṟ pirinta talivaṉ iṭiccurattu āṟṟātākiya neñciṉik kaḻaṟiyatu.
uri
neñcē - oṉṟu terintu uritticiṉ - yātēṉum oṉṟiṉit tuṇintu colvāyāka; ciṟiyili vēliṉ periya koṉṟu - ciṟiya ilikaḷiyuṭiya vēla maraṅkaḷil periyavaṟṟi vīḻttu; kaṭāam cerukkiya - matam cerukkit tiriyum; kaṭuñciṉa muṉpiṉ kaḷiṟu niṉṟuiṟanta nīral īrattu - mikka ciṉamum vaṉmiyumuṭiya kaḷiṟu kaḻittu oḻinta ciṟunīrāl uḷatākiya īrattiṉ kaṇ; pālvī tōl muli akaṭu nilam cērtti - pāl vaṟṟit tōl curuṅkiya muliyiyuṭiya vayiṟṟi nilattiṟ paṭivittu; paci aṭa muṭaṅkiya piṅkaṇ cennāy - paci varuttutalāl curuṇṭu kiṭakkum cennāyiṉ poruṭṭu; māyā vēṭṭam pōkiya kaṇavaṉ - keṭāta vēṭṭi mēṟ ceṉṟa kaṇavaṉākiya āṇnāyiṉ; poyyā marapiṉ puṇarvu niṉintu iraṅkum - tappāta muṟimiyāl mīṇṭuvarum varavi niṉintu varunti yuṟiyum; viruntiṉ veṅkāṭṭu varuntutum yām - putumi yuṭittākiya vevviya kāṭṭiṭiyē niṉintu varuntukiṉṟōm yāmāka; āḷviṉikku akalvām eṉiṉum - mēṟkoṇṭaviṉi kuṟittu mēlum celva tāyiṉum; mīḷvām eṉiṉum - viṉi karutātu mīḷat tirumpic celvatāyiṉum; nī tuṇintatu - nī koṇṭa tuṇiviṉi e.ṟu.
veṅkāṭṭu varuntutum yām; ākaliṉ, akalvām eṉiṉum, mīḷvām eṉiṉum, nī tuṇintatu oṉṟu, neñcē, terintu uritticiṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. cennāy, kaṇavaṉ puṇarvu niṉintu iraṅkum eṉa iyiyum, 'ciṉ' muṉṉilikkaṇ vanta aci; kaṭāam cerukki mati maruṇṭamiyiṉ, pulliya kāliyum ciṟu ciṟu ilikaḷiyumuṭiya vēlamarattik kaḷiṟu koṉṟu cāyttateṉka. veyil vīṟṟirunta vempal aruñcurattil kuḷirnta niḻal tarum maraṅkaḷ iṉmiyiṉ kaḷiṟu kaḻitta ciṟunīrāl uḷatākiya īrattil cennāyp piṇavu vīḻntu kiṭantatām. veyil vemmi mikku varuntuṅkāl, nāyiṉam īranilam kaṇṭaviṭattut taṉ vayiṟu nilattiṟ paṭiyak kiṭappatu iyalpu. pāliṉṟi vaṟṟiya mulittōl tiraṅkit tōṉṟutaliṉ pālvī tōlmuli eṉṟār. cennāy, nāyiṉattuḷ oṉṟu. vēṭṭi kuṟittuc cellum cennāy ataṉ kaṇ tappuva tiṉmiyiṉ māyāvēṭṭam eṉṟum, taṉpāl aṉpu celuttum āṇiṉ naṉṟiyiṉi maṟavātu ataṉ iṉpa varavu nōkkum iyalpu tōṉṟap poyyā marapiṉ puṇarvu, eṉṟum, puṇarvu niṉiyap piṟakkum iṉpattiṉum vēṭṭam niṉiya eytum varuttam mikku niṟṟaliṉ, puṇarvu niṉintu eṉṟum, anniṉivu tōṉṟiyatum puṇarvu peṟutal kūṭāmiyāl varuntutal tōṉṟa iraṅkum eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. paciyum, veyil vemmiyum mikku varuttum vevviya kāṭṭiṉkaṇ tuṇiyiṉ varavi niṉital uyirkku iyalpāyiṉum, cennāyiṉ piṇavu atu. ceyvatu putumiyāka iruttaliṉ viruntiṉ veṅkāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. neñciṉi  uḷappaṭut
turittaliṉ varuntutum eṉap paṉmi viṉi tarappaṭṭatu.
maṉattiṉkaṇ nikaḻum kātaṟkāmap pūcalukku āṟṟātu taṉ neñciṉi neruṅkik kūṟukiṉṟāṉātalāl, eṭutta eṭuppilēyē oṉṟu terintu uritticiṉ neñcē eṉṟāṉ. mēṟkoṇṭa viṉimēṟ cēṟal naṉṟu eṉa orukālum, kātaliyi nōkkic celavu viṭuttu mīḷal naṉṟu eṉa orukālum niṉintu teḷiviṉṟit kalaṅkik kiyaṟutal paṟṟi oṉṟu terintu eṉṟum, mēṟcēṟal mīḷal eṉṟa iraṇṭu niṉivukaṭkum iṭiyē neñcam ceyalaṟ ṟoḻivatu kaṇṭu uritticiṉ eṉṟum urittāṉ. puṉkāṟ ciṟiyili vēliṉ periya maraṅkaḷik kolvatāl kaḷiṟṟukkup payaṉ yātum illi eṉpatu tōṉṟap puṉkāṟ ciṟiyili vēliṉ periya koṉṟu eṉṟum; eṉiṉum, kaḷiṟu ataṉic ceytaṟkuk kāraṇam kaṭāam cerukkiya taṉṟip piṟitu iṉmiyiṉ kaṭāam cerukkiya eṉṟum; kaṭuñciṉamum, peruvaliyum kāraṇamāka vēliṉ periya maraṅkaḷik koṉṟatu eṉpataṟku avaṟṟi vitantum kūṟiṉāṉ. kaṭuñciṉa muṉpiṉ kaḷiṟu kaṭāam cerukkit tiriyum pōtu ataṉatu ciṟunīr aḷaviṟ perukit tī nāṟṟam mikku niṟkumātaliṉ, ataṉ īratti īyum ēṟumpumallatu piṟa evvuyirum nāṭāvākap paci mikutiyāl uṭalvāṭi, ōyvuṟṟa cennāy, taṉmey vetupput taṇitaṟ poruṭṭuk kaḷiṟṟiṉ ciṟunīr ceyta īrattiṉkaṭ kiṭappatu kaṇṭu kūṟuvāṉ, kaḷiṟu niṉṟu iṟanta nīral īrattup pālvī tōṉmuli akaṭu nilañ cērttip paciyaṭa muṭaṅkiya eṉṟāṉ. paci mikutiyāl uṭal vāṭik kiṭakkum iṭattiṉ tīmi niṉiyātu āṅkē iruntu varuntum cennāyp piṇaviṉ nilikaṇṭa 
ataṉ kaṇavaṉ, ataṉpaci taṇippatu kuṟittu vēṭṭam ceṉṟamiyiṉ vēṭṭam pōkiya kaṇavaṉ eṉṟum, vēṭṭattil peṟak karutiyataṉip peṟṟaṉṟi mīḷāta peruntakimi tōṉṟa, māyāvēṭṭam eṉṟum, vēṭṭam muṭintu mīḷvatu orutaliyāyiṉum iṭiyē uṇṭākum taṉimiyāl tuyarum, poṟukkalākāmiyāl melivum koṇṭu iraṅkuvatu kaṇṭu, poyyā marapiṟ puṇarvu niṉintu iraṅkum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. piṇaviṉ paci taṇittaṟ kaṇ atu poyttal iṉmiyiṉ, poyyā marapiṟ puṇarvu eṉac ciṟappikkiṉṟāṉ. vaṟumiyāl  vāṭip paciyāl melintu kiṭakkum piṇaviṉ poruṭṭuc cennāyiṉ āṇ vēṭṭam celvatiyum, pirivāṟṟāta piṇavu taṉimi niṉiyat tōṉṟum varuttattil ataṉ uḷḷam celvatiyum talimakaṉ eṭuttōtiyatu. vaṟumiyiṉ koṭumiyi muṟpaṭa niṉintu poruḷvayiṟ pirintu pōtarum taṉ pirivi āṟṟātu maṉiyiṉkaṇ oppaṉiyiṉṟip puḻutipaṭinta nilattiṟ kiṭakkum tali makaḷatu niliyic cennāyp piṇaviṉ kiṭakki kāṭṭa, vēṭṭam pōkiya kaṇavaṉatu varavu niṉintu atu iraṅku vatu avaḷatu maṉaniliyi etirpeytu kāṭṭak kaṇṭa kāṭcip payaṉ eṉa aṟika. veyil vemmiyum pacik koṭumiyum mika varuttiya pōtum, kātal vēṭki avaṟṟāl poṉṟātu niṉṟu uyirvāḻkkiyi nili peṟuvikkum putumi talimakaṉatu talimiyaṟivi maruṭṭu taliṉ viruntiṉ veṅkāṭṭu varuntutum yāmē eṉṟāṉ. curattiṭik kaṇṭa cennāyiṉ kāṭci, viṉimēṟ celvatu ataṉit tavirttu mīḷvatu eṉṟa iruvaki niṉivu
kaḷit talivaṉ uḷḷattil eḻuppi, oṉṟum tuṇiyā vakiyil avaṉi alittamiyiṉ, āḷviṉikku akalvām eṉiṉum mīḷvām eṉiṉum eṉṟum, tuṇi
yāmi āṇmikku iḻukkum kālakkeṭum payat taliṉ, nī tuṇintatuvē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. ikkūṟṟiṉ kaṇ āḷviṉikku akalvām eṉṟa karuttē muṟpaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ, poruḷ vayiṟ pirinta talivaṉ ataṉi muṭit tallatu mīḷāṉ eṉa aṟika.
104. pēricāttaṉār
talimippaṇpu niṟinta naṉmakkaḷ iruvariṭiyē iyaṟki neṟiyiṟṟōṉṟiya kātal toṭarpu avarkaḷ mēṟ
koṇṭa kaḷavoḻukkattāl nāḷum vaḷarntu ciṟantu varuki
yil, avaḷ taṉ tōḻipāl, avaṉatu iṉṟiyamiyāmiyip pulappaṭutti avaṉatu uḷḷam variviṉkaṇ īṭupaṭutaṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa urikkumāṟu terivittāḷ. tōḻi ataṉi naṉkuṇarntu talimakaṉi etirppaṭum pōtellām, kuṟip
pākavum veḷippaṭiyākavum varivukaṭāvu vāḷāyiṉaḷ. āyiṉum talivipāl tōṉṟiya kātalaṉpu mēlum perukic ciṟappatu vēṇṭit talivaṉ tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kaḷa
voḻukkatti nīṭṭittuvaralāṉāṉ. iraviṉum pakaliṉum vāyppu nērum pōtellām talimakaṉ kaḷavu veḷippaṭiṉ varum ētattukkum iraviṉkaṇ tāṉ varum vaḻiyiṭi eytum iṭiyūṟṟukkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum añcātu varuvatu tali
makaṭkup peruvaruttatti viḷivippat taṉatu āṟṟāmiyi
yum; accattiyum tōḻikkup paṉmuṟi terivittāḷ. tōḻiyiṉ muyaṟci, karutiya payaṉi uṭaṉ viḷiviyāmiyāl, talimakaṭku accamum avalamum aṭiyat tōṉṟi avaḷatu maṉatti alikkaluṟṟaṉa. iravukkuṟikkaṇ orukāl tali
makaṉ ciṟippuṟamāka vantiruppatu uṇarnta talimakaḷ, tōḻi
yoṭu collāṭupavaḷ pōlat tāṉē kuṟippākat tali
makaṉi varivu kaṭāvuvāḷāyiṉaḷ, "tōḻi, uyariya mali
kaḷum piḷantu keṭumāṟu iṭiyēṟu muṭuki eṟiyum perumaḻi poḻitalāl, nīr perukic cellum neṟiyiyalpu teriyāta immaḻikkālattil, mikka iruḷparaviya naḷḷiravil, eṉ kātalaṉ mārputarum iṉpattiyē niṉintu uyir vāḻkiṉṟē ṉākaliṉ, ikkārmaḻi poḻiyum naḷḷiravil avar varum vaḻiyiṉ koṭumiyiyum arumiyiyum niṉiyātu taṉ
ṉalamē pēṇuvōr eṉṉippōl piṟar yāvar uḷar oruvarum ilar" eṉṟāḷ.
ivvuriyiṉkaṇ, talimakaḷatu varivuvēṭkiyi itukāṟum tōḻi vāyilākak kēṭṭu vanta talimakaṉ. tāṉē talimakaḷatu iṉṟiyamiyāta peruṅkātal niliyi uṇarntu kōṭaṟkuriya kuṟippum, taṉporuṭṭut taṉ iṉiya uyiriyum poruḷ ceyyātu neṟiyiṉatu ētam nōkkātu talimakaṉ varutalit talimakaḷ eṇṇit taṉṉi nontu koḷḷum kuṟippum iṉitu viḷaṅkak kaṇṭa pēricāttaṉār ip
pāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaṟṟit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
pūmpoṟi uḻuvip pōḻvāy1 ēṟṟi
tēṅkamaḻ cilampiṟ kaḷiṟṟoṭu porīṉē
tuṟukal mīmici uṟukaṇ añcāk
kuṟakkuṟu mākkaḷ pukaṟciyiṉ eṟinta2
toṇṭakac ciṟupaṟip pāṇi ayala3
pintāṭ centiṉip paṭukiḷi ōppum
ārkali veṟpaṉ mārpunayan tuṟitaliṉ4
yāṉē yaṉṟiyum uḷarkol pāṉāṭ
pāmpuṭi viṭara ōṅkumali miḷira
urumuccivan teṟiyum poḻutoṭu perunīr
pōkkaṟa vilaṅkiya cāral
nōkkaruñ ciṟuneṟi niṉiyu mōrē.
itu, talivi āṟupārttuṟṟa accattāṟ colliyatu.
uri
pūmpoṟi uḻuvip pōḻvāy ēṟṟi - aḻakiya poṟikaḷiyuṭiya puliyiṉatu piḷantāṟpōl akaṉṟa vāyiyuṭiya āṇ; tēṅkamaḻ cilampil - tēṉiṉatu maṇam nāṟum malippakkattil; kaḷiṟṟoṭu poriṉ - mēyum kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyuṭaṉ pōr ceyyumāyiṉ; tuṟukal mīmici - pakkattē niṟkum uyariya periya pāṟiyiṉ mēlē iruntu koṇṭu; uṟukaṇ añcā - tamatu ceyalāl tamakku varakkūṭiya tīṅkiṟkuc ciṟitum añcutaliṉṟi; kuṟakkuṟu
mākkaḷ - kuṟavaruṭiya iḷañciṟuvarkaḷ; pukaṟciyiṉ eṟinta - appōr kāṇum makiḻcciyāl muḻakkiya; toṇṭakac ciṟupaṟip pāṇi - toṇṭakam eṉṉum ciṟupaṟiyik koṭṭuvatāl uṇṭākum ōci; ayala pintāṭ centiṉip paṭukiḷi ōppum - maṟupakkattēyuḷḷa paciya tāḷiyuṭiya civanta maṇikaḷik koṇṭa tiṉippuṉattē paṭiyum kiḷikaḷi veruṭṭi yōṭṭum; ārkali veṟpaṉ mārpu nayantu uṟitaliṉ - āravārattiyuṭiya mali
nāṭaṉākiya talimakaṉatu mārputarum iṉpamoṉṟē niṉintu īṇṭu uyirvāḻkiṉṟē ṉākaliṉ; yāṉē yaṉṟiyum uḷarkol - taṉṉalamē karutum eṉṉit tavira vēṟu evarēṉum ivvulakil uḷarāvarō; pāṉāḷ - naḷḷiravum; pāmpuṭi viṭara - pāmpukaḷ uṟiyum muḻiñcukaḷiyuṭiya; ōṅkumali miḷira - uyarnta mali piḷantu keṭumpaṭi; urumuc civantu eṟiyum poḻutoṭu - iṭiyēṟu miṉṉi muṭuki eṟiyum, iṭiyum kārpparuvamum kalanta ikkālattil, perunīr pōkkaṟa vilaṅkiya cāral - maḻiyāṟ perukiya nīr pōkkaṟṟut tēkkuṟumāṟu kuṟukkiṭṭu niṟkum maliyiṉatu cāraṟkaṇ; nōkkaruñ ciṟuneṟi niṉiyumōr - kaṇṭaṟintu cellutaṟ kariya akala millāta ciṟuneṟipaṟṟi varutaliṉ arumiyiyum koṭumiyiyum niṉintu añcupavaruḷ e-ṟu.
veṟpaṉ mārpu nayantuṟitaliṉ pāṉāṭ poḻu
toṭu cāral ciṟuneṟi niṉiyumōruḷ yāṉē yaṉṟiyum uḷarkol; oruvarum irār eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. ēṟṟi kaḷiṟṟōṭu poriṉ kuṟakkuṟumākkaḷ. uṟukaṇ añcātu eṟinta pāṇi ayala centiṉip paṭukiḷi ōppum ārkali veṟpaṉ eṉa iyiyum. piḷantāṟ pōla akaṉṟu tōṉṟutaliṉ; puliyiṉ vāy pōḻvāy eṉap
paṭṭatu. "poṟikiḷa ruḻuvip pōḻvā yēṟṟi1" eṉa oḷaviyārum kūṟuvar. makkaḷ vaḻakku arukiya perumalic cāral eṉṟaṟkut tēṅkamaḻ cilampu eṉṟār kaḷiṟum uḻuviyum peruñ ciṉaṅ koṇṭu pōr ceyyumiṭattut tamatu iruppup pulaṉāyiṉ avi tamakkum ūṟu ceyyum eṉṟu niṉiyātu kuṟakkuṟu mākkaḷ toṇṭakac ciṟu
paṟiyi muḻakkutaliṉ, uṟukaṇ añcā eṉṟum, ap
pōr nikaḻcci akkuṟumākkaḷiṉ uḷḷattil accam tōṟṟātu uvaki tōṟṟuvittatu eṉṟaṟkup pukaṟciyiṉ eṟinta eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. pōreṉiṟ pukalum maṟa
māṇpuṭiya makkaḷ eṉpatu itaṉāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭuvatu kāṇka. "toṇṭakam, kuṟiñci nila vāṇaruṭiya ciṟupaṟi vaki; "toṇṭakac ciṟupaṟi pāṉāḷ yāmattum kaṟaṅkum2" eṉap piṟarum kūṟupa pāṇi, īṇṭup paṟiyiṉatu muḻakkiṉ mēṟṟu. mārpu ākupeyar; pirinili ēkāram vikārattāl tokkatu kol - aiyapporuṭṭu. perukiya nīr vaḻintu nīṅkutaṟkuriya pōkku uṇṭākāvāṟu uyariya mali kuṟuk
kiṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ, perunīr pōkkaṟa vilaṅkiya cāral eṉṟum, pōtiya akalamiṉṟi oṟṟiyaṭip pātiyāka uḷḷa vaḻi eṉpatu tōṉṟac ciṟuneṟi eṉṟum atutāṉum nīr perukip pōkkaṟṟut tēṅkiyataṉāl maṟintoḻin
tamiyiṉ, nōkkaruñ ciṟuneṟi eṉṟum, atu maṉak
kaṇṇukkaṉṟip puṟakkaṇṇukkut tōṉṟātu maṟintami
yiṉniṉiyumōr eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. ciṟuneṟi eṉṟatu ākupeyarāl ataṉkaṇ viḷiyum ētattiyum kuṟittatu.
uḷḷuṟiyāl, variyātu oḻukum talimakaṉ iraviṉ
kaṇ taṉittu varuvataṉāl eytum ētam niṉiyātu iv
vūravar pēralar viḷivippar eṉṟum, atu tali
makaṉit tāṉ etirppaṭāvāṟu iṟceṟippum atu vāyi
lāka iṟantupāṭum eytuvikkum eṉṟum kuṟikkiṉṟā
ḷākaliṉ, talimakaḷ uḻuvip pōḻvāyēṟṟi kaḷiṟ
ṟoṭu poriṉ kuṟakkuṟu mākkaḷ uṟukaṇ añcātu pukaṟciyiṉ eṟinta toṇṭakac ciṟupaṟip pāṇi, ayalē
yuḷḷa centiṉip paṭukiḷiyi ōppum tiṟatti eṭuttu moḻintāḷ. makaḷirkkuk kātalaṉ mārpiṉkaṇ pērārvam uḷatātal iyalpātaliṉ ataṉiyē ciṟappāka viḻin
tuṟiyum taṉṉi, ārkali veṟpaṉ mārpu nayantu uṟitaliṉ eṉṟāḷ. 'tīmperum poyki yāmiyiḷam pārpput tāymukam nōkki vaḷarntici ṉāaṅku, atuvē aiyaniṉ mārpē, aṟintaṉi oḻukumati aṟaṉumāratuvē"1 eṉṟum, "puḷiṅkāy vēṭkit taṉṟu niṉ malarnta mārpivaḷ vayāa nōykkē2" eṉṟum makaḷir urittal kāṇka. kārkālattē ikkaḷavu nikaḻtaliṉ, appōtu pakaṟkālattum kārmaḻi parantu katiravaṉi maṟittaliṉ, iravuppōtu pōla iruṇṭu tōṉṟumāka, naḷḷiravil pēriruḷum perumuḻakkoṭu kūṭiya iṭiyum maḻiyum kalantu iyaṅkuvōrkku mikka iṭukkaṇ ceyyum eṉpati neñcāl niṉittuk kalaṅkukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, pāṉāḷ pāmpuṭi viṭara ōṅkumali miḷira, urumuc civantu eṟiyum poḻutu eṉṟāḷ. iruṭkum maḻikkum añcārāyiṉum, yāvarum iṭiyēṟṟiṉ vaṉmikku etirēṟṟu niṟpatu ilar eṉpataṉāl ataṉ vaṉmiyi ōṅkumali miḷira urumuc civantu eṟiyum eṉṟāḷ. cellum neṟiyiyalpic ciṟakka uṇarntu celvōrkkup pāṉāḷiravum kārmaḻiyiṉ iṭiyēṟum pēraccam payantu iṭiyūṟu ceyyāvāyiṉum, ceṉṉeṟi ciṟitāyum maḻi
nīr perukiyum pōkkaṟa niṟintu maṟikkappaṭumāyiṉ, cellutal ciṟitum iyalātatoṉṟu eṉṟum, an
neṟipaṟṟic celvōr eppaṭiyum neṟiyiḻantu peruntīṅkiṟ kuḷḷāvar eṉpatu teḷivātaliṉ, iravuvarum talimakaṉ avvētam eytuvaṉ kol eṉa eṇṇi alamarukiṉṟā ḷātalāl, ciṟu neṟiyiṉ iyalpu niṉivōr uyir
vāḻārākat taṉṉalamē pēṇi yāṉ oruttiyē uyirtāṅki uṟikiṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ, nōkkaruñ ciṟuneṟi niṉiyu
mōruḷ yāṉē yaṉṟiyum uḷarkol eṉṟāḷ. eṉ poruṭṭu emperumāṉākiya talimakaṉ ittuṇi ētat
tukku uḷḷāki varuntuvati iṉi āṟṟēṉ eṉṟāḷāyiṟṟu. iṉi yāṉē yaṉṟiyum uḷarkol eṉṟatu, talimakaṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ varutaliṉ uḷavākum ētatti niṉiyātu tōḻi iravukkuṟi cuṭṭiyatu naṉṟaṉṟu eṉpatupaṭa urit
talu moṉṟu. itaṉāṟ payaṉ talimakaṉ virintu varintu kōṭali mēṟkoḷvāṉāvatu.
105. muṭṭattirumāṟaṉ
muṭṭat tirumāṟaṉ eṉṟa iccāṉṟōr peyar accup
piratiyil muṭattirumāṟaṉ eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. muṭat
tirumāṟaṉ eṉṟa pāṭattiṉum muṭṭat tirumāṟaṉ eṉpatu ciṟantu tōṉṟukiṟatu. muṭṭam eṉpatu "teṉṉāṭṭu vaḷḷuva
nāṭṭil"1 uḷatu; itu mutal irācarācaṉ kālattil mummuṭic cōḻanallūr eṉap peyar māṟṟam eytiyatu kalveṭ
ṭārāycciyāḷar naṉkaṟintatu. ivar tamatu nāṭṭuc cēra
maṉṉaṉāṉa kuṭṭuvaṉic ciṟappippatē itaṟkuc cāṉṟu. ivar peyar māṟaṉ eṉa iruppatu kaṇṭu, ivar pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyil tōṉṟiya aracar eṉak karutuvōru muṇṭu. teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻp pakutiyiluḷḷa muṭṭam eṉṉum ūrkkuriyarāyp pāṇṭi vēntarkaḷāl tirumāṟaṉ eṉṟa ciṟappup peṟṟavarāka eṇṇutaṟkum iṭamirukkiṟatu. ivar pāṭiyaṉavāka iraṇṭu pāṭṭukkaḷ ittoki nūluḷ tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṉpuṭik kātalarāṉa talimakkaḷ kaṭimaṇam 
puṇarntu kaṟpuk kaṭaṉpūṇṭu ilvāḻvu naṭātti varukiyil, kātalaṉākiya talimakaṉ poruḷvayiṟ pirintēkum kaṭami
yāl kātalipāl viṭipeṟṟuc celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyā uṇmi aṉpiṉarākaliṉ, kātali
pāl uṇṭākiya kātaṟṟoṭarpu, pirintu ēkum kātalaṉ uḷḷattil kaḻipaṭar viḷivittu aṟivu kalakkamuṟac ceytatu. taṉ kaṭimaṉiyiṉ nīṅki neṭuñcurattil mikka cēṇiṟ ceṉṟā ṉāyiṉum, avaṉatu neñcu karikaḻi kātalāl kiyaṟavuppaṭṭu mēṟkoṇṭa celavik kiviṭṭu, mīḷat taṉ kātali uṟiyum kaṭimaṉikkē tirumpi viṭumāṟu tūṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. toṭaṅkiya viṉikkaṇ iṭiyē maṭaṅkip piṉcēṟal āṇmikku iḻukku eṉpati avaṉatu talimiyaṟivu eṭuttukkāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttavum, avaṉ taṉ neñciṉi nōkki, "neñcē maṉikkaṇ uṟiyum namkātali arumpaṭar uṟṟu āṟṟātu varuntuvaḷ eṉpatu kāraṇamāka iṭic curattē mīḷvatu karutukiṉṟāy; mēṟkoṇṭa celavu kaṭattaṟkariya curaṅkaḷik kaṭantu pōtaṟ kuriyatu eṉpataṉit toṭakkattē eṇṇā
toḻintaṉi; ippōtu neṭuntolivu vantuḷḷāy; aṅṅaṉamirukka, nī ivviṭattu mīḷak karutuvatu naṉṟaṉṟu, 
vāḻi eṉat teruṭṭi mēṟkoṇṭa celaviṟku ataṉi oruppaṭuppāṉāyiṉāṉ.
avaṉatu kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, kātalvāḻviṉ kalakkuṟu ciṟappum, poruḷ karutic cellum kaṭamiyiṉ uyarvum, mēṟkoṇṭa viṉi iṭiyaṟavupaṭiṉ eytum ētattukku añcum accamum, anniliyil allatu kūṟik kātaluṇarvukku aṭimip
paṭuttum neñciṉit teruṭṭi aṟivu tuṇiyāka āṇmi paṟṟuk kōṭāka āḷviṉiyē nōkkit toṭarntu cellum maṉattiṭpamum māṇpuṟa viḷaṅkutaliṉ, avaṟṟi viyantu ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittu āciriyar muṭṭat tirumāṟaṉār pāṭukiṉṟār.
muḷikoṭi valanta muḷḷari ilavat
toḷirciṉi atira vīci viḷipaṭa
vevvaḷi vaḻaṅkum vēypayil maruṅkiṟ
kaṭunaṭi yāṉi kaṉṟoṭu varunta
neṭunīr aṟṟa niḻalil āṅkaṇ
aruñcurak kavaliya eṉṉāy neṭuñcēṇ
paṭṭaṉi vāḻiya neñcē kuṭṭuvaṉ
kuṭavaric cuṉiya māyitaḻk kuvaḷi
vaṇṭupaṭu vāṉpōtu kamaḻum
taṇkamaḻ ōti1 arumpaṭar uṟavē.
iḵtu, iṭiccurattu mīḷaluṟṟa neñciṉit talimakaṉ kaḻaṟiyatu.
uri
muḷikoṭi valanta muḷḷari ilavattu - ularnta koṭikaḷ piṉṉik koṇṭu kiṭakkum muḷ poruntiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ilavamarattiṉ; oḷirciṉi atira viḷipaṭa vīci - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa kiḷikaḷi acittu muṟiyumāṟu vīci; vevvaḷi vaḻaṅkum - vevviya kāṟṟu mōtum; vēypayil maruṅkil - mūṅkilkaḷ niṟinta pakutiyil; kaṭunaṭi yāṉi kaṉṟoṭu varunta - virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya piṭiyāṉi taṉ kaṉṟoṭu kūṭi niṉṟu varunta; neṭunīr aṟṟa niḻalil āṅkaṇ - neṭuntūram nīr ciṟitum iṉṟivaṟṟiya niḻalillāta avviṭaṅkaḷ; aruñcurak kavaliya - kaṭattaṟ kariya kavartta vaḻikaḷiyuṭiya; eṉṉāy - eṉpatu muṉpē niṉiyāmal; neṭuñcēṭpaṭṭaṉi - neṭuntolivu vantuviṭṭāy; neñcē - vāḻiya; kuṭṭuvaṉ kuṭavaric cuṉiya - kuṭṭu
vaṉuṭiya kuṭamalik kaṇ uḷḷa cuṉiyiṭattu malarnta; māyitaḻk kuvaḷi - kariya itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya kuvaḷiyiṉ; vaṇṭupaṭu vāṉpōtu kamaḻum - vaṇṭu, moykkum periya pūviṉatu maṇam vīcum; taṇkamaḻ ōti - taṇṇiya maṇam kamaḻum kūntaliyuṭiyavaḷāṉa nam kātali; arumpaṭar uṟa - nīṅkutaṟ kariya varuttatti eyti varunta e.ṟu.
neñcē, neṭunīr aṟṟa niḻalil āṅkaṇ aruñcurak kavaliya eṉṉāyāy, taṇkamaḻōti arumpaṭar uṟa, neṭuñ
cēṭpaṭṭaṉi yāka ippōtu mīḷaluṟuvatu eṉṉi, vāḻiya, eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. muḷikoṭi - vaṟṟiyularnta koṭi; valattal - cuṟṟik koḷḷutal, ilavamaram muḷḷuṭiya tākaliṉ muḷḷari ilavam eṉ
ṟār; muḷḷilavu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. muṟintu keṭutal viḷitalātaliṉ viḷipaṭa eṉṟār. mūṅkilkaḷ putarputarāka vaḷarntu niṟkum iyalpiṉa vātaliṉ mūṅkiṟkāṭu vēy
payil maruṅku eṉappaṭṭatu. yāṉikaḷ virinta naṭi
yuṭiya eṉpavākaliṉ, kaṭunaṭiyāṉi eṉac ciṟappittār. "kālkiḷarn taṉṉa vēḻam"1 eṉa nakkīrarum kūṟuvar. nīraṟṟa āṅkaṇ, niḻalil āṅkaṇ eṉa iyiyum, neṭumiyic curattuk kēṟṟiṉumām. eḷitiṟ kaṭakka lākāmipaṟṟi aruñcurak kavaliya eṉṟār. kavali, kavarnta vaḻikaḷ, vāḻiya kuṟippumoḻi, kuvaḷi vāṉpōtu, vaṇṭupaṭu vāṉpōtu eṉa iyikka, vāṉ pōtu, aḻakiya malar.
vevvaḷi ilaviṉ ciṉi yatira vīci vaḻaṅkum vēypayiṉ maruṅkil niṉṟa yāṉi kaṉṟoṭu varunti niṟpatu kaṇṭa talimakaṟku, taṉ piriviṉāl uṇṭāya varuttam alit
talāl talimakaḷ taṉ putalvaṉoṭu niṉṟu maṉikkaṇ eytum tuyaram niṉiviṭit tōṉṟavum. anniliyil vēṭki vayappaṭṭa avaṉ neñcam mēṟcelvati viṭuttu mīḷvām eṉa urikkaluṟṟatu; ataṉāl, "neñcē nī karutuvatu tavaṟu; curattiṉ vemmiyum, arumiyum nōkki mēṟ celavu muṉintaṉi yāyiṉ, itaṉi muṉṉarē eṇṇiyiruttal vēṇṭum; toṭaṅkiya ceyal iṭiyil maṭaṅkutal iḻukku eṉpati niṉiyāyāyiṉi eṉpāṉ. aruñcurak kavaliya eṉṉāy eṉṟum, mēṟkoṇṭa viṉi muṭittaṟku neṭuñcēṇ pōnta nī, maṭaṅki mīḷaluṟuva
taṉāl poruṭkēṭum vaciyum eytum eṉpati aṟin
tili; niṉ aṟiyāmi iruntavāṟu eṉṉē! eṉpāṉ.. neṭuñcēṇ paṭṭaṉi eṉṟum, vāḻiya eṉṟum kūṟi
ṉāṉ. itaṉi nī toṭakkattē niṉiyāmiyāṉ nam kātali peruntuyar uḻappāḷāyiṉaḷ eṉpāṉ, taṇkamaḻ ōti arumpaṭar uṟa eṉṟum, toṭaṅkum niliyil eṉ kāta
likkum, curattiṭi mīḷaluṟuvatāl eṉakkum naṟṟuṇi yāyiṉāyalli eṉṟaṟku vāḻiya eṉṟum kuṟitturittāṉ. itaṉāl talivaṉatu talimimāṇpu veḷippaṭutal kāṇka.
106. toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ
maṉiyaṟam purintoḻukum māṇpuṭiya talimakkaḷ vāḻvil orukāl talimakaṉ taṉ kātaliyiṉ nīṅkic ceyaṟkuriya viṉi kuṟittup pirintu pōyiṉaṉ. ceṉṟa viṭattut tāṉ mīḷutaṟkuk kuṟitta paruvam vantatu; mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyum muṭitalāl avaṉ uḷḷam taṉ kātalipāl celvatāyiṟṟu viṉiyiṭattuk kātal vēṭkiyi niṉiyāta avaṉ uḷḷat
til, viṉimuṭivil vēṟu niṉittaṟ kiṉmiyiṉ, āḻn
tirukkum kātal niṉivu mēṟpaṭṭu eḻuntu avaṉ 
uṇarvu muṟṟum cūḻntu koṇṭatu; paṇṭiya niṉivukaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka eḻuntaṉa. anniliyil mēṟkoṇṭa viṉi kuṟittup piriya vēṇṭiya kaṭamiyum, cevviyum eytiya pōtu, ataṉi avaṭku urittu avaḷatu viṭipeṟṟa nikaḻcci avaṉ maṉaniliyil māṇpuṟṟu niṉṟatu. orunāḷ kaṭaṟkarikkut tāṉ taṉ kātaliyuṭaṉ tērēṟic ceṉṟatum, aṅkē avaḷ alikaḷāṟ koḻittu otukkappaṭṭa 
ekkar maṇalil mēynta ciṟuciṟu naṇṭukaḷi alittu viḷi
yāṭiyatum; avaṉ maṉak kaṇṇil kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. paṉmuṟi
yum; palliṭattum alavaṉpiṉ ōṭi alintamiyāl ciṟitu pōtil avaṭku acivu tōṉṟavē, avaḷi aṇuki, ārva moḻi vaḻaṅki āṟṟuvitta talimakaṉ, avaḷ ayarcci nīṅki aṉpāl iṉpuṟṟa niliyil tāṉ piriya vēṇṭiya nilimi
yiṉi mellak kūṟi viṭi vēṇṭiṉaṉ. pōṉa curattip puḷiyiṭṭaḻittāṟ pōlak kaḻinta avalam avaḷpāl mīḷat tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ, avaḷ maṟumoḻi vaḻaṅkutaṟ kēṟṟa vali
yiḻantu arukil niṉṟa ñāḻaṟ potumpiṟkuc ceṉṟu ataṉ iṇarkaḷik koytu avaṟṟik kiyāṟ picittu utirkku mukattāl taṉatu āṟṟāmimikutiyiyum pirivarumiyiṉ peṟṟiyiṉiyum, varuttattiyum avaṉ kāṇat terivittāḷ. piṉṉar, avaḷ teḷiyat takuvaṉa kūṟit taṉ maṉikkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ aṉpāṟ piṇippuṇṭu oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyārāy oḻukum kātalar vāḻvil, pirivu tōṉṟiṉ aḵtu iruvar uḷḷattilum niṉṟu eytuvikkum avalac ceykiyiyum viṉimuṭivil tōṉṟi avaratu kātali māṇpuṟuttum ciṟappiyum kaṇṭa toṇṭimāṉ iḷan
tiriyaṉ peruviyap puṟṟāṉāka, avaṉatu pulamiyuḷḷatti ivi paṇikoḷḷavē ippāṭṭu veḷippaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
aṟitalum aṟitiyō pāka peruṅkaṭal
eṟitiri koḻīiya1 ekkar veṟikoḷa
āṭuvari alavaṉ ōṭuvayiṉ āṟṟā2
tacii3 oḻinta vacitīr kuṟumakaṭ
kuyaviṉeṉ ceṉṟiyāṉ uṇṇō urippa
maṟumoḻi peyarttal āṟṟāḷ naṟumalar
ñāḻal añciṉit tāḻiṇar koḻuti
muṟitimirn tutirtta kiyaḷ
aṟivañar uṟuvi āymaṭa niliyē.
itu, piriviṭip paruvavaraviṉkaṭ paṇṭu nikaḻnta tōr kuṟippu uḷḷiya talimakaṉ ataṉit tāṅkaṟ killāṉāy mīḷkiṉṟāṉ tērppākaṟkuc coṟṟatu.
uri
pāka-; aṟitalum aṟitiyō-nī itaṉi naṉku aṟi
vāyaṉṟē; peruṅkaṭal eṟitiri koḻīiya ekkar veṟi
koḷa-periya kaṭaliṭattē eḻuntu vīcum alikaḷāl koḻikkappaṭṭa nuṇmaṇaṟ parappu nuṭaṅkumāṟu; āṭuvari alavaṉ ōṭuvayiṉ āṟṟātu-viḷiyāṭukiṉṟa vari
poruntiya naṇṭukaḷ ōṭi yoḷikkum iṭattut tāṉ avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉē ōṭamāṭṭātu; acii-taḷarntu; oḻinta vacitīr kuṟumakaṭku-alavaṉāṭṭum viḷiyāṭalik kiviṭṭirunta kuṟṟamillāta ceyaṟ paṇpuṭiya iḷiya
vaṭku; uyaviṉeṉ ceṉṟu-varuttamuṭiyēṉāyc ceṉṟu neruṅki: yāṉ uḷ nō urippa-eṉ uḷḷattē tōṉṟiya pirivu kaṇṇiya eṉ maṉa nōyi mella urittēṉāka; maṟumoḻi peyarttal āṟṟāḷ-maṟumoḻi yoṉṟum kūṟa iyalātavaḷāy; naṟumalarñāḻal añciṉit tāḻiṇar koḻuti-naṟiya pūkkaḷit tāṅkiya ñāḻaliṉ aḻakiya kiḷiyiṭattē tāḻnta pūṅkottukkaḷik kōti; muṟi timirntu utirnta kiyaḷ-iḷantaḷirkaḷip paṟittuk kiyāṟpicintu utirkkum ceyalic ceytu; aṟivu 
añar uṟuvi-kaṇṭa eṉ aṟivukku varuttatti uṇṭu
paṇṇiṉa avaḷatu; āymaṭanili-aḻakiya iḷamic ceyki niliyi (e.ṟu).
pāka ekkar, alavaṉ ōṭuvayiṉ āṟṟātu acii 
oḻinta kuṟumakaṭku uyaviṉeṉ ceṉṟu yāṉ uḷnō urippa maṟumoḻi peyarttal āṟṟāḷ iṇarkoḻuti muṟitimirntu utirttakiyaḷ, uṟuvi, āymaṭa niliyi aṟitalum aṟitiyō eṉakkūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. aṟitalum aṟiti eṉṟatu. teḷiya aṟivāyaṉṟō eṉpatu
paṭa niṉṟatu. "peruvari nāṭaṉiaṟiyalu maṟiyēṉ1" eṉap piṟarum ivvāypāṭē vaḻaṅkumāṟu kāṇka. veṟi maṇamumām acital taḷartal, vaci, kuṟṟam, ekkariṭattu mēyum alavaṉi alikkum viḷiyāṭṭil ataṉ uyirkkō uṭaṟkō ciṟutīṅkum eytāvāṟu alippatu paṟṟit talimakaḷi vacitīr kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟār. maṟu
moḻi peyarttal āṟṟāḷ eṉṟatu, maṟumoḻi vaḻaṅkak karuttuṇṭāyiṉum nā eḻāmi tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. añar, tuṉpam, uṟuvippāḷi uṟuvi eṉṟār. 'evaṉ ceytaṉaḷ ippērañaruṟuvi' eṉṟum, "celavutalik koṇṭa peru
vituppuṟuvi2" eṉṟum cāṉṟōr urippatu kāṇka.
pirintuṟiyum talimakaṉ uḷḷattil paruvavaravum viṉimuṭivum talimakaḷi niṉippittaliṉ piri
vuṇarttiya ñāṉṟu avaḷ eytiya vēṟupāṭu maṉak
kaṇṇil tōṉṟak kaṇṭu,  aṟitalum aṟitiyō pāka eṉattaṉ pākaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ pirivuk kuṟippi mutaṟkaṇ kaṭaṟkari ekkar maṇalil alavaṉi alittu viḷi
yāṭṭayarnta pōtu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, ataṉi vitantu, ekkarveṟikoḷa āṭuvari alavaṉ ōṭuvayiṉ āṟṟātu acii oḻinta vacitīr kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟum, ataṉi mikka varuttattuṭaṉ tāṉ ceṉṟu avaṭku uritta kuṟippi, uyaviṉeṉ ceṉṟu yāṉ uḷnō urippa eṉṟum. moḻintāṉ. nō, varuttam; nōyumām. ettuṇit tiṇṇiyōrkkum kātalarip pirintuṟivatu mikka tuṉpan taruvatoṉ ṟākaliṉ, uyaviṉaṉ ceṉṟu eṉavum, pirivāl uḷatākum varuttatti uṇṇō eṉavum urittaliṉ arumi tōṉṟa urippa eṉavum eṭuttu moḻintāṉ. tuyar kalanta coṟkaḷāl talimakaṉ taṉ pirivukkuṟippip pulappaṭuttiyatum, taliviyatu uḷḷam varuttam niṟintu nāppuṭi peyarā vakiyil avaḷatu uṇarvu muḻuvatum kavarntu koṇṭamiyiṉ maṟumoḻi peyarttal āṟṟāḷ eṉṟum, taṉ etir niṟkavum māṭṭātukāṉaṟ cōlikkaṇ arukil niṉṟa ñāḻaṟ potumpariṭic ceṉṟu taṉ kalaṅkiya niliyi maṟikka muyaṉṟa tiṟatti naṟumalar ñāḻal añciṉit tāḻiṇar koḻuti eṉṟum, kātal vēṭki niṟinta pōtum icceyal makaḷiriṭattē nikaḻtal iyalpākaliṉ pirivuk kuṟippāl nikaḻnta vēṟupāṭu viḷaṅka, muṟitimirntu utirtta kiyaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. kātaṟkūṭṭam kaṇṇiya niliyāyiṉ, naṟumalar koytu iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭu mēṟkoḷḷappaṭu meṉa aṟika. muṟitimirntu utirttal, uḷḷattil tōṉṟiya tuṉpam kāraṇamākap piṟanta muṉivu meyn niṟuttaltaliviyatu meṉmiyum, iḷamiyum cuṭṭutaṟku āymaṭanili eṉṟum viṉiyē āṇmikku uyireṉṟum, kātalarip pirintu ceyyum viṉiyiṉum vīṟumikka viṉi
yāṇmi piṟitilli yeṉṟum, taṉ iḷamiyāl aṟiyāmiyum; pirivu nikaḻtaṟku muṉpē atu nikaḻntatu pōlak karuti, mēṉi vēṟupaṭum meṉmiyum viḷaṅka āymaṭanili eṉṟum, avaḷpāl appōḻtu veḷip
paṭṭa paṇpum ceyalum eṉ aṟivip peritum kalakki
viṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟku aṟivu añar uṟuvi eṉṟum urittāṉ eṉak koḷka. itaṉāṟ payaṉ, pākaṉ kēṭṭut tēri
virintu celuttuvāṉāvatu.
107. peruvaḻutiyār
illiruntu nallaṟam purintoḻukum talimakkaḷiṭiyē talimakaṉ kaṭami kuṟittup pirintu ceṉṟu uṟiya vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. kātaliyi uṭaṉ koṇṭu cēṟal aṟamaṉmiyiṉ talimakaḷ maṉikkaṇ iruntu taṉakkuriya kaṭaṉkaḷi āṟṟi vantāḷ āyiṉum avaḷ uḷḷattil vēṭkinōy aṭikkaṭi eḻuntu alittamiyiṉ uṇṭiyiṟ curuṅkiyum uṟakka miṉṟiyum kālam kaḻintamiyiṉ avaḷatu uṭampu melintatu; eṉiṉum, kaṭami kuṟittuc ceṉṟa kātalaṉākiya talimakaṉ taṉ talimip paṇpukku ēṟpa mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyi naṉku āṟṟip pukaḻ mikuntu varum ceytiyik kēṭkun tōṟum avaḷuṭiya neñcam iṉpam ciṟantu iṉitu makiḻvatāyiṟṟu. ivvaṇṇam uṭaṟkaṇ melivum uḷḷattē uvakiyum niṉṟu tikaḻavē orunāḷ talimakaḷ ataṉi niṉintu viyantu nakittāḷāka. avaḷ karuttaṟiyāta tōḻi avala mikutiyāṟ piṟanta āṟṟāmiyāl talivi taṉakkuḷḷē nakittu varuntukiṉṟāḷ eṉak karuti vēṇṭuvakūṟi avaḷi vaṟpuṟuttaluṟṟāḷ. anniliyil talimakaḷ tōḻiyi nōkki puli mutaliya koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ iyaṅkum aruñcuram kaṭantu ceṉṟa kātalarporuṭṭu avarip piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟa eṉ neñcam tāṉ ceyta nalviṉip payattālavaruṭiya viṉinalam kaṇṭu viyantu iṉpuṟāniṟpa attakiya nalam illāta eṉ uṭampu curuṅki, piṟarkaṇṭu alarkūṟumāṟu tīviṉip payaṉākiya tuṉpattukku uṟiyuḷāyiṟṟu; eṉ oruttiyiṭattē neñcam nalviṉi 
ceytu iṉpattukku uriyatākalum uṭampu tīviṉi ceytu tuṉpattukkuriyatākalum kaṇṭu niṉikkun tōṟum eṉakku naki tōṉṟā niṉṟatu, kāṇ eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉ kaṇṇē talimakaḷatu kaṭami paṟṟiya maṉa
nalamum, pirivāṟṟāmi paṟṟiya melliyaṟ poṟi nalamum, taṉittaṉiyē viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭa peruvaḻutiyār ippāṭṭiṉ
kaṇ aḻakuṟat toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
uḷḷutoṟum nakuveṉ tōḻi vaḷḷukirp
piṭipiḷan tiṭṭa nāril veṇkōṭṭuk 
koṭiṟupōl kāya vāliṇarp pālik
kaṭuvaḷi1 tūkkaliṉ ilitīr neṟṟam
kalliḻi aruviyiṉ olleṉa olikkum
pullili ōmiya pulivaḻaṅ kattam
ceṉṟa kātalarpiṉṟip2 paṭṭa
neñcē nalviṉip pāṟṟē īṇṭoḻin
tāṉāk kuvi3 malinta
yāṉē tōḻi nōyppā lēṉē
piriviṭi melinta talivi tōḻikkuc coṟṟatu.
uri
tōḻi-; uḷḷutoṟum nakuvaṉ-niṉikkuntōṟum eṉṉiyē niṉintu nakuvēṉāyiṉēṉ; vaḷḷukirp piṭi-periya nakaṅkaḷiyuṭiya piṭiyāṉiyāl; piḷantiṭṭa nāril veṇkōṭṭuk koṭiṟu pōl kāya-piḷakkappaṭṭut tōl urikkappaṭṭataṉāl nāriṉṟi veṇmiyāyt tōṉṟum kiḷikaḷiyum kuṟaṭu pōṉṟa kāykaḷiyu
muṭiya; vāl iṇarppāli-veḷḷiya pūṅkottuk
kaḷit tāṅki niṟkum veṭpāli maramum; kaṭuvaḷi tūkka
liṉ-mikka kāṟṟup pōntu tūkki acittalāl; ilitīr neṟṟam kalliḻi aruviyiṉ olleṉa olikkum - iliyutira niṉṟa neṟṟukkaḷ maliyiṉiṉṟu vīḻum aruvipōlac calacala eṉa olikkum; pullili ōmiya-pulliya ili
kaḷiyuṭiya ōmi maraṅkaḷum uṭiya; puli vaḻaṅku attam-pulikaḷ ulāvum vaḻikaḷik kaṭantu; ceṉṟa kātalar piṉṟippaṭṭa-ceṉṟa nam kātalar piṉṉē ceṉṟoḻinta; neñcu nalviṉippāṟṟu - eṉ neñcam nalviṉi
yiṉ pāṟpaṭṭu iṉpuṟutaṟku uriyatāka; īṇṭu oḻintu-immaṉiyiṭattē iruntu; āṉāk kuvi malinta yāṉ-piṟar īramiṉṟik kūṟutalāṉ poṟuttaṟkariya alari ēṟṟuṟiyum yāṉ; nōyppālēṉ - tīviṉip pāṟpaṭṭut tuṉpuṟutaṟ kuriyaḷākiya eṉatu niliyi e-ṟu.
tōḻi kātalar piṉṟippaṭṭa eṉ neñcu nalviṉip pāṟṟāy iṉpuṟutaṟkuriyatāka. īṇṭoḻintu kuvi malinta yāṉ nōyppālēṉāṉa niliyi uḷḷutoṟum nakuveṉ kāṇ. eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. yāṉi
yiṉ kālaṭiyil uḷḷa nakam kūrmiyum, perumiyum uṭimiyiṉ, vaḷḷukirp piṭi eṉṟār. "kāṉayāṉi katuvāy vaḷḷukir. irumpaṉi itakkiyiṉ oṭiyum1" eṉṟu piṟa cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. vāliṇarppāli eṉavē veṭpāli maramāyiṟṟu piṭiyāṉi pāli
marattiṉ kiḷiyip paṟṟi īrttalāl, mēṟpaṭṭi nīṅkit tōṉṟum uṭpakutiyi vitantu, nāril veṇkōṭu eṉṟār. piḷantiṭṭa eṉṉum peyareccam kāraṇap poruṭṭu koṭiṟu. kollaṉ ēntum kikkuṟaṭu. kāy muṟṟip paḻuttuḷḷavi neṟṟu eṉappaṭum ularnta neṟṟiṉkaṇ uḷḷuṟiyum vitiyum tōṭum pirintu kāṟṟu alikkum pōtu olittal iyalpu. kaṭuvaḷi pōntu kiḷikaḷiyum, neṟṟukkaḷiyum tūkki alittalāl uṇṭākum ōci, calacalaveṉa iruttaliṉ, ataṟku malippāṟikaṭ kiṭiyē ciṟuciṟu kaṟkaḷi alittuk koṇṭu iṟaṅkum aruvi oliyi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, ilitīrneṟṟam kalliḻi yaruviyiṉ olikkum eṉṟār. kuvi, alar; "ūravar kuvi eruvāka; aṉṉi col nīrāka nīḷum innōy2" eṉpatu kāṇka.
talimakaṉatu pirivāṟṟātu mēṉi melintu tōṉṟum talimakaḷik kaṇṭu tōḻi varuntiṉāḷāka, avaḷit tēṟṟit tāṉ āṟṟiyirukkum tiṟatti veḷippaṭa moḻikiṉṟāḷ ātaliṉ, uḷḷutoṟum nakuveṉ 
tōḻi eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē taṉ maṉattē āṟṟāmik kuriya varuttamiṉmi ōraḷavu terivittavā ṟāyiṟṟu. talimakaṉ ceṉṟa curattarumi kūṟuvāḷ. vāliṇarp pāliyiṉ ilitīr neṟṟam kaṭuvaḷi tūkkaliṉ kalliḻi aruviyiṉ olikkum eṉṟum, pulivaḻaṅku attam eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. neṟṟam aruviyiṉ olikkum eṉṟatu. curam pulivaḻaṅkum kaṭumiyuṭiyatāyiṉum celavu iṉitām eṉṟum, ataṉāl neñcam iṉpuṟutaṟku uriyatu āyiṟṟu eṉṟum kuṟittavāṟu neñcam avariyē niṉintu avaruṭiya kuṇañ ceyalkaḷik kaṇṭu makiḻnta vaṇṇam iruppatu urippāḷ, ceṉṟa kātalar piṉṟippaṭṭa neñcē nalviṉip pāṟṟu eṉṟāḷ mēṉi melivu kaṇṭu āṟṟāta tōḻikku urittaliṉ. āṉāk kuvi malinta yāṉ eṉṟāḷ. iḵtu īramil kūṟṟam ēṟṟalar nāṇal uṭaṉ cellātu maṉiyiṭattē iruntoḻinta eṉ uṭampōṭē taṅkip pirivut tuṉpam uḻappatu paṟṟit taṉ uyir tuṉpattuk kētuvākiya tīviṉip pāṟpaṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉpāḷ, nōyp pālēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. uṭaloṭu kūṭi iyaṅkum uyirkkut tuṇiyāy niṉṟu ceyvaṉa tavirvaṉavaṟṟi uṇarttum neñcam, avarpiṉ ceṉṟu iṉpuṟuvatum ataṉit taṉvari niṟuttip payaṉ koḷḷutaṟ kuriya uyir atu ceyyātu uṭampiṭi niṉṟu varuntuvatum kāṇa avaṟṟiṉ maṭami tōṉṟi naki viḷivikkiṉṟatu 
eṉpāḷ uḷḷutoṟum nakuveṉ eṉṟāḷ. ivvāṟṟāl talimakaḷiṉ poṟiyum, niṟiyum kaṇṭu tōḻi viyantu makiḻvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
108. iḷavēṭṭaṉār
talimip paṇpukaḷāl mēṉmiyuṟṟa talimakkaḷ tammiṟṟām taṉittuk kāṇap piṟanta kātalāl karuttu orumittuk kaḷaviṉkaṇ akkātaṟ piṅkūḻi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. oruvari yoruvar kaṇṭavaḻit tōṉṟum iyaṟkiyīrppuk kātal
pōlat tōṉṟiṉum; atu naṉ muṟiyil vaḷarāviṭiṉ uyiroṉṟiya kēṇmiyāy nalam payavātu. uṇṭu viḷi
yāṭi vaḷarum iḷamiyuṭal taṉṉip pōlvēṟu uṭampu
kaḷi nalkum cevvī yeytuvatum akkālattē āṇuṭampu peṇṇiyum, peṇṇuṭampu āṇiyum īrttu uṭamputaru ceyaṟkaṇ īṭupaṭuttuvatum, āṇum peṇṇumāyk kāṇap
paṭum uyiriṉattukku iyaṟki. inta iyaṟki yīrppu māvum puḷḷumākiya uyiriṉattuḷ eṅkum kāṇappaṭuvatuṇṭu; āyi
ṉum, ivvāṟu īrppuṇṭu iṇintu iyalum iyipu. uṭamputaru ceyalmuṭintatum niliyiṉṟi oḻivatu vilaṅkiṉat
tuḷḷum, puḷḷiṉattuḷḷum potuvākak kāṇappaṭum; orucila puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷē ivvitikku vilakkāka uḷḷaṉa. makkaḷiṉam ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟāy uyarntu oru kālattil iyaṟkiyīrppukku uḷḷāki oṉṟupaṭṭavi; piṉpu  oṉṟi ṉoṉṟu piriyā
tirukkum peṟṟi vāyntuḷḷaṉa. piriyā vakiyil makkaḷ uyirip piṇittu niṟkum aṉpu kātalaṉpu eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kātal niṟintu oṉṟiyoṉṟu iṉṟi
yamiyāta aḷavil peruki niṟkum pōtu, atu kātaṟkāmam eṉap paṇṭi nūlkaḷil pōṟṟi yurikkappaṭṭatu. atu
paṟṟiyē kātalaṉpu niṟinta nili kāmakkātal eṉṟum kaṭṭurikkappaṭukiṟatu. āṇum, peṇṇumāy niṟkum iḷa
makkaḷuḷ, uṭamputaru cevviyil iyaṟkiyīrppuk kuḷḷāki iṇivuṟum makkaḷiṭiyē paṇpāl ciṟanta makkaḷāyiṉār. tam iruvaril tōṉṟiya īrppu, kātalāy kātaṟkāmamāy niṟivuṟṟu; oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāta nilimi
yiṉi eytutaṟku muyalvar. ammuyaṟci avarkkum, piṟarkkum teriyāta vakiyil naṭipeṟuvatu iyaṟki. akkālattē perumiyum, aṟivuraṉum perukavuṭiya āṇmakaṉ iyaṟki
yīrppukku iriyākik kūṭi nīṅkum vilaṅku pōlātu; taṉ uṭampukku uyirpōla uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta vāḻkkit tuṇiyāka appeṇṇuyir niṉṟu nilavutaṟ poruṭṭu; ataṉpāl tōṉṟiya kātali vaḷarac cey
vatiyē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭoḻukuvaṉ; atu niṟin
tālaṉṟi avaḷ avaṉukkum avaṉ avaḷukkum vāḻkkit tuṇiyāka amivatilli. ataṉi muṟṟuvikkum variyil kaḷavoḻukkam iyaṅkum; atu muṟṟiya vaḻiyē vāḻkkit tuṇiyāyp palaraṟiya amiyum tirumaṇam nikaḻum; ataṉ piṉṉar amivatu kaṟpuvāḻvu. uṭamputaru ceykik kuriya kūṭṭam kaṟpilaṉṟik kaḷaviṉkaṇ nikaḻtal illi. ataṉāl kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukum kātalariṭiyē, iyaṟkiyīrppu viñci niṉṟu kaṟpuneṟik kuriya kaṭimaṇatti muṭittuk kōṭaṟkuriya vēṭkiyi mikuttavaṇṇam irukkum. iyaṟkiyīrppu vaḻic cellum uḷḷatti niṟuttit taṉ aṟivu vaḻic celuttum talimippaṇpāl talimakaṉ, kātalaṉpu ciṟappatu karutik kaṭimaṇattuk kuriya muyaṟciyi nīṭṭippaṉ. vēṟṟu varivu niṉintum ūravar kūṟum alarukku añciyum, iraviṉkaṇ taṉṉik kāṇṭaṟku varum talimakaṟku iṭiyil uḷatākum ūṟu niṉintum vēṭki mikutiyāl āṟṟāmiyāl melin
tum talimakaḷ mikka varuttameyti virivil varintu
koḷḷumāṟu talimakaṉit tōḻi vāyilākat tūṇṭuvāḷ.
taliviyatu kuṟippiṉvaḻi yoḻukum tōḻi, orunāḷ talimakaṉi etirppaṭṭu, "peruma, nīyō niṉatu nāṭṭuk kuṟavar tām viḷitta tiṉippuṉattukku yāṉi mēya varuvatu kāṇiṉ: kaṇiyum, kavaṇum koṇṭu, pērāravāram ceytu ataṉi veruṭṭi yaccuṟuttip pōkkum nāṭu eṉṟatu. pakip
poruḷāyiṉum naṉku paḻakiyavaḻip, piriyuṅkāl ataṉāl viḷiyum tuṉpam poṟuttaṟ karitām; nī eṅṅaṉam ivaḷ toṭarpi maṟantu kiviṭṭaṉiyō, yāṉ aṟikilēṉ; niṉ pirivāl ivaḷ nutal pacantu peritum varunti niṉ toṭarpi maṟavātu niṉintu varuntukiṉṟāḷ; itaṟkiṭiyē yāṉ ceyvaki yaṟiyātu mikavum varuntāniṉṟēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ tiṉippuṉatti yāṉi neruṅkak kaṇṭa kuṟavar, kavaṇum, kiṇiyum, viḷiyum koṇṭu veruṭṭuvar eṉṟa kuṟippu, taliviyiṉ nalampeṟak karuti varum niṉvaravi īṇṭu em tanti taṉṉiyar aṟiyiṉ peruntīṅku viḷivippar eṉpati uḷḷuṟutti, nī ivaḷi variyātu nīṭṭittaliṉ ivaḷpāl niṉ toṭarpik kiviṭṭāy pōlkiṉṟāy; atu niṉakku evvāṟu iyaṉṟatu; paḻakiviṭṭāl pakiyum piriviṉkaṇ peruntuṉpam tarumē, atu niṉpāl poyp
paṭṭatu pōlum eṉṟatu. peṇmiyiṉ oṭpatti uyartti niṟpatu kaṇṭa āciriyar iḷavēṭṭaṉār avaṟṟi ip
pāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.

4toṭarpiyāṅku viṭṭaṉi nōkō yāṉē
itu variyātu nīṭṭittu oḻukali āṟṟāḷāya tōḻi talimakaḷatu āṟṟāmi kūṟi varivukaṭāyatu.
uri
mali ayal kalitta mipaṭu viyaṉpuṉam - malikku ayaliṭattē yuḷḷa puṉattiṉkaṇ taḻittuḷḷa mukil paṭiyum akaṉṟa tiṉikkolliyil; tuṇiyiṉ tīrnta kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉi - tuṇiyākiya taṉ piṭiyiṉiṉṟu nīṅkiṉa mikka ciṉam poruntiya kaḷiṟu; aṇiyak kaṇṭa akkuṭik kāṉavar - neruṅkuvatu kaṇṭa ciṟiya kuṟiccikaḷil vāḻum kuṟavar; kaṇiyar kiṇiyar kipuṉi kavaṇar - ampu celuttik kiṇippaṟi koṭṭik kiyiluḷḷa kavaṇāṟ kalli eṟintu; viḷiyar puṟakkuṭi ārkkum nāṭa - viḷik kurali eḻuppit tam ūrkkup puṟattē niṉṟu āravāram ceytu veruṭṭum malināṭaṉē; paḻakiya pakiyum pirivu iṉṉātu - uṭpaki yuṭiyarāyiṉār neṭitu paḻakip piṉ nīṅkuvarāyiṉ avaratu pirivum piriviṉkaṇ tuṉpamāka irukkum; mukiyēr ilaṅkeyiṟṟu iṉṉaki maṭanti - mulliarumpu pōl viḷaṅkum paṟkaḷiyum iṉiya muṟu
valiyu muṭiya maṭantiyākiya ivaḷatu; cuṭarpuri tiru
nutal pacappa - cuṭarpōl oḷiceyyum aḻakiya neṟṟi pacali pāya; toṭarpu yāṅku viṭṭaṉi - ivaḷatu naṭpi ev
vāṟu kiviṭṭaṉi; yāṉ nōku - yāṉ varuntukiṉṟēṉ 
(e-ṟu).
nāṭa, pakiyum pirivu iṉṉātu; aṟṟāka, maṭanti nutal pacappa, toṭarpu yāṅku viṭṭaṉi, yāṉ nōku eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. yāṉi aṇiyak kaṇṭa kāṉavar ārkkum nāṭa eṉa iyiyum. maliyayal, malic
carivilum aṭiyilum uḷḷa iṭam. mipaṭuviyaṉpuṉam, kariya maḻimukil paṭiyum puṉakkolli. maliyayaliluḷḷa puṉakkollikaḷ, vitittaṉa taḻittu viḷitaṟkēṟṟa vaḷamum īramum uṭimi tōṉṟa, maliyayaṟ kalitta viyaṉpuṉam eṉṟār. yāṉi taṉiyātu iṉattōṭē kūṭi vāḻum iyalpiṉa tātalāl taṉittu varuvataṉit tuṇi
yiṉ tīrnta yāṉi eṉavum, ataṉ uḷḷattē iṉattōṭu kūṭi uṟitaṟkuriya aṉpum īramum iṉmiyiṉ kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉi eṉavum vitantōtiṉār. ataṉ varuki kaṇṭa kāṉavar nēr niṉṟu porutal kūṭāmiyiṉ, kaṇi muta
lāyiṉa koṇṭu ataṉi accuṟutti veruṭṭuvārāyiṉar eṉṟaṟkuk kaṇiyar kiṇiyar kipuṉi kavaṇar viḷiyar puṟakkuṭi ārkkum nāṭa eṉṟār. kaṇiyar kiṇiyar eṉpaṉa "kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ cekcik kaṇṇiyaṉ kuḻalaṉ kōṭṭaṉ1" eṉṟāṟ pōla niṉṟamiyiṉ ivvāṟu uri kūṟappaṭṭatu. kuṭiyiṉ akattēyiruntu ārppiṉ, puṉam aṇiya varum kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉi tām vāḻum kuṭikkuṭ pukuntu ataṉi muṟṟavum aḻittuviṭum eṉpataṉāl puṟakkuṭi ārkkum eṉṟār. paḻakiya paki eṉṟataṉāl, uṭpakiyātal pulaṉāyiṟṟu. pēyōṭāyiṉum pirivu iṉṉātu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyi2 uṭkoṇṭu niṉṟatu. pēyppaṇpātal, pakimip paṇpātal, iṉṟi uyiroṉ
ṟiya kēṇmi yuṭiyaḷātal tōṉṟa, mukiyēr ilaṅ
keyiṟṟu iṉṉaki maṭanti eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. kaṇ kāṇattōṉṟum pōtu peruki niṟkum uḷḷaṉpē, kāṇāppōtu niṉinta vaḻiyum tōṉṟi niṟkum iyipiṉit toṭarpu eṉṟār.
taṉittu varum yāṉi tām viḷitta puṉatti aṇiyak kāṇum kāṉavar kaṇiyum kavaṇum koṇṭu tuṉpuṟuttik kiṇiyum viḷiyum koṇṭu accuṟutti veruṭṭuvar eṉṟataṉāl, taṉittu varum niṉṉik kāṇiṉ emmuṭiya tanti taṉṉiya rākiya tamar tuṉpuṟutti varuttuvar eṉa uḷḷuṟuttu uritta vāṟṟāl tōḻi, iṉi ikkaḷaviṉik kiviṭṭu nī varintu kōṭalē ceyaṟpālatu eṉṟāḷām. varitali mēṟkoḷḷātu, nīṭṭittali nōkkiṉ, nī talimakaḷiṉ toṭarpik kiviṭṭāy pōlum eṉpāḷ. toṭarpu viṭṭaṉi eṉṟum, nī vārāta poḻutu eytāta peruntuya rellām eyti varuntiṉum ataṉāl nutal pacatta lallatu vēṟupaṭa yātum niṉiyāḷ eṉat taliviyatu kātal māṇpu veḷippaṭa, mukiyēr ilaṅkeyiṟṟu iṉṉaki maṭanti cuṭarpuri tirunutal pacappa eṉṟum, ittuṇip peruṅkātal uṭiyāḷatu toṭarpu niṉṉi iṉṟiyamiyāmi viḷaṅkavum, nī at
toṭarpiṉi maṟantu variyātu nīṭṭikkiṉṟaṉi; iḵtu aṉpuṭiyārkku eḷitiṉ iyaluva toṉṟaṉṟē eṉpāḷ, toṭarpu yāṅkuviṭṭaṉi eṉṟum, viṭaṟku iya
lāmiyi vaṟpuṟuttaṟku ētuvāka paḻakiya pakiyum piri
viṉṉātē eṉṟum, niṉ oḻukkattukku itukāṟum tuṇi

109. mīḷip perumpatumaṉār
perumpatumaṉār eṉṟa peyaruṭiya cāṉṟōr vēṟu uṇmiyāl ivar mīḷip perum patumaṉār eṉṟu ciṟappikkap peṟukiṉṟār. mīḷi eṉpatu mīḷikkuṉṟu eṉṉum mūtūriṉ curukkam. itu teṉ peṇṇik kariyil makata maṇṭalam eṉak kalveṭṭuk kūṟum nāṭṭiluḷḷatōr ūr. maliyamāṉ
kaḷukkuriya mānāṭu piṟkālattē cēti nāṭu eṉavum makatanāṭu eṉavum iraṇṭākap pirintu nilaviṟṟu: appiriviṉuḷ, makatanāṭu makata maṇṭalam eṉavum vaḻaṅkuvatuṇṭu1. mīḷik
kuṉṟu, malāṭāṉa caṉanāta vaḷanāṭṭup peṇṇit teṉkari makatamaṇṭalattup paḻaṅkūrppaṟṟil uḷḷateṉak kalveṭṭuk
kaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. perum patumaṉār eṉpatu iccāṉṟō
ruṭiya iyaṟpeyar. ivar pāṭiyatāka ivvoru pāṭṭut
tāṉ toki nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. perum patumaṉār eṉa vēṟoru cāṉṟōr ittoki nūluṭ kāṇappaṭuvatu koṇṭu avarē ivareṉak karutiṉōrum uṇṭu.
uṇmiyaṉpāl uḷḷam piṇippuṟṟu ōruyirum īruṭam
pum pōl oṉṟi vāḻnta kātalar iruvaruṭiya ilvāḻvil, kātalaṉākiya talimakaṉ kaṭami kuṟittup pirivāṉāyiṉāṉ. pirintavaṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākak kuṟitta paruvam varuntuṇiyum pirivuttuyari āṟṟiyirunta talimakaṭku apparuvam varavum avaṉ vārāmiyāl, āṟṟāmi mikuntatu; nutal pacantatu; mēṉi melintatu; toṭiyum vaḷiyum nekiḻntu nīṅkiṉa. atu kaṇṭa tōḻi, kuṟitta paruvam vantum talimakaṉ vārāmiyāl talimakaḷ tuyarmikutal aṟintu varuntuvāḷāyiṉāḷ. poḻutu kaḻiyak kaḻiyat tōḻi varuttam mikkut taliviyatu āṟṟā
miyi eṇṇi maṉam tuḷaṅkiṉāḷ. tali makaḷōṭu col
lāṭaluṟṟu, "pirivuṟṟār eytum pētuṟavu ittaṉmiya pōlum" eṉap pēcavum. talimakaḷ avaḷi nōkki, tōḻi, eṉṉip paṉmuṟiyum viṉavuvatiṟ payaṉ eṉ? yāṉ eytiya varuttam vāyviṭṭu urikkum elliyik kaṭantuviṭṭatu. vāṭik kāṟṟu vantulavum iruḷmālip pōtil vēṟiṭattē koṇṭuviṭa vēṇṭiya nallāṉ pacuvi, avvāṟu ceyyātu atu niṉṟa toḻuviṉkaṇ tali cēṟu paṭintamiyāl kīḻē paṭukka iyalātapaṭi talik kayiṟṟi īrttuk kūriyiṉ ucciyil kaṭṭik kīḻē kuṉiyāvāṟu nimirttu niṟuttip piṇikkap 
peṟṟa vaḻi, aḵtu enniliyil varuntumō anniliyil taṉiyaḷākiya yāṉ maṉam kalaṅkip pulampukiṉṟēṉ; immaṉak kalakkattōṭē eṉakkup pakaṟpōtum iruṇmālip pōtum kaḻikiṉṟaṉa; ivaṟṟik kāṇum yāṉ evvaṇṇam maṉa
vamitiyuṭaṉ āṟṟi yiruppēṉ" eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ.
itaṉkaṇ, talimakaḷ taṉatu niliyiṉi viḷakkuṅkāl, cēṟu paṭṭa toḻuviluḷḷa nallāṉ kīḻē kuṉiyāvāṟu talikkayiṟṟāl nimirttut toḻuviṉ mēl kūri ucciyiṟ kaṭṭappeṟṟamiyāl tariyil paṭukkavum iyalātu, vēṟu iṭattukkup pōkavum iyalātu varuntum tuṉpa niliyiṉik kūṟiyatu mīḷip perumpatumaṉāruṭiya pulamiyuḷḷattit toṭṭamiyiṉ ataṉ viḷivāka ippāṭṭu uruppaṭṭu veḷivaruvatāyiṟṟu.

uri
oṉṟutum eṉṟa toṉṟupaṭu naṭpiṉ-uṭalāl iruvarākiya yām uyirāl oruvarāyiṉēm eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiya piṟappoṭu toṭarum paḻamittākiya naṭpiṉiṉṟu; kātalar akaṉṟeṉa-kātalarākiya talivar pirintu ceṉṟatākak karuti; kalaṅkip pētuṟṟu-maṉam kalakkamuṟṟu aṟivu mayaṅki; aṉṉavāyiṉa naṉṉutal niliyeṉa-attaṉmiya vāyiṉa nalla neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiya talimakaḷiṉ nilitta paṇpukaḷ eṉṟu colli; viṉavalāṉāp puṉiyiḻi-mēlum pala kēṭṭal amiyāta aḻakiya iḻi yaṇinta tōḻi; iṉi kēḷ-ippoḻutu kēṭpāyāka; urikkalākā evvam-vāyviṭṭuc colla muṭiyātaṉavāyiṉa eṉ tuṉpam; immeṉa vāṭi irikkum iruḷkūr poḻutil - immeṉṟa ōciyuṭaṉ vāṭik kāṟṟup pōntu alikkum iruḷ niṟinta iravup pōtil; toḷiyuṭit toḻuvil-cēṟupaṭinta toḻuviṉkaṇ; tūṇil aṟṟattu-tūṇ il
lāta kuṟiyāl; uccik kaṭṭiya kūḻi āviṉ-talikkayiṟu toḻuviṉ kūri yucciyilē kaṭṭappeṟṟa kuṟumiyāṉa pacuviṉuṭiya; niliyiṉiṉ oruvēṉāki-nilimiyip pōla niṟkavum kiṭakkavum māṭṭāta niliyuṟṟu; ulamara-uḷḷam cuḻaṉṟu varuntumāṟu; ippakal maṭi poḻutu-ippakal maṟiya varum mālippōtu kaḻiyāniṉṟatu, kāṇ (e-ṟu).
puṉiyiḻi, kēḷ, iṉi, evvam urikkalākā; kūḻi āviṉ niliyiṉiṉ oruvēṉāki ulamarap poḻutu kaḻiyum eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. kātalar akaṉṟeṉa, kalaṅki, pētuṟṟu naṉṉutal nili aṉṉavāyiṉa eṉa viṉaval āṉāp puṉiyiḻi eṉa iyiyum. toṉṟupaṭu naṭpu, piṟapputtōṟum toṭarum naṭpu; iṉi, kaḷavuk kālattu iyaṟkip puṇarcci peṟṟa ñāṉṟu "piriyēṉ piriyiṉ uyir tariyēṉ" eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttit talimakaṉ ceyta naṭpiṉi īṇṭu oṉṟutum eṉṟa toṉṟupaṭu naṭpu eṉṟār eṉṟalu moṉṟu. akaṉṟeṉa eṉṟatu ceyteṉa eṉṉum eccaviṉi. aṉṉa vāyiṉa eṉṟatu, talivi eytiya kalakkattukkum, pētuṟavukkum ēṟṟa ceyal vakikaḷi kūṭṭattiṟ piṟakkum iṉpam kūṟalākāmi paṟṟi akam eṉappaṭṭāṟ pōlap piriviṉkaṇ uḷatākum tuṉpamum appeṟṟit tākaliṉ, urikkalākā evvam eṉṟār. toḷi, cēṟu, "toḷiporu poruṭṭun tōṉṟuvaṉa māya1" eṉṟu piṟarum vaḻaṅkupa. naṟṟūṇ illāta kuṟiyāl āviṉ talikkayiṟu toḻuviṉ kūriyil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. kūḻi pōtiya uyaramillātatu. uccik kaṭṭiya āviṉ talikkayiṟu: kūriyiṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟamiyiṉ toḻuvil cuḻaṉṟu tiriyumē yaṉṟit toḷi uṭimiyiṉ kiṭakkavum, kaṭṭap peṟṟamiyiṉ nīṅkavum iyalātu varuntumeṉa aṟika. talikkayiṟu kūri yucciyiṟ kaṭṭappeṟṟa miyiṉ kūḻi yāviṟkuk kiṭakka māṭṭāmiyum tari cēṟupaṭin tirutta
lāl niṟka māṭṭāmiyum koṇṭu toḻuvil cuḻaṉṟu tirivatu pōlat talimakaḷ kātalaṉoṭu aṉpāl piṇikkappaṭṭa miyiṉ pirivāṟṟi iruttalāṟṟāmiyum vāṭi varuttutalāl uṟakkamillāmiyum eyti varuntumāṟu koḷka.
paṇṭu kaḷaviṉkaṭ kūṭiya ñāṉṟu namatu innaṭpu piṟapput tōṟum toṭarntu varum paḻamiyāṉa naṭ
pātaliṉ, iṉṟu nām ōruyirum īruṭalumāyp poruntiṉē
māka, iṉi yāṉ piriyēṉ piriyiṉ uyir tariyēṉ" eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉami tōṉṟa, oṉṟutum eṉṟa toṉṟupaṭu naṭpu eṉṟum, poruḷ viṉi paṟṟiya kaṭami kuṟittu nikaḻnta pirivu toṉṟupaṭu naṭpiṟku māṟāka nikaḻttum pirivu eṉak karutiṉami tōṉṟa, kātalar akaṉṟeṉak kalaṅki eṉṟum, maṉam kalaṅkiya vaḻi aṟivu pētuṟuvatu payaṉākaliṉ, pētuṟṟu eṉṟum, kalakkap pētuṟavukaṭ kēṟpac collattakātaṉa, ceyyattakātaṉa ceytoḻukutaliṉ tōḻi varuntumāṟu tōṉṟa, aṉṉavāyiṉa naṉṉutal nili
yeṉa eṉṟum, avaḷ poruṭṭut tāṉ ceyyat takuvaṉa yāvi eṉap paṉmuṟiyum tōḻi vaṇaṅkik kēṭkumāṟu viḷaṅka, viṉavalāṉāp puṉiyiḻi eṉṟum talimakaḷ kūṟiṉāḷ. paṉmuṟi iṟiñcik kēṭṭum talimakaḷ tāṉ oṉṟum uriyāmikkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvāḷāy, "iṉik kēḷ urikkalākā evvam" eṉṟāḷ. tuṉpam mikkuc colliṉ elli kaṭantamiyiṉ urikkalākātāyiṟṟu. vāṭiyiṉ varuttam iravup pōtil mikuntu evvuyiriyum varuttutaliṉ, toḷiyuṭit toḻuvil niṉṟa kūḻi āviṟkum poruntumāṟu kūṟi, āviṉ niliyum taṉ niliyum oṉṟāy amintavāṟupaṟṟi, āviṉ niliyiṉ oruvēṉāki eṉṟāḷ. ulamaral, alamarutal, āvukku ulamaral toḻuviṉkaṇ cuḻaṉṟu varutal; talimakaṭku ulamaral, talimakaṉip paṟṟiyē niṉintu niṉintu varuntutal. tuṉpam mikuntu colvari iṟantatāyiṉum,  poḻutu kaḻintavaṇṇa miruttaliṉ, eṉatu āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu varuntaṟka eṉat tōḻiyi āṟṟuvippatu payaṉ.
110. pūtaṉār
ivar peyar, accuppaṭiyilum, maturittamiḻccaṅka ēṭṭilum pōtaṉār, pottaṉār eṉak kāṇappaṭiṉum; ēṉi ēṭukaḷil pūtaṉār eṉṟē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. pūtaṉār eṉpatu payiṉṟa vaḻakkiṟṟākaliṉ, atuvē īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ippūtaṉ eṉṟa peyar makkaḷiṭiyē ki.pi. patiṉāṉku patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari vaḻaṅki vantami kalveṭṭuk
kaḷāl aṟikiṉṟōm. pūtaṉār eṉpatē ippāṭṭip pāṭiya cāṉṟōr peyarāyiṉ, naṟṟiṇi 29-ām pāṭṭip pāṭiyōrum ivarum oruvar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. iṉi, pottaṉār eṉpatē pāṭamāyiṉ, pottaṉ eṉpatu ivaratu iyaṟpeyarām, pottaṉatu ūr eṉṟa poruḷil pottaṉūr eṉṟu ōr ūr kāviri vaṭakariyil maḻanāṭṭil tiruveyilūrkku aṇmiyil uḷatu; tiruveyilūr ippōtu vēlūr eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
kaḷavuneṟiyil kātaluṟṟuc ciṟantu piṉ kaṭimaṇam ceytu koṇṭu kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭu nallaṟam ceyyat toṭaṅkiya talimakaḷi, maṉiyaṟam toṭaṅkiya ciṉṉāṭ kellām avaḷi vaḷartta cevili avaḷatu maṉikkuc ceṉṟu kaṇ
ṭāḷ. aṅkē talimakaḷ ceyyum maṉiyaṟattiṉ māṇpi nēriṟ kaṇṭu peruviyappuṟṟāḷ. tāṉ vaḷartta makaḷ kaṟpum, kāmamum, naṟpāloḻukkamum, melliyaṟ poṟiyum, niṟiyum, viruntu puṟantarutalum, cuṟṟam ōmpalum ākiya naṟpaṇpu
kaḷāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvatu kāṇap piṟakkum iṉpattiṉum; mikkatu piṟitiṉmiyiṉ ceviliyiṉ uḷḷam uvaki niṟintatu. piṉṉar avaḷ talimakaḷpāl viṭi peṟṟukkoṇṭu avaḷ piṟantuvaḷarnta toṉmaṉikku vantāḷ. aṅkē taliviyip peṟṟa naṟṟāy taṉ makaḷuṭiya maṉinalatti aṟiya virumpiṉa ḷāka, cevili, "talimakaḷi yāṉ kaṇṭatum, avaḷ ciṟupeṇṇākak kālil poṟcilam paṇintu viḷiyāṭum cevviyil pāl kalanta cōṟṟi oru vaḷḷatti lēnti, oru kiyil ciṟukōl koṇṭu, iccōṟṟi uṇka eṉa accuṟut
tiṉāl, narimutu ceviliyar ōṭip paṟṟa muṭiyātu melintu ayarumāṟu pūmpantariṭiyē āṭṭaṅ kāṭṭiya nili eṉ niṉivukku vantatu; attuṇi iḷiyaḷākiya avaḷuṭiya iṉṟiya niliyi nōkkiṉ, avaḷpāl aṟivum, oḻukkamum, ciṟantu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟi eppoḻutu evvāṟu uṇarntāḷ eṉa eṉ neñcil viyappiṉ mēl viyappu vīṟu koṇ ṭeḻuntatu; taṉṉik koṇṭa kaṇavaṉuṭiya kuṭi orukāl vaṟaluṟṟatu. ataṉi aṟinta avaḷ tanti avaṭku vēṇṭum uṇavup poruḷik koṭuttu viṭṭāṉāka, ataṉi vēṇṭā eṉa vilakkiviṭṭut taṉ koḻunaṉ tēṭit tantatu ciṟitāyiṉum, ataṉip poḻutu maṟuttuṇṭu vāḻum poṟpuṟu māṉamāṇpuṭiyaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāḷ; eṉṉē avaḷatu maṉimāṇpu!" eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ viḷaṅkum talimakaḷatu maṉiyaṟac ciṟappu makaḷir aṉivarkkum māṇputaru ceyalāka viḷaṅkutaliṉ, itaṉi āciriyar pūtaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
piracam kalanta veṇcuvit tīmpāl
virikatirp poṟkalat toruki ēntip
puṭippiṟ cuṟṟum pūntalic ciṟukōl
uṇṇeṉ ṟōkkupu puṭippat teṇṇīr
muttarīp poṟcilam polippat tattuṟ
ṟarinarik kūntaṟ cemmutu ceviliyar
1parimelin toḻiyap pantar ōṭi
ēval maṟukkum ciṟuviḷi yāṭṭi
aṟivum oḻukkamum yāṇṭuṇarn taṉaḷkol
koṇṭa koḻunaṉ kuṭivaṟaṉ uṟṟeṉak
koṭutta 2tanti koḻuñcō ṟuḷḷāḷ
oḻukunīr nuṇaṅkaṟal pōlap
poḻutumaṟut tuṇṇum ciṟumatu kiyaḷē.
itu makaḷnili yurittatu; maṉimaruṭciyumām.
uri
piracam kalanta veṇcuvit tīmpāl-tēṉ kalanta cuvi mikka veṇmiyāṉa iṉiya pāli; virikatir poṟkalattu oruki ēnti-virinta katiroḷiyiyuṭiya poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷḷattiṟ peytu oru kiyil ēntik koṇṭu; puṭippiṉ cuṟṟum - ōṅkiya vaḻi vaḷintu cuḻalum; pūntalic ciṟukōl-melliya nuṉiyiyuṭiya ciṟu
kucciyi; uṇ eṉṟu okkupu puṭippa - uṇkiṟāyā illiyā eṉa accuṟutti ōcciya vaḻi; teṇṇīr muttu arip poṟcilampu olippa-teḷinta nīrmiyiyuṭiya muttukkaḷi ariyākap peyta aḻakiya kāṟcilampu olikka; tattuṟṟu - tuḷḷi yōṭi; arinarik kūntal cemmutu ceviliyar - meṉmiyāka naritta kūntaliyum cemmiyāṉa maṉattiyumuṭiya mutiya ceviliyar; pari melintu oḻiya - toṭarntu paṟṟa muṭiyāmal varunti ayarntu niṉṟoḻiya; pantar ōṭi - mullikkoṭi vaḷaraviṭṭu niṟkum pantariṉ kāṟkīḻ ōṭiniṉṟu; ēval maṟukkum ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭi - iṭṭa ēvalit taliyacittu maṟukkum ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭup peṇṇākiya ivaḷ; aṟivum oḻukkamum yāṇṭu u'99rn
taṉaḷ kol - aṟṟam kākkum karuviyākiya nallaṟivum viḻuppantarum nalloḻukkamum evviṭattu eppōtu kaṟṟāḷ kollō aṟiyēṉ; koṇṭa koḻunaṉ kuṭi vaṟaṉ uṟṟeṉa - taṉṉi maṇantu koṇṭa kaṇavaṉatu kuṭi vaṟumi yuṟṟatāka aṟintu; tanti koṭutta koḻuñcōṟu uḷḷāḷ - atu nīṅkutaṟkeṉat taṉ tanti nalkiya koḻu
viya poruḷi ēlātu; oḻukunīr nuṇaṅkaṟal pōla - tuḷittoḻukum nīr tuḷikka naṉintu uṭaṉē pularum nuṇmaṇal pōla; poḻutu maṟuttuṇṇum ciṟu matukiyaḷ - pacitta poḻutu uṇṇātu ūṇ kiṭitta poḻutu uṇṇum kaṟpuvaliyu muṭiya ḷāyiṉāḷ, kāṇ (e-ṟu).
ceviliyar ēnti, okkupu puṭippa, olippa, tattuṟṟu, avar melintoḻiya ōṭi maṟukkum ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭi yāṇṭu uṇarntaṉaḷ kol; ciṟumatukiyaḷu māyiṉāḷ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. piracam, tēṉ, niṟap paṇpiyum iṉimip paṇpiyum vitantu kūṟaliṉ veṇ cuvit tīmpāl eṉṟār. mika melliya kōl eṉṟaṟkup puṭippiṉ cuṟṟum ciṟukōl eṉappaṭṭatu. iṉiya coṟkaḷukkuc cevicāykkāvaḻi accuṟuttuvatu  iyalpākaliṉ, uṇṇeṉṟu ōkkupu puṭippa eṉṟār. poṟcilampu eṉṟaviṭattup poṉmi aḻa keṉṉum poruṭṭu; poṉṉukku ōci iṉṟeṉa aṟika. ari, meṉmi; "ariyē aimi1" eṉpar āciriyar. cemmutu ceviliyar eṉṟaviṭattuc cemmi maṉattukkum, mutumi uṭaṟkum amintaṉa. ciṟu peṇ iṉitu nuḻintōṭum pantarkkīḻē tām cella iyalāmiyiṉ ceviliyar pari melintu oḻintaṉar. pari, celavu, pantar, maṉikaḷil vaḷarkkap peṟum mulli vayali mutaliya koṭikaḷ iṉitu ēṟip paṭarumāṟu amikkap peṟuvaṉa; ippantarkaḷ ciṟuciṟu kālkaḷ niṟuvappeṟṟaṉa vākaliṉ, ciṟupeṇkaḷ ōṭi viḷiyāṭutaṟku eḷiyavāy, mutiyōr iyaṅkutaṟkuk kūṭāvāy uḷḷaṉa. ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭallatu vēṟoṉṟam ceyyāta iḷamic cevvi kuṟittu, ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭi eṉṟār. ciṟumi, uṭaliṉ ciṟumi; ciṟumatukiyaḷ eṉpuḻiyum ituvē koḷka. aṟivum oḻukkamum talimakaḷpāl avaḷ viḷiyāṭṭup paṇpōṭu uṭaṉvaḷarntaṉa vāyi ṉum, cevili  avviḷiyāṭṭē, nōkkiṉamiyiṉ aṟivu oḻukkaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci avaḷ aṟiyappaṭāmiyāṉ ippōtu viḷiyāṭṭu oṭuṅka avi viḷaṅkit tōṉṟak kaṇṭu viyantu makiḻvāḷāyiṉaḷ. koḻuñcōṟu, mikka uṇavup poruḷ poḻutu maṟuttuṇṭal, uṇṭaṟkuriya poḻutil uṇavu iṉmiyiṉ uṇavu kiṭitta pōtu poḻutu nōkkātu uṇṭal. "poḻutu maṟuttuṇṇum iṉṉā vikal2" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatum, vaṟumiyuṟṟu vāṭuvōṉ "poḻutu maṟuttuṇṇum uṇṭiyēṉ3" eṉpatum kāṇka.
talimakaḷ naṭattum maṉiyaṟattiṉ māṇpu kaṇṭu viyappu mikak koṇṭa cevilikkut talimakaḷiṉ iḷami uruvam neñciṉkaṇ tōṉṟutaliṉ, akkālattu avaḷ ceyta ceyalvakikaḷi niṉintu muṟiyē kūṟukiṉṟa miyiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ avaḷ pāluṇavu maṟuttōṭiya tiṟatti, tīmpāl poṟkalattu oruki ēntic ceviliyar koṭuppa ataṉi maṟuttu avarkaḷ pari melintoḻiyap panta rōṭi maṟukkam ciṟuviḷiyāṭṭi eṉṟāḷ. maṟuttaṟ kākāvāṟu iṉimi mikat tēṉ kalanta tīviya pāl eṉpāḷ, piracam kalanta veṇcuvit tīmpāl eṉṟum, kaṇkavarum oḷiyum tūymiyumuṭiya kalattil appālit tantami tōṉṟa, virikatirp poṟkalattu oruki ēnti eṉṟum, mēṉi mēṟpaṭiṉ nōy ceyyāvakiyil, orukiyil ciṟukōl ēntiṉamiyiṉ, puṭippiṉ cuṟṟum pūntalic ciṟukōl eṉṟum, pāluṇavip paṉmuṟiyum maṟuttaliṉ accuṟutti uṇpippatu karuti, uṇṇeṉṟu ōkkupu puṭippa eṉṟum, ataṟkum añcātu avaḷ mullip pantarkkīḻ ōṭiyatu niṉintu, pantar ōṭi eṉṟum, ōṭuṅkāl avaḷuṭiya kāṟcilampu olittatum cemmutu ceviliyar avaḷip paṟṟamāṭṭārāy ayarntami yum tōṉṟat teṇṇīr muttarip poṟcilampu olippat tantuṟṟu eṉṟum, cemmutu ceviliyar parimelin toḻiya eṉṟum ceviliyar ayarcci taṉakku veṟṟi tantami yiṉ avaratu ēvali maṟuttu viḷiyāṭuvatē ceyalākak koṇṭaṉaḷ eṉṟaṟku, ēval maṟukkum ciṟuviḷiyāṭṭi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. celvamikuti tōṉṟa, teṇṇīr  muttu arip poṟ cilampu eṉṟum, talimakaḷatu ceyki yāl ceviliyar tamatu mutumi kāraṇamāka melintā rāyiṉum, avaḷ evvāṟṟālēṉum pāli yuṇṭu pacitīrtal vēṇṭum eṉṟa cemmi maṉappaṇpil tīrntilar eṉṟaṟku arinarik kūntal cemmutu ceviliyar eṉṟum ciṟap pittār. taṉ coṟ ceyalkaḷil cōrvu uṇṭākāmal taṟkāttut taṟkoṇṭa talimakaṉip pēṇi niṟkum peruntakimi kaṇṭu viyantu makiḻtaliṉ, aṟivum oḻukka mum yāṇṭu uṇarntaṉaḷ kol eṉṟāḷ. iḷamic cevvi muḻutum taṉṉōṭē iruntamiyiṉ, yāṇṭu uṇarntaṉaḷ kol eṉṟāḷ. taṉṉik koṇṭa kaṇava ṉukkut taṉṉip poruḷākap peṟṟa tanti taṉakkut taruvaṉavaṟṟik kōṭaṟku urimi yuṇṭāyiṉum, atu koṇṭu ceyyum maṉiyaṟam tāṉ uḻantu ceyyum taṉiyaṟam pōl ciṟavāmip paṟṟi, koṇṭa koḻunaṉ kuṭivaṟaṉuṟṟeṉak koṭutta tanti koḻuñcōṟu uḷḷāḷ eṉṟāḷ, koṇṭa koḻunaṉ eṉṟatu.. koḻunarkkuk koḷaṟkurimiyum, koṭutta tanti eṉṟatu. tantikkuk koṭi urimiyum iyalpāyk kiṭantavāṟu cuṭṭiṉāḷ. tantiyum uyar niliyil vittē koḻuñ cōṟu aḷittāṉ eṉṟaṟku ītta eṉṟātal tanta eṉṟātal kūṟātu koṭutta tanti eṉṟāḷ. maṟuttaṟ kākāta ciṟappum mikutiyum tōṉṟak koḻuñcōṟu eṉac ciṟappikkap paṭṭatu. vaṟumi cuṭaccuṭa varuttiṉum vāṭṭamuṟāta maṉat tiṭpatti eṇṇi matukiyaḷ eṉṟāḷāyiṉum, iḷamip paṇpiyum uṭaṉ pārāṭṭic ciṟumatu kiyaḷ eṉṟāḷ. piṟar taṉṉip paṉmuṟi iṟiñci uṇpikka uṇṇum ciṟuviḷiyāṭṭi iṉṟu taṉ kātalaṉip parintu uṇpikkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ ivaṭku ivvaṟivu eṅṅaṉam tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatum, iḷamiyil ceviliyar mutalā yiṉōruṭiya ēval maṟuttoḻukiya ivaḷ, taṉ koḻunaṉ ēvaṟkuṟippik kūṟāmi uṇarntu avaṉ uḷḷam uvaki mikumāṟu ceytoḻukum cīrtta oḻukkamuṭiyaḷāyatu eṅṅaṉam eṉpatum cevili uḷḷattit tikippittaṉa eṉa aṟika.
"kaḻiviṉum varaviṉum nikaḻviṉum vaḻikoḷa nallavi urittalum allavi kaṭitalum, cevilik kuriya vāku meṉpa1" eṉpataṉ uriyil "ākum" eṉṟataṉālē cevili naṟṟāykku uvanturippaṉavum koḷka eṉṟu kūṟi, ip pāṭṭikkāṭṭi, "itu maṉiyaṟam kaṇṭu maruṇṭu uvantu kūṟiyatu" eṉṟum, "ciṉaṉē pētimi nimpiri nalkuravu, aṉināl vakiyum ciṟappoṭu varumē"2 eṉpataṉ uri yil itaṉikkāṭṭi, "itu kuṭi vaṟaṉuṟṟeṉa nalkuravu kūṟiyum kātalic ciṟappittaliṉ amintatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
111. ulōccaṉār
talimakaṉ kaṭami kāraṇamākap pirintiruntāṉāka, avaṉ kuṟitta paruvavaravu nōkkit talimakaḷ ārvamuṟ ṟiruntāḷ, paruvavaravu paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ pala iyaṟkiyil nikaḻavum avaṟṟik kaṇṭa talimakaṭkuk kātalaṉpāl karuttu mikuva tāyiṟṟu. pirivāl uḷatākiya mēṉi melivu orupuṟam varuttavum paruvavaravu, etirē tōṉṟi uḷḷattil kātal niṉivukaḷi eḻuppi avaḷi varuttiṉa. atu kaṇṭu āṟṟāmi mikka tōḻi, tali makaṉuṭiya varavu kuṟittup perumutu peṇṭiri viṭuttu viricci kēṭṭāḷ. avarkaḷum ataṉic ceytu talimakaṉ kuṟitta paruvattil tavaṟātu tāḻātu vantu cērvaṉ eṉa vaṟ puṟuttiṉar. atu kēṭṭu uvakiyuṟṟa tōḻi talimakaḷpāl, "aṉṉāy, kaṭalil mīṉvēṭṭam puriyum paratavar ciṟuvar timil mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟu cuṟavum piṟavumākiya mīṉkaḷip piṭittu avaṟṟuṭ pariyaṉavaṟṟit tam timiliṭam koḷḷumāṟu tuṇṭittuk koṇarntu kariyiluḷḷa maṇaṟ parappil iṟak kuvar eṉa viricci kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, namatu kātalar tāḻātu varuvar" eṉa moḻintāḷ. 
tōḻiyiṉatu ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ tāṉ kēṭṭa viricciyip paṭṭāṅku eṭutturikku mukattāṉ talimakaḷi āṟṟuvikkum kuṟippik kaṇṭa ulōccaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
atta iruppip pūviṉ aṉṉa
tuyttali iṟavoṭu 1tokumīṉ peṟīiyar varivalip paratavar karuviṉic ciṟāar
maraṉmēṟ koṇṭu māṉkaṇam takimār
ventiṟal iḷiyar vēṭṭeḻun tāṅkut
timilmēṟ koṇṭu tiriccuram nīnti
vāḷvāyc cuṟavōṭu2 vayamīṉ keṇṭiya
niṇampey tōṇiyar3 oṇmaṇal iḻitarum
peruṅkaḻip pākkam kalleṉa
varumē tōḻi koṇkaṉ tērē.
itu viricci peṟṟup pukunta tōḻi talivikku urittatu.
uri
atta iruppip pūviṉ aṉṉa - vaḻikkariyil niṉṟa iruppimarattiṉ pūvip pōṉṟa; tuyttali iṟavoṭu - taliyil tuyyiṉi yuṭiya iṟāmīṉuṭaṉē; tokumīṉ peṟīiyar -palavāyt tokka vēṟu mīṉkaḷiyum peṟum poruṭṭu; vari valip paratavar karuviṉic ciṟāar-varintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa valiyiyuṭiya paratavar iṉattu vaṉ ṟoḻil puriyum iḷiyavarkaḷ; maraṉ mēṟ koṇṭu māṉkaṇam takimār-maraṅkaḷiṉ mēl ēṟiyiruntu veḷiyil mēyum māṉkūṭṭattit taṭukkum poruṭṭu; ventiṟal iḷiyar vēṭṭeḻun tāṅku-vevviya valiyiyuṭiya vēṭṭuva iḷiñarkaḷ virumpi eḻuntāṟpōla; timil mēṟkoṇṭu-paṭakiṉ mēl ēṟikkoṇṭu; tiriccuram nīnti-alikaḷ eḻuntu mōtum kaṭalakattē ceṉṟu; vāḷvāyc cuṟavoṭu vayamīṉ keṇṭiya -vāḷ pōṉṟa vāyiyuṭiya cuṟāmīṉkaḷōṭu valiya perumīṉkaḷip piṭittut tam paṭaku koḷḷumāṟu tuṇṭitta; niṇampey tōṇiyar-tuṇṭaṅkaḷākiya ūṉ peyta tōṇikaḷōṭu vantu; oṇmaṇal iḻitarum-oḷḷiya maṇaṟparappil iṟakkum; peruṅkaḻip pākkam kalleṉa-periya kaḻiyaru kuḷḷa paratavar vāḻum pākkam kalleṉṟu āravārikka; tōḻi-; koṇkaṉ tēr varum-nam talivaṉatu tēr varum, kāṇ (e-ṟu).
tōḻi, ciṟāar, mīṉpeṟīiyar, iḷiyar vēṭṭeḻun tāṅkuk koṇṭu, nīnti, keṇṭiya niṇam pey tōṇiyar, iḻitarum pākkam kalleṉak koṇkaṉ tēr varum eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. attam, vaḻi, kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāka mēyum ēṉimīṉkaḷit tokumīṉ eṉṟār. karuviṉi eṉṟa viṭattuk karumi vaṉmi kuṟittu niṉṟatu; "karuṅkik kāṉavaṉ"1 eṉṟāṟ pōla paratavar iṉattu iḷiyaric ciṟāar eṉṟār. nāṟpuṟamum pārttu ōṭātavāṟu takit taṟku marattiṉ mēl ēṟik koṇṭaṉar eṉpār, maraṉmēṟ koṇṭu māṉ kaṇam takimār eṉṟār. vayamīṉ uṭal pariya valiya mīṉ, keṇṭutal tuṇṭittal; "iṉcili yeḻilēṟu keṇṭip puriya, niṇam poti viḻuttaṭi neruppiṉ vitteṭuttu2" eṉac cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvatu kāṇka. tiriccuram, kaṭaṟparappu; "tiric curam uḻanta tiṇṭimil1" eṉa varutal kāṇka. tōṇi pāy kaṭṭiya paṭaku, cuṟāmīṉiṉ vāy, vāḷ pōṟ kūriya paṟkaḷuṭaṉ nīṇ ṭiruttalāl, vāḷvāyc cuṟavu eṉṟaṉar; "vāḷvāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉittuṟi nīnti2" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. keṇṭiya perumīṉ tuṇṭaṅkaḷ niṇam veḷippaṭṭuk kiṭattaliṉ niṇam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
talimakaṉatu pirivāṟṟātu varuntum talimakaḷ poruṭṭu, viricci niṟpāṉ ceṉṟa tōḻikku, viricci niṟkum perumutu peṇṭir urittataṉik koṇṭeṭuttu moḻikiṉṟā ḷākalāṉ. paratavar makaḷāṉa mutupeṇṭu, tāṉ aṟikiḷaviyākiya paratavar ceyaliyē urikkiṉṟāḷ. iṟavum piṟavumākiya mīṉkaḷ palavāyt tokkuk kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāy mēytaliṉ avaṟṟik kaṇṭa paratavar timil mēṟ ceṉṟatu. poruḷviṉikaṭ kuriya kālamum iṭamum oruṅku vāyppak kaṇṭa talimakaṉ ēvaliḷiyarum piṟarum uṭaṉ varac ceṉṟa kuṟippiyum paratavar vāḷvāyc cuṟavum vayamīṉum piṟavum tōṇiyiṟ peytu koṇarntu oṇ maṇaṟkaṇ iṟakkuvatu. pirintu ceṉṟa talimakaṉ tāṉ ceṉṟa nāṭṭil īṭṭiya poṉṉiyum poruḷiyum koṇarntu maṉikkaṇ iṟakkutaliyum, paratavar ceyaliṉ nalam kaṇṭa pākkattavar ceyta āravāram, talivi maṉik kaṇ nikaḻum viruntiyum kuṟittu niṟṟaliṉ, avaṟṟit tōḻi viriyak kūṟak karuti, ivvāṟu viricci kūṟutaliṉ, talimakaṉ tāḻātu varutal orutali eṉpāḷ, varumē tōḻi koṇkaṉ tērē eṉṟāḷ. itaṉāl, tali makaṉatu nalvaravit talivi uṇarntu āṟṟiyirup pāḷāvatu payaṉ.
112. peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār
kārpparuva varavil, viṉimuṟṟi mīṇṭu varuvatākac collip pirinta talimakaṉatu colvaḻi niṉṟu talivi taṉakkuriya kaṭaṉkaḷi iṉitu āṟṟi vantāḷ. avaṉ kuṟitta paruvamum eytuvatāyiṟṟu; āyiṉum, avaṉ varavupaṟṟiya kuṟippoṉṟum talimakaṭku eytātākavē, avaṭku āṟṟāmi mikuntatu. kārpparuva varavu nōkki vēṉiliṉ  vemmi ciṟitu ciṟitākat taṇintu, avaṭkup paruvavaraviṉ paṇpit teḷivittuviṭṭatu. 'āṭavarkku viṉiyē uyir' eṉpatiyum, mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyi muṭittallatu avar iṭiyil maṭaṅki mīḷār eṉpatiyum tōḻi naṉku aṟintavaḷ; ātalāl, avaṟṟi eṭuttōti vaṟpuṟuttuvati viṭuttu, vemmi taṇintu varum vēṉiliṉ iṟutikkālam kārpparuva maṉṟu; perumaḻi peyyum kālamākiya atuvē talimakaṉ varavu kuṟitta kālam eṉac colli āṟṟuvittu vantāḷ. ciṉṉāṭkaḷil mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyi veṟṟipeṟa muṭittut talivaṉ mīḷkiṉṟa ceyti tōḻikkut terintatu; avaṉ kuṟitta maḻikkālamum uṭaṉ vantatu.  ataṉāl makiḻcci mītūrnta tōḻi, talimakaḷi nōkki, talimakaṉ viṉimuṭittu vīṟukoṇṭu viḷaṅkum ceytiyiṉum, avaṉatu āṟṟāmi tīrtaṟku maruntāvatu avaṉatu varavallatu piṟitiṉmiyiṉ, viṉiveṉṟiyi uḷḷuṟut turittu, "aṉṉāy, peruṅkal nāṭaṉākiya talimakaṉ. niṉakku vaṟpuṟutta col poyyātu varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ; avaṉatu varavi muṉṉaṟintu peritum virumpiya maḻimukil kaṭalakam ceṉṟu nīr mikak koṇṭu, niṟam karuttut tokutiyuṟṟu, maliyakam paṭarntu kaṇṇimippatu pōl miṉṉi iṭittu maḻi peyvāṉ vantuḷḷatu kāṇ.  iṉi avaṉ varutal orutali yākaliṉ, nī varuntutal oḻintu tūtākap pōntu avaṉ varukiyit terivitta immaḻikku ettakiya viruntu ceyvām kūṟuka" eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaṉuṭiya viṉiyiṉ veṉṟi viḷakkattiyum kuṟitta poḻutiṉ kaṇ mīṇṭu pōtarum avaṉatu vāymic ciṟappiyum vitantu kūṟum tōḻi. tali makaṭku avaṉatu varukiyi maḻivaraviṉ vāyilākak kāṭṭi ammaḻikku ettakiya viruntu yām ceyaṟpālatu eṉa naki arumpik kūṟiyatu, peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻāratu  perumpula miyit tūṇṭiṟṟāka inta aḻakiya pāṭṭut tōṉṟuva tāyiṟṟu.
viruntevaṉ ceyvām 1tōḻi cāral
arumpaṟa malarnta karuṅkāl vēṅkic
curumpimir aṭukkam pulampak2 kaḷiṟaṭ
ṭurumpil uḷḷat tirumpuli3 vaḻaṅkum
peruṅkal nāṭaṉ varavaṟintu virumpi
mākkaṭal mukantu maṇiniṟak karuvit
tāḻnīr naṉantaliaḻuntupaṭac4 cāay
maliyimip patupōl miṉṉic
cilival lēṟṟōṭu cērnta5 immaḻikkē
itu, paruva varaviṉkaṇ āṟṟāḷāya taliviyit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.
uri
viruntu evaṉ ceyvām-viruntāka yām yātaṉic ceyvām, kūṟuka; tōḻi-; cāral arumpaṟa malarnta karuṅkāl vēṅki-cāraliṭattu arumpu ciṟitum illiyāka muḻutum malarnta kariya aṭiyiyuṭiya vēṅkimaraṅkaḷ niṟpatāl; curumpu imir aṭukkam pulampa-vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum malip pakkaṅkaḷ etiroli ceyya; kaḷiṟu aṭṭu-kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyik koṉṟum; urumpil uḷḷattu irumpuli vaḻaṅkum-nalivillāta uḷḷattuṭaṉē periya puli ulāvu kiṉṟa; peruṅkal nāṭaṉ varavu aṟintu-periya mali nāṭa ṉākiya nammuṭiya talimakaṉ poyyātu varutali muṉ ṉaṟintu; virumpi-tāṉum avaṉi virumpiyatu pōla; mākkaṭal mukantu maṇiniṟak karuvi-periya kaṭalnīrik kuṭittu nīlaniṟamuṭiya maḻittokutiyāki; tāḻnīr naṉan tali aḻuntupaṭa -tāḻnta nilimiyiṉiyuṭiya akaṉṟa iṭamellām mēlum āḻntu tāḻumāṟu; cā ay-meli vittu; mali imippatu pōl miṉṉi-malimuṭikaḷ kaṇimippatupōla miṉṉi; cilival ēṟṟoṭu cērnta immaḻikku - muḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya valiya iṭiyōṭu vantu cērnta immaḻi mukilkaḷukku (e-ṟu).
tōḻi, nāṭaṉ varavaṟintu virumpi. mukantu, karuviyāki, naṉantali aḻuntupaṭa, cāay, miṉṉi, cērnta immaḻikku viruntu evaṉ ceyvām. kūṟuka eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. irumpuli, aṭukkam pulampa, aṭṭu, vaḻaṅkum eṉka. cili vallēṟu eṉpataṉuḷ vaṉmiyi iṭippiṟa vara lākki ciliyēṟu eṉpataṉi viṉittokiyākak koḷ ḷiṉum amiyum, pūkkaḷiṉ ceṟivum mikutiyum tōṉṟa, arumpaṟa malarnta eṉṟār. "arumpaṟa vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmari1" eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. vēṅki marattiṉ aṭimutal karuniṟamuṭimiyiṉ karuṅkāl vēṅki eṉap paṭṭatu; "karuntāḷ vēṅki2" eṉṟu piṟāṇṭum varutal kāṇka. malippakkattē vēṅkimaraṅkaḷ niṟinta kāṭṭiṉ kaṇ puliyum kaḷiṟum porumiṭattu eḻum muḻakkam mali yakam eṅkum etirolikkumāṟu viḷaṅka, curumpimir aṭukkam pulampak kaḷiṟaṭṭu eṉṟār. urumpu, nalivu "urumpil kūṟṟat taṉṉaniṉ tiruntu toḻil vayavar3" eṉac cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu kāṇka. varavu aṟintu virumpi eṉṉum toṭarāṟṟalāl, muṉṉaṟintu virumpiṉāṟpōla eṉap poruḷ tantatu. mākkaṭali mukantamiyiṉ mukil maṇiniṟam koṇṭatu eṉṟu koḷka. karuvi, tokuti āḻntupaṭa eṉpatu aḻuntupaṭa eṉa vantatu. cāay eṉṟatu cāyvittu eṉap piṟaviṉip poruṭṭu. maḻi mukil miṉṉutali imippataṟku oppākak kūṟal cāṉṟōr marapu; "tuñcuvatu pōla iruḷi viṇpaka imippatu pōla miṉṉi4" eṉa varutal kāṇka.
talimakaṉ taṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟa kāliyil vaṟ puṟuttic ceṉṟa paruvam varak kaṇṭa talimakaḷ, āṟṟāḷāy mikavum varuntukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, avaṭku avaṉatu varukik kuṟippi muṉṉuṇarntu pōnta tōḻi, avaṭku ataṉi mella uṇarttum karuttiṉaḷāy, viruntu  evaṉ ceyvām tōḻi eṉṟāḷ. maṉiyaṟattiṉ māṇpukkuriya uyarntōr variṉum, avar varuki yuṇarttum tūtu variṉum, avarkaṭku viruntu ceytu ciṟappippatu tamiḻ vāḻviṉ taṉip paṇpu; ataṉāṟṟāṉ, viruntu evaṉ ceyvām eṉat tōḻi eṭuttu moḻintāḷ. "viṉikalantu veṉṟīka vēntaṉ maṉi kalantu, māli ayarkam viruntu1" eṉṟu talivi kūṟuvatu kāṇka. pukaḻum iṉpamum perukkum peruñcelvam vēṇṭic ceṉṟa talimakaṉ, vēṇṭiyavāṟē cuṟṟamum tuṇiyum pukaḻntu paravap poruḷi īṭṭi ici peṟu kiṉṟāṉ eṉpati uḷḷuṟukkum kuṟippiṉāl vēṅki malarnta cāralil vēṭṭam pukka irumpuli aṭukkamellām pulampak kaḷiṟaṭṭu vaḻaṅkum eṉṟāḷ. urumpil uḷḷattu irumpuli eṉṟāḷ, ataṉāl talimakaṉatu viṉimāṇpi vitantu kuṟittal karuttākaliṉ, itukāṟum illātirunta maḻimukil ippoḻutu iruḷpaṭac ceṟintu varutaṟkuk kāraṇam itu eṉpāḷ. varavaṟintu virumpi eṉṟum; peyta nīr paḷḷattē ceṉṟu taṅkutal iyalpākaliṉ, tāḻnīr naṉantali aḻuntupaṭa eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. poruḷiṉmi
yāl tāḻntirukkum cuṟṟattukkum iravalar kūṭṭattukkum īṭṭi varum celvatti īntu vāḻvikkum talimakaṉatu ēṟṟam itaṉāl cuṭṭiyavāṟu. talimakaṉ pōntu tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷip pakuttuṇṭu makiḻvataṉ muṉṉē, avaṉ ceṅkōl vaḻi niṟkum maḻimukil tāṉ mākkaṭal ceṉṟu nīr mukantu maṇiniṟam peṟṟup pōntu malittali yamarntu maḻi peyvāṉ camintatāka nī āṟṟāmi koṇṭu ayarcci yuṟuvatu ākātu eṉpāḷ, mali imippatu pōl miṉṉic cilival lēṟṟoṭu cērnta im
maḻikkē eṉṟāḷ. talimakaṉ varukiṟāṉ eṉṟu colliya vaḻik kēṭkum talimakaḷ uḷḷam ēlātu etirmaṟik kuṟippuk koṇṭu varuntutaṟ kiṭamuṇmiyiṉ, avaḷatu uḷḷantāṉē avaṉ varavi uyttuṇarntu koḷḷa vikkum tiṟam tōḻi kūṟṟil amivatu kāṇka. talivi avaṉatu varavuṇarntu makiḻvāḷāvatu ippāṭṭiṉ payaṉātal aṟika.
113. eyiṉanti makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār
maṇam puṇarnta ciṉṉāṭkellām talimakaṉ mēṟ
koṇṭa pirivu talimakaḷ uḷḷattil peruvaruttattit tōṟṟuvittatu. yāṉ niṉṉiṟ pirintu cellutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟiya col talimakaḷ ceviyiṟ pukutalum, avaṭku āṟṟāmi peritāyiṟṟu; mitīṭṭiya kaṇkaḷil nīrāṟākap perukiṟṟu; neypeytu nīviya kūntal nekiḻntu carintu avaḷ mukatti mūṭiviṭṭatu; kuḻaliṉum iṉiya iciyiṉ nalam vāynta taṉ kuraleṭuttu aḻat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. atu kāṇappoṟāta talimakaṉ' avaḷi melliya iṉiya coṟkaḷāl āṟṟuvittā ṉāyiṉum. aḻutu niṉṟa avaḷatu tōṟṟam, avaṉ neñcakkiḻiyil, vallōṉ eḻutiya uyirōviyam pōla oṉṟiviṭṭatu. ataṉāl avaṉ pirintu ceṉṟukoṇṭirukkiyil, curattiṭiyē nilaviya kāṭci
kaḷuṭ cila, avaṉ neñcil nilavum avaḷatu uruvit tōṟṟu
vikkavē, avaṉatu maṉaniṟi ciṟitu kalaṅkiṟṟu. yām peruṅ
kāṭukaḷ pala kaṭantu ittuṇi neṭuntoliyil van
tuḷḷōm; nam kātaliyip pirinta ñāṉṟu viḷaṅkiya avaḷatu tōṟṟam nampōl neṭuñcuraṅkaḷik kaṭantu vantuḷatē; iḵtu eṉṉē! eṉa viyantu taṉakkuḷḷē kūṟikkoṇṭāṉ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, kātalāṟ piṇippuṇṭu kaṭami vayappaṭṭu nīṅkic cellum kaṭṭiḷaṅkāḷiyiṉ aṟam tiriyā uḷḷam eytum kalaṅkañar nili vaṭittuk kāṭṭappaṭuvatu kaṇṭa eyiṉanti makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār, ataṉi yāvarum kaṇṭu iṉpuṟumāṟu ippāṭṭiṉāl collōviyam ceykiṉṟār.

iḵtu iṭiccurattu āṟṟāṉāya talivaṉ colliyatu.
uri
