talimakkaḷ uḷḷattil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta kātal, kaḷaviṉ kaṇ tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam, kuṟivaḻic cēṟal mutaliya neṟikaḷil ciṟantu varuṅkāl talimakaṉatu aḻivil kūṭṭam peṟuvatil talimakaṭku vēṭki perukuvatāyiṟṟu. talimakaṉō, talivipāl tōṉṟiya kaḷavukkātal taṉṉiyiṉṟi amiyāta aḷavu perukuva teṇṇik kaḷavē virumpi yoḻukiṉāṉ. ivvoḻukkatti neṭitu tāḻkka viṭāmal virivil variviṉkaṇ celutta vēṇṭumeṉṟa kaṭamiyit tōḻi uṇarntāḷ. talimakaṉ taliviyatu kātaṟ perukki aṟiyāṉ pōlavum, tāṉ ataṉi naṉkaṟintu talimakaṟ kuṇarttum takuti mikavuṭiyāḷ pōlavum niṉikkumāṟu, tōḻi talimakaṉmuṉ ceṉṟu viriya varintu koḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭuvatu taṉakku mikka ceyalām eṉpati uṇarntāḷ. āyiṉum, avaṉ kaḷavē virumpi yoḻukiyatu talivipāl mēṉi vēṟupāṭum āṟ ṟāmiyum tōṟṟuvittamiyiṉ. kālam tāḻppatu avaṭkum ākātāyiṟṟu. ākavē, talimakaḷi varintu kōṭal vēṇṭu meṉat talimakaṉi veḷippaṭiyākak kēṭpatu muṟimi yaṉṟāyiṉum, kuṟippāl uṇarttuvatu kuṟṟamākātu eṉat tōḻi uṇarntāḷ. orunāḷ, talimakaṉ taliviyatu peru maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟamāka vantuniṟpatu uṇarntu, talivi yoṭu collāṭuvāḷāy, "tōḻi, namatuūr cīṟū rāyiṉum iṅkēyuḷḷa taṇkuṭi vāḻnar tammi nōkki varum virun tiṉarip pēṇit tammiṭamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷip pāttuṇṭu vāḻum paṇpumēmpaṭṭavar. kaṭaltiri koḻitta putumaṇal paranta kāṉaṟcōlikkaṇ vāluḷip puravi pūṭṭiya tēriṉa rāy vanta talivaroṭu kūṭi makiḻāta muṉpu, iccīṟūr uṟi taṟku mikavum iṉitāyiruntatu; ippōtu alar urikkum periya ūrāy nammi varuttā niṉṟatu; itaṟku eṉ ceyvēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
ik kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, vēṭkimikutiyāl mēṉi vēṟupāṭum nutaṟpacaliyum eyti varuntum talimakaḷ nilimi kaṇṭa ayal makaḷir eṭukkum alaruriyāl accamum, ataṉāl avvūr vāḻtaṟku amiyāta iṉṉāmiyum uṭiya tāyiṟṟu eṉak kuṟippāl tōḻi yuṇarttum coṉṉalattil īṭupaṭṭa tūṅkal ōriyār ippāṭṭiṉip pāṭukiṉṟār.
tūṅkal ōli ōṅkumaṭaṟ peṇṇi
māvari putitta maṇaṉmali muṉṟil
variyāt tāram varuvirun tayarum
taṇkuṭi vāḻnar aṅkuṭic cīṟūr
1iṉitumaṉ amma tāṉē paṉipaṭu
palcuram uḻanta nalkūr pariya
muḻaṅkutirip putumaṇal aḻuntak koṭkum
tārmaṇip polinta puravit
tērōr2 tammoṭu nakāa ūṅkē3
itu, varivu nīṭṭippa alarām eṉak kavaṉṟa tōḻi ciṟip puṟamākac colliyatu.
uri
tūṅkal ōli ōṅkumaṭaṟ peṇṇi - toṅkukiṉṟa ōlikaḷiyuṭiyatāy uyarnta maṭalkaḷ poruntiya paṉiyiṉatu; mā ari putitta maṇalmali muṉṟil - kariya aṭimarappakuti maṟiya mūṭiyirukkum maṇal paranta muṟṟat tiṉkaṇ; variyāt tāram varuviruntayarum - variyaṟi yiṉṟip perukiya tamatu poruḷit tammi nōkki varum viruntiṉarkkup pakuttuṇṭu makiḻum; taṇkuṭi vāḻnar aṅkuṭic cīṟūr - kuḷirnta maṉikaḷil vāḻvōruṭiya aḻakiya kuṭikaḷ niṟinta icciṟiya vūr; iṉitumaṉ - emakku iṉitāy iruntatu, kāṇ; amma - itaṉik kēṭpāyāka; paṉipaṭu palcuram uḻanta nalkūr pariya - paṉiyāṟ kuḷirnta pala kāṭukaḷik kaṭantataṉāl valikuṉṟiya celaviṉiyuṭiya; muḻaṅkutirip putumaṇal aḻuntak koṭkum - muḻaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭalalikaḷāl otukkappaṭṭa putumaṇaṟkaṇ kālkaḷ aḻuntutalāl varuntum; tārmaṇi polinta puravi - mālipōl maṇikaḷāṟ polinta kutirikaḷip pūṭṭiya; tērōr tammoṭu nakāa ūṅku - tērūrntu vanta nam talivaruṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻvataṟku muṉpellām e-ṟu.
tērōr tammoṭu, nakāa ūṅku, viruntayarum aṅkuṭic cīṟūr iṉitumaṉ, ippoḻutu atu kaḻintatu eṉa, oḻiyiciyāl eñciyataṉip peytu kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kīḻnōkkit tāḻntu toṅkum ōliyit tūṅkal ōli eṉṟum, maṭalkaḷ tāḻāmal mēṉōkki niṉṟami tōṉṟa ōṅkumaṭal eṉṟum kuṟikkiṉṟār. mā - karumi, paṉiyiṉ aṭippakuti muṟṟum maṇaliṟ putintu maṟiya niṟpatu kuṟittu, mā ari putitta maṇalmali muṉṟil eṉṟār. ittuṇi eṉa aḷavu kāṇa iyalāta poruṭ perukkam variyāt tāram eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. vaṟumi yiṉ vemmi aṟiyāta kuṭi eṉṟaṟkut taṇkuṭi eṉṟār. kuṭikaḷuṭ piṉṉatu vīṭukaḷiṉ mēlniṉṟatu. maṉ - oḻiyici, pakalil mikka vemmiyum iraviṟ perumpaṉiyum uṭimi yiṉ paṉipaṭu palcuram eṉṟum, pālinilattu iyaṅkuvōr iravuppōtiṟ cēṟal iyalpākaliṉ, paṉipaṭu curam eṉavum kūṟiṉār. nalkūrtal - īṇṭu valiyaḻital, pari - celavu, putumaṇaṟparappu mikavum meṉmiyuṭiyatākaliṉ, kutiri yiṉ kālkaḷ naṉku putintu viṭutalāl, tēri īrkka māṭṭātu nāṟpakkamum tirumpit tirumpic cuḻaluvatu koṇṭu koṭkum eṉṟār.
virintu varitali mēṟkoḷḷātu talimakaṉ kaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭittatu poṟātu meliyum tali makaḷ poruṭṭut tōḻi, talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattē van tiruppatu uṇarntu avaṉik kāṇātāḷ pōlat talivi yoṭu collāṭukiṉṟāḷ. cīṟūrt taṇkuṭi vāḻnar maṉimāṭci yuṟṟut tām īṭṭiya poruḷāl varu viruntōmpi māṇpuṟuvatu kāṇum yāvarkkum atu mikka iṉpam tantu vantatu eṉpāḷ, aṅkuṭic cīṟūr iṉitumaṉ amma eṉṟu paṭṭāṅku moḻintāḷ. cīṟūrkkaṇ ciṟantu tōṉṟum kāṭcikaḷ pala irukkavum, avaṟṟi viṭuttut taṇkuṭi vāḻnaratu viruntōmpum tiṟatti vitantōtiyatu, tāmum avvāṟu maṇamuṭittu maṉiyaṟam mēṟkoṇṭu varuvirun tōmpum māṇpupeṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa viḻivit talimakaṟkuk kuṟippāy uṇarttiyavāṟu, talimakaṉoṭu kūṭi makiḻvataṟkumuṉ cīṟūr iṉitā yiruntatu. eṉavē, ippōtu aṉṉatākātu alar kūṟi iṉṉāmi ceyvatāyiṟṟu eṉṟāḷām. iṉi, neṟiyiṉkaṭ putumaṇal parantiruntami aṟiyātu celuttiyatāl, kutirikaḷ kāl putiyuṇṭu mēlē cella iyalāmal varuntum eṉṟatu, talimakaṉatu kātaṟṟoṭarpu aṟiyātu talivipāl putitu tōṉṟiya vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭa tāyarum tamarum uṇmi kāṇarāyk kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum kānta ḷākiya veṟiyāṭṭiṉum muyaṉṟu varuntāniṉṟaṉar eṉak kuṟippāyt tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷām. ākavē, talimakaṉ toṭarpu peṟumuṉ uṟitaṟku iṉitāyirukka iccīṟūr, alar kūṟi iṉṉāmi ceytavāṟum, kaṭṭuṅ kaḻaṅkum veṟiyu mākiya ceyalkaḷāl maṉiyakam varuttam ceyta vāṟum kūṟit tōḻi varitali virintu ceyka eṉak kuṟippāl talivaṉi vaṟpuṟuttuvatu payaṉāyiṟṟu.
136. nattaṅ koṟṟaṉār
ivar peyar naṟṟaṅ koṟṟaṉār eṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu, nattaṅ koṟṟaṉār eṉpatu nattaṉ eṉpārkku makaṉāṉa koṟṟaṉār eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. nattaṉ eṉṟō naṟṟaṉ eṉṟō āṇmakkaḷ peyar tāṅkiyiruntatāka nūluḷḷum vaḻakkiṉuḷḷum itukāṟum kāṇappaṭavilli. ākavē, nattam eṉṉum ūravarāṉa koṟṟaṉār eṉṟu koḷvatu porutta māka irukkiṟatu. nattaṅ koṟṟaṉār eṉṟa pāṭamē porutta mākap pērāciriyar viyāpurip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ tiruttam ceytaṉar. nattam eṉṟu peyar koṇṭa ūrkaḷ pala irut taliṉ ivaratu ūr iṉṉa nāṭṭatu eṉa varintu kūṟaṟku illi. iccāṉṟōr nattam eṉṉum ūriṉarātalāl nattak koṟṟaṉār eṉappaṭṭārāka, avar peyar, piṉpu nattaṅ koṟṟaṉār eṉṟu āyiṟṟu. paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ iṭiyē cāttaṉ. koṟṟaṉ eṉṟa iru peyarkaḷum peritum payiṉ ṟuḷḷaṉa. cāttaṉ eṉpatu vaṇikaruḷḷum koṟṟaṉ eṉpatu pōr purintoḻukum maṟavar iṭiyilum ciṟantu tōṉṟuvaṉa. vaṇikar tiraḷukkuc cāttu eṉpatum veṟṟikkuk koṟṟam eṉpatum tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷ. pakivar tōṟṟu uyi ruyntu ōṭa velvatu veṟṟi; avarkaḷik koṉṟu aḻippatu koṟṟam. makkaṭ peyarum ūrppeyarum vaṭamoḻiyāka māṟat toṭaṅkiya piṟkālattē, koṟṟaṉ eṉpatu vicayaṉ eṉa māṟiyataṉāl, appeyar innāḷil vaḻakku vīḻntu pōyiṟṟu. cāttaṉ eṉpatu māttiram iṉṟum nāṭṭuk kōṭṭi nakarat tār kuṭiyil cāttappaṉ eṉṟu payila vaḻaṅkivarukiṟatu. ic cāṉṟōr pāṭiyaṉavāka vēṟu pāṭṭukkaḷ kiṭittila.
talimakkaḷil, taliviyiṉ uḷḷattil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta kātal naṉku muṟukip perukumāṟu, talimakaṉ, kaḷavu neṟi yiṉiyē virumpi varintu kōṭalic ciṟitu nīṭṭippā ṉāyiṉaṉ. nāḷum vaḷarum kātaṟ perukkāl talimakaṭkut talivaṉatu aḻivil kūṭṭattiṉ mēṟ ceṉṟa vēṭki mikutalāl uṟakkamiṉmiyum uṇṭiyiṟ curukkamum, uṭal melivum uṇṭāki avaḷi varuttat toṭaṅkiṉa avaḷatu nutal pacantatu; aṇintirunta kivaḷikaḷ uṭal melivu paṟṟik kaḻaṉṟōṭuva vāyiṉa. taṉpāl uḷatākiya ivvēṟupāṭu piṟarkkut teriyiṉ alar tōṉṟumē eṉṟa accamum avaḷ uḷḷatti alippatāyiṟṟu. itaṉit talimakaṟku uṇartti viriyat taṉṉi varintu koḷḷumāṟu tūṇṭuvatē iṉit tāṉ ceytaṟpālatu eṉṟu teḷinta talivi, orunāḷ talivaṉ taṉ maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattē vantu niṟpatu kaṇṭu tōḻikku urippavaḷ pōl, avaṉ ceviyil naṉku paṭumāṟu tōḻi, piṇiyuṟṟavarkku avar virumpuvaṉa koṭātu vēṟākiya maruntu koṭuppatu maruttuvar aṟam; anta aṟavōrip pōlavē eṉ tantiyum, eṉkivaḷi kaḻaṉṟōṭuvatu kuṟittu yāṉ kavaluvatu kaṇṭu, iṉamāṉa iṟukiya vaḷikaḷi nalkātu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟāka vaḷiyiṉum iṭi akalam ciṟukiya toṭiyik koṇarntu eṉ kiyiṟ ceṟittu viṭṭār; ataṉāl, eṉ tōḷ melivupaṟṟi vaḷi kaḻaliṉum toṭiyik kaṭantu cella iyalāmiyiṉ eṉ vēṟupāṭu piṟar aṟitaṟku illiyāyiṟṟu. ippērutaviyic ceyta eṉ tanti vāḻvārāka" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaḷ tāṉē muṟpaṭṭut tali makaṉi varintu koḷḷumāṟu veḷippaṭa urittal ākāmi paṟṟik kuṟippāl tōḻiyoṭu taṉ vaḷipaṟṟiyum toṭi paṟṟiyum collāṭumāṟṟal uriyāṭit taṉ karutti uṇart tiya oṭpattik kaṇṭa nattaṅ koṟṟaṉāratu pulamiyuḷḷam peruviyappil āḻntu ivviṉiyapāṭṭi vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu.
1tiruntukōl elvaḷi vēṇṭiyāṉ aḻavum
arumpiṇi uṟunarkku vēṭṭatu koṭātu
maruntāyntu koṭutta aṟavōṉ pōla
eṉṉi vāḻiya palavē 2maṉṉiya
malikeḻu nāṭaṉoṭu emmiṭic ciṟiya
talippiri vuṇmi aṟivāṉ pōla
nīppa nīṅkātu variṉvari amintu
tōṭpaḻi maṟikkum utavip
pōkkil polantoṭi ceṟīi yōṉē.
itu, ciṟippuṟamākat talivi tōḻikku urittatu; tōḻi talivikku urittatūu mām.
uri
tiruntu kōl elvaḷi vēṇṭi yāṉ aḻavum - aḻakuṟat tiraṇṭu viḷaṅkum eṉ kivaḷi kaḻaṉṟōṭuvatu kuṟittu yāṉ varunti aḻutēṉāka; arumpiṇi uṟunarkku vēṭṭatu koṭātu - viriya nīkkutaṟkariya nōyuṟṟavarkku avarkaḷ virumpiyatu koṭāmal; maruntu āyntu koṭutta aṟavōṉ pōla - māṟāṉa cuviyiyuṭiya maruntiṉi ārāyntu koṭuttu utaviya maruttuvaṉākiya aṟavōṉip pōla; eṉṉi pala vāḻiya - eṉ tanti pallāṇṭukaḷ vāḻvāṉāka; maṉṉiya malikeḻu nāṭaṉoṭu - nilipeṟṟa mali poruntiya nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉuṭaṉ; emmiṭi ciṟiya talippirivuṇmi aṟivāṉ pōla; nīppa nīṅkātu - kātalaṉ nīṅkiyavaḻit tāṉum nīṅkāmalum; variṉ vari amintu - avaṉ puṇariṉ taṉ ellikkaṇ niṉṟum; tōḷpaḻi maṟikkum utavi - tōḷiṭattu uṇṭākum melivu puṟattārkkut tōṉṟātē maṟittu utavutali yuṭiya; pōkkil polam toṭi ceṟīiyōṉ - ōṭṭaṟṟa poṉṉāṟ ceyta toṭiyiṉic ceṟittāṉ ākalāṉ  e-ṟu.
elvaḷi vēṇṭi yāṉ aḻavum, aṟavōṉ pōla eṉṉi vāḻiya, toṭi ceṟīiyōṉ ākalāṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. nīppa nīṅkātu, amintu maṟikkum utavip polantoṭi pōkkil polantoṭi eṉa iyiyum. vaḷivum, neḷivum, mēṭupaḷḷamu miṉṟi, aḻakiya tiraṭci yamiyac ceyyappaṭṭami tōṉṟat tiruntu kōlvaḷi eṉṟār. piṇikku maruntu ataṟku māṟupaṭṭa poruḷātaliṉ, piṇiyuṟṟōrkku vēṭṭatu koṭāmi iyalpu eṉa aṟika. "piṇikku maruntu piṟamaṉ1" eṉac cāṉṟōr urippatu kāṇka. uyir niṟṟaṟ kuriya uṭampukku ākkam ceytal aṟaviṉiyum, kēṭu ceytal tīviṉiyu mākaliṉ, ākkam tarum maruttuvaṉi aṟavōṉ eṉṟār. maruntu tarum nūlum aṟanūlātaliṉ annūlvaḻi niṟṟal paṟṟi aṟavōṉ eṉṟār eṉiṉumām. kuṟiyiṭattē kūṭip piritalum oruvaḻittaṇappum uṭpaṭac ciṟiya talip pirivu eṉṟār. tōṭpaḻi tōḷiṭattu uṇṭākum melivu; melivu puṟattārkkut tōṉṟiṉ alar viḷivittu iṟ ceṟippu mikuvittut talimakaṉik kūṭāvāṟu tuṉpam ceyvatu paṟṟit tōṭpaḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu. "varuntutōṭ pūcal2" "vāṭutōṭ pūcal3" eṉṟatum ikkaruttēpaṟṟi eṉka poṉṉukkuk kuṟṟamāvatu ōṭutalākaliṉ, ōṭṭaṟṟa naṉ poṉṉip pōkkil poṉ eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kuṟippar. "pōkkil polaṅkalam niṟiya4" eṉpatu kāṇka. vaḷi yiṉum toṭiyiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ciṟitu kuṟintu kikkaṇ ceṟintu kiṭakkum eṉa aṟika, vaḷi vēṇṭiyavaṭkut toṭi yaḷittatu nōykku maruntu koṭuppatu pōlāyiṟṟu eṉka.
viriya varintu kōṭali niṉiyātu, kātaluṟavu ciṟappatu kuṟittuk kaḷavē virumpi yoḻukutalāl, aḻivil kūṭṭattiṉmēṟ ceṉṟa avā mikku melivuṟṟa tali makaḷ, vaḷikaḷ kaḻaṉṟōṭutaṟku āṟṟāḷāy varuntiṉami tōṉṟat tiruntukōl elvaḷi vēṇṭi yāṉ aḻavum eṉṟāḷ. kivaḷi nillātu kaḻaṉṟōṭuvatāl, uṭal melivip piṟar naṉku aṟiyac ceyvatu paṟṟi, el vaḷi eṉac ciṟappittāḷ. eḷitiṉ nīṅkāmip paṟṟip piṇi arumpiṇi eṉappaṭṭatu. piṇiyuṟṟōrkku uṇṭākum vēṭki appiṇiyatu vaḷarccikku ētuvātaliṉ, maruttuvar ataṉ nīkkattukku ētuvāy māṟupaṭṭa cuviyiyuṭiya poruḷiyē  ārāyntu koṭuppar. "nōy nāṭi nōy mutal nāṭi atu taṇikkum vāy nāṭi vāyppac ceyal7" vēṇṭutaliṉ, maruntāyntu koṭutta aṟavōṉpōla eṉṟāḷ, vaḷi nillāmiyāl uḷavākum alarum iṟ ceṟippum piṟavumākiya iṭiyūṟukaḷ tōṉṟā vaṇṇam, taṉ tanti tanta toṭi kāttamippaṟṟi, eṉṉi vāḻiya palavē eṉṟāḷ. uṇmi uṇarntu iṟceṟittu varutta vēṇṭiya tanti ataṉic ceyyāmiyiṉ vāḻiya palavē eṉṟāḷ eṉṟumām. talimakaṉatu kūṭṭam peṟṟavaḻic ceṟintum, avaṉ nīṅkiyavaḻi nekiḻntum, vaḷikaḷ talivi eytiya vēṟupāṭṭip pulappaṭuttiṉami tōṉṟa, nīppa nīṅkātu variṉ vari amintu eṉṟum, vaḷipōlātu kikkaṇ ceṟippuṟṟu. avvaḷikaḷ nekiḻntavaḻiyum kaḻaṉ ṟōṭātavāṟu taṭuttu niṟuttu mukattāl, tōḷ melivu kaṇṭu piṟar alar kūṟāvāṟu kātta ciṟapput tōṉṟat tōṭpaḻi maṟikkum utavip pōkkil polantoṭi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. toṭiyiṉ ceṟippu ivvāṟu payaṉ paṭuvatu nōkkumiṭattut tōḷ eytum melivum ataṟ kētuvākiya talimakaṉ kaḷavoḻukkamum aṟintami yuṇṭu eṉak kāṭṭutalāl, nāṭaṉoṭu nammiṭic ciṟiya talippirivuṇmi aṟivāṉ pōla eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, tamatu kaḷavuneṟi tāyar tantiyarkkup pulaṉākum niliyil perukip piṟar alar kūṟum aḷaviṟ ciṟantami kūṟi, itu kaṇṭum talimakaṉ varintukōṭali niṉiyā toḻukal naṉṟaṉṟu eṉak kuṟippāl talivi varivu kaṭāyiṉami kāṇka. itaṉāṟpayaṉ, ciṟippuṟattē niṉṟa talimakaṉ kēṭṭut taliviyatu kātaṟperukkiṉ uṇminiliyit teruṇṭu varivāṉāvatu.
137. peruṅkaṇṇaṉār
maṇṇavar aṟiya maṇam puṇarntu maṉivāḻvil tōyntu maṉiyāḷoṭu kūṭi nukarum iṉpattil tiḷit tāṭiya talimakaṉ kaṭami kāraṇamākap pirivu tōṉṟak kaṇṭu uḷḷam pētuṟalāṉāṉ; avaṉ uḷḷattē kātalum kaṭamiyum kaṭumpōr puriyaluṟṟaṉa. kaṭamivaḻi neñcamum, kātalvaḻi uṇarvum niṉṟaṉa. nilavulakiṉ nīṅki neṭuvāṉattukku ap pālum, ataṟkappālum mika viriviṟ ceṉṟu tirumpum iyalpiṟṟākiya neñciṉi nōkki, "neñcē, nī cēṇ akaṉṟu ceṉṟu varavalla tiṇmiyuṭiya eṉpatu orutali; attakiya nī iv viḷiyavaḷiṉ taṇṇiya naṟumaṇam kamaḻum kariya kūntaliṭattum, melliya tōḷiṭattum peṟum pēriṉpattit tuṟantu ivaḷiṟ pirintu cellak karutukiṉṟāy; ataṉi ārāyumiṭattu, nī pirintu ceṉṟu ceyyak karutum poruḷ  nī virumpiyavāṟē ceṉṟu ataṉip peṟṟu nīṭuvāḻvāyāka; ivvulakattil ceytaṟ kariyatoṉṟu eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. nī virumpiyavāṟē ceṉṟu ataṉi peṟṟu nīṭuvāḻ vāyāka, ivaḷ īṇṭut taṉit toḻiya vārēṉ kāṇ" eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭāṉ. akkūṟṟu āciriyar peruṅkaṇṇaṉāratu ārāycciyit tūṇṭiviṭṭatu.
itaṉkaṇ, talimakaḷpāl talivaṉ uḷḷattē ōṅki niṟkum kātaṟpiṇippu ēṉip poruṭpiṇippiṉum vali yuṭiyatāy avaṉi ataṉmēṟ cellāvāṟu taṭuppatum, akkātali veṉṟu ceṉṟeytum poruḷ ariyatoṉṟu eṉpatum iḷami yuḷḷattiṉ ceyal vakiyāy viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭu ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaṟṟit toṭuttup pāṭu kiṉṟār.
taṇṇiya kamaḻum tāḻiruṅ kūntal
taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ maṭa1nal lōḷvayiṉ
piriyac cūḻntaṉi yāyiṉ ariyatoṉ
ṟeytiṉi vāḻiya neñcē cevvari
aruvi yāṉṟa nīril nīḷiṭik
kayantali maṭappiṭi uyaṅkupaci kaḷiiyar
peruṅkaḷiṟu 2tolicciya muṭattāḷ ōmi
aruñcuram celvōrk kalkuniḻal ākum
kuṉṟa vippiṟ kāṉam
ceṉṟucēṇ akaṟal 3valliya nīyē
itu, talivaṉ 4celavaḻuṅkiyatu.
uri
taṇṇiya kamaḻum tāḻiruṅ kūntal - kuḷirnta maṇam kamaḻum tāḻnta kariya kūntaliyum; taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ - periya melliya mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷiyum; maṭa nallōḷ vayiṉ - iḷamiyum aḻakumuṭiya tali makaḷiṉiṉṟu; piriyac cūḻntaṉi - pirintu ceṉṟu poruḷ ceyvatu karutiṉāy; āyiṉ - ārāyumiṭattu; neñcē; ariyatu oṉṟu - aḵtu arumi vāynta toṉṟē; eytiṉi vāḻiya - ataṉi eyti nī nīṭuvāḻvāyāka; cevvari aruvi āṉṟa nīril nīḷiṭi - cevviya maliyiṉiṉṟu viḻum aruvi vaṟṟiṉamiyiṉ nīrillāta nīṇṭa vaḻiyiṉkaṇ; kayantali maṭappiṭi uyaṅkupaci  kaḷiiyar - periya taliyi uṭiya iḷiya piṭiyāṉi eyti varuntum paciyiṉip pōkkutaṟ poruṭṭu; peruṅkaḷiṟu tolicciya muṭattāḷ ōmi - periya kaḷiṟṟāl vīḻttappaṭṭa vaḷinta aṭimaratti yuṭiya ōmimaram; aruñcuram celvōrkku alkuniḻal ākum - cellutaṟ kariya curattik kaṭantu cellum makkaḷ taṅkutaṟ keṉac ciṟuniḻalic ceyyum; kuṉṟa vippiṉ kāṉam - kuṉṟukaḷi iṭiyiṭiyē yuṭiya kāṭukaḷi; ceṉṟu cēṇ akaṟal valliya nī - kaṭantu cēymiyiluḷḷa nāṭukaṭku mika virintu ceṉṟu mīḷavalla nī e-ṟu.
neñcē, ceṉṟu cēṇ akaṟal valliya nī, maṭa nallōḷ vayiṉ piriyac cūḻntaṉi; āyiṉ, aḵtu ariya toṉṟu; ataṉi eytiṉi vāḻiya; yāṉ ivaḷ īṇṭu oḻiya vārēṉ eṉa eñcuvaṉa peytu kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. ney peytum pūccūṭiyum oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭa kūntal eṉpār. taṇṇiya kamaḻum kūntal eṉṟār. tāḻa nīḷutal kūntaṟkuc ciṟappu tōḷ peruttu meṉmi peṟ ṟiruttal peṇkaṭku ilakkaṇam paṇi, mūṅkil, maṭam, iḷami, talimippaṇpukaḷ aṉittum tiraṇṭu uruk koṇṭāṟ pōlpava ḷākaliṉ nallōḷ eṉṟār. aḵtu, eñciniṉṟatu. ataṉi eṉṟatu avāynili eytiṉi, muṟṟeccam; iṉi, eytiṉi yākuvi eṉa urittu muṭit talum oṉṟu. yāṉ vārēṉ eṉṟatu kuṟippeccam. alku niḻal, kuṟinta aḷaviṟṟākiya niḻal; "ōmip paṭuciṉi payanta poruntāp pukarniḻal1" eṉa varutal kāṇka. iṉi, taṅkutaṟ kēṟṟa niḻal eṉṟumām; "palpūṅ kāṉat talku niḻal acii2" eṉa varum. vippu, ūrkaḷumām. nilavulaka elliyik kaṭantu maṟumi ulakukkum ceṉṟu mīḷum tiṟam neñcuk kuṇmiyiṉ cēṇ akaṟal valliya eṉṟār.
poruḷ ceyal vēṇṭum kaṭamivaḻi niṉṟa neñcu muṉṉum, putumaṇa viṉpam nalkum kātalvaḻi niṟkum uḷḷam3 piṉṉum niṉṟu īrppa, iṭiniṉṟu varuntum talimakaṉatu oṭpam. uḷḷatti neñciṉvaḻi niṟuttik kaṭamikkaṇ pukuttum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭutalāl, avvuḷḷam uvakkumāṟu, ataṟkup poruḷākiya taliviyiṉ nalatti vitantu, taṇṇiya kamaḻum tāḻiruṅ kūntal taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ maṭanallōḷ eṉṟu puṉintu kūṟu kiṉṟāṉ. taṉ kiyāl tīṇṭi oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭu uḷḷattē uyarntu tōṉṟutaliṉ mutaṟkaṇ kūntaliyum, vēṭṭapoḻutellām vēṭṭapaṭi iṉpam tantu ciṟappit tamiyiṉ piṉṉart tōḷiyum ciṟappittāṉ. pirintu cēṟali vaṟpuṟuttum kaṭamippōri niṉiyātu, kātaliṉpamē niṉivikkum taliviyatu iḷaminalatti maṭanallōḷ eṉṟu cuṭṭiṉāṉ. inniliyilum kaṭami yuṇarvu melivuṟātu pirintu cēṟali vaṟpuṟuttutalāl piriyac cūḻntaṉi eṉṟu neñcukkuk kūṟiṉāṉ. avaṉatu aṟiviṉ kaṇ tikaḻum oṭpam, kātaliṉpattiyum kaṭamic ciṟappiyum cīrtūkki ārāyap pukuntu, kātaliṉ arumi yiyum kaṭami payakkum perumiyiyum niṉintu, kaṭami neṟi mēmpaṭuvati viyantu, āyiṉ aḵtu ariya toṉṟu eṉṟu tuṇintāṉ. ceyaṟku ariya toṉṟiṉic ceypavarē periyā rākaliṉ, apperiyōr perumivaḻi niṟkum talimakaṉ, kātalvaḻi niṉṟu uḷḷattit taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉpaṭuttuvāṉāy neñciṉi nīkki niṟutti eṉ kātali īṇṭoḻiya niṉṉoṭu vārēṉ, nī ceṉṟu avvariya toṉṟiṉi eyti nīṭuvāḻka eṉpāṉāy eytiṉi vāḻiya eṉṟum, taṉittuc cēṟaṟkuriya vaṉmi neñciṟku uṇṭu eṉpatic ciṟappittuc ceṉṟu cēṇ akaṟal valliya nīyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
peruṅkaḷiṟu tolitta ōmi maram maṭappiṭiyiṉ paci kaḷintu aruñcuram celvōrkku alkuniḻal nalkum eṉṟataṉāl, nammāl īṇṭut tuṟakkappaṭum nam kātali, pacali uṇṇappaṭṭu nammi nāṭi, varum cuṟṟattārkku nammoṭu kūṭic ceyyum nallutavip payaṉ kuṉṟuvaḷ eṉat tāṉ vārātu maṟuttaṟkut talimakaṉ ētuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉakkoḷka. cevvari aruvi yāṉṟa nīril nīḷiṭi eṉṟum, kuṉṟa vippiṉ kāṉam eṉṟum curattiṉ koṭumi kūṟiyatu, celavarumiyic ciṟappittaṟku eṉka. itaṉāṟ payaṉ talivi kēṭṭup pirivuṭaṉ paṭuvāḷāvatu.
138. ammūvaṉār
kaḷavu neṟikkaṇ oḻukum talimakaṉ varitali mēṟ koḷḷātu akkaḷavē virumpi oḻukuvā ṉāyiṉāṉ. āyi ṉum, iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu paṭutalāl, talimakaḷit talip peytu kūṭum tiṟam paṉmuṟiyum iṭiyaṟṟup pōkavē, talimakaḷatu kātaṟperukkit talimakaṉ neruṅki aṟintu kōṭaṟku vāyā tāyiṟṟu. avaṉatu aḻivil kūṭṭattiṉmēṟ ceṉṟa avaḷatu ārvam nāḷum perukiṉamiyiṉ avaṭku āṟṟāmiyum, atu kāraṇamāka uṭalil melivum tōṉṟiṉa. uṭampum, uyirum ettuṇi vāṭṭam eytiṉum talimakaṉi nēriṟ kaṇṭu urittal talimip paṇpiṉ taṉiyuruvākiya tali makaṭku iyalpu maṉṟu, ilakkaṇamu maṉṟu, talimakkaḷatu vāḻvu aṟattil tiriyātu ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa orē kuṟikkōḷuṭaṉ vēṇṭuvaṉa utavi varum uyirttōḻi, taliviyatu āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu talimakaṟkuk kuṟippākavum, veḷippaṭiyākavum kūṟi virivil varintu koḷḷumāṟu talivaṉit tūṇṭi vantāḷ. avaḷatu uriyiṉit talimakaṉ aṉpuṭaṉ cevi maṭuttu vantāṉāyiṉum, kātal vaḷarcci kaṇṇiya karuttāl avaṉ kaṉavukkūṭṭattiyē virumpi yoḻukiṉāṉ. itaṟkuḷ, talivipāl tōṉṟiya vēṟupāṭu kaṉavoṭumayaṅkal, kaṇṭuyil maṟuttal, uṇṭiveṟuttal, uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal mutaliya ceyalkaḷāl piṟar iṉitaṟintu koḷḷaṟkuriya elliyi aṭintatu. ayalmaṉi makaḷirum ūravarum tammuḷ alarkūṟat talippaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl talimakaṭku āṟṟāmi mikkuṟṟatu. uḷḷattil alaraccamum uriyil aḻukiyum tōṉṟit talimakaḷi uṭaṟṟat talip paṭṭaṉa.
atukaṇṭa tōḻi, "iṉi itaṉit talimakaṟku  uṇartti virintu varivu mēṟkoḷḷac ceyva tallatu  ceyal piṟitilli eṉa eṇṇi, orunāḷ talimakaṉ talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟamāka vantu niṟpatu kaṇṭu, taliviyoṭu collāṭupavaḷ pōlat talimakaṉ cevi naṉku kēṭkumaḷavil niṉṟu kūṟuvāḷāy, "muṉpu orunāḷ nām āyamakaḷiruṭaṉ kūṭi nīrttuṟikkaṇ pāvi niṟuvi viḷiyāṭṭayarntē māka, appōtu, nam talivaṉāṉa tuṟivaṉ pōntu, nām niṟuviya vaṇṭaṟpāvikku neytal māli toṭuttut tanta ceytiyi ivvūravar tam kaṇkaḷāl nēriṟ kaṇṭa tuṇṭē yaṉṟi, ataṉiṉ piṉṉar nām kāṉaṟ cōlikkaṇ tamittuk kaṇṭu kaḷavu mēṟkoṇṭoḻukiṉami nēriṟ kaṇṭatu illi yaṉṟō? avvāṟāka ippoḻutu kaḷavu nikaḻcci muḻutum naṉku aṟintu ivvūr alar kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam yātākalām? eṉṟu collukiṉṟāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talivaṉ toṭarpuṇṭāya nikaḻcciyum, ataṉvaḻit tōṉṟiya alarum, ataṉāl tali makaḷ eytiya āṟṟāmiyum viḷaṅki niṉṟu, iṉiyum varivu nīṭṭippiṉ ētam palavām eṉpatu kāṭṭik kēṭṭu niṟkum talimakaṉ uḷḷatti variviṉkaṭ celuttum tiṟam kaṇṭa ammūvaṉār ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
uvarviḷi yuppiṉ 1kuṉṟunavil kuppi
maliyuyttup pakarum niliyā vāḻkkik
kaṇaṅkoḷ umaṇar uyaṅkuvayiṉ oḻitta
paṇṇaḻi paḻampār veṇkuru kīṉum
taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ muṉṉāḷ nammoṭu
pācaṭik kalitta kaṇikkāl neytal
2neṟittaru toṭali pāvi 3tiiya
kaṇṇaṟi vuṭimi yallatu nuṇviṉi
4iḻiyaṇi makaḷir viḻaviṉ āṭum
muḻaṅkutiri iṉcīr 5tūṅkum
aḻuṅkal mūtūr aṟintaṉṟō iṉṟē.
iḵtu, alarāyiṟṟeṉa āṟṟāḷāya talimakaṭkut talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi colliyatu.
uri
uvar viḷi uppiṉ kuṉṟu navil kuppi - uvar nilattu viḷinta uppiṉatu kuṉṟupōlum kuviyiṉi; mali uyttup pakarum niliyā vāḻkki - malināṭukaṭkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟpatāl ōriṭattum nilipeṟa  nillāta vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya; kaṇaṅkoḷ umaṇar uyaṅkuvayiṉ oḻitta paṇṇaḻi paḻampār - kūṭṭamākac cellum uppu vaṇikar tamatu paṇṭi muṟintataṉāl viṭṭoḻittamiyiṉ kaṭṭut taḷarnta paḻiya pārmarattiṉkaṇ; veṇkuruku īṉum - veḷḷiya kokku mutaliya nīrkkurukukaḷ kūṭamittu muṭṭiyiṭṭu vāḻum; taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ - taṇṇiya kaṭaṟkari nāṭṭut talimakaṉ; muṉ nāḷ - muṉpu orunāḷ; nammoṭu - nammoṭu kūṭi; pācaṭik kalitta kaṇikkāl neytal - paciya ilitaḻitta neytaṟ pūviṉ; neṟittaru toṭali pāvi tiiya - itaḻoṭittut toṭukkap paṭṭa māliyi vaṇṭaṟ pāvikkuc cūṭṭiya ceyal  vaki; kaṇṇaṟivuṭimi yallatu - kaṇṇāl nēril kaṇ ṭaṟintami yallatu; nuṇviṉi iḻiyaṇi makaḷir viḻaviṉ āṭum - nuṇṇiya toḻiṟpāṭu aminta iḻikaḷi aṇinta makaḷir viḻā nāḷil kūṭiyāṭum; iṉcīr muḻaṅku tiri tūṅkum - tuṇaṅkik kūttiṉ iṉiya tāḷattuk kēṟpa muḻaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭalalikaḷ olikkum; aḻuṅkal mūtūr aṟintaṉṟō iṉṟu - āravārattiyuṭiya immūtūr piṟitiyātum aṟintatu illiyē; aṟṟāka, iṉṟu ivvūr alar urittaṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? e-ṟu.
umaṇar oḻitta paḻampār, kuruku īṉum tuṟivaṉ, muṉṉāḷ nammoṭu kūṭi, neytal neṟittaru toṭali, pāvi cūṭṭi viḷiyāṭiyatik kaṇṇaṟivuṭimi yallatu, makaḷir āṭum iṉcīrkkut tiritūṅkum mūtūr piṟitu yātum aṟintaṉṟō iṉṟu; aṟṟāka, ivvūravar, iṉṟu alar urittaṟkuk kāraṇam yātākalām eṉa ēṟpaṉa peytu kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. uvarmaṇ mikka kaḷar nilatti uvar eṉṟār. kuppi, kuviyal, mali, ākupeyar, nāṭutōṟum ūr ūrāyc ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceyvatu paṟṟi, niliyā vāḻkkik kaṇaṅkoḷ umaṇar eṉṟār. palarāyk kūṭic cellum pāṉmiya rākaliṉ, kaṇaṅkoḷ umaṇar eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār. paṇṇaḻi pāri, paḻampār; iṭiyaṟā iyakkattāl taḷarn toḻitalāl paṇṇutal iḻantu paḻiyatākiya pārmaram. kaṭaṟkari maṇaṟpāṅkiṉkaṇ vīḻntu kiṭakkum pārmaram kokku mutaliya paṟavikaḷ kūṭamittu vāḻtaṟku iṭaṉātal iyalpu. uṭal muḻutum veṇṇiṟam peṟutaliṉ, kok kiṉatti veṇkuruku eṉṟār. "pacuṅkāl veṇkuruku1" eṉpar piṟarum. taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ, ammuc cāriyi al vaḻikkaṇ vantatu; "mellam pulampa"1 eṉṟāṟ pōla, neytaṟpūviṉ itaḻi oṭittut toṭukkappaṭuvatu paṟṟi neytal neṟittaru toṭali eṉṟār; "neṟi ceyta neytal"2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. pāvi, vaṇṭaṟ pāvi, tuṇaṅki, "paḻuppuṭi yiruki muṭakki yaṭikkat toṭakkiya naṭiyatu tuṇaṅkiyākum3". itaṉic ciṅkik kūttu eṉṟum kūṟupa. cīr, tāḷa vaki aṟṟāka eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam.
talimakaṭkum, talimakaṟkum uḷatāya toṭarpiṉi ūravar aṟintu alar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu ciṟippuṟat tāṉākiya talivaṉukkut terivikkum karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, attoṭarpu tōṉṟiya varalāṟṟik kuṟittaṟku nīrt tuṟikkaṇ paṇṭu pāvi vittu viḷiyāṭiya nikaḻcciyi eṭuttut taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ muṉṉāḷ nammoṭu pācaṭik kalitta kaṇikkāl neytal, neṟittaru toṭali pāvi tiiya eṉṟum, ataṉi ūravar aṟintu kōṭaṟ kēṟpa, aṉṟu nammoṭu āyamakaḷir palar cūḻa iruntaṉar eṉṟum, nīrāṭuvāṉ vanta piṟarum ataṉik kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟum kuṟittaṟkut taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ eṉṟum, pāvikkut toṭali tiiya kaṇṇaṟi vuṭimi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. atupaṟṟi itukāṟum alar kūṟāta ūravar. iṉṟu kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉi eṉa ārāyvāḷāyt tōḻi, iṭiyē nikaḻnta iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutalāya kaḷavu nikaḻccikaḷ ūravar aṟiyātaṉa eṉpāḷ, kaṇṇaṟi vuṭimi yallatu aḻuṅkal mūtūr aṟintaṉṟō iṉṟē eṉṟu iyampiṉāḷ.
ippoḻutu, kaḷaviṉkaṇ nikaḻnta maṟinta oḻukkam, ūravar eṭuttu alar tūṟṟum aḷaviṟkup pulappaṭṭu viṭṭamiyiṉ, virintu varintu kōṭalē ceyaṟpālatu eṉavē itaṉāl ciṟippuṟattu niṉṟa talimakaṉ kēṭṭut teruḷvāṉāvatu ikkūṟṟiṉpayaṉ eṉa vuṇarka. umaṇar oḻitta paḻampāriṉ paṇṇaḻivu kaṇṭu veṇkuruku muṭṭi yiṭṭu vāḻum eṉṟataṉāl, talimakaṉāl varivu nīṭṭikkap paṭṭa taliviyatu mēṉi vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu, ūravar alar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉa uḷḷuṟuttu urittamiyiṉ, ataṉit tōḻi veḷippaṭiyāka moḻintilaḷ eṉa aṟika. makaḷi rāṭum tuṇaṅki iṉcīrk kēṟpak kaṭalali olikkum eṉṟatu. varivu nikaḻuntuṇiyum ivvūr alarurikkum eṉṟavāṟu, eṉavē, ataṉ viḷivu tali makaṭku ēta mātali yuṇarntu varintu koḷka eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiya vāṟu.
139. peruṅ kōcikaṉār
peruṅkaṇṇaṉ, peruntēvaṉ, perumpatumaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla iccāṉṟōr peruṅkōcikaṉ eṉap peyar peṟṟavar. kōcikaṉ eṉpatu kōcikam eṉṉum tukil vaki neyyum toḻilic ceypavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷatu. tēvāṅku eṉṉum tukilvaki neyvōr tēvāṅkar eṉa iṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu. kōcikariṉattu mutalvaṉatu ciṟappup peyarāṉa kōcikaṉ eṉpatu piṉṉar avaṉ vaḻivantōrkku iyaṟpeyarāy aminta pōtu, iccāṉṟōr tammuṭiya peṟṟōrāl kōcikaṉ eṉak kuṟikkap peṟṟāreṉa uṇartal vēṇṭum. pāpilōṉiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta kiciyar nam nāṭṭu mēlik kaṭaṟkariyil kōcar eṉṟa peyarāṟ kuṭi pukuntu, paṭṭiṉum, paruttiyiṉum āṭi neytu vāḻnta pōtu, avar neyta tukilvaki kōcikam eṉappaṭṭa tākalāṉ, ik kōcikaṉār kōcar iṉattavarākalām eṉpar cilar. vēṟucilar, kucikaṉ eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip peyar kōcikaṉ eṉat tamiḻil tirintatu eṉpar. ic cāṉṟōr vāḻnta kālattil vaṭamoḻi eḻuttu vaḻakkiṉṟi oru cila riṭiyē caṅkēta moḻiyāy nilaviṉamiyiṉ, ēṉi makkaḷ vāyilum eḻuttilum parantu nilaviya tamiḻ vaḻakkiṟ kalappataṟku vāyppilli; "paṭiva vuṇṭip pārppaṉa makaṉē, eḻutāk kaṟpiṉ niṉ ceyal" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatoṉṟē vaṭa moḻi annāḷil eḻuttu vaḻakkiṉṟi iruntamikkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum. iṉṟu vaṭamoḻikku vaḻaṅkum tēvanākari yeḻuttu marāṭṭa moḻikkuriyavi eṉpar. nākariyiṉ vēṟākak kāṇappaṭum kirantavaki teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻi ṉiṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭup pāliyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupiṟa moḻikaḷi ṉiṉṟum eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉavē yaṉṟi vēṟalla eṉpati iṉṟiya makkaḷ naṉku uṇarvar. makkaḷuṭiya araciyal, vāṇikam, poruḷiyal mutaliya tuṟikaḷil paravi niṟkum āṅkilam pōṉṟa moḻiyē vēṟu moḻiyiṟ pukuntu tiritaṟku iyalumēyaṉṟi, makkaḷatu pēccu vaḻakkilum araciyal vāṇikam poruḷiyal mutaliya tuṟikaḷilum oru pōtum vaḻaṅkāmal, mikamikak kuṟukiya elliyil niṉṟa vaṭamoḻi piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ vaḷarccikkuc coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkip paravac ceytatu eṉpatu uṇmiyaṟivuṭiyōr koḷḷattakka taṉṟu. orucila  coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṟ kalakka vantatu kaṇṭa tolkāppiyaṉār, "vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi1 eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākummē" eṉṟu variyaṟi ceyvārāyiṉar. niṟka, ikkōcikaṉār pāṭiyaṉavākap pāṭṭukkaḷ ēṉit toki nūlkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭavilli.
illiruntu nallaṟam purintoḻukum talimakkaḷatu kaṟpu vāḻviṉkaṇ, maṇam puṇarnta ciṉṉāṭ kellām kaṭami kāraṇamākat taṉ kātaliyiṉ nīṅkik kārkāla varavil mīḷuva tākac collit talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟāṉ. ceṉṟavaṉ kuṟitta kālavelliyi nōkkit talimakaḷum pirivut tuṉpatti āṟṟit taṉakkuriya māṇpuṭiya aṟaṅkaḷic ceytu vantāḷ. kātalaṉum kuṟitta kāla velli piḻiyātu kār varumuṉṉē vantu cērntāṉ. maṉiyakam muḻutum maṅkalamāṭciyuṟṟatu, ūravarum nāṭṭavarum oruṅkē iṉitu vāḻntaṉar. kārkālam vantatu; vāṉameṅkum maḻi mēkam paraviyatu. kāṟṟum mikka kuḷircciyuṭaṉ vīcaluṟṟatu. pirintiruntu mīṇṭa talimakaṉ paḷḷiyiṭattē irukkiyil, nilaṉum kāṟṟum kuḷirap paruva maḻi naṉku peyvatāyiṟṟu. kātaliyiṉ kavavukki nekiḻāta kaviṉkaṇṭu pēriṉpamuṟṟa talimakaṉ kārmukili nōkki ṉāṉ; maḻi peyta mukil vāṉaḷāvi niṟkum malimukaṭu tōṟum taṅkip parantu iṉiya kāṭci vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. "mukilē, yāḻiṭattu eḻum paṭumalip paṇpōlum ici kalanta maḻiyiṉi nī uṭiyi; yām taḻitta kūntaliyuṭiya kātaliyoṭu kūṭi iṉitu irukkiṉṟōm. innallūrkku iravup pōtil naṉmaḻi poḻintu pērutaviyiṉic cey tuḷḷaṉi; ākalāṉ, ulakiṟku āṇiyāyp palarum toḻumāṟu niṉṟa kuṉṟukaḷiṉ mukaṭutōṟum virumpip parantu taṅkuvāyāka" eṉṟu aṉpuṭi naṉmoḻi vaḻaṅki iṉpuṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
avaṉatu ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, tāṉ pirintirunta kālattut taṉ varavu nōkki āṟṟiyirunta talimakaḷatu kaṟpunalattik kuṉṟu kaḷiṉ mukaṭutōṟum taṅkiya maḻimukiliṉ iruppi vāḻttu mukattāl pārāṭṭuvatum, iravuppeyal poḻintu toḻiṟ payaṉ peṟac ceyta mukiliṉ utaviyip pārāṭṭu mukattāl kātaliyiṉ kūntali aṇiyākap peṟṟu nukarnta iṉpattic ciṟappippatu mākiya kuṟippukkaḷ amintu kiṭantatu kaṇṭa peruṅkōcikaṉār, avi yaṉittum oruṅkē amiya ippāṭṭiṉip pāṭukiṉṟār. ippāṭṭum ēṭukaḷil citin tum piṟaḻntum uḷḷatu.
ulakiṟ kāṇi yākap palartoḻap
palavayiṉ niliiya kuṉṟiṟ kōṭutoṟum
1mēyiṇi uriiyarō peruṅkali eḻili
paṭumali niṉṟa nalyāḻ vaṭinaram
peḻīi yaṉṉa uṟiyiṉi 2yāmē
vaṇarntoli kūntal māa yōḷoṭu
puṇarntiṉitu3 nukarntaṉam āyiṉ 4muḻaviṉ
immeṉa imirum cāral nallūr
viravumalar utira vīci
iravuppeyal poḻinta utavi yōyē.
itu, talivaṉ viṉimuṟṟi vantu paḷḷiyiṭattāṉākap peyta maḻiyi vāḻttiyatu.
uri
ulakiṟku āṇiyāka - ulakiyalākiya tērkku accāṇiyāyp payaṉpaṭumāṟu; palartoḻa - yāvarum paravumāṟu; palavayiṉ niliiya kuṉṟiṉ kōṭutoṟum - palaviṭattum nilipeṟṟu niṟkum kuṉṟaṅkaḷiṉ mukaṭu tōṟum; mēyiṉi uriiyarō - virumpit taṅkip paravu vāyāka; peruṅkali eḻili - periya muḻakkattiyuṭiya maḻimukilē; paṭumali niṉṟa nalyāḻ vaṭinarampu eḻīi yaṉṉa - paṇṇuk kaminta nalla yāḻiṉatu vaṭitta narampi yicittu paṭumalip paṇṇi eḻuppiṉāṟ pōṉṟa; uṟiyiṉi - ōciyiṉiyuṭiya maḻiyip peyyāniṉṟaṉi; yām -; vaṇarntoli kūntal māayōḷoṭu - neṟittut taḻitta kūntaliyum māmi niṟattiyumuṭiya ivaḷoṭu; puṇarntu iṉitu nukarntami - kūṭiyiruntu peṟum iṉpanukarcciyiṉip peṟṟu makiḻkiṉṟēm; āyiṉ - ākavē; muḻaviṉ immeṉa imirum cāral nallūr - muḻavu muḻakkam pōl immeṉa muḻaṅkum maliccārali yaṭutta nallūrkkaṇ; viravumalar utira vīci - palavakiyāṉa pūkkaḷ tammil viravi yutirumāṟu kāṟṟu vīca; iravuppeyal poḻinta utaviyōy - irāmaḻiyip peytu ivvāṟu aḻivil kūṭṭatti utaviṉā yākalāṉ e-ṟu.
peruṅkali eḻili, uṟiyiṉi, yām nukarntaṉam; āyiṉ, nallūrkkaṇ malar utira vīci, iravup peyal poḻinta utaviyōy; ākalāṉ, kuṉṟiṟ kōṭutoṟum mēyiṉi uriiyarō eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. ulakiyal naṭattaṟku maḻi iṉṟiyamiyāmip paṟṟi ulakiya lākiya tērkku āṇi yeṉṟār. "uḻuvār ulakattārk kāṇi1" eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. maḻi vēṇṭiya makkaḷ maliyiṭattē kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu paṇṭi yōr marapākaliṉ palar toḻa eṉṟār; "malivāṉ koḷkeṉa vāṉpali tūuy, māri yāṉṟu maḻimēk kuyarkeṉak, kaṭavuṭ pēṇiya kuṟavar mākkaḷ"2 eṉpatu kāṇka, mikap paḻaṅkālattē kuṟiñci nilattē nilaviya maḻi vaḻipāṭu maruta nilattu intira viḻāvāka māṟiṟṟeṉak koḷka. palar toḻa mēyiṉi urii yarō eṉa iyiyum ōriṭattē yaṉṟip palaviṭattum niṟṟaliṉ palavayiṉ nili iya kuṉṟu eṉṟār.
uriiyar, paravuka eṉṉum poruṭṭāya viyaṅkōḷ muṟṟu viṉi kaṭaṉīr mukantu viṇṇiṭattē tāṉum uyarntu, tāṉ poḻiyum maḻinīrāl ulakiṉiyum uyarttum perumic ceyali viyantu maḻimukili, peruṅkali eḻili eṉac ciṟappikkiṉṟār. paṭumali, pālip paṇvaki ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu; avi yēḻum, cempāli, paṭumalippāli, cevvaḻippāli, arumpāli, kōṭippāli, viḷarippāli, mēṟcempāli, yeṉavarum, ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpic cilappatikāra arumpata vuri, aṭiyārkku nallār urikaḷiyum, nūṟkaruttiyum urikaḷiṉ karuttiyum pallāṇṭukaḷ ārāyntu kaṇṭa aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ eḻutiya yāḻ nūlil kāṇka. paṭumalip paṇṇik kuvāṇam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatuṇṭu; "paṭumali yeṉpatu pakariṟ kuvāṇam1" eṉṟu piṅkalanti nūlāciriyar kūṟuvar. "paṭumali niṉṟa payaṅkeḻu cīṟiyāḻ2" eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvatu kāṇka. vaṇartal, neṟittal; "maṇṇār maṇiyiṉ vaṇarkural vaṇṭārppa3" eṉṟa viṭattu vaṇartaṟku neṟittal eṉṟē parimēlaḻakarum urittaṉar; "vaṇarntoli yim pālāṉ4" eṉṟataṟkuk kaṭikuḻaṉṟu taḻikkiṉṟa aim pāliṉi yuṭiyāḷ eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urippar. iravup pōtil nilavum kuḷircci avviraviṟ peyyum maḻiyāl paṉmaṭaṅku mikutaliṉ, iravup peyaliyē uḻavar peritum virumpuvar. "irāmaḻi poḻinta īri yīram" eṉpatu taṉippāṭṭu, uḻavut toḻiṟkup pērutavi yātal nōkkiya iravup peyal poḻinta utavi eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār.
maṉikkaṇ ṇiruntu aṟampurintu vāḻum naṉmakkaṭku maḻi yilliyāyiṉ ulakiyal illi eṉpatu yāvarum naṉkaṟinta uṇmi yātal paṟṟi, ulakiṟku āṇiyāka eṉṟum, nāṭṭil ciṟappum pūcaṉiyum tāṉamum tavamum naṭipeṟutal vēṇṭi uyarntōr palarum maḻiyip paravuvarātaliṉ palar toḻa eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. "nīriṉ ṟamiyā tulaku eṉiṉ yāryārkkum, vāṉiṉ ṟamiyā toḻukku1" eṉṟum "ciṟappoṭu pūcaṉi cellātu vāṉam vaṟakkumēl vāṉōrkku mīṇṭu" "tāṉam tavam iraṇṭum taṅkā viyaṉulakam, vāṉam vaḻaṅkā teṉiṉ2" eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. kāṟṟāl alippuṇṭu nāṟṟiciyum parantuceṉṟu tēyntu iṟuvatu tavirttu malimukaṭukaḷil taṅkip parantiruppatu maḻimukiṟku naṉṟeṉpatu tōṉṟak kuṉṟiṟ kōṭu toṟum mēyiṉi yuriiyarō peruṅkali eḻili eṉṟāṉ. "peytu puṟan tantu poṅkalāṭi viṇṭuc cērnta veṇmaḻi pōlac ceṉṟaṟāviyarō3" eṉavum, "taṉṟoḻil vāytta iṉkura leḻili teṉpula maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟaṟṟāṅku4" eṉavum cāṉṟōr urippar. miṉṉum muḻakkamum iṉṟi maḻi niṉṟu peyyuṅkāl maṉivayiṉ iruppōrkku makiḻcciyum, ammaḻiyiṭat teḻum ōci yāḻici pōlum iṉimiyi yum nalkutalāl, paṭumali niṉṟa nalyāḻ vaṭinarampu eḻī iyaṉṉa uṟiyiṉi eṉap pārāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ; "iṭiyum muḻakku miṉṟip pāṇar, vaṭiyuṟu nalyāḻ narampicit  taṉṉa, iṉkural aḻituḷi talii5" eṉa āciriyar iṭik kāṭaṉār eṭuttōtuvatu kāṇka. vāṉam yāḻici pōlum maḻiyiṉip peytu ulaki makiḻvippatu pōla, yāmum em kātaliyik kūṭi iṉituṟiyum iṉpam peṟṟēm eṉpāṉ, yāmē vaṇarntoli kūntal māayō ḷoṭu puṇarntiṉitu nukarntaṉam eṉṟāṉ. maḻimukilē, nī uṟi peṟṟu iṉpuṟṟāy; yām mā ayōḷoṭu uṟital peṟṟu iṉpuṟṟēm eṉa makiḻntu kūṟiyavāṟu eṉka. iraviṭip peyyum maḻiyāl kuḷirmikutalāl kātalarkku muyakka viṉpam ciṟattaliṉ iravup peyal poḻinta utaviyōyeṉṟāṉ. utavi, ulakiṟkut toḻil utaval; kātalarkkuk kalaviṉpam utaval. itaṉāl talivi kēṭṭu makiḻvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
140. pūtaṅkaṇṇaṉār
ikkaṇṇaṉār, pūtaṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉappaṭutali nōkkiṉ ivar tanti pūtaṉ eṉappaṭuvar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. pūtaṉār eṉa nallicic cāṉṟōr niraluḷ oruvar uḷar; avarkku makaṉāyiṉ ivar pūtaṉārmakaṉār kaṇṇaṉār eṉappaṭuvar. pūtaṉṟēvaṉār, eṉṟa cāṉṟōr oruvar cāṉṟōr niraluḷ uḷar. avar orukāl ikkaṇṇaṉārkku uṭaṉ piṟantōrāka irukkalām. iruvar colnaṭiyum peritum ottu iyaluvatāl uṭaṉpiṟantōrākak kōṭal porutta mēyākum. inneṟiyē nōkkiṉ pūtampullaṉār eṉak kuṟun tokiyuḷ varum cāṉṟōrum ivarkku uṭaṉ piṟan tōrā kalām. ivar peyar cila ēṭukaḷil pūṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟa teṉpar; āyiṉum pūtaṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉpatu tāṉ pala ēṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭuvatu. ivar pāṭiyatāka ivvoru pāṭṭuttāṉ uḷatu.
kaṭipoḻiṟkaṇ malar koytu viḷiyāṭum viruppāl āyamakaḷir iṭiyē pōnta talimakaḷ, arumpaṟa malarnta vēṅki maramoṉṟu aḻakuṟa viḷaṅkak kaṇṭu, ataṉpāl taṉ karuttic celuttit taṉittu niṟkiyil, kaṭṭiḷamiyum kalaṅkā āṇmiyumuṭiya talimakaṉ avvaḻiyē vantavaṉ avaḷik kaṇṭāṉ; avaḷum avaṉik kaṇṭāḷ; iruvar uḷḷaṅkaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭaṉa. ciṉṉāḷil iruvarum kātala rāyiṉar, avviṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōkiyil, taṉ kaṇ pārviyālum piṟa kuṟippukkaḷālum taṉakkuriya uyirt tōḻiyit talimakaṟkuk kāṭṭi, avaḷatu tuṇimi peṟṟā laṉṟit tamatu uḷḷattil tōṉṟi iruvariyum, piṇikkum kātal veṟṟipeṟātu eṉpati naṉku uṇarttiṉāḷ. tali makaṉ talivi kāṭṭiya tōḻiyatu tuṇipeṟa vēṇṭi, avaḷum talimakaḷum taṉittēkum cevvi nōkki ceṉṟu, taṉ kātaṟkuṟipput tōṉṟa uriyāṭiṉāṉ; taliviyatu kuṟip puṇarāmiyāl, tōḻi avaṉi maṟuttu nīṅkuvāḷāyiṉāḷ. piṟitorukāl kiyuṟi cila koṇarntu, vaṇaṅkiya cāyalum paṇinta moḻiyum uṭiyaṉāy avaṟṟi ēṟkumāṟu vēṇṭi ṉāṉ. appoḻutum talimakaḷ kuṟippit tōḻi uṇarātu muṉpōl maṟuttaliyē ceytāḷ. ivvaṇṇam talimakaṉatu muyaṟci paṉmuṟiyum vīḻcciyuṟavē, avaṉatu uḷḷattil acivu piṟantatu. orunāḷ talimakaḷ cantaṉat taḻiyum vēṟupiṟa taḻikaḷum koṇṭu naṉku ularavittuc ceyta nuṇṇiya poṭi tēyttu mūḻkiya taṉ neṭiya kūntal nīrāṭiya pōtu kaḻintaṉa pōka eñciniṉṟu āṅkāṅku oṭṭik kiṭanta nuṇtukaḷ utirumāṟum kūntal pularumāṟum nuṉiyil muṭi yiṭṭut toṅkaviṭṭu, ēṉi āyamakaḷirum uyirttōḻiyum uṭaṉviḷiyāṭap pantāṭa lāṉāḷ. maṉiyiṉ muṟṟattē parappap peṟṟirunta putumaṇalil immakaḷir pantāṭukiyil, tāṉ uruṭṭiya pantiṉ vaḻiyē talimakaḷ ōṭi viḷiyāṭiyatu, kāṇpār. kaṇkaṭku iṉiya viruntuceytatu, avaḷ tāṉ āṭiya pantiṉmēl taṉ kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum celut tiṉaḷēyaṉṟi orupuṟattē niṉṟu avaḷip pārtta tali makaṉik kaṇṭāḷilli. atukaṇṭa avaṉatu neñcam, "talivikku niṉpāl aṉpilli; avaḷ niṉakku aruḷpava ḷākat tōṉṟavumilli; ākavē iṉi avaḷpiṉṉē ceṉṟu niṉ kātaṟ kuṟiyic colli irappatu payaṉpaṭātu;  im muyaṟciyik kiviṭuka" eṉa muṉintu kūṟaluṟṟatu, kaṭi poḻiviṉkaṇ kaṇṭa kāṭciyālum, vaḻinilik kāṭci yālum, uḷḷattē tōṉṟi vaḷarum kātal niliyiṉi naṉku aṟintava ṉākaliṉ, neñciṉ muṉivukku avaṉatu uḷḷam uṭaṉpaṭavilli, "pantiṉpiṉṉē cellutalāl aṉpilaḷ eṉa eṇṇaṟka; mēlum avaḷ namakku aruḷuva ḷāyiṉum aruḷāḷāyiṉum piṟṟinili muṉital perumi yaṉṟu; ēṉeṉil yāṉ uṟṟu varuntum kātaṟ piṇikku maruntu avaḷi yaṉṟi vēṟilli yākalāṉ" eṉṟu moḻintāṉ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaṉ taṉ uḷḷat teḻunta kātalit tōḻi aṟiyumāṟu ceytu avaḷatu naṭpip peṟa muyalum muyaṟciyiṉ arumiyum, arumiyuṭitteṉṟu acāvāta avaṉatu maṉam acitalum, avaṉatu taliminalam nōkkiya neñcam avaṉi irantu piṉṉiṟṟalukku muṉitalum,  avaṉatu peruntakimi nōkkiya uḷḷam paṇivu vaṟpuṟutti ammuyaṟciyik kiviṭāmik kāttalum orupāl viḷaṅka, orupāl talivi yuḷḷattil tōṉṟiya kātal peṇmik kuriya ellikkaṇ niṉṟu ciṟakkum poṟpum, puṟattē avaḷatu kātaṟkuṟippup pulappaṭat tōṉṟāvāṟu tāṅkiniṟkum poṟi yum tikaḻak kaṇṭa pūtaṅkaṇṇaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaṟṟi niṟuttip pāṭukiṉṟār.
koṇṭal māmaḻi kuṭakkērpu kuḻitta
ciṟukōl iṇara peruntaṇ cānta
vakicēr 1nuṇpoṭi takipeṟat timiri
2pulariṭat tutirtta tukaḷpaṭu kūḻip
3peruṅkaṇ āyam uvappat tanti
4neṭuntēr vaḻaṅkum5 nilavumaṇal muṟṟattup
pantoṭu peyarum parivi lāṭṭi
aruḷiṉum aruḷā6 ḷāyiṉum peritaḻintu
piṉṉili muṉiyaṉmā neñcē eṉṉatūum
aruntuyar avalam tīrkkum
maruntupiṟi tilliyāṉ uṟṟa nōykkē.
itu, kuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṉi neruṅkiyatu.
uri
koṇṭal māmaḻi kuṭakkērpu kuḻitta - kīḻkāṟṟuk koṇarnta maḻimukil maḻiyiṉip peytu mēṟṟici nōkki eḻuntu cēṟalāl taḻitta; ciṟukōl iṇara peruntaṇ cānta vakicēr nuṇpoṭi - ciṟukompukaḷil pūṅkottukkaḷit tāṅkum periya kuḷirnta cantaṉat taḻiyum vēṟu taḻi vakiyum cērttuc ceyta nuṇṇiya poṭiyi; takipeṟat timiri - aḻaku uṇṭākak kuḻittut tēyttu nīrāṭi; pular iṭattu utirtta tukaḷpaṭu kūḻi - pularnta viṭattut taṭṭi utirttalāl nuṇṇiya tukaḷ paṭintu tōṉṟum kūntali muṭittuḷḷa; peruṅkaṇ  āyam uvappa - periya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya āyamakaḷir kaṇṭu makiḻumāṟu; tanti neṭuntēr vaḻaṅkum nilavumaṇal muṟṟattu - tanti celuttum neṭiya tēr cellum veḷḷiya maṇal paranta muṟṟat tiṉkaṇ; pantoṭu peyarum parivilāṭṭi - tāṉ viḷiyāṭum pantiṉ piṉṉē ōṭu mukattāl nampāl aṉpillātavaḷākiya talimakaḷ; aruḷiṉum aruḷāḷāyiṉum - namakku aruḷ ceyyiṉum ceyyā toḻiyiṉum; peritu aḻintu - peritum varunti; piṉṉili muṉiyalmā - irantu piṉ niṟṟaṟkaṇ veṟup paṭiyā toḻika; neñcē -; eṉṉatūum - ettuṇiyum; aruntuyar avalam tīrkkum maruntu - nīkkutaṟkariya tuyarat tāl eṉ valiyaḻivu pōkkum maruntu; piṟitu illi - vēṟē yātum illi; yāṉ uṟṟa nōykku - yāṉ eyti varuntum kātal nōykku e-ṟu.
neñcē, cānta vakicēr nuṇpoṭi timiri, utirtta tukaḷ paṭu kūḻip peruṅkaṇ āyam uvappa, muṟṟattup pantoṭu peyarum parivilāṭṭi, aruḷiṉum aruḷāḷā yiṉum,  piṉṉili muṉiyal; nōykku avalam tīrkkum maruntu piṟitilli yākalāṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. koṇṭal māmaḻi, kīḻk kāṟṟuk koṇarum maḻimukil, vaṭakiḻakkiliruntu teṉmēṟku nōkki vīcum kāṟṟu koṇṭal eṉṟum, atu vīcum kālam tamiḻa kattukkuk kārkālamākaliṉ ataṉāṟ koṇarappaṭum maḻi mukil koṇṭal māmaḻi eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. "elliyum iravum eṉṉātu kalleṉak koṇṭal vāṉmaḻi poḻinta vikaṟi1" eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. maḻikuṉṟi veṟitu vīcik kuḷir ceyvatāl iv vaṭakīḻk kāṟṟu vāṭi eṉavum, vaṭanti eṉavum vaḻaṅkum, ērpu, eḻuntu cēṟal; iṇar kottu periya maramāka vaḷartaliṉ peruntaṇ cantaṉam eṉṟār. cantaṉat taḻi kuṟiñcittaḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ taḻikaḷik kaṟpāṟi kaḷiṉ mēl veyilil ulartti nuṇpoṭi yākki nīriṟ kuḻittu ney peyta taliyil tēyttu mūḻkiṉāl neyppaci nīṅkuvatu iṉṟum malināṭṭu makkaḷ vaḻakkil kāṇalām. cantaṉattōṭu vēṟu taḻikaḷ cērppatu paṟṟiyē cānta vaki cēr nuṇpoṭi eṉṟār. ippoṭiyit tēyttu nīrāṭuvatāl, talimayir tūymiyum naṟumaṇamum, vaḷarcciyum peṟutalāl takipeṟat timiri eṉṟār.  nīril mūḻki naṉku paṭintāṭiṉum nuṇpoṭikaḷ muṟṟavum nīṅkāmiyiṉ pularaviṭṭu utirttal marapātaliṉ, pular viṭat tutirtta tukaḷ eṉṟu kuṟittār. peruṅkaṇ, makaḷirkku ilakkaṇam, nilavumaṇal, nilavoḷi pōlum veṇmaṇal, piṉṉili, piṉṉē niṉṟu irantu vaḻipaṭal, muṉiyal, alīṟṟu etirmaṟi viyaṅkōḷ, 'mā', viyaṅkōḷ aciccol1 aruntuyar avalam, nīkkutaṟkariya tuyarattāl viḷiyum valiyaḻivu, attuyarukkuk kāraṇam nōyeṉak koḷka. nōyiṉ viḷivu, tuyaramum avalamum ātaliṉ iraṇṭiyum oruṅkē kūṟiṉār. kāmanōykku maruntu, kāmanukarcciyē ātal paṟṟi maruntu piṟitilli eṉṟār. "piṇikku maruntu piṟamaṉ aṇiyiḻi taṉnōykkut tāṉē maruntu2" eṉpar teyvap pulavar.
iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṟa mūṉṟu niliyiṉum talimakaḷit talippeytu kātaliṉpam kiyuṟap peṟṟa talimakaṉatu neñcam, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattāl talimakaḷatu kātali vaṟpuṟutti maṇantu koḷḷaṟku oruppaṭṭu niṉṟamiyiṉ talimakaḷāṟ cuṭṭik kāṭṭappaṭṭa tōḻikkum, taṉakkum, talimakaṭkum uḷa tākiya kātaṟṟoṭarpi aṟivuṟuttaṟku ēṟṟa naṭpiṉi mutaṟkaṇ ceytu koḷvāṉ vēṇṭi. talimakaḷ viḷi yāṭum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟavaṉ, iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattum taṉṉi etir nōkki niṉṟāṟ pōlātu, tāṉ uruṭṭiyāṭum pantiṉvaḻi niṉṟu taṉṉi nōkkāmi kaṇṭu pantoṭu peyarum parivilāṭṭi eṉṟāṉ. talimakaṉ varavum vaḻipāṭum tōḻiyiṉ karutti īrttu avaṉ vēṇṭiya naṭpuc ceytaṟkup pōtiya iṭam nalkāmiyiṉ, tali makaḷ avaṉatu varavu uṇarntum uṇarātāḷ pōṉṟu pantuvaḻic celvāḷāyiṉāḷ. avaḷatu ceyal vakiyi ārāynta aṟiñar, "ceṟāac ciṟu collum ceṟṟārpōl nōkkum, uṟāar pōṉṟuṟṟār kuṟippu1" eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. āyamakaḷir kaṇṭu uvakkumāṟu, kūntal kāṟṟil paṟakkap panteṟintu āṭuvatu, taṉṉik kaṇṭa vaḻik kiḷarntu avaṉ uḷḷat teḻum kātali ammakaḷir kaṇṭu uṇarāmip poruṭṭu, "peruṅkaṇ āyam uvappat tanti muṟṟattup pantoṭu peyarum eṉṟaviṭattup peruṅkaṇ āyam eṉṟatu, taṉ kātaṟkuṟippi avarkaḷ kaṇṭuṇarva reṉat talimakaḷ añciṉami uṇarttiṟṟu. makaḷiratu uvakiyiyum panti yumē nōkkit taṉṉi nōkkāmi kaṇṭu parivilāṭṭi eṉṟāṉ. inniṉivukaḷāl talimakaḷ orukāl taṉṉi aruḷvaḷ eṉṟum, orukāl taṉṉi aruḷāḷ eṉṟum māṟimāṟi alamarutalāl aruḷiṉum aruḷāḷāyiṉum eṉṟum, aruḷuva ḷāyiṉ avaṉ vaḻi niṟkum tōḻi taṉ kuṟiyuṟavu uṇarntu āvaṉa ceyvaḷ; tōḻi, atu ceyyā miyiṉ aruḷāḷ pōlum eṉa niṉintavaḻi, neñcam peritum taḷarntu īṇṭu ivaḷik kuṟiyirantu niṟṟaliṟ payaṉiṉṟeṉa vaṟpuṟuttavum, ataṉit teruṭṭuvāṉāy, "muṉivillār muṉṉiyatu eytāmi il2" eṉṟāṟpōla, neñcē peritu aḻintu muṉiyaṉmā eṉṟāṉ. maruntu piṟitāyiṉ ivaḷi muṉital takum; nōy ceyta ivaḷē maruntumātaliṉ muṉital kūṭātu eṉpāṉ. maruntu piṟi tilli yāṉ uṟṟa nōykkē eṉṟum, uṭalaḷavāy niṟkum piṟa nōykaḷ pōlātu, uyirkkut tuyarattiyum uṭalukku avalattiyum ceyvatu innōy eṉpāṉ aruntuyar avalam eṉṟum kūṟit tēṟukiṉṟāṉ. kuḻitta cāntam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ taḻikaḷi vāṭṭi nuṇpoṭi ceytu takipeṟak kūntalil timirvar eṉṟataṉāl, nammi ivaḷ vāṭṭi varuttiyallatu iṉpuṟa aruḷāḷ eṉak kuṟippāl neñcu muṉiyāmikku ētuk kūṟiya nayam aṟika. ivvāciriyaruṭiya uṭaṉpiṟantōrāṉa pūtaṉ tēvaṉārum, "peruntōḷ kuṟumakaḷ allatu maruntu piṟitilli yāṉ uṟṟa nōykkē3 eṉṟu kūṟuvatu īṇṭu oppunōkkat takkatu. itaṉāṟ payaṉ tali makaṉ taṉ āṟṟāmiyi neñcoṭu kūṟi āṟṟik koḷvatu.
141. aricilaṅ kumaraṉār
aricil eṉpatu kāviriyiṉ kiḷiyāṉa aricilāṟu kāviri yiṉiṉṟum piriyumiṭattē kuṭantikku arukēyiruntu maṟintu pōṉa ūr1.  aricil kiḻār eṉṉum cāṉṟōrum iv vūravarē, ikkumaraṉāric cilliyaṅ kumaraṉār eṉat tiru. viyāpurip piḷḷiyiṉ kiyeḻuttup paṭi kūṟa, accuppaṭi calliyaṅ kumaraṉār eṉat tiruttik kāṭṭap putuppaṭṭiyēṭu ivar peyari aricilaṅ kumaraṉā reṉak kuṟittami koṇṭu mēlē kuṟitta uṇmi vaṭivu uyttuṇarappaṭṭatu. ik kumaraṉār, cōḻa nāṭṭuk kiḷḷiyiyum aricilāṟṟiyum ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ eṭuttup pāṭuvatu ivar ariciṟ kumaraṉār eṉappaṭuvati vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. ivar pāṭiyatāka ivvoru pāṭṭoḻiya vēṟu kāṇappaṭavilli.
paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaḷ vāḻvil tattam vāḻkkikkut tuṇiyit tāmē tērntu koḷḷum urimi makkaḷiṭamē iruntatu. āṭavar maṉiviyiyum makaḷir kaṇavaṉiyum tāmē tērntu koṇṭaṉar. tiruvaḷḷuvar, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kūṭi naṭattum ilvāḻkkikku uriya aṟaṅkaḷi urikkap puku miṭattu maṉiviyi vāḻkkit tuṇi yeṉṟu kuṟippatē itaṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟu. makaḷir āṭavar pōlap puṟattē taṉittuc ceṉṟu poruḷ viṉikaḷic ceyyum vaṉmiyuṭiya rallarākaliṉ, avaruṭiya tirumaṇattil māttiram peṟ ṟōrkkup poṟuppuc ciṟitu mikuntiruntatu. makaḷir, tamatu vāḻkkit tuṇiyākat tērntu koḷḷum āṭavaṉuṭiya kuṭinalam āḷviṉi nalam mutaliya kūṟukaḷil, poyyum vaḻuvum pukāta vaṇṇam ārāyntu kāṇpatil tavaṟuvarātaliṉ, appoṟuppu peṟṟōrkaḷiṭam iruntatu. makaḷir aṟattoṭu niṉṟa vaḻiyum, koṇṭutalik kaḻinta vaḻiyum, peṟṟōr tam makaḷir karuttu vaḻiyē ceṉṟoḻivar. iṉṉōraṉṉa urimik kūṟukaḷāl, poruṭṭuṟiyil peṟṟōr īṭṭiya poruḷi makkaḷ peṟuvatil iṉṟiya muṟimiyil annāḷiya tamiḻ makkaḷ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭiruntaṉar. peṟṟōr īṭṭiya poruḷ koṇṭu makkaḷ tamatu vāḻkkiyi naṭattuvatu ciṟappu aṉṟu; avaravarum tamatu vāḻkkikkuriya tuṇivarit tēṭik koḷvatu pōlat tuṇiyākiya poruḷiyum tēṭik koṇṭu vāḻvatē ciṟanta aṟamum kaṭamiyumākum; peṟṟōr īṭṭiya poruḷ tām īṭṭiyatu aṉmiyiṉ, maṉiyaṟam puriyum makkaḷ ataṉit "tamatu" eṉak karutuva tilli. tām aritiṉ muyaṉṟu īṭṭiya poruḷiyē, "tamatu" eṉak koṇṭu pāttuṇṭal mutaliya aṟaṅkaḷic cey toḻukiṉar. peṟṟōrkkup piṉṉar avar poruḷ makkaḷic cērtalē muṟi. āyiṉum, paḻantamiḻar, tam peṟṟōr īṭṭiya poruḷit tam vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum makkaḷukkē urimi ceytu, tam peṟṟōr peyariyum tammakkaṭku iṭṭu makiḻntaṉar. ataṉālēyē, tam vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum putalvaṉit tanti peyaraṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar.
itu niṟka, maṉiyaṟam pēṇum makkaḷiṭiyē āṭavaṉ poruḷ kuṟittum viṉikuṟittum maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic celvatu marapu. orukāl, talimakaṉ poruḷ vēṇṭit taṉ maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic cella vēṇṭiya kaṭami yuṭiya ṉāṉāṉ. putumaṇat tōṉṟalātaliṉ, avaṉ uḷḷatti maṉiviyiṉ pirivarumi taṭukkaluṟṟatu. āṉāl, taṭiyuṇṭu maṭaṅkutal āṇmi yaṉṟu.
makkaḷ uyirkku aṟivu, viḻivu, ceyal, eṉṟa mūvakic ceyaṟ paṇpukaḷ uṇṭu. ivi, teḷivu ,kalakkam, mayakkam, eṉṟa mūṉṟu nilikaḷil iyakkamuṟum. avvakiyil aṟiviṉ kaṇ, teḷivum, kalakkamum, mayakkamum, uḷavām. ivvāṟē viḻivu ceyalkaḷum iyaṅkum. uyirp paṇpukaḷiṉ iyakkat tukkuk karuviyāvatu uṭampu; avvuṭampiṉ iyalpukku ēṟpat teḷivu mutaliya nilikaḷ uṇṭākum. uṭalvaṉmiyum maṉattiṇmiyum cevvē vāynta vaḻit teḷivu nili peṟutalāl aṟivum, viḻivum, ceyalum, naṉku amintu makkaḷit talimi, māṇpu uṭiyarākat tikaḻvikkum. attalimi māṇpuṭiya āṭavaṉ uḷḷattil, aṟivu teḷivuṟṟu mēṉiṟkum pōtu, kātaliṉpa vāḻvukkuk kaṭami yiṉ ciṟapput tōṉṟip poruṭpirivukkut tuṇivu piṟap pikkum; viḻivu mēṉiṟkum pōtu avaṉ kātaliṉpap pēṟṟil karuttic celuttuvaṉ. kalakka niliyil kātaliyum, kaṭamiyiyum māṟimāṟi niṉintu mayaṅkuvaṉ. poruḷ kuṟittup piriyak karutum talivaṉ, teḷiniliyil kalakkamum mayakkamum iṉṟi iruppaṉ; anniliyil, avaṉ taṉ pirivit talimakaṭkup piyak kuṟippikka, avaḷ āṟṟāḷāvatu uṇarntu, avaḷi oruppaṭutta vēṇṭip pirivi virum pātāṉ pōlat taṉ neñciṉi muṉṉē niṟutti ataṉōṭu collāṭu vāṉāyt taliviyiṉ cevippulaṉāmāṟu cila urikkiṉṟāṉ. yāṉikaḷāl timirappaṭṭa aṭiyiyuṭiya koṉṟi maraṅkaḷ caṭimuṭitta tāpatar pōlat tōṉṟum, aruñ curattik kaṭantu cēṟaṟku añcukiṉṟē ṉilli; avi peritum eḷiyavi; āṉāl, cōḻaṉāṉa kiḷḷikku uriya ampar eṉṉum ūric cūḻntōṭum aricilāṟṟiṉ  karumaṇal pōṉṟa kūntaliyuṭiya kātaliyākiya ivaḷi viṭṭu nīṅka killēṉ; ākaliṉ, neñcē, nī māttiram ceṉṟu varuka eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaṉatu kaṭami yuṇarviṉmuṉ kaṭumi mikka curattiṉ celavu eḷitākat tōṉṟuvatum, eṉiṉum, talivi pāṟ ceṉṟa kātaluṇarvu viñci niṟpatum, neñciṉic ceṉṟu varuka eṉpatāl, kaṭamivaḻi niṉṟu muṭivil pirintēkutal talivaṉ ceyalāvatum amivatu kaṇṭa aricilaṅ kumaraṉār ippāṭṭiṉip pāṭukiṉṟār.
iruñcē 1ṟāṭiya koṭuṅkavuṭ kayavāy
māri yāṉiyiṉ maruṅku2 tīṇṭip
poriyari ñemirnta puḻaṟkāyk koṉṟi
nīṭiya caṭiyō ṭāṭā mēṉik
kuṉṟuṟi tāpatar3 pōlap palavuṭaṉ
eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭip poṟpat tōṉṟum
aruñcuram eḷiyamaṉ neñcē1 cerumāṇ
vēntaroṭu2 poruta palpiṇart3 taṭakki
ēntukōṭ ṭiyāṉi iciveṅ kiḷḷi
vampaṇi yuyarkoṭi ampar cūḻnta
aricilan teḷḷaṟal aṉṉivaḷ
viriyoli4 kūntal viṭṭami kalaṉē.
itu, poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭiya neñciṟkut talivaṉ collic celavaḻuṅkiyatu.
uri
iruñcēṟu āṭiya koṭuṅkavuḷ kayavāy māri yāṉi yiṉ - kariya cēṟṟip pūcikkoṇṭa vaḷinta kavuḷiyum periya vāyiyu muṭiya maḻiyil naṉinta yāṉiyiṉatu; maruṅku tīṇṭi - pakkam urāytalāl; pori yari ñemirnta - porintatu pōṉṟa aṭimarattiṉ paṭṭi tēynta; puḻaṟ kāyk koṉṟi - uḷḷē puḻi poruntiya kāykaḷi yuṭiya koṉṟi maram; nīṭiya caṭiyoṭu āṭā mēṉi - nīṇṭa caṭiyum nīrāṭāta mēṉiyu muṭiya; kuṉṟuṟi tāpatar pōla - kuṉṟukaḷil vāḻum tavattōrkaḷip pōla; palavuṭaṉ - palavāy; eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭi poṟpat tōṉṟum - veyil paranta neṭiya iṭiveḷi aḻakuṟat tōṉṟum; aruñcuram eḷiyamaṉ - ariya kāṭṭuvaḻikaḷ kaṭantu cēṟaṟkup peritum eḷiyaṉavē; neñcē-; cerumāṇ vēntaroṭu poruta - ceruvil mēmpaṭṭa vēntar pala ruṭaṉ pōr ceytu ciṟanta; palpiṇart taṭakki ēntu kōṭṭu yāṉi - palavāya caruccarikaḷiyuṭiya periya kiyiyum uyarnta kōṭukaḷiyumuṭiya yāṉip paṭiyi yuṭiya; ici veṅkiḷḷi - pukaḻē virumpum cōḻaṉāṉa kiḷḷik kuriya; vampu aṇi uyarkoṭi - putitu puṉintu niṟuttap peṟṟa aḻakiya uyarnta koṭi niṉṟu viḷaṅkum; ampar cūḻnta aricil am teḷḷaṟal aṉṉa - ampar nakarattic cuṟṟiyōṭum aricilāṟṟiṉ teḷḷiya karu maṇali yotta; ivaḷ viriyoli kūntal - ivaḷatu maṇamūṭṭappeṟṟa taḻitta kūntaliṟ kiṭantu peṟum iṉ patti; viṭṭu amikalaṉ - viṭṭu nīṅkiyiruttali yāṉ āṟṟē ṉākaliṉ, nīyē ceṉṟu varuka e-ṟu.
neñcē, koṉṟi tāpatar pōlat tōṉṟum aruñ curam eḷiyamaṉ; kiḷḷiyiṉ ampar cūḻnta aricilan teḷ ḷaṟal aṉṉa ivaḷ kūntal viṭṭu amikala ṉākaliṉ, nīyē ceṉṟu varuka eṉa eñcuvaṉa peytu kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. iruñcēṟu, kariya cēṟu; "iruñcēṟ ṟakal vayal1" eṉṟāṟpōla, koṭuṅkavuḷ eṉpuḻik koṭumi vaḷivu, kaya, perumi, māriyāṉi, maḻiyil naṉintu cēṟupaṭṭu ularnta yāṉi; "māri yāṉiyiṉ vantu niṉṟaṉaṉē2" eṉpatu kāṇka. mēṉiyiṟpaṭṭu ularnta cēṟṟi utirttaṟkuk koṉṟi marattiṉ poriyariyil timirtalāl porukkaminta paṭṭi tēyntami viḷaṅka, maruṅku tīṇṭip poriyari ñemirnta koṉṟi eṉṟār. ñemirtal urāytal; timirtal eṉṟumām. "timira māvuṭaṟ kuṅkumac cētakam, timira māvoṭum cantoṭum tēykkumāl3" eṉṟu kamparum kūṟutal kāṇka. puḻaṟkāy, uḷḷē puḻi poruntiya kāy, koṉṟikkāyik koṇṭu kuḻalceytu icittal paṇṭiyōr marapu. "kōvalar koṉṟiyan tīṅkuḻal maṉṟutō ṟiyampa4" eṉac cāṉṟōr urippataṉālum aṟika. nīḷa vaḷarntuḷḷami paṟṟi nīṭiya caṭi eṉavum, uṇṇāmi nīrāṭāmi mutaliya ceyalkaḷ tavattōrkku uriyavātaliṉ, āṭā mēṉi eṉavum kūṟiṉar. āṭā mēṉi kūṟiṉār. poruḷākiya koṉṟi, māriyāṉi maruṅku tīṇṭi urāytalāl maṇ mācu paṭintiruppatupaṟṟi, eṉṟūḻ, veyil, eḷiyamaṉ eṉpuḻi maṉṉic col ākkam kuṟittu niṉṟatu. pōriṉ kaṇ veṟṟiyāl māṭciyuṟātāri vēṟal vīṟākāmiyiṉ, cerumāṇ vēntaroṭu poruta eṉṟum, paṉimarattil tōṉṟum varivariyāka uḷḷa caruccarikaḷ pōla, yāṉiyiṉ kiyilum avi kāṇappaṭutalāl pal piṇart taṭakki eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. pukaḻeṉiṉ uyirum koṭukkum poṟpuṭi maṟavēntaṉ eṉṟaṟku, iciveṅkiḷḷi eṉṟār. kiḷḷi, cōḻaṉatu peyar. kiḷḷi eṉṟa peyaruṭiya cōḻamaṉṉar palar iruntiruttaliṉ, ikkiḷḷi iṉṉāṉ eṉpatu viḷaṅkavilli. vampu, putumi, ampar, cōḻa nāṭṭil iṉṟum uḷḷatōr pērūr, aricil, aricilāṟu, ampar cūḻnta aricil eṉṟāṟ pōla ñāṉacampantarum "aricilam porupuṉal ampar mānakar" eṉpar. ākalāṉ 'nīyē ceṉṟu varuka' eṉpatu kuṟippeccam.
iḷamiyuḷḷattip paṟṟik kāmaviṉpattukku eḷi yaṉākkum vakiyil viñci niṟkum kātalit taṉ vari niṟuttātu melivuṟṟu ataṉvaḻi niṉṟu poruṭ pirivuk kuriya aruñcuram cēṟaṟku talimakaṉ añcukiṉṟāṉ pōlum eṉṉum kuṟiyuṇṭākātavāṟu taṉṉip pēṇu kiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, aruñcuram cēṟal eḷiyamaṉ eṉṟum, eḷiya ceyalit tāṉ mēṟkoḷḷātu, neñciṉi mēṟkoṇṭu celka eṉak kuṟippāl urikkumāṟu tōṉṟa, neñcē eṉṟum, tāṉ celavu mēṟkoḷḷāmikku ētu aruñcurac celaviṉum talimakaḷi āṟṟuvittal ariya ceyal eṉṟum, ariyatu ceytalē talimikku māṇpeṉṟum urippāṉ, ivaḷ virimeṉ kūntal viṭṭu amikalaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ pirivi āṟṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpati, itaṉik kēṭkum talimakaḷ uyttuṇarntu koḷḷumāṟu kuṟippāy urikkiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, veḷippaṭiyil ivaḷ kūntaliviṭṭu ami kalaṉ eṉṟāṉ. iruñ cēṟāṭiya yāṉi tīṇṭi ñemirntatu poṟutta poriyarik koṉṟi, puḻaṟkāy tāṅki eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭip poṟpuṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟataṉāl iṉmiyatu iḷivu eytāmi nīṅkum poruṭṭu nikaḻttum taṉ piri vāṟṟiya vaḻi vāḻkki iṉpamum pukaḻum eyti ilaṅkum eṉak kuṟippāl talimakaṉ talimakaṭku uṇarttiyavāṟu aṟika.
142. iṭikkāṭaṉār
naṟṟiṇiyurikārarāṉa piṉṉattūr tiru. a. nārāyaṇacāmi aiyarum, ṭākṭar tiru. u.vē. cāmināta ayyarum, iṭikkāṭu eṉṉum ūriṉa rātalpaṟṟi iccāṉṟōr iṭikkāṭaṉār eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟār eṉṟaṉar. veṇkāṭu, maṟikkāṭu, ārkkāṭu eṉṟa ūrkaḷic cērntavarkaḷi veṇkāṭṭāṉ maṟikkāṭṭāṉ, ārkkāṭṭāṉ eṉpatu peruvaḻakkēyaṉṟi, veṇkāṭaṉ,maṟikkāṭaṉ, āṟkāṭaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatilli. veṇkāṭaṉ, eṉpatākilum civat tirumuṟikaḷil tiru veṇkāṭṭu iṟivaṉik kuṟittu niṉṟu piṉpu iṟivaṉ peyari makkaṭku iṭṭu vaḻaṅkum marapu perukiya pōtu, makkaṭpeyarāy varuvatāyiṟṟu. ēṉiyavi avvāṟu vara villi; ataṉāl, iṭikkāṭṭār eṉṟa poruḷil iṭik kāṭaṉār eṉṟa peyar vantateṉak kōṭal cevvitākat tōṉṟukiṉṟilatu.
iṉi, kāṭaṉ eṉpatu iccāṉṟōratu iyaṟpeyar eṉṟum, iṭi eṉpatu iṭiyar iṉattik kuṟippa teṉṟum aka nāṉūṟṟu urikārarāṉa tiru. vēṅkaṭācalampiḷḷi kūṟuvar. iṭiyarāyiṉ, iṭiyaṉ cēntaṉ koṟṟaṉār, eṉavum iṭiyaṉ neṭuṅkīraṉār eṉavum, ivar variciyuḷ niṟkum nallicic cāṉṟōr kuṟikkappaṭuvatu pōla, ivarum iṭiyaṉ kāṭaṉār eṉṟu peyar kuṟikkappaṭa vēṇṭum; avvāṟu eṅkum kāṇavilli.
iṭi eṉṟatu kāṭaṉāratu ūrik kuṟippatākak koṇṭāl taṭi yētum irātu, iṭikkuṭi1 eṉavum, iṭiyūr2 eṉavum ūrkaḷ pala paṇṭināḷil iruntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ivaratu ūrātalāl, ivarip paṇṭiyōr iṭikkāṭaṉār eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉār. iṭikkāṭṭūr3 eṉṟōr ūr uṇṭu; āyiṉum avvūravar iṭikkāṭaṉār eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭār eṉa uṇarka. piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya tiru vālavāyt tiruviḷiyāṭaṟpurāṇam "piṉṉamil kapilaṉ tōḻaṉ peyar iṭikkāṭaṉ eṉpāṉ" eṉṟu kuṟippatum īṇṭu nōkkattakkatu. ūcimuṟi yeṉṉum ceyyuḷvakiyip pāṭiyavar iṭikkāṭaṉār eṉavum, avarum ivarum oruvarē eṉavum karutupava ruṇṭu; avarkaḷ ivviruvarum kālattāl vēṟupaṭṭavar eṉpatu niṉittilar.
ippāṭṭip pāṭiya iṭikkāṭaṉār kālattil, cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ cōḻanāṭṭi āṇṭu vantāṉ. avaṉ pakivarkku accam tarum aṭal mikka aracaṉāy viḷaṅkutaṟku avaṉatu paṭiyiṉ perumiyeṉak kāṭṭi, aḵtu avaṉ kuṭicciṟappu eṉṟum, avvakiyil avaṉatu āṟṟal, "puri tīrntaṉṟu; atu putuvatō aṉṟu" eṉa urippar. avaṉuṭiya āṭci nalattāl, nāṭu "taṇpuṉaṟ pūcal allatu nontu kaḷika vāḻi vaḷava eṉṟu niṉ muṉitaru  pūcal kaṉaviṉum aṟiyātu" eṉṟum, ataṉāl atu, "puli puṟaṅkākkum kuruḷipōla" viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟār. avaṉatu nāṭṭavaruḷ, kaḷamar vāḷi mīṉiyum, uḻavar yāmi iṟicciyiyum, karumpaṟuppavar karumpiṉ tēṉum, makaḷir kuvaḷiyum tantu viruntiṉaric ciṟappippar. avaṉ tamiḻ vaḷarkkum cālpu niṟin tiruntati, "maliyiṉ iḻintu māk kaṭal nōkki, nilavari iḻitarum palyāṟu pōlap pulava rellām niṉṉōkkiṉarē" eṉpataṉāl ciṟappittuk kāṭṭu kiṉṟār.
ivaratu pulami nalatti viḷakkum pāṭṭukkaḷ palavum mullik kāṭṭiṉ iyalpi eṭuttup pakarkiṉṟaṉa. mullik kāṭu cemmaṇṇilamāka uḷḷatu; aṅkē niṟkum maraṅkaḷil pūttu viḷaṅkum pala niṟap pūkkaḷil nīla niṟamuṭiya pūkkaḷ kāṭaṉāratu pulamik kaṇṇukku iṉpam ceykiṉṟaṉa. cem maṇ parappil nīlamaṇi pōlap pūkkaḷ vīḻntu kiṭappati maṇimiṭip pavaḷam eṉap puṉintu kūṟuvārāy, maṭappiṇi iraliyoṭu kūṭik kurunta marattiṉ niḻalil vativatum, vaṇṭu pāṭuvatum, piṭavam malarvatum mayilāṭuvatum ceyya, "vari maṇal maṇimiṭip pavaḷam pōla aṇi mikak kāyāñ cemmal tāayp palavuṭaṉ īyal mūtāy īrmpuṟam varippap, pulaṉaṇi koṇṭa kāretir kāli" eṉṟum, "ceṟimaṇal nivanta kaḷartōṉṟu iyavil, kuṟumōṭṭu mūtāy kuṟukuṟu ōṭi, maṇimaṇṭu pavaḷam pōlak kāyā aṇimiku cemmal oḷippaṉa maṟiyak kārkaviṉ koṇṭa kāmar kāli" eṉṟum ivar kār kālak kāṭciyivittu iṉimiyuṟa eṭuttōtukiṉṟār. kāṭukaḷil mayilkaḷ tōkiyi virittāṭum kāṭci, makaḷir āṭuvatu pōlum amiti koṇṭu tōṉṟuvati, "tōki, kāmar kalavam parappi ēmuṟak, kolli yuḻavar koḻuniḻa loḻitta vallirik kuruntiṉ vāṅkuciṉi yiruntu kiḷikaṭi makaḷiriṉ viḷipaṭap peyarum" eṉpatu ivaratu collōviyam. "āṭutalit turuviṉ tōṭu ēmārppak kaṭikōṟ ciṟutī aṭiya māṭṭit, tiṇkāl uṟiyaṉ pāṉiyaṉ ataḷaṉ, nuṇpal tuvali yorutiṟam naṉippat taṇṭukā lūṉṟiya taṉinili iṭiyaṉ" eṉa iṭiyaṉatu iyaṟki nalatti iṉpuṟa icippatu kaṇṭu pōlum. cāṉṟōr cilar ivari iṭiyar iṉattiṟ cērttaṉar. ivar pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ pala ēṉit toki nūlkaḷilum uṇṭu.
maṇaviṉi muṭittu maṉiyaṟam purintu varum talimakaṉ, kaṭami kāraṇamākat tōṉṟiya viṉiyoṉṟi muṭittaṟku orukāl taliviyip pirintu ceṉṟāṉ. viṉimēṟ ceṉṟatu, talimakkaḷ vāḻkkikkup pukaḻum iṉpamum nalkum poṟ puṭimikaṇṭu, avaṉ kuṟitta kālam nōkkip pirivāṟṟi yiruntāḷ talimakaḷ. viṉimēṟ cellum āṭavarkku, viṉinikaḻum kālattu, uḷḷam pirintuṟiyum kātalipāṟ celvatilli. uḷḷam muṟṟum, viṉi naṉku vāyttuc ceppamuṟa muṭiyuṅkāṟum ataṟkēṟṟa ceyal muṟikaḷic cūḻnta vaṇṇam iruttaliṉ, maṟintu kiṭakkum kātaluṇarvu, viṉi muṭiviṉkaṇ civveṉa eḻuntu avaṉik kātalipāl virintu cella muṭukum. "kiḻavi niliyē viṉiyiṭattu uriyār; veṉṟik kālattu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum" eṉa āciriyarum kūṟutal kāṇka. mēṟkoṇṭa viṉi muṭintatum talimakaṟkut taṉ maṉivi pāl niṉivuṇṭāyiṟṟu. maṉi nōkki mīḷaluṟṟavaṉ taṉ tērip paṇṇac ceytu pākaṉi nōkkit taṉ kātaliyiṉ iyalpik kūṟuvāṉāyk "kātali yāvāḷ, maṉiyiṉkaṇ iravum pakalum eppōtum varu viruntu nōkki ōmputalil peru viruppa muṭiyaḷ;  yām avaḷiṟ piriyuṅkāl vaṟpuṟutta paruvam nōkki nammi niṉintu avaḷ āṟṟiyirukkum peruṅ kaṟpiṉaḷ; avaḷ uṟiyum ūr, tammi mēykkum iṭiyaṉ taṇṭukāl vitta taṉi niliyuṟṟu orupāl maḻittuḷi naṉippa niṉṟu, vāyitaḻi maṭittu eḻuppum viḷikkuralik kēṭkum āṭṭiṉam pātu kāppuṟṟami eṇṇi avaṉic cūḻntu taṅkum kāṭṭiṭatta uḷatu kāṇ" eṉṟu collukiṟāṉ.
talimakaṉuṭiya kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, mēṟkoṇṭa viṉi muṭiyuṅkāṟum kātaliyiṉ kātali niṉiyātirunta taliva ṉuṭiya kaṭṭāṇmiyuḷḷam, viṉimuṭintaviṭattu ataṉi niṉintu iṉpuṟuvatum, avviṉpam tāṉum avaḷ taṉ maṉikkaṇṇē iruntu ceyyum viruntiṉmēl niṟpatum, avaḷi virintu ceṉṟu aṭiya vēṇṭumeṉa eḻunta vēṭkiyi veḷippaṭa moḻiyātu, avaḷ uṟiyum ūr puṟaviṉkaṇṇatu eṉpatum, taṉ varavu kēṭkum cuṟṟattār makiḻvareṉpatum amintiruppatu kaṇṭa iṭikkāṭaṉār avaṟṟi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
vāṉikupu corinta vaṇpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ
1pāṉi koṇṭa palkāl melluṟi
ñelikōṟ kalappi ataḷoṭu curukkip
paṟippuṟat tiṭṭa pālnoṭi iṭiyaṉ 
nuṇpaḵ ṟuvali orupuṟam naṉippat 
taṇṭukāl vitta 2taṉinili maṭiviḷi
ciṟutalit toḻuti 3ēmārn talkum
puṟavi ṉatuvē poyyā yāṇar
alli lāyiṉum viruntuvariṉ uvakkum
mulli cāṉṟa kaṟpiṉ
melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ uṟiviṉ ūrē.
itu, viṉimuṟṟi 4mīḷaluṟum talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.
uri
vāṉ ikupu corinta vaṇpeyal kaṭināḷ- vāṉam kāl vīḻttuc corinta vaḷaviya maḻi peyta mārikkālattu iṟutiyil, pāṉi koṇṭa palkāl melluṟi - pāṉi vikkappaṭṭa pala kālkaḷ vāṅkip piṉṉiya meṉmiyāṉa uṟiyiṉ kaṇ; ñeli kōl kalappi ataḷoṭu curukki - tīkkaṭikkōl mutaliya karuvikaḷi iṭappeṟṟa ciṟu piyit tōṟpikkuḷ vittu vāyic curukkik  kaṭṭi; paṟippuṟattiṭṭa pāl noṭi iṭiyaṉ - paṉi yōliyāṟ piṉṉappaṭṭa pāyi mutukiṭattē koṇṭa pāl vili kūṟum colliṉa ṉākiya iṭiyaṉ; nuṇpal tuvali orupuṟam naṉippa - nuṇṇiya palavāya maḻittuḷi muṉpuṟattē orupāl naṉikka; taṇṭu kāl vitta taṉinili maṭiviḷi-kiyil tāṅki niṟuttiya neṭiya kōli yūṉṟi ataṉmēl orukāli vaḷittu vittu niṟkum taṉitta niliyum maṭitta vāyiṉiṉṟu eḻum viḷiyoliyum muṟiyē kaṇṭum kēṭṭum; ciṟutalit toḻuti ēmārntu alkum - ciṟutta taliyiyuṭiya āṭṭiṉ tiraḷ avaṉatu pātukāppaṭintu cūḻntu taṅki makiḻum; puṟaviṉatu - mullik kāṭṭiṭattē yuḷatukāṇ; poyyā yāṇar - iṭiyaṟāta putu varuvāyi yuṭimiyāl; allilāyiṉum viruntu variṉ uvakkum-irāp poḻutā yiruppiṉum vanta virunti virintu virumpi ēṟṟup puṟantarum; mulli cāṉṟa kaṟpiṉ - kaṇavaṉ teḷitta collit tēṟiyiruttaṟku vēṇṭum tiṇmiyaminta kaṟpiṉiyum; melliyal kuṟumakaḷ-melliyaṟ poṟiyum uṭiya iḷiyavaḷākiya kātaliyāvāḷ; uṟivu iṉ ūr - uṟitalāl iṉimiyi yuṭiyatākiya ūr; ākalāṉ, nī tēri viriyac celuttuka e.ṟu.
uṟiviṉūr puṟaviṉatu eṉa muṭikka, iṭiyaṉ, tuvali naṉippa, kālvitta taṉinili kaṇṭum, maṭiviḷi kēṭṭum, toḻuti ēmārntu alkum puṟavu eṉa iyiyum. uvakkum kuṟumakaḷ, kaṟpiṉ kuṟumakaḷ, melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ eṉa iyikka. ākalāṉ mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam. vāṉ ikupu corital, maḻi kāl vīḻttup peytal. vaṇpeyal, vaḷaviya peyaliyuṭiya mārikkālam eṉa aṉmoḻit toki yākkuka. vāṭikkāṟṟum cilpeyalum uṭiya kaṭimārikkālam eṉṟaṟku, vāṉikupu corinta vaṇ peyaṟ kaṭināḷ eṉṟār. "vanta vāṭic cilpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ1" eṉṟum "tii niṉṟa taṇpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ2" eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kuṟittal kāṇka. kūḻ koṇṭu cellum pāṉi uṟiyiṭi vittu eṭuttuc cellappaṭuvatu paṟṟip pāṉi koṇṭa uṟi eṉṟum, pala kālkaḷāka vakuttut tirittup piṉṉappaṭuvatupaṟṟip palkāl eṉṟum, tōḷiṟ kiṭappatupaṟṟi meṉmiyuṟa amikkap peṟṟami tōṉṟa melluṟi eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "tiṇkāl uṟiyaṉ pāṉiyaṉ ataḷaṉ, nuṇpal tuvali orutiṟam naṉippattaṇṭu kālūṉṟiya taṉinili yiṭiyaṉ1" eṉṟu piṟāṇṭum ivarē kūṟutal kāṇka. ñelikōl. tīkkaṭikōl. "annuṇ avirpuki kamaḻak kimmuyaṉṟu, ñelikōṟ koṇṭa peruviral ñekiḻic centī2" eṉap piṟarum, paṟi paṉiyōlip pāy; "paṟippuṟa iṭiyaṉ3" eṉac cāṉṟōr kuṟippar. pāl noṭi, pālvili neṭuveḷiyil taṉi niṟṟaliṉ, taṉinili kūṟiṉār. nāṟṟiciyum parantu ceṉṟu mēyum āṭṭiṉattit tāṉ kāṇṭaṟkum taṉṉi avi kāṇṭaṟkum taṉinili vēṇṭappaṭṭateṉa uṇarka. vāyitaḻiyum nāviyum maṭittuk kiyiṟ cuṭṭuvirali vaḷittu vittu eḻuppum oli maṭiviḷi eṉappaṭṭatu; maṭiviḷi cēymiyiṟ ceṉṟu mēyum āṭṭiṉit taṉpāl varuvittaṟku eḻuppappaṭum; "kāṭuṟi iṭiyaṉ āṭutalip peyarkkum maṭiviṭu vīḷi4" eṉpatu kāṇka; ciṟutalit toḻuti, āṭṭiṉ tiraḷ "ciṟutalit turu5" eṉṟum, "ciṟutali āyam6" eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟupa. ēmārtal pātukāppuṟutal, "ēmārār, kōṅkēṟiṉār7" eṉpataṉālu maṟika. yāṇar, putumi; putiya varuvāy mēṟṟu. poyyāmi, varuvāy iṭiyīṭupaṭṭuk kuṉṟāmi, iraviṉkaṇ virun tōmpal iṉitaṉṟu eṉṉum vaḻakkup paṟṟi, alli lāyiṉum viruntu variṉ uvakkum  eṉṟār. mulli iruttal eṉṉum kaṟpu neṟi.
viṉi muṟṟiyatum mīḷaluṟṟa talimakaṉ uḷḷam, tāṉ pirintu pōnta talimakaḷām kātaliyi niṉittatum, avaṭkut tāṉ mīḷuvatākak kuṟitta kūtirp paruvam neñciṉkaṇ eḻuntu tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ, ataṉi vāṉ ikupu corinta vaṇpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ eṉṟu moḻin tāṉ. akkālattē kāṉameṅkum kārmaḻiyāl taḻittu iṉiya kāṭci nalkuvatum, iṭiyarkaḷ kāṉattil tam āṭṭiṉatti mēya viṭṭup pēṇip puṟantaruvatum, tali makaḷ cuṟṟam cūḻvara maṉiyaṟam purintoḻukum māṇpiṉi niṉivuṟuttavē, ataṉic ciṟitu virivākak kūṟiṉāṉ. iṅṅaṉam talimakaḷatu maṉimāṇpu tōṉṟavum, māṇ puṭiya avaḷi uṭaṉē ceṉṟu kāṇṭal vēṇṭum eṉap peruvēṭki eḻutalum, ataṉip pākaṟku veḷippaṭak kūṟal vēṇṭāmiyiṉ kuṟippāl avaḷ uṟiyum ūr puṟaviṉ kaṇṇatu eṉṟāṉ. viruntōmpuvatu tāṉ maṉi yaṟattiṉ māṇpuṭiya nōkka meṉpatit talimakaḷ naṉku aṟintu oḻukum iyalpiṉaḷ eṉpāṉ. alli lāyiṉum  viruntu variṉ uvakkum eṉṟum, anta nallaṟam tāṉ illāmiyiṉ peruka naṭipeṟāmiyiṉ, talimakaḷ uḷḷattē varuttam eytiṟṟāyiṉum, tāṉ vaṟpuṟutta collit tēṟiyirukkum kaṟpu nalatti viyantu, mulli cāṉṟa kaṟpiṉ eṉṟum. kaṟpāl peruntiṇmi yuṭiya ḷāyiṉum, celavu tāḻkkumāyiṉ āṟṟātu peru varuttam eyti vēṟupaṭum meṉmi mikkavaḷ eṉṟaṟku, melliyal kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟum, āṇṭu avaḷ iruttalāl puṟaviṉ kaṇṇatākiya ūr iṉimi mikutalāl nām itukāṟum irunta ivviṭam ippoḻutu iṉṉātāyiṟṟu eṉpāṉ. uṟiviṉ ūr eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. "iruntōmpi ilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi vēḷāṇmi ceytaṟ poruṭṭu1" eṉṟum, "viṉi kalantu veṉṟīka vēntaṉ maṉikalantu, māli ayarkam viruntu2" eṉṟum āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva ṉār kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṭiyaṉ maṭiviḷi kēṭṭuc ciṟutalit toḻuti ēmārn talkum eṉṟatu, namatu varavu kēṭṭut talimakaḷ cuṟṟam cūḻa nammi etirēṟṟu iṉpuṟuvaḷ eṉṟāṉām.
143. kaṇṇakaṉ koṟṟaṉār
kaṇṇakaṉ eṉpāruṭiya makaṉātal paṟṟi iccāṉṟōr kaṇṇakaṉ koṟṟaṉār eṉappaṭukiṉṟār. kaṇṇakaṉār eṉṟa peyaruṭiya cāṉṟōr tokinūlkaḷilum innūlilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar; itaṉāl, kaṇṇakaṉ eṉṟu peyar vippatu paṇṭināḷil tamiḻariṭiyē iruntu vantami terikiṟatu. kaṇṇakaṉār eṉṟa peyar paṟṟiya ārāycciyil "kaṇṇaṉ nākaṉ eṉpatu kaṇṇakaṉāreṉṟu piḻipaṭa eḻuti yiruntamiyāl irunta paṭiyē patippikkap paṭṭatu" eṉṟu tiru. a. nārāyaṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷārkaḷ. orukāl aḵtu uṇmi yāyiṉum, kiṭitta ēṭukaḷ pala vaṟṟilum kaṇṇakaṉār eṉṟa pāṭamē koḷḷappaṭṭiruppatu nōkkattakkatu. iccāṉṟōr peyarilum kaṇṇakaṉ koṟṟa ṉār eṉpatē kāṇappaṭutalāl, ippaṭiyum orupeyar irun tirukkalām eṉa eṇṇutaṟku iṭam valiyuṟukiṟatu. kaṇṇaka ṉār eṉpatu kaṇṇaṉākaṉār eṉpataṉ marūu muṭivāy niṉṟu piṉpu atuvē makkaṭkuriya iyaṟpeyarāka māṟi yirukkalām. ikkaṇṇakaṉ eṉpatu, cila ēṭukaḷil kaṇṇakāraṉ eṉṟum, kaṇṇakarāṉ eṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kaṇṇakaṉ eṉpati eḻutumiṭattu ṉakara meyyi aṭittut tirunta eḻutiya nili, kaṇṇakāraṉ eṉavum kaṇṇakarāṉ eṉavum paṭikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatākak kōṭalē muṟiyākum. ēṭukaḷil meyyeḻuttiṉ taliyil puḷḷiyirātu. ṉakara mey aṭikkappaṭumāyiṉ (ṉa) eṉap pakkattē puḷḷiyiṭappeṟum. ap puḷḷiyum kāl kōl arivaṭṭamāka niṟkum. paṭippavar ataṉi ēṟṟavāṟu paṭittu viṭuvar; eḻutuvōr ataṉi eḻutiviṭuvar. ēṭeḻutumiṭattu, oruvar paṭikka oruvar eḻutuvar. oruvarē pārttu eḻuta muṭiyātu. itupaṟṟiyē "tavattukku oruvar tamiḻukku iruvr" eṉpatu paḻamoḻiyāyiṟṟu.
talimakkaḷiṭiyē kātaluṟavu tōṉṟik kaḷavu neṟiyil vaḷarntu varukiyil, talimakaḷ uḷḷattiṟ perukiya kātal, avaṉiyiṉṟi oru kaṇamum amiyāta niliyi eytavum, tōḻi varintu koḷḷumāṟu talimakaṉi vaṟpuṟuttiṉāḷ; avaṉum uṇmiyi uṇarntu varitaṟkuriya muyaṟciyiṉic ceyyaluṟṟāṉ. anniliyil talimakaḷatu kātal māṇpi aṟiyāta avaḷuṭiya peṟṟōr, vēṟṟōr varivu vēṇṭi viṭutta cāṉṟōrkku uṭaṉpaṭac camintaṉar. ataṉit talimakaḷum talimakaṉum uṇarntaṉar. vēṟuvaḻiyiṉmi kaṇṭa talimakaṉ talimakaḷit taṉṉuṭaṉ koṇṭutalik kaḻiyat tuṇintāṉ. kuṟitta nāḷil tōḻi tuṇiceyyat talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ taṉ maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic ceṉṟu cērntāḷ. iruvarum talimakaṉatu pērūril, "koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇamoṭu" naṭakkum vatuvimuṟiyil cāṉṟōr tirumuṉ maṇam purintukoṇṭu maṉiyaṟam mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ūra varum cāṉṟōrum paravat taṉ maṉiyiṉkaṇ taṉ makaṭku "nāṭaṟi naṉmaṇam" ceytu makiḻum  ciṟapput taṉakku eytāmi yāl, talimakaḷip peṟṟa tāy peritum varuttamuṟṟāḷ. taṉ maṉiyiṉkaṇ irunta pōtu taṉmakaḷ ēṉi makaḷiruṭaṉ kūṭi viḷiyāṭiya ōriyāṭṭi niṉintāḷ; taṉ maṉiyil naṭṭu vaḷartta noccic ceṭiyiṉi nōkkuntōṟum avaḷatu uḷḷam urukit taṇṇīrāyk kaṇvaḻip perukiṟṟu talimakaḷ pēṇi vaḷarttu moḻikaṟpitta kiḷi "aṉṉāy aṉṉāy" eṉap pēcumpōtellām tāyuḷḷam tāṅkarum tuyarattāl taṭumāṟṟamuṟṟatu; talimakaḷ pōkku mēṟkōṭaṟku muṉṉāḷ nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷi niṉivu kūrntāḷ. kuṟṟam yārmēṟṟu eṉa ārāyntāḷ. makaḷ vaḻuvilaḷ eṉṟu kaṇṭāḷ, avaḷ uḷḷattil tōṉṟip peruki uṭaṉpōkkiṟkut tuṇinta kātalaṟatti viyantāḷ. ūrmakaḷir tammiṟ kūṭi uritta uriyik kēḷviyuṟṟa yāṉavaḷi nōkki, "aṉṉāy ūravar urikkum iṉṉā iṉṉuriyiṉik kēṭṭi, eṉṟu oṉṟum aṟiyātēṉ pōlak kuṟippāy mella uriyāṭi yirukkalām; avvāṟiṉṟi, orukāl, avaḷi nōkki, "niṉ kūntal putumaṇam nāṟukiṉṟatu" eṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉāka, ataṟku avaḷ uritta viṭiyiṉ karutti ārāyā toḻintēṉ. aḵtu ippoḻutu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ataṉi appoḻutē teḷintu vāykāvā toḻintatu eṉ kuṟṟamē" eṉṟu eṇṇi varuntiṉāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, makaṭpōkkiya tāy, makaḷatu kuṟṟam niṉintum, koṇṭu talikkaḻinta talimakaṉi nontum urittaṟ kuriya ḷāyiṉum, avaṟṟic ceyyātu kātaluṇarviṉ māṇpu niṉintu viyappatum, makaḷuṭiya kuṟippuriyi niṉintu tāṉ vāy kāvātu oḻinta kuṟṟa muṇarntu varuntu vatum, makaḷatu kātal oḻukkatti maṉattāl ārāyntu kuṟṟamiṉmi tērvatum kaṇṭa koṟṟaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
aitē kāmam amma1 oyyeṉat
tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟat
tōri āyamum nocciyum kāṇṭoṟum
nīrvār kaṇṇēṉ kaluḻum eṉṉiṉum
kiḷḷiyum kiḷiyeṉak2 kūum iḷiyōṉ
vaḻuvilaḷ maṉṟa3tāṉē kuḻīi
ampal mūtūr alarvāyp peṇṭir
iṉṉā iṉṉuri kēṭṭiciṉ 4eṉṉā
aṟiyēṉ pōla uyirēṉ5
naṟiya nāṟumniṉ katuppeṉ ṟēṉē6
itu, makaṭpōkkiya tāy kūṟiyatu; maṉimaruṭciyu mām7
uri
aitu kāmam - viyappuṭiyatāy irāniṉṟatu kātaṟkāmam; amma-; oyyeṉa - virivāka; tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟattu -putuvatākak koṇarntu parappiya maṇal niṟinta aḻakiya maṉimuṟṟattil; ōriāyamum nocciyum kāṇ ṭoṟum - ōriviḷiyāṭ ṭayarum eṉ makaḷiṉ āya makaḷiriyum avaḷ naṭṭu vaḷartta noccic ceṭiyiṉiyum kāṇuntōṟum; nīrvār kaṇṇēṉ; kaluḻum eṉṉiṉum - nīr coriyum kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyēṉāyp pulampum eṉṉiṉum; kiḷḷiyum kiḷiyeṉak kūum - avaḷ vaḷartta kiḷiyum aṉṉāy eṉak kūvi varuntukiṟatu; iḷiyōḷ vaḻuvilaḷ - iḷamakaḷākiyaavaḷ kuṟṟamuṭiya ḷallaḷ; maṉṟa - teḷivāka; kuḻīi - tammiṟ kūṭikkoṇṭu; ampal mūtūr alarvāyp peṇṭir - ampal poruntiya paḻamiyāṉa ivvūriluḷḷa alar tūṟṟum vāyiyuṭiya ētilāṭṭiyar urikkum; iṉṉā iṉṉuri  kēṭṭiciṉ eṉṉā -kēṭṭaṟku iṉṉāmi yuṭittāyk kātaloḻukkattukkuc ciṟanta tākiya alaruriyik kēṭpāyāka eṉṟu; aṟiyēṉ pōla - oṉṟum aṟiyātavaḷ pōla; uyirēṉ - mella urit tēṉumallēṉāy; naṟiya nāṟum niṉ katuppu eṉṟēṉ - putiya naṟiya maṇam kamaḻāniṉṟatu niṉ kūntal eṉṟu vāykāvātu kūṟiṉēṉ ākalāṉ e-ṟu.
amma, kāmam aitu, nakarmuṟṟattu āyamum nocciyum kāṇṭoṟum. oyyeṉa nīrvār kaṇṇēṉāyk kaluḻum eṉṉiṉum kiḷḷiyum kūvum; iḷiyōḷ vaḻuvilaḷ; peṇṭir kuḻīi urikkum iṉṉāiṉṉuri kēṭṭiciṉ eṉṉā aṟiyēṉ pōla uyirēṉāy niṉ katuppu naṟiya nāṟum eṉṟu vāykāvā toḻintē ṉākalāṉ yāṉē vaḻuvuṭiyēṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. ai, viyappu, kāmam eṉṟatu kātaṟ kāmatti; "vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ ciṟantatu, kātaṟkāmam1" eṉṟu kuṉṟam pūtaṉār kūṟutal kāṇka. 'amma', uriyaci, ñemirtal, parattal, itu ñemartal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. "varimaṇal ñemarak kaṟpaka naṭakkum perumitap pakaṭu2" eṉpatu kāṇka. nakar, perumaṉi ōri, makaḷir āṭum nīr viḷiyāṭṭuvaki. nocci, noccic ceṭi; itaṉi naṉku vaḷaraviṭiṉ periya maramumām. itaṉ niḻaviṟ ciṟu peṇkaḷ maṇalvīṭu amittu viḷiyāṭuvar. kaṇṇēṉ, uyirēṉ eṉpaṉa muṟṟeccam. kiḷiyeṉak kūum moḻiyāl taṉ iṉameṉak karuti aṉṉāy eṉak kūvum. peṇṭir kuḻīi eṉa māṟikkūṭṭi urikkum eṉa orucoṟ peytu iyikka. urikkum, colleccam; ici yeccamumām. ampal, ayalaṟiyāvāṟu iruvar tammuḷḷē pēcikkoḷḷum paḻippuri; alar iruvarkku mēṟ palar kūṭi uriyāṭum paḻippuri. "ampalum alarum kaḷavu3" eṉṟa iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ uri kāṇka. kēṭ ṭaṟku iṉṉātāyiṉum, iṉpappayaṉ viḷivittalāl uri, iṉṉā iṉṉuri eṉappaṭṭatu. uyirttal, meṉmiyāka urittal. ākalāṉ eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam.
talimakaḷ uḷḷattil tōṉṟip perukiniṉṟa kātaṟ perukkut taṉṉip peṟṟa tāyiyum, uṭaṉpayiṉṟa makaḷiriyum, peṟṟa makavu pōl vaḷartta nocciyiyum, kiḷḷiyiyum toṭarpaṟa maṟakkac ceytami kaṇṭu viyantu kūṟukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, aitē, kāmam amma eṉṟum, kāṇpār kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarntu viḷiyāṭaṟku viruppatti uṇṭupaṇṇumāṟu putumaṇal parappip polivuṟuttap peṟṟa perumaṉi eṉṟaṟkut tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟam eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. "īkik kaṇṇi yilaṅkat tiit tarumaṇal iyalvōḷ1" eṉpatu kāṇka. ōri, nīrviḷiyāṭṭu; pantu eṟintu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu vakiyumākum. uṭaṉiruntu ōri āṭum makaḷir, ōri makaḷireṉappaṭuvar iḷamakaḷir perumpālum maṉi muṟṟattu maṇaṟparappil āṭuvareṉa aṟika. "cīrkeḻu viyaṉakarc cilampu naka iyali, ōriyāyamoṭu pantu ciṟitu eṟiyiṉum2" eṉpataṉāl ītuṇarappaṭum. maṉikkaṇ nocci naṭṭu makaḷirāl vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu, peruñcelvaratu perumaṉiyil nikaḻum marapu; "maṉivaḷar nocci"3 eṉavum, "maṉiyiḷa nocci"4 eṉavum varutal kāṇka. makaḷoṭu toṭarpuṭiya makaḷiriyum poruḷkaḷiyum kāṇum pōtu makaḷ niṉivu neñciṉkaṇ eḻutaliṉ, ammakaḷip pōkkiya tāykkup perun tuyaruṇṭātal iyal pākalāṉ, ōri yāyamum nocciyum kāṇṭoṟum nīrvār kaṇṇēṉ kaluḻum eṉṟāḷ. "uḷḷiṉum uḷḷam vēmē yuṇkaṇ maṇivāḻ pāvi naṭikaṟ ṟaṉṉa eṉ aṇiyiyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ āṭiya maṇiyēr nocciyum teṟṟiyum kaṇṭē5" eṉṟu piṟāṇṭum makaṭ pōkkiya tāy pulampik kūṟuvatu kāṇka. iṉi, kiḷḷiyit tāṉ peṟṟa piḷḷipōla vaḷarttut taṉ moḻi kaṟpittāḷākaliṉ, avaḷi nōkki atu kūṟuvatu naṟṟāykku mikka varuttam ceytaliṉ, eṉ ṉiṉum kiḷḷiyum kiḷi yeṉak kūum eṉṟāḷ; taṉṉiṉum kiḷḷi uḷḷa tuṇarum maṉavuṇar villatā kaliṉ, ataṉ kūkkural tuyar mikac ceyva tāyiṟ ṟeṉka. "pāviyum pantum pavaḷavāyp piṅkiḷiyum, āyamum oṉṟum ivi niṉiyāḷ - pāl pōlum, āynta moḻi yiṉāḷ celluṅ kol kātalaṉpiṉ"1 eṉpatu kāṇka.
ivvaṇṇam varuttam, mikkuk kaṇ kaluḻantu varuntum tāyiṉatu uḷḷam, peṟṟa tāyar mutalāyiṉāri viṭuttuk kātalaṉ piṉ ceṉṟatu vaḻuvaṉṟō eṉa eḻum ārāycciyil talippaṭṭu, iḷami yuṭiya ḷātaliṉ, talimakaḷpāl vaḻuvilli eṉpatu uṇarntu, iḷiyaḷ vaḻuvilaḷ maṉṟa eṉṟāḷ. ūriṉkaṇ eṅkēṉum oru muṭukkaril iṉṉāreṉṟu aṟiyāta iruvar cevikkuḷ nilavum paḻip puri ampalāy arumputavāl, ataṉi ūr mēl vittu ampal mūtūr eṉṟum, piṉṉar, makaḷir kūṭṭattiṟ paravip palar kēṭkumaḷaviṟ collappaṭuvatu alarātalāl, ataṉi ūr mēl vittu ampal mūtūr eṉṟum, piṉṉar, makaḷir kūṭṭattiṟ paravip palar kēṭkumaḷaviṟ collappaṭuvatu alarātalāl makaḷirmēlēṟṟi alarvāyp peṇṭir eṉṟum  kūṟiṉavaḷ, avvalaruriyāl tali makaṟkum talimakaṭkum toṭarpu vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭut tiru maṇam taṭiyiṉṟi nikaḻtaṟku ētuvātalāl, iṉṉā iṉṉuri eṉṟum, ataṉi maṉiyavar aṟiyāmalum, tāṉ avaḷatu kaḷaviṉi aṟiyātāḷ pōlavum, nayamāka, mella uriyātu, alar kēṭkap piṟanta uḷḷattuṭippiṉāl kaṭintu kēṭṭami taṉpāl nikaḻnta kuṟṟamāka eṇṇi varuntukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, iṉṉā iṉṉuri kēṭṭiciṉ eṉṉā aṟiyēṉ pōla uyirēṉ eṉṟum, vāy tiṟantu vemmi tōṟṟum kuralil kaṭintu urittataṉi, naṟiya nāṟum niṉkatuppu eṉṟēṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taṉakku iṉṉātākavum talimakaṭku iṉitākavum amintamiyiṉ alari iṉṉā iṉṉuri eṉṟatum, aṟiyātār pōla urippatu kēṭpōrkku vaḻuvuri yākāmiyum, "niṉ katuppu naṟiya nāṟum; in naṟumi niṉpāl paṇṭu tōṉṟiya taṉṟu. ippoḻutu putitu tōṉṟi niṉ oḻukkattil vēṟupāṭuṇmiyik kāṭṭukiṟatu; itu niṉikkēyaṉṟi nī piṟanta kuṭikkum iḻukkām" eṉṟu urittuk kaṭintavāṟum viḷaṅka virittuk kūṟuvatāl cevili oruvāṟu āṟṟāmi nīṅkuvatu ip pāṭṭiṉ muṭipayaṉ.
144. kaccippēṭṭup peruntaccaṉār
kāñcimā nakarkkuk kaccippēṭu eṉpatum peyar. iṅkē cāṉṟōr palar tōṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāñcik koṟṟaṉār eṉpōrum, kaccik katakkaṇṇaṉār eṉpōrum in nakarattava rāvar. caṅka kālattil toṇṭi vēntarkaḷukkut tali nakaramāṉa itu, "palvēṟu vakiyiṟ paṇinta maṉṉar. toyyā veṟukkiyoṭu tuvaṉṟupu kuḻīic cevvi pārkkum ceḻunakar" eṉavum, "karuṅkik kollaṉ irumpu vicit teṟinta, kūṭat tiṇṇici verīi māṭattu, iṟiyuṟi puṟaviṉ ceṅkāṟ cēval, iṉṟuyil iriyum poṉtuñcu viyaṉakar1" eṉavum pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu; caṅka kālatti yaṭuttu, maṇimēkali mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟiya kāppiyak kālattil, "tēvar kōmāṉ kāval mānakar, maṇmicik kiṭanteṉa vaḷantali mayaṅkiya poṉṉakar2" eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kacci nakaric cūḻnta nāṭu kāñci nāṭu eṉa annāḷil vaḻaṅ kiṟṟu. "poṉṉeyiṟ kāñci nāṭu3" eṉa varutal kāṇka. eṉavē, kacci mānakarkkuk kāñci yeṉṉum peyar caṅka kālattukkum maṇimēkali kālattukkum iṭiyil tāṉ mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiṉami peṟṟām. kaccippēṭu eṉṉum peyar pallavar kālamutal neṭuṅkālam vari vaḻakkiliruntami kalveṭṭukkaḷāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṟatu. innakarip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷi ārāyumiṭattuk kāñci nakaram tāmarippū vaṭivil amintiruntamiyum, naṭuviṭam kacciyeṉavum, cūḻavuḷḷa pakuti kaccip pēṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉamiyum, piṉṉark kāñci mānakar eṉṟa peyarē nāḷaṭivil peruvaḻakkāyiṉa miyum uṇarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālattē puttarum camaṇarum vantu taṅkiya pōtu, kāñcinakar eṉṟa peyar vaṭavarkku naṉku terintatu; matil cūḻnta nakarip puram eṉṉum vaṭanūṉ marapupaṟṟi, kāñcipuram eṉṟāki, atuvē iṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu. kāñcipuramāki niṟkum paṇṭiya kaccippēṭṭiṉ kaṇ taccut toḻil purintu koṇṭu tamiḻp pulamiyāl nallicic cāṉṟōrāy viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ. ic cāṉṟōr kaccippēṭṭut taccaṉār eṉavum, ivariṉum iḷiyōr oruvar ivarvaḻi niṉṟu cāṉṟōr niralil iruntamiyiṉ, iruvariyum vēṟupaṭuttap peruntaccaṉāreṉavum, iḷiya vari iḷantaccaṉār eṉavum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkiṉar. mēlum, taccut toḻilil pukaḻmikkavarkku aracarkaḷ peruntaccaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭam nalkuvatu iṭikkālac cōḻapāṇṭiyar āṭciyilum vaḻakkāka iruntuḷatu. ataṉāl iccāṉṟōr peruntaccaṉār eṉappaṭukiṉṟār eṉpatum oṉṟu. ivaratu iyaṟ peyar maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. ivarippaṟṟi vēṟu kuṟippoṉṟum kiṭittilatu. ivar pāṭiyaṉavāka ittoki nūṟkaṇ iraṇṭu pāṭṭukkaḷē uḷḷaṉa. ippāṭṭilum iṭiyē aṭikaḷ citintum piṟaḻntum irukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṭantalippaṭṭum, tōḻiyiṉ naṟṟuṇiyāl pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟiyākiya iruvakik kuṟiyiṭattuk kaṇṭum kūṭiyum, tattam uḷḷattil tōṉṟiya kātaluṟavip perukki vanta talimakkaḷatu oḻukalāṟṟil nāṭkaḷ cila kaḻiyak kaṇṭa tōḻi, ivvoḻukkatti nīḷaviṭātu taṭuttut talivaṉi varintu kōṭaṟkaṇ karuttuc cellumāṟu tūṇṭum arumpaṇiyi mēṟkoṇṭāḷ. aṟivum ceṟivum āṇmiyum paṭitta talimakaṉātaliṉ, avaṉmuṉ tāṉ ulakiyali mika aṟintu uyarntāḷpōla, viriya varintu koḷka eṉa veḷip paṭiyākak kūṟuvatu tōḻikku nayamākat tōṉṟavilli. talimakaṉ uyarvukkum tōḻiyākiya taṉatu tāḻvukkum ivvāṟu uriyāṭutal nalloḻukkamākātu eṉpati avaḷ naṉku uṇarntāḷ. talimakaṉiyum talimakaḷiyum pōlat tōḻi talimic ciṟappu ilaḷāyiṉum, avarkaḷuṭiya tōḻamikku ēṟpa aṟivē uḷḷamākavum aṉpē moḻi yākavum aṟamē ceyalākavum tōḻiyiṉ uruvil amintu niṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl talimakaṉi varivukaṭāvum ceyalit tōḻi mutaṟkaṇ kuṟippākac ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. oru muṟikkup paṉmuṟi tōḻi collavum, talimakaṉ, talivi yatu kātal perukit taṉṉi yiṉṟi amiyāta aḷavukku vaḷarkkum kuṟippiṉāl, kaḷavoḻukkattiyē virumpi niṉ ṟāṉ. avaṉ karuttu atuvāka, talimakaṭkuk kaṟpu neṟikkaṭ peṟum avaṉatu aḻivil kūṭṭattil avā mikuntatu. avaṉi nērniṉṟu nōkkit taṉṉi varintu koḷḷumāṟu veḷip paṭiyāka vēṇṭuvatu taṉatu peṇmikkum talimikkum māṟāy niṉṟatu. tōḻiyiṉatu muyaṟci virintu payaṉ nalkāmi orupuṟam niṟka, iraviṉkaṇ uḷavākum iṭiyīṭukaḷ orupuṟam niṉṟu varuttat talimakaṉ taṉittu ariya kāṭukaḷiyum, kāṭṭāṟukaḷiyum kaṭantu varuvatu peruṅ kavaliyi uḷa tākki avaḷatu uḷḷatti alikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. an niliyil, orunāḷ iravu kāṭṭil piṭiyoṭu kūṭi vāḻum peruṅkaḷiṟoṉṟukkum, puliyoṉṟukkum pōr uṇṭāka, kaḷiṟu puliyāṟ kollappaṭṭiṟantatu. atukaṇṭu añci alamantu ōṭiya piṭi vēṟoru pakkatti aṭiyavum, aṅkē vāṉattil kuḻumiya maḻi mukiliṉ kūṭṭam miṉṉi iṭittu muḻaṅkak kaṇṭatu. ammuḻakkam puliyiṉ muḻakkam pōṉṟu iruntamiyiṉ piṭiyāṉi peritum veruvi, mūṅkilāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa tūmpu eṉṉum icikkaruvi pōlak kataṟip piḷiṟa luṟṟatu. vīḻnta yāṉiyiṉ taciyi ap puliyum ataṉ piṇavum tiṉṟu pacitīrntu ceṉṟu oṉṟoṭoṉṟu piṉṉik kiṭakkum perum putaṟkuḷ oṭuṅkik kiṭantaṉa. arukē kāṭ ṭāṟṟil aṇmiyiṟ peyta perumaḻiyāl nīr peruki mikka virivuṭaṉ ōṭikkoṇṭiruntatu. ciṟitupōtil akkāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē taṉṉan taṉiyaṉāyt talimakaṉ kāṭṭāṟṟik kaṭantu vantu cērntāṉ. avaṉik kaṇṭatum talimakaṭku makiḻcci orupālāka, avaṉi avviravil varuvitta taṉ aṟiyāmi niṉintu āṟāt tuyaramuṟṟāḷ. kaṇkaḷil nīr āṟupōl peruki oḻukiṟṟu. neñcam kavaliyuṟṟu varuntuva tāyiṟṟu. maṟunāḷ talimakaṉ pōntu talimakaḷ uṟiyum peru maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattē niṉṟāṉ. avaṉatu varaviṉi aṟiyātāḷ pōla avaṉ ceviyil naṉku paṭumāṟu in nikaḻccikaḷi muṟiyē eṭuttu aḻakuṟat toṭuttu urippā ḷāyiṉāḷ.
ivvuriyiṉkaṇ, talimakaḷ uḷḷataṉ uṇmi kūṟu mukattāl talimakaṉ uḷḷatti variviṉkaṇ celuttum matinuṭpam kaṇṭa peruntaccaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ vittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
peruṅkaḷi ṟuḻuvi tākkaliṉ irumpiṭik
karuvimā maḻiyiṉ aravam añci1
2vāṅkamik kaḻiyiṉ naralum pūcaṟkup
pōtēr uṇkaṇ kaluḻavum3 ētila
pēti neñcam kavali kavaṟṟa
īṅkā kiṉṟāl tōḻi4 āṅkaṇ
5piṇavuppaci tīriya6 pēḻvāy irumpuli
piṇaṅkaril oṭuṅkum aṇaṅkaruṅ kavaliya
aviraṟal oḻukum viricelaṟ kāṉyāṟṟuk
kariyaruṅ kuṭṭam tamiyar nīnti
viravumalar poṟitta tōḷar
iraviṉ varutal7 aṟiyā tēṟkē.
iḵtu, āṟṟātu8 ētam niṉintu kavaṉṟa talivi ciṟip
puṟamākac colliyatu.
uri
peruṅkaḷiṟu uḻuvi tākkaliṉ - periya kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyip puli tākkik koṉṟamiyāl; irumpiṭi - atu kaṇṭu añci yōṭiya peṇyāṉi; karuvi māmaḻiyiṉ aravam añci - tokuti koṇṭa maḻimukil ceyyum muḻakkattik kēṭṭu añci; vāṅku amik kaḻiyiṉ naralum pūcaṟku - vaḷinta mūṅkilā ṉākiya tūmpu pōlap piḷiṟum ōciyik kēṭṭu; pōtu ēr uṇkaṇ kaluḻavum - pūp pōṉṟa eṉ kaṇkaḷ kaṇṇīr coriyavum; ētila pēti neñcam kavali kavaṟṟa - tāṉ ceyyum ceyaṟkuriya ētu oṉṟum kāṇutal illāta ēḻi neñcam kavali eyti varuttavum; īṅku ākiṉṟu - iṅku eṉ nili ivvāṟāka; tōḻi-; āṅkaṇ-akkāṭṭiṭattē; piṇavup paci tīriya pēḻvāy irumpuli-peṇpuliyiṉ pacitīra uṇpitta akaṉṟa vāyiyuṭiya periya puli; piṇaṅkaril oṭuṅkum aṇaṅku aruṅkavaliya-koṭi kompukaḷ tammiṟ piṉṉip piṇaṅkiya tūṟukaḷiṉ iṭiyē oṭuṅki yirukkum accam poruntiya ariya kavartta vaḻikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭiṉkaṇ; avir aṟal oḻukum viricelal kāṉyāṟṟu-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīr niṟintu virintu ōṭum kāṭṭāṟṟil; kariyaruṅ kuṭṭam tamiyar nīnti-kariyillāta āḻamāṉa maṭukkaḷit taṉiyāka nīnti varutalāl; viravumalar poṟitta tōḷar-pala vakiyāka viraviya pūkkaḷ paṭinta tōḷkaḷi yuṭiyarāy; iravil varutalaṟiyātēṟku - kātalar iraviṉkaṇ varutaliṉ arumiyi aṟiyāta eṉakku e-ṟu.
tōḻi, āṅkaṇ iravil kāṉyāṟṟuk kuṭṭam nīntit tōḷarāy varutal aṟiyātēṟkuk kaṇ kaluḻavum neñcam kavaṟṟavum īṅkākiṉṟu eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. uḻuvi, puli, tākkutal kōṟaṟ poruṭ ṭākaliṉ, īṇṭuk kāriyattiṉmēl niṉṟatu. peruṅkaḷiṟu virumpiya piṭi yeṉpatu tōṉṟa irumpiṭiyeṉṟār. miṉṉum iṭiyum tokka maḻi mukil karuvi māmaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu. karuvi, tokuti, aravam, muḻakkam. maruṇṭataṉ kaṇṇukku iruṇṭa tellām pēy eṉṟāṟ pōlap puli porak kaṇṭu maruṇṭu ōṭi irumpiṭikku maḻi mukiliṉ muḻakkam accam payappatāyiṟṟu. vāṅkamik kaḻi, vaḷinta mūṅkilā ṉāya tūmpu eṉṉum icikkaruvi; itaṉip peruvaṅkiyam eṉṟum, kaḷiṟṟuyir eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa; "kaṇṇiṭi viṭutta kaḷiṟṟuyirt tūmpiṉ"1 eṉavum "kaṇṇaṟut tiyaṟṟiya tūmpu"2 eṉavum, "ōykaḷiṟu eṭutta nōyuṭi neṭuṅki, tokucoṟ kōṭiyar kāmpiṉ uyirkkum"1 eṉavum cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu kāṇka. naralutal, muḻaṅkutal, kavali, muṉṉatu varuttam; piṉṉatu, kavarttavaḻi, kavaṟṟal kavali eytuvittal. piṇavu, peṇpuli, kavaliya kāṉ yāṟu eṉa iyiyum. aṟal, nīr kāṭṭāṟākaliṉ nīrp perukkiṟku virivu iyalpāyiṟṟu. kuṭṭam, āṟṟiṭiyē yuṇṭākiya āḻnta maṭukkaḷ, pū niṟinta marañ ceṭikaḷiṉūṭē varutaliṉ, viravumalarpoṟitta tōḷar eṉṟār. nīrp perukkāl kari yaḻintu kāṇṭaṟ karitāyiṉami tōṉṟak kariyaruṅ kuṭṭam eṉṟār. 'āl' acinili.
illiṉkaṇ iruntu koṇṭu iravukkuṟikkaṇ tali makaṉit talikkūṭi yoḻukum talimakaḷ, paṉmuṟiyum iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu paṭṭami niṉintum. iraviṉkaṇ varum talimakaṟku vaḻiyil uḷavākum ētaṅkaḷi niṉintum varuntukiṉṟā ḷātaliṉ, avaṭkuk kāṭṭiṭi nikaḻum nikaḻcci niṉivil tōṉṟa ataṉi eṭuttut talivaṉ kēṭka moḻipavaḷ, talimakaṉic cuṭṭip peruṅkaḷiṟu eṉṟum, avaṉ varum vaḻi koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷāl perun tīṅku viḷivippaṉa eṉṟaṟku uḻuvi tākkaliṉ eṉṟum, uḻuvi kollutaliṉ eṉṟataṟku  añci ataṉi nāvāṟ kūṟamāṭṭātu apporuṇmiyē tōṉṟa tākkaliṉ eṉṟum vaḻiyiṉatu ētam niṉintu tāṉ uḷḷam tuḷaṅkit tuyaruṟṟu varuntum tiṟatti viriyak kūṟaluṟṟu, veḷippaṭak kūṟalākāmiyiṉ, piṭiyiṉ ceyaṉmēl vittu, karuvi māmaḻiyiṉ aravam añci vāṅkamik kaḻiyiṉ naralum pūcaṟku eṉṟum kaṇ kaluḻtali niṟuttavum, kavali nīṅkutaṟkuriya kāraṇam kaṇṭu ataṉi vilakkavum māṭṭātu neñcam nīr celvaḻip pulcāyumāṟu pōlak kavalikaḷiṉ vaḻiniṉṟu varuntiṉamiyiṉ ētila pēti neñcam eṉṟum, ivvāṟāyiṟṟeṉpa tallatu vēṟu vakiyāl viḷakkalā kāmiyiṉ īṅkā kiṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kaḷiṟu uḻuviyāl tākkuṟṟatu kaṇṭu añci ōṭiya piṭi, maḻi muḻakkiṟ kañcip pulampum eṉṟataṉāl, iravuk kuṟik kaṇ varum talimakaṉ varavu, allakuṟi mutaliyavaṟṟāl iṭiyīṭu paṭṭami kaṇṭu varunti yuṟiyum talimakaḷ, kāvalar kaṭukutal, tāytuñcāmi, nāy tuñcāmi mutaliya iṭiyīṭukaḷāl talivaṉatu varavu ciṟitu tāḻkkiṉum, avaṟku ētamuṇṭāṅkoleṉa añcip pulampiṉami kuṟippāy urittavāṟu eṉa aṟika. iṉi, ivviravuk kuṟikkaṇ varutali maṟuttu, varintu koḷḷumāṟu kuṟip pāy urikkiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, talimakaṉic cuṭṭi āṅkaṇ eṉṟum, avaṉ varum kāṭṭiṉiyum, ataṉ vaḻiyē varu vōrkku ētam viḷivikkum pulikaḷiyum
