kātal veṟṟi peṟiṉ peṇṇēval ceytoḻukum pētiyāyk kīḻmi eytukiṉṟāṉ. inniliyil, talimakaṉ uḷḷattil naṭanta kātaṟ kaṭamip pōril kaṭami veṟṟi eytavē, avaṉ poruṭ pirivi mēṟkoṇṭu kātalipāl viṭipeṟṟuc ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ ceṉṟa pōtu nāṭṭil maḻi kuṉṟak kaṭuṅkōṭi nilaviṟṟu. veyiliṉ vemmi mikuntu niṉṟatu. veḷiyē celvārkku vaḻikaḷ peruṅkoṭumiyic ceytaṉa. nīrppaci aṟutalāl nilappakuti pala iṭaṅkaḷil veṭittuk kiṭantatu. nīrvēṭki taṇiyāmal māvum puḷḷum makkaḷuyirum piṟavum peritum varuntiṉa. ataṉik kaṇṭa talimakaḷ talimakaṉukku vēṉil vemmiyāl viḷiyum varutta mikutiyi eṇṇi varuntuvāḷāyiṉāḷ. aṟivum, aṟamum, aṉpum eṉṟa mūṉṟumē maṉamoḻi meykaḷākak koṇṭa tōḻiyoruttiyuṇṭu. talimakaḷuṭiya uḷḷattil talimakaṉatu āṇmiyiyum viṉit tiṭpattiyum paṟṟiya uṇarvu iṉṟi; vēṉil vemmiyum, neṟiyiṉ koṭumiyum talimakaṉatu āṟṟāmiyumē mēlōṅki niṟpatu attōḻikkut terintatu. tali makaṉuṭiya viṉiyāṇmiyiṉ vīṟupāṭ ṭiṉi uṇarātu pōvāḷāyiṉ, talimakaḷatu etirkāla vāḻvu iṉpa niṟivu peṟātu eṉpati uṇarntu ataṉi avaṭku urippāḷāyt "tōḻi, vēṉil vemmi mikutalāl ula kuyirkaḷ peritum varuntum kālattē talivar ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟu iyampukiṉṟāy; uṇmiyum atuvē; ataṉi nāṉum eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; āyiṉum ēṉik kār, kūtir, paṉi eṉṟa paruvaṅkaḷ mūṉṟum viṉiceytaṟkuc ciṟantaṉa. allavātaliṉ, ippōtu ceṉṟatutāṉ ciṟappu" eṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kūṟiyatu talimakaṭkut teḷivum āṟutalum taravilli, nāṭkaḷ kaḻintaṉa; talimakaṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākak kuṟitta paruvam varuvatāyiṟṟu. ciṉṉāṭkaḷil mēṟkoṇṭa poruḷ viṉi muṭitta talivaṉ taṉ maṉinōkki varukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟa ceyti muṟpaṭa vantatu. ataṉit talimakaṭku uṇarttaluṟṟa tōḻi, talimakaṉatu viṉiyāṇmiyiṉmuṉ vēṉiliṉ vemmiyum, neṭuñcurattiṉ koṭumiyum, viṉiyiṉ perumiyum ciṟumi yuṟṟut tōṟṟōṭiṉa eṉṟa karuttuppaṭa; vaḻiccelvōri oru kāraṇamu miṉṟiyē koṉṟu vīḻtti avar uṭalik kāṭṭilē irakkamiṉṟik kiṭattic cellum kaṇṇaṟikaḷāṉa koṭiyavar vāḻum kāṭṭiṭic ceṉṟā rāyiṉum; avar kaḷāl ūṟu ciṟitu miṉṟi, iṉitu ceṉṟu mīḷum āṇmiyum, āṟṟalum, paṭivaliyum, tōḷvaliyum uṭiyar nam kātalar eṉpataṉi nī uṇarāmiyāl; yāṉ avar ceṉṟatu naṉṟu eṉṟēṉāka nī eṉ collit teḷintāyilli" eṉṟāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ talimakaḷ aṟiyāvāṟu talivaṉpāl amintuḷḷa āṇmittiṟatti aṟivippa tuṭaṉ; avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa poruḷ muṭittu varum tiṟatti aṟivippatum oruṅkē amiyak kaṇṭa pēyaṉār avaṟṟi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ peytu pāṭukiṉṟār.
uṟituṟan tirunta muḷivem puṟaviṟ1
ceṅkatirc celvaṉ teṟutaliṉ maṇpaka
ulakumika varunti ayarvuṟu2 kālic
ceṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum naṉṟucey taṉareṉac
collil teḷippavum teḷital cellāy
ceṅkōl vāḷik koṭuvil āṭavar
vampa mākkaḷ uyirttiṟam peyartteṉa
veṅkaṭaṟ ṟaṭimutal paṭumuṭik 3keḻīiya
uṟupacik kuṟunari kuṟukal cellātu
māṟupuṟak koṭukkum attam
ūṟilar ākutal uḷḷā māṟē
itu, poruḷmuṭittu vantāṉ eṉpatu vāyilkaḷāṟ kēṭṭa tōḻi talivikku urittatu.
uri
uṟi tuṟantirunta muḷi vempuṟavil - maḻiyiṉmiyiṉ ularnta vevvitākiya kāṭṭiṉkaṇ; ceṅkatirc celvaṉ teṟutaliṉ - civanta oḷikkatir kaḷiyuṭiya celvaṉākiya ñāyiṟu vetupputalāl; maṇ paka ulaku mika varunti ayar vuṟukāli - nilam veṭittu ulakuyirkaḷ mikavum varuttit taḷarvuṟum vēṉil paruvattē; ceṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum - pirintu ceṉṟā reṉiṉum; naṉṟu ceytaṉar eṉac collil teḷippavum - ēṉik kārmutaliya paruvaṅkaḷ ēṟpuṭiya vaṉmiyiṉ nallatē ceytār eṉṟu tūya coṟkaḷāl nī teḷintu koḷḷal vēṇṭit teḷitturittum! teḷi tal cellāy - nī teḷiyātu varuntutaliyē cey toḻintāy; ceṅkōl vāḷik koṭuvil āṭavar - cevvitāy nēriya ampiṉiyum vaḷinta villiṉiyu muṭiya, kaḷvar; vampamākkaḷ uyirt tiṟam peyartteṉa - putiyarāy varuvōrik koṉṟu avartam uyirip pōkkiyataṉāl; veṅkaṭaṟṟu aṭimutal paṭumuṭi keḻīiya - vevviya kāṭṭil utirntu kiṭakkum ili kaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭu mikka muṭināṟum piṇattip porunti yuṇṇum; uṟupacik kuṟunari - mikka paciyiyuṭiya kuṟunarikaḷ; kuṟukal cellātu māṟupuṟak koṭukkum - nāṟṟam poṟātu aṇukāmal piṉṉē ceṉṟu niṟkum; attam ūṟila rākutal uḷḷāmāṟu - vaḻikaḷi ūṟu ciṟitumiṉṟik kaṭantu varuvar eṉpati niṉiyāmiyāl e.ṟu.
puṟaviṉkaṇ ceṅkatirccelvaṉ teṟutaliṉ, ulaku varunti ayarvuṟukāli, ceṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum, naṉṟē cey taṉar eṉac collil teḷippavum, attam ūṟila rākutal uḷḷāmiyāl teḷital cellāy eṉak kūṭṭi, ip pōtu vantārākaliṉ iṉiyēṉum teḷivāyāka eṉa eñci niṉṟa coṟkaḷip peytu viṉimuṭivu ceyka. uṟi - maḻi; "uṟivēṇṭu poḻutiṟ peyalpeṟṟōrē1" eṉpatu kāṇka. muḷital - īramiṉṟi ularntu keṭal; "muḷi puṟkāṉam2" eṉpatu kāṇka. ceṅkatirc celvaṉ - ñāyiṟu. veyil vemmi mikutiyāl īram pularnta cila iṭaṅkaḷ veṭippatu iyalpeṉa aṟika. annilappakuti kaḷimaṇ kalanta pakutiyāyiṉ veṭippukkaḷ mika akalamum āḻamumāka irukkum. ulaku, nalliyal piḻanta mullippakutiyum ataṉ kaṇ vāḻum uyirkaḷum kuṟittu niṉṟatu; mivari yulakam, kāṭuṟi yulakam, perumaṇa lulakam eṉṟāṟpōla "uṟi tuṟan tirunta vempuṟavu" eṉpatum itu nōkki yeṉka. ayarvu - taḷarcci mikuti. ēṉik kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi eṉpaṉa maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic ceṉṟu poruḷ viṉi ceytaṟkēṟṟa vaḷaviya kālamiṉmiyiṉ vēṉiṟpōtu naṉṟāyiṟ ṟeṉka. tāṉ vakutturittalum, cāṉṟōr kūṟiyaṉa eṭutturittalum, eṉaccol iruvaki yātaliṉ iraṇṭum aṭaṅkac collil eṉṟār. teḷital cel lāy, teḷiyā yāyiṉi eṉṟavāṟu. ampiṉ kōliṭattuk kōṭṭa muṇṭāyiṉ virintu iṉitu cēṟal ākāmi paṟṟic ceṅkōl vāḷi yeṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. piṟarum "ceṅkō lampiṉar"1 eṉavum, "kurutiyoṭu paṟitta ceṅ kōl vāḷi"2 eṉavum kūṟutal kāṇka. vampamākkaḷ, vēṟunāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum putitāy varum makkaḷ. kaṭaṟu - kāṭu, aṭi - ili; muṭināṟum piṇatti muṭiyeṉṟatu ākupeyar. ummi eñci niṉṟatu; muṭināṟṟam paṟṟi vantu kūṭikkoḷvatu paṟṟi, paṭumuṭi keḻīiya eṉṟum, virumpi yuṇṭaṟ kēṟṟa mikka paciyuṟṟu varuntiṟṟāyiṉum muṭināṟṟa mikutiyāl kuṟukāmi terittaṟkuk kuṟunari kuṟukal cellātu māṟupuṟak koṭukkum eṉṟum, kūṟiṉār. 'māṟu', ētup poruḷpaṭa vanta iṭiccol; "maṟampukal mintaṉ malinta māṟē"3 eṉa varutal kāṇka.
maḻiyiṉmiyālum, veyil vemmiyālum mullippuṟavu nalliyal piḻantu veṭippukkaḷ uṟṟut taṉṉakattē vāḻum uyirkaṭku varuttam nalkuvataṉāl, avi āṟṟātu melikiyil avvaḻiyē celvōrkku ataṉāl uṇṭākum iṉṉalukku elliyiṉmiyiṉ; atu cēṟaṟku ēṟṟataṉṟu eṉpāḷ ulakumika varunti ayar vuṟukāli eṉṟum, kālamum ataṟku vāyppuṭittaṉṟu eṉṟaṟkuc ceṉṟaṉarāyiṉum eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. ñāyiṟu teṟutaliṉ, ulaku mika varunti ayarpuṟuvatu kūṟit talimakaḷ ceṉṟatu taṉakku āṟṟāmi tantu varuttam eytuvikkiṉṟatu eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟik koṇṭeṭuttu moḻintu, ataṉi oru maruṅku taḻuviyum maṟuttum col nikaḻttuvatu paṟṟi. āyiṉum eṉṟatu, talivi kūṟṟi maṟut taṟku. uyarvaṟa vuyarnta talimakaṉāṉa kātalaṟku ikkālamum neṟiyum iṭiyūṟu ceyvaṉa valla eṉpatupaṭa itu niṉṟatu. iccelavāl kālamuṇarntu ceytaṟkuriya poruḷ viṉi kaḷic ceytu muṭikkum viṉit tiṇmiyum, kaṭipōkac ceyalāṟṟum viṉiyāṇmiyum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutaliṉ, ataṉi vitantu naṉṟu ceytaṉar eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. talimakaṉatu ceyal naṉmiyit tāṉ pala ētu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷāl vaṟpuṟuttiṉamiyum, cāṉṟōr ceṉṟa neṟi yeṉap piṟar kūṟiyavaṟṟi eṭutturittu vaṟpuṟuttiṉami yum tōṉṟa collil teḷippavum eṉṟum; ivvāṟu tōḻi yuritta vaṉpuṟikaḷāl talimakaḷ tēṟṟamum, teḷivum koḷḷātu varuntiyiruntami paṟṟit teḷital cellāy eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṉi ippōtu tōḻi kūṟaṟkuk kāraṇam, talimakaṉ ceyviṉi muṟṟi mīṇṭāṉ pōlum eṉat talimakaḷ uyttuṇarntu koḷḷutal vēṇṭi eṉa uṇarka. vevviya kaṭaṟṟiṉkaṇ putiyarāy varuvōr cellutal kūṭātākavum, ceṉṟamiyiṉ, putu vōri vampa mākkaḷ eṉṟum; avarkaḷuṭiya aṟi yāmikku iraṅkātu uyir kollum maṟavaratu koṭumi pulappaṭa uyirttiṟam peyartteṉa eṉṟum; iṟanta makka ḷiṉ uṭampi erittalō maṇṇiṟ putittalō ceyyātu, kāṭṭilikaḷāl mūṭip piṇan tiṉṉum narikaḷum neruṅkā vāṟu muṭināṟu māṟu iṭṭuc cellum avaratu vaṉ kaṇmi tōṟṟuvittaṟku uṟupacik kuṟunari kuṟukal cel lātu māṟupuṟak koṭukkum eṉṟum; eṉavē attakiya koṭiyavar iyaṅkum kaṭuñcuram celvōr pērāṟṟalum, peruvaliyum uṭiya rāyiṉaraṉṟi ūṟiṉṟip peyartal iyalātu eṉṟum; talivaruṭiya talimimuṉ akkoṭi yōratu koṭumiyum, curattiṉ vemmiyum āṟṟalaḻintu oḷimuṉ iruḷpōlak keṭumeṉṟum; ataṉāl avar ūṟu oru ciṟitu miṉṟi vantuḷḷār kāṇ eṉpāḷ; ūṟila rākutal eṉṟum, itaṉi yāṉ vaṟpuṟuttiya pōtum, avaratu māṇpiṉi uḷḷik kāṇāmiyāl nī teḷital cellātu varuntiṉi eṉpāḷ uḷḷāmāṟu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
naṉṟu ceytaṉareṉac colliṉ teḷippavum. talivar ūṟilarāyp peyarvar eṉpati nī uḷḷāmiyāl teḷi tal cellāy āyiṉi; yām naṉku aṟivō mākaliṉ miki yeṉṉum accattāl niṉṉic cūḻa iruntum neruṅkik kūṟutal māṭṭēmāyiṉam eṉṟaṟku, vēṭarkaḷ kāṭṭu ilikaḷāl mūṭic ceṉṟa piṇatti uṟupacik kuṟunari kuḻīi yiruntum kuṟukal cellātu māṟu puṟakkoṭukkum eṉṟu tōḻi uṇarttiṉāḷ. itaṉāl, talivaṉatu vara vuṇarntu talivi makiḻvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
165. nalveḷḷiyār
āciriyar nalveḷḷiyārip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ muṉpē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ nalla poruḷvaḷamum tūya iṉiya colvaḷamum peṟṟavi. ivar peyar nal vēḷviyār, nālkūr veḷḷiyār eṉṟu ēṭeḻutiṉōrāl kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veḷḷiyār eṉṟatu payiṉṟa vaḻakkiṟ ṟātaliṉ, atuvē īṇṭu mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. nālkūr eṉpatu uṇmip pāṭamāyiṉ atu cōḻa nāṭṭu nāṅkūrātal vēṇṭum.  nāṉku eṉpatu, paṇṭi nāḷil nālku eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉami tokinūlkaḷāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭṭatu. veḷḷi, veḷḷi eṉpaṉa makkaṭkup peyarātal iṉṟum tamiḻakattil uṇṭu.
taliviyi viriya varintu koḷḷātu mēṟkoṇṭa kaḷaviṉiyē nīṭṭittu vantāṉ talimakaṉ. avaḷ uḷḷat teḻunta kātal peruki avaṉi oru noṭiyum pirintirukka māṭṭāta niliyiṉi eytuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉi yuṇarnta tōḻi veḷippaṭiyākavum kuṟippākavum talivaṉik kāṇum pōtellām varivu kaṭāviṉāḷ. kaḷaviṭip peṟum iṉpattiyē avaṉ peritum virumpi niṉṟamiyiṉ tōḻikku maṉavaruttam mikuntatu. ataṉit talimakaḷum uṇarntāḷ. "nam karuttip paṉmuṟiyum pallāṟṟāṉum eṭutturittum, kātalar, varintu kōṭali niṉiyā rāyiṉ, nammi maṇantu kōṭaṟkuriya nallaṉpu illāta avaratu naṭpu avvaḷavil niṉṟoḻika eṉa viṭuvatu tāṉ avarkkum ottatu" eṉat tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. kūṟiṉavaḷ, "ataṉi urittavaḻiyum, avar pakaliṉum iraviṉum namporuṭṭu vantu iṭarppaṭu vatik kiviṭal ilar; vaḻiyarumiyō kāvaṟ kaṭumiyō ētamikutiyō oṉṟum niṉiyātu vantu pōvatil ciṟitum kuṉṟiya tilarākaliṉ yāṉ ceyva taṟiyātu varuntukiṉṟēṉ; inniliyil notumalar makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭi viṭutta tūtukaḷ pala nam maṉikkaṇ vantuḷḷaṉa. tantiyum taṉṉi yarum avarkaṭku makaṭkoṭi nērvarāyiṉ namatu nili eṉṉāvatu? eṉṟāḷ. ataṉāl talimakaṭku accamum avalamum perukavē, tōḻi, maṟupaṭiyum taliviyi nōkki, "tōḻi, iṉi nām tāyarkku aṟattoṭu niṟpatallatu vēṟu uytiṟam illi" eṉṟāḷ. avar collāṭṭiṉic ciṟip puṟam pōntu kēṭṭirunta talimakaṉ, talimakaḷ aṟattoṭu niṟkuṅkāl, tāṉ avaḷi varitaṟku muyalāviṭiṉ taṉ āṇmikku mācum paḻiyumām eṉṟu eṇṇi, virintu ceṉṟu makaṭkōṭaṟ kuriyaṉa muyaṉṟu varivoṭu varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, varivu kaṭāyavaḻiyum varitali muyalāta talimakaṉ uḷḷatti, virintu varivu mēṟkoḷḷumāṟu cūḻnturikkum tōḻiyiṉ mati nuṭpattik kaṇṭa cāṉṟō rākiya nalveḷḷiyār, ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār. karutiya karuttuk kēṭpōr uḷḷattil oḻiyātu tōṉṟi uriya payaṉi viḷikkumāṟu eñcak kūṟum colnalam ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ciṟantu niṟpatu nōkkattakkatu.
amarkkaṇ āmāṉ aruniṟam uḷkātu
paṇitta pakaḻip pōkkuniṉintu 1kāṉavar
aṇaṅkoṭu niṉṟatu malivāṉ koḷkeṉak 
kaṭavuḷ ōṅkuvari pēṇmār vēṭṭeḻuntu
kiḷiyoṭu makiḻum kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ
aṭitarun tōṟum2 arumitaṉak kurippa
nappuṇar villā nayaṉil lōrnaṭ
paṉṉa ākuka 3eṉṉā
4ōlkāṉ tōḻi palkiṉa tūtē
itu, notumalar variyum paruvattut tōḻi talivikku aṟattoṭunili payappac colliyatu; varivu malintatūumām.
uri
amarkkaṇ āmāṉ aruniṟam uḷkātu - amartta kaṇṇiyuṭiya āmāviṉ marumam nōkkik kāṉavar talivaṉ viṭutta pōtu ataṉ uḷḷē ūṭuruvic cel lātu; paṇitta pakaḻip pōkku kāṉavar niṉintu - vaḷintu ceṉṟa ampiṉ celavukkuriya kāraṇattik kāṉavar tammiṟ kūṭi ārāyntu; aṇaṅkukoṭu niṉṟatu - immalikkaṇ aṇaṅku vantuṟṟatu pōlum; malivāṉ koḷkeṉa - teyvam maliyēṟi vāṉakam celvatāka eṉṟu; kaṭavuḷ ōṅkuvari pēṇmār - kaṭavuḷ taṅkutalāl uyarnta maliyi vaḻipaṭuvārāy; vēṭṭu eḻuntu kiḷi yoṭu makiḻum kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ - virumpic ceṉṟu uṟaviṉa rōṭē kūṭi viḻā eṭuttu makiḻum kuṉṟukaḷiyuṭiya nāṭaṉākiya talimakaṉ; aṭitarum tōṟum - nampāl varuntōṟum; arumi taṉakku urippa - avaṉatu kūṭṭam peṟutaṟkuḷḷa arumikaḷi avaṟku veḷippaṭiyākavum kuṟippākavum paṉmuṟiyum eṭuttuc colliyum; nappuṇarvu illā nayaṉ illōr naṭpu aṉṉa ākuka eṉṉā - nammi varintu koḷḷāmikkuriya nallaṉpu illātāruṭiya naṭpu evvāṟu niṉṟuvaṟṟumō avvāṟu ākuka eṉṟu collavum; olkāṉ - iraviṉum pakaliṉum vāyppuḻip pōntu taliyaḷi ceytalil kuṉṟuvatilaṉ; tōḻi -; palkiṉa tūtu - mikap palavāy uḷḷaṉa makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭi notumalar viṭuppa vantuḷḷa tūtukaḷ; ākavē, iṉi nām tāyarkku aṟattoṭu niṟpatallatu vēṟu ceyvaki illi e-ṟu.
tōḻi, kuṉṟanāṭaṉ aṭitaruntōṟum, arumi urippavum, naṭpu eṉṉa. aṉṉavākuka eṉṉavum olkāṉ; ākaliṉ, iṉi aṟattoṭu niṟṟaliyaṉṟi vēṟu ceyal illi eṉak kūṭṭiviṉi muṭivu ceyka, kāṉavar' pēṇmār, eḻuntu makiḻum kuṉṟanāṭaṉ eṉa iyiyum.
maruṇṭa pārvi yuṭimipaṟṟi āmāviṉ kaṇ amartta kaṇ eṉappaṭṭatu, aruniṟam, mārpu; īṇṭu marumam kuṟittu niṉṟatu, uḷkutal - ūṭuruvic cēṟal, paṇittal - kiḷitta leṉṉum poruḷ vaḻiyākak kōṇutal kuṟittu niṉṟatu, pakaḻi - ampu. nērmiyum kūrmiyum oruṅkuṭiya ampu. nērmi tiṟampik kōṭiyatu tīkkuṟiyātal paṟṟik kāṉavar tammiṟ kūṭi ataṟkuriya ētuviṉi ārāytaliṉ pōkku niṉintu eṉṟum, kuṉṟakam aṇaṅku tāk kuṟṟatu eṉavum, ataṟku vaḻipāṭutāṉ kaḻuvāyāvatu eṉavum tēṟiṉamiyiṉ aṇaṅkoṭu niṉṟatu eṉṟum, mali vāṉ koḷkeṉak kaṭavuḷ ōṅkuvari pēṇmār eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. kuṉṟiṉkaṇ maḻimikap peyyiṉum peyal kuṉṟiṉum aṇaṅku tākku eṉṟu koṇṭu kāṉavar vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu paṇṭināḷi marapu, "malivāṉ koḷkeṉa uyarpali tūuy, māri yāṉṟu maḻimēk kuyarkeṉak kaṭavuḷ pēṇiya kuṟavar mākkaḷ1" eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. mali vāṉkoḷka eṉpataṟku mali yēṟuka eṉiṉum amiyum, teyva vaḻipāṭṭiṉkaṇ cilar teyva maruḷkoṇṭu teyvam kūṟuvatākac cilacolli muṭivil maruṭci teḷitalit teyvam maliyēṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatum, koḷvatum iṉṟum toṇṭi nāṭṭu ūrp puṟaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum peruvaḻakku. vāṉkoḷal oru collāy, ēṟutal, mīccelal eṉap poruḷpaṭum. ummi vikārattāl tokkatu. puṇar villāmikku ētu nayaṉiṉmi eṉa aṟika. nayaṉ - nallaṉpu, nayaṉillōr naṭpu eṉṉavākumō - aṉṉavākuka eṉa virittu iyittuk koḷka. olkutal - kuṉṟutal, tūtu - uyartiṇip poruṭkaṇ vanta aḵṟiṇic col; vēntu - aracu amiccu eṉṟāṟpōla, 'pēṇmār', mārīṟṟu viṉimuṟṟu; viṉiyoṭu muṭital itaṟku ilak kaṇameṉa aṟika.
kaḷavē virumpi variyātu nīṭṭittami niṉintu, tōḻi, avaṉ varuntōṟum varivu kaṭāvuvatil oḻi yāmiyiṉ kuṉṟanāṭaṉ aṭitarun tōṟum arumi taṉakku urippavum eṉṟāḷ. arumi, vaḻiyarumiyum, kāvalarumiyum, tāyar mutaliyōr tuñcāmiyum, alla kuṟip paṭutalu mākiyavaṟṟāl talimakaṉit talippeyal arumi, ataṉik kēṭṭa māttiriyē teruṇṭu vari taṟpāla ṉākavum, talimakaṉ atu ceyyātu kaḷavē virumpiṉami tōṉṟa urippavum eṉṟāḷ. empāl mikka aṉpiṉi yātaliṉ, niṉatu naṭpu nayaṉillār naṭpup pōla niṉṟu vaṟṟuka eṉat taṉvayiṉ urimiyum, avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum paṭak kūṟiṉami tōṉṟa nappuṇarvillā nayaṉillōr naṭpu aṉṉa vākuka eṉṟāḷ. ataṉāl avaṉ varivu karutāmipaṟṟi eṉṉā eṉṟum, varivu muyalāṉāyiṉum kaḷaviṉkaṇ vantu pōvatil orunāḷum kuṉṟāmiyiṉ avaṉi aṉpilaṉ eṉat tuṇitaṟkum iṭaṉiṉṟu eṉpāḷ olkāṉ eṉṟum, notumalar varivu talimakaḷ cevikku iṉṉā tākaliṉ ataṉik kūṟaluṟuvatu paṟṟit tōḻi eṉṟum, iṉic ceyaṟpālatu aṟattoṭu niliyē eṉa uyttuṇarak kūṟutaliṉ palkiṉa tūtē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. toṭutta ampu, āmāviṉ aruniṟam pukātu paṇittuc ceṉṟa pōkku niṉintu, kaṭavuṭ pēṇik kāṉavar makiḻvar eṉṟatu, virintu varital vēṇṭi nām talimakaṟ kuritta kūṟṟup payaṉpaṭāmiyiṉ tāyarpāl aṟattoṭu nili vakiyāl notumalar varivuvilakki makiḻvōm eṉat tōḻi kuṟippāy uritta vāṟu.
166. marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār
taṉ maṉam kātalitta taliviyi maṇantu maṉiyaṟam purintu varum talimakaṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta toru kaṭami kuṟittup piriyum karuttiṉa ṉāṉāṉ. āyiṉum, ataṉi avaṉ taṉ kātalikku urikku muṉpē, avaḷ uṇarntu koṇṭu pirivāṟṟāta varuttattāl mēṉi vēṟupaṭalāṉāḷ. avaḷatu avvēṟupāṭṭik kaṇṭa talimakaṉ peritum viyappuṟṟu avaḷit teḷivikkak karuti, naṅkāy, niṉ naṉmēṉiyum naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻum kūntalum, poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlap polivuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. uṉ maṉakkātaliṉ māṇ puṇarttum miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷum vaṉap paminta tōḷum, kuvaḷi malarum mūṅkilum pōṉṟu aḻaku tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; ivaṟṟik kāṇum yāṉ maṉam makiḻntu aṟaneṟikkaṇ nilipeṟa niṉṟu payaṉpeṟṟa mēlōr pōla, iṉpuṟu kiṉṟēṉ; peṟṟa putalvaṉum poytalāṭip polikiṉṟāṉ; yāṉ vēṟu nāṭu ceṉṟu ceytu muṭikka vēṇṭiya viṉi yoṉṟum illēṉ; ivvāṟṟāl oru kuṟiyum illāta yāṉ yātu kāraṇampaṟṟi niṉṉiṟ pirivēṉ; niṉpāl eṉ uḷḷattu uḷatākiya kātalō eṉiṉ atu kaṭaliṉum peritāy irāniṉṟatu; ātalāl, illāta pirivu niṉintu nī iṉintu vēṟupaṭutal naṉṟaṉṟu" eṉṟu collaluṟṟāṉ.
avaṉuṭiya kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaḷiṉ mēṉinalamum, kaṟpu māṇpum, putalvaṟpēṟum ākiya nalam puṉinturittu, vēṟu nāṭu ceṉṟu ceytaṟ kuriya toru viṉiyiṉmi kūṟumāṟṟāl, taṉatu nāṭṭu akavayiṉ ceṉṟu ceytaṟ kuriya toru viṉi kuṟittut tāṉ piriyak karutuvatum, vēṟu nāṭu cellāvāṟu avaḷpāl uḷatākiya peruṅkātal iṭiniṉṟu taṭuppatum, aḻakuṟa amintami kaṇṭu marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlum yāḻaniṉ
naṉṉar mēṉiyum nāṟiruṅ katuppum 
pōtum paṇiyum pōlum yāḻaniṉ
mātar uṇkaṇum1 vaṉappami tōḷum
ivikāṇ ṭōṟum2 akamalin tiyāṉum
aṟanili peṟṟōr3 aṉiyēṉ ataṉṟalip
4poṉpōṟ putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ
viṉiyum vēṟupulat tilaṉē niṉiyiṉ
yātaṉiṟ5 pirivām maṭanti
kātal tāṉum kaṭaliṉum peritē
itu, celavuk kuṟippuṇarntu vēṟupaṭṭa kiḻattikkut talivaṉ colliyatu.
uri
poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlum - cempoṉṉum nīla maṇiyum pōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa; niṉ naṉṉar mēṉiyum nāṟiruṅ katuppum - niṉṉuṭiya aḻakiya mēṉiyum maṇam kamaḻum kariya kūntalum; pōtum paṇiyum pōlum - kuvaḷippūviyum mūṅkiliyum pōlap polikiṉṟaṉa; niṉ mātar uṇkaṇum vaṉappami tōḷum - niṉ kātal cāṉṟa mitīṭṭiya kaṇkaḷum aḻakaminta tōḷkaḷum; ivi kāṇṭōṟum - iviyiṟṟik kāṇuntōṟum; yāṉum akammalintu - yāṉum maṉamakiḻntu; aṟam nili peṟṟōr aṉiyēṉ - aṟaneṟikkaṇ niṉṟōr peṟum payaṉip peṟṟēṉāyiṉēṉ; ataṉṟali - ataṉmēlum; poṉpōṟ putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ - poṉ pōlum eṉ makaṉum poytalāṭip poṟpuṟukiṉṟāṉ; vēṟu pulattu viṉiyum ilaṉ - vēṟṟunāṭṭakavayiṉ ceṉṟu ceytaṟkuriya viṉiyoṉṟum illēṉ; niṉi yiṉ - ārāyumiṭattu; yātaṉiṟ pirivām - yātu kāraṇatti muṉṉiṭṭu yāṉ niṉṉip pirital kūṭum; maṭanti - maṭan tiyē; kātal tāṉum kaṭaliṉum peritu - niṉpāl eṉakku uḷatākiya kātalō eṉiṉ kaṭaliṉum peritāy irā niṉṟatu; ākaliṉ yāṉ pirivēṉ eṉṟu karuti nī vēṟupaṭutal vēṇṭā, kāṇ e-ṟu.
maṭanti, niṉmēṉiyum katuppum poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlum; uṇkaṇum tōḷum, pōtum paṇiyum pōlum; ivi kāṇṭōṟum yāṉum akamalintu, aṉiyēṉ; ataṉṟalip putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ; yāṉ viṉi yum ilaṉ; kātal tāṉum kaṭaliṉum peritu; yātaṉiṟ pirivu ām; ākavē, nī vēṟupaṭutal vēṇṭā eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. maṇi - nīlamaṇi makaḷir kūntaṟku maṇiyi uvamaṅkūṟal marapu; "maṇimaru ḷimpāl"1 "maṇiyiruṅ katuppu"2 eṉa varutal kāṇka. naṟuneyyum naṟumalarum cūṭuvatāl kūntal naṟumaṇam nāṟuvatupaṟṟi nāṟiruṅ katuppu eṉṟār. mātar, kātal, pōtu īṇṭuk kuvaḷip pūviṉ mēṟṟu; kaṇṇukkuc ciṟanta uvama mātaliṉ, paṇi - mūṅkil. "aṟattāl varuvatu iṉpa"3 mākaliṉ, iṉpattāl aka malintāri aṟamnilipeṟṟā raṉiyar eṉṟār. peṟa larumi paṟṟip poṉpōṟ putalvaṉ eṉpa; "poṉpōṟ putalvaṉō ṭeṉṉīttōṉē"4 eṉpatu kāṇka. poy tal - ciṟuvar viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu vaki. pirivām eṉṟataṉit taṉmiviṉimuṟṟākak koḷḷātu, pirivu ām eṉap pirittup pirivu uḷatām eṉa urikka 'yāḻa', acinili.
taṉ pirivukkuṟip puṇarntu vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit teruṭṭaluṟukiṉṟavaṉ, poṉṉum maṇiyu mākiya poruḷ karutip pirikiṉṟēṉ eṉak kavala vēṇṭā; eṉpāl avaṟ ṟāl kuṟiyilli eṉpāṉ, poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlum niṉ mēṉiyum katuppum eṉṟāṉ. vēṟupaṭṭāḷi makiḻ vittaṟku avaḷ mēṉi nalattip pārāṭṭutaliṉum ciṟanta vaki vēṟiṉmiyiṉ niṉ naṉṉar mēṉi eṉṟum, nāṟiruṅ katuppu eṉṟum ciṟappittāṉ. piṉpu kaṇṇic ciṟappittum tōḷit tivantum avaḷi iṉpuṟuttukiṉṟamiyiṉ, pōtum paṇiyum pōlum yāḻa niṉ, mātar uṇ kaṇum vaṉappami tōḷum eṉṟāṉ. mēṉiyum kūntalum pārāṭṭak kēṭṭuc ciṟitu mukañcivantu piṇaṅkiṉāḷ pōla nōkkiya talimakaḷit tāṉum nērpaṭa nōkki, avaḷuṭiya kaṇṇiyum tōḷiyum pārāṭṭutalum avaḷ muṟuvalittāḷāka, akkāliavaḷ pārviyil tikaḻnta kātaṟciṟappi viyantu mātar uṇkaṇ eṉṟāṉ. mēṉiyum, katuppum, kaṇṇum, tōḷum, kāṇuntōṟum kātal kimmikku, uḷḷam uvakiyāl niṟital tōṉṟa, ivi kāṇṭōṟum akammalintu eṉṟāṉ. poruḷi naṉṉeṟiyil īṭṭutalum nalvaḻiyiṟ celaviṭutalu maṉṟi aṟam vēṟiṉmiyiṉ, ataṉāl poruṭpayaṉākiya iṉpam iṭiyaṟaviṉṟik kivarap peṟutaliṉ, aṟanili peṟṟōr aṉiyēṉ eṉṟāṉ. avarpāl naṉmakkaṭ pēṟum miṭi yiṉmiyum uḷavātal orutaliyātal paṟṟi, poṉpōṟ putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ eṉṟum, viṉiyum vēṟu pulattu  ilaṉē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. putalvaṟ peṟātār pukaḻ viḷikkum pōrviṉikku uriyarallar eṉa vilak kuṇpa rātaliṉ, putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ eṉa ataṉāṉum kuṟivilaṉ eṉavum, vēṟu pulam ceṉṟu ceyyum viṉiyiṉum vīṟuṭiyatu piṟitilli eṉpāṉ viṉiyum vēṟu pulattu ilaṉ eṉavum, aṅṅaṉam oṉṟu uḷatāyiṉum, atu kuṟittu niṉṉip piriyāvāṟu kātal kaṭalpōṟ peruki ataṉi mēṟkoḷḷāvāṟu ceyyu meṉpāṉ, kātaltāṉum kaṭaliṉum peritu eṉavum kūṟiṉāṉ. ivvāṟṟāl kuṟippāl pirivuṇmiyum, kātalarip pirintu ceyvatiṉum ataṟkuc ciṟappu vēṟiṉmiyum uṇart tiṉāṉāyiṟṟu. talivi āṟṟāmi tīrvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
"karaṇatti ṉamintu muṭinta kāli1" eṉṟa nūṟpāviṉ kaṇ, "pirivi ṉeccattup pulampiya iruvarip parivu nīkkiya pakutik kaṇṇum" eṉpataṉ uriyil ippāṭṭik kāṭṭi, "ikkūṟṟu iruvarmāṭṭum okkum" eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
167. ulōccaṉār
illiruntu aṟampurin toḻukum talimakaṉ vāḻvil, ūrp puṟattē vāḻum parattiyarpāl avaṉukkut toṭarpu uṇṭākiṟatu. taṉakkē yuriya talimakaṉatu mārpu nalattip piṟamakaḷir kāṇiṉum poṟāta maṉampaṭittavaḷ talivi. makkaḷiṉattu miki makaḷirākiya parattiyar, ulakil vāḻtaṟ kuriya rāyiṉum, tam nalattittantē vāḻtal vēṇṭum eṉṟa vitiyiṉmiyiṉ, atu ceytoḻukum parattipāl talimi makaḷirkku naṉmatipput tōṉṟuvatilli. avarkaḷ āṭal, pāṭal, aḻaku eṉṟa tammuṭiya nalaṅkaḷic celvarkkuk kāṭṭi, avar tarum poruḷpeṟṟu vāḻkki naṭattumiṭattu, taṅkaḷ maṉaṅkavarum māṇpuṭiyārit tammuṭiya āṭal mutaliya ceyalvakikaḷāl makiḻvikkuṅkāl, tam aḻakumikka uṭaliyum tantoḻivatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. avaruṭiya aḻakum peṇmiyu mākiya nalaṅkaḷil karuttic celuttum āṭavar, peruñ celvam tantu avarip pēṇuvatōṭu, avarit tamakkē uriyarāka varintu koṇṭu avaratu iṉpatti nukarntuṟivatum uṇṭu. avvāṟu variyappaṭṭa paratti yaruṭaṉ kūṭi yoḻukum talimakkaḷ oḻukkam puṟat toḻukkam eṉṟum, parattimi yoḻukkam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. tammi maṇantu kōṭaṟ kēṟṟa āṭavar illāmi yāl, maṇamiṉṟi vāṭum parattiyar, maṉivāḻvil uṟiyum āṇmakkaḷ uḷḷattit tampāl īrttu, avarkkut tamatu nalatti īntu poruḷpeṟṟu vāḻtalāl; avar palarum cīrkēṭu eytāvāṟu purappatu talimakkaṭkuk kaṭaṉākak karutappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, āṭal pāṭal aḻakukaḷāl ciṟappuṟum parattiyar, ūrkaḷil naṭakkum viḻākkaḷil iciyum kūttum nikaḻtti, makkaḷi iṉpuṟuttuvatu toḻilākak koṇ ṭiruntaṉar. nāṭṭiṟkut talivarāya āṭavarapparattiyar nikaḻttum kūttukkaḷi muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattuvatu annāḷil muṟiyākak karutappaṭṭatu. cēravēntaṉāṉa āṭukōṭ pāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ, taṉ nakarattup parattimakaḷirāṭum tuṇaṅkik kūttukkut talikki tantu toṭaṅkiṉami kāraṇamāka, avaṉ tēviyākiya, "īritaḻ maḻikkaṇ pēriyal arivi, oḷḷitaḻ aviḻakam kaṭukkum cīṟaṭip palcila kiṇkiṇi ciṟuparaṭu alippak kolpuṉaltaḷiriṉ naṭuṅkuvaṉaḷ niṉṟu niṉ, eṟiyar ōkkiya ciṟuceṅkuvaḷi, īyeṉa irappavum ollāḷ. nī emakku yāriyō1" eṉṟu ciṉantu kūṟip peyarnta ceyti, nāṭṭiṉ talimakaṉ parattiyarkkut talikki tantu pēṇiyatu muṟiyātaliyum; ataṉiyum, talimimakaḷir poṟātu vēṟupaṭutaliyum vaṟpuṟuttumāṟu kāṇalām. iḵtu annāḷil nilaviya peruvaḻak keṉpati, "melliṇark kaṇṇi milinta mintar, elvaḷi makaḷirtalikki tarūuntu2" eṉpataṉālum naṉkuṇaralām, vēntaratu talikki peṟṟu āṭal pāṭal nikaḻttum makaḷirit talikkōl makaḷir eṉṟum, ataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka avar mūṅkilāṟ ceytu poṟpūṇiṭṭuc ciṟappittu nalkappeṟum kōlit talikkōl eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivvāṟu talikkōl  peṟum vaḻakkāṟu nam tamiḻakattil iṭikkālac cōḻavēntar kālattum iruntami kalveṭṭukkaḷāl terikiṟatu3. ivvāṟu talikki tantum, āṭal pāṭal aḻakukaḷi nayantum parattiyar toṭarpu peṟum puṟattoḻukkatti āṇmakkaḷ, tam maṉimakaḷir karuvuṟṟirukkum kālattiṟ kikkoḷvatu vaḻakku. attoṭarpu maṉiviyāvāḷ karuvuyirttup peṟṟa makaṉ naṭikaṟṟu viḷiyāṭum cevvi eytumvari iruntu varum. itaṟkiṭiyē makaṉmukam kāṇṭal. maṉivi maṟupūppeytal (neyyāṭṭīraṇi) ākiya nikaḻccikkālattē, avaṉ attoṭarpu nīṅki maṉiyiṉkaṇṇē aṭaṅki yoḻiva tuṇṭu, puṟattoḻukkattiṉ nīṇṭa elli, makaṉ taḷarnaṭi yiṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭayarum kālamām eṉa aṟika.
talimakaṉatu puṟattoḻukkam taliviyiṉ uḷḷattil poṟāmiyum, peruñciṉamum uṇṭupaṇṇuvatu iyalpu. avaḷuṭiya iṉmukamum, nalvaravēṟpum karuti avaṉ parattiyar maṉikkaṇ vāḻum pāṇaṉit tūtu viṭuvatuṇṭu; pāṇar, talimakaṉ maṉiyil talimakaṭkum avaṟkum uṇṭākum pulavi, ūṭal ākiyavaṟṟi naṭuniṉṟu tīrkkum iṉpap paṇiyic ceytu oḻukuvar. talimakaṉ poruḷ viṉi kuṟittup pirinta vaḻi āṅkē ceṉṟu avaṉ peṟum veṟṟi yiṉip pāṭip pukaḻ niṟuvuvatum, avaṉ ceyyum āṇmi, koṭi mutaliya ceyal vakikaḷic ciṟappittup pāṭuvatum pāṇar marapu.
orukāl puṟattoḻukum talimakaṉ ceyali yuṇarntu ciṉam mikuntu vāyil maṟuttup piṇaṅki niṟkiṉṟāḷ avaṉ maṉik kiḻattiyākiya talimakaḷ; talimakaṉ ataṉi vāyilkaḷāl aṟikiṉṟāṉ. māṇpuṭiya taṉ maṉiyiṉkaṇ iruntu peṟum iṉpatti nāṭiya avaṉatu talimiyuḷḷam peritum varuntiṟṟu ataṉāl avaṉ pāṇaṉit taṉ maṉikkut tūtāka viṭuttāṉ. talimakaṉ pirivāl mēṉi vēṟupaṭṭu, nutal pacantu, uṭal melintiruntāḷāyiṉum, avaṉatu puṟattoḻukkattāl uḷatāya poṟāmi avaḷ uḷḷattil ōṅki yiruntatu. avaḷē talimakaṉ maṉikkup pēraraciyākaliṉ āṅkuṟiyum ēvalarum paṇippeṇkaḷum piṟa makaḷirum avaḷ kuṟippiṉvaḻi yoḻukuvatu iyalpu. pāṇaṉ tūtāka vantatit talimakaḷ kaṇṭāḷ; malar pōlum mukam civantatu; kaṇkaḷ civantaṉa; vāyitaḻ tuṭikkaluṟṟaṉa. uṭal melivāl uḷḷamum vaṉmiciṟitu kuṉṟiyiruntamiyiṉ, ataṉkaṇ eḻunta ciṉam eḷitiltaṇivatāyilli. anniliyil avaḷuṭiya tōḻi muṉvantu pāṇaṉik kaṇṭu, "pāṇaṉē, nī talimakaṉ viṭuppa vantuḷḷaṉi; nīyō, collum poruḷum naṉi ciṟappac curuṅkac collal mutaliya nalamellām amiyap pēci, yārporuṭṭup pēcukiṉṟāyō avarkku eytiya varuttam aṟavē māṟṟum vaṉmiyuṭiyi" eṉṟāḷ. pāṇaṉum taṉcol kēṭṭu maṉam kaṉintu meliyu māṟu pukaḻurikaḷ pala moḻiyaluṟṟāṉ. talivi vaḻiniṉṟu ciṉantupēca vanta tōḻiacciṉatti iḻantāḷ; "pāṇa em talivi mikka iḷamiyuṭiyavaḷ; muṉṉāḷ kāṉaṟcōlikkuc ceṉṟavaḷ, aṅkē muṉpu talimakaṉōṭu iṉpuṟṟirunta tiṟam niṉivukku vara anniliyil avaṉatu pirivu tōṉṟip pētuṟavu tantatu. ataṉāl avaḷ nutal muḻutum pacali pāyntu oḷikuṉṟi viṭṭatu; ataṉi uṇarāmal nī vaṇaṅkiya niliyum, paṇinta moḻiyum koṇṭu nallurikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟāy; avaṟṟālellām taliviyiṉ nutaṟpacappu nīṅkātu eṉpatu terintāyilli" eṉṟu urittāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ ivvuriyil, talimakaṉatu puṟattoḻukkam poṟāmiyāl talivi uḷḷattil nilavum ciṉamikutiyum, ataṉāl avaḷ eytiya pacappum kūṟi, nī pōntu kūṟum paṇimoḻikaḷ ippacappiṉi nīkkum valiyuṭiyavalla vākaliṉ, nī, niṉ talimakaṉpālē celka eṉa maṟutturikkum nayum tōṉṟuvatu kaṇṭa ulōccaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
karuṅkōṭṭup puṉṉik kuṭakkuvāṅku peruñciṉi
viruntiṉ 1veṇkuru kārppiṉ āay
vaṇmakiḻ nāḷavip paricil peṟṟa 
paṇṇami neṭuntērp pāṇiyiṉ olikkum
taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ tūtoṭum vanta 
payaṉteri paṉuval 2pitaṟ pāṇa
niṉvāyp paṇimoḻi kaḷiyā paṉmāṇ
putuvī ñāḻaloṭu puṉṉi tāam
maṇaṅkamaḻ kāṉal nuṇaṅkeḻil iḻanta3
iṟiyēr elvaḷik kuṟumakaḷ
piṟiyēr tirunutal pāaya pacappē.
itu, tōḻi pāṇaṟku vāyiṉmaṟuttatu; tūtoṭu vanta pāṇaṟkuc colliyatūumām.
uri
karuṅkōṭṭup puṉṉik kuṭakku vāṅku peruñciṉi - kariya kompukaḷiyuṭiya puṉṉiyiṉ vaḷinta periya kiḷiyiṉkaṇ iruntu koṇṭu; viruntiṉ veṇkuruku ārppiṉ - putiyavāy vanta veṇkurukukaḷ olikkumāyiṉ; āay - āy aṇṭiraṉuṭiya; vaṇmakiḻ nāḷavi - vaḷaviya kaḷḷuṇṭu makiḻntirukkum nāḷōlakkattil; paricil peṟṟa paṇṇami neṭuntērp pāṇiyiṉ olikkum - iravalarkaḷ paricilākappeṟṟa paṇṇutal aminta neṭiya tērkaḷiṉ maṇiyolipōla olikkum; taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ tūtoṭu vanta - taṇṇiya tuṟivaṉ poruṭṭut tūtāka vantuḷḷa; payaṉ teri paṉuval pital pāṇa - payaṉuṇ miyi ārāyntu teriya vēṇṭiya coṟkaḷiyuṭimiyāl varuttamuṭiya ṉākiya pāṇaṉē; niṉ vāyp paṇi moḻi kaḷiyā - niṉ vāyiṉiṉṟu varum meyyē paṇivāṉa coṟkaḷ tāmum nīkkuvaṉa vākā, kāṇ; paṉmāṇ putuvī ñāḻaloṭu puṉṉi tāam - palavāy māṇpuṟa malarnta putuppūkkaḷiyuṭiya ñāḻaluṭaṉē puṉṉi malarkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum; maṇaṅkamaḻ kāṉal - naṟuviya maṇaṅ kamaḻum kāṉaṟ cōli; nuṇaṅku eḻil iḻanta - nuṇṇiya taṉ aḻakiṉi iḻanta; iṟi ēr elvaḷik kuṟumakaḷ - iṟi poruntiya oḷiyiyuṭiya vaḷi yaṇinta iḷiyava ḷākiya taliviyatu; piṟiyēr tirunutal pāaya pacappu - piṟittiṅkaḷ pōṉṟu aḻakiya nutaliṭattē paranta pacappi e.ṟu.
tuṟivaṉ tūtoṭu vanta pāṇa, kāṉal. nuṇaṅkeḻil iḻanta, elvaḷik kuṟumakaḷ tirunutal pāaya pacappi niṉvāyp paṇimoḻi kaḷiyā, kāṇ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. eṉavē, nī iṉi iṅkē niṟpatiṟ payaṉiṉmi yiṉ niṉ talimakaṉpālē celka eṉṟavāṟām, kuṭakku vāṅku ciṉi, mika vaḷinta ciṉi; oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi, kuṭakku, vaḷivupporuṭṭākiya kuṭa eṉṉum uric collaṭiyāka vantatu; itu kuṭam, kuṭakku, kuṭanti eṉa vēṟu vēṟu vaṭivil tirintu varum. viruntu - putumi veṇ kuruku - veḷḷiya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya nīrppaṟavikaḷ; paruvaṅ kaḷ māṟupaṭun tōṟum vēṟuvēṟu nāṭukaṭkuc cēṟal puḷḷiṉaṅkaṭku iyalpu; nilavulakil itukāṟum ārāycci yāḷarkaḷāl 30,000 puḷvakikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; ippuḷḷiṉam ōr āṇṭiṉ perumpakuti orunāṭṭil irukkumāyiṉ avi annāṭṭukku uriyavākak karutappaṭum. uṇmiyē nōkkiṉ puḷḷiṉaṅkaṭku "yātāṉum nāṭā māl ūrāmāl" eṉpatu poruntum, orukālattil airōppāviṉ mēlikkōṭiyil kāṇappaṭum puḷḷiṉam, orukāl āciyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyilum, kīḻkkōṭiyilum kāṇappaṭuva tuṇṭu. maḻikkālattil māttiram āciyā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷi liruntu vaṭairōppāvukku mūvāyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa puḷvakikaḷ pōkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar7. itaṉi nōkkiṉ. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟiñci mutalāka vakut turitta nilappakutikaṭkuk karupporuḷ kūṟumiṭattup puḷḷiṉam, ennilattuk kāṇappaṭiṉum annilattukkē uriyatāka vārātu eṉṟum, āyiṉum enta nilattu vantuḷatō anta nilattukkuriya karupporuḷām eṉṟum urippārāy, "ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum. annilam poḻutoṭu vārā" eṉṟum, "āyiṉum vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itupaṟṟiyē vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantirunta veṇkurukiṉi, viruntiṉ veṇkuruku eṉṟār. makiḻ-kaḷ. āay - potiyiṉmalip pakutiyiluḷḷa āykkuṭiyit talinakarākak koṇṭu iṉṟuḷḷa kollattukkum tiruvataṅkōṭṭukkum iṭiyi luḷḷa vēṇāṭṭic caṅkakālattil āṇṭu vanta vēḷir kulak kuṟunilat talivaṉ; ivaṉi vēḷāy eṉṟum āay aṇṭiraṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvar. annāḷil mēli nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pōnta tālami mutaliya yavaṉarkaḷ ip pakutiyi āynāṭu eṉṟē kuṟittuḷḷaṉar; piṟkālat tiru vāṅkūr vēntarkaḷ taṅkaḷi vēṇāṭṭaṭikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷvatum ikkaruttē paṟṟi yeṉa aṟika, āay aṇṭiraṉuṭiya vaḷḷaṉmiyi uṟiyūr ēṇiccēri muṭamōciyār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr. pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. tērppāṇi, tēriṟ kaṭṭiya maṇiyiṉ ōci. collum poruḷum ovvāta poyyuri eṉṟaṟkup payaṉ teri paṉuval eṉṟār; meyyuri pōl tōṉṟi ārāyu miṭattup poyyāy oḻiyum col eṉṟavāṟu. piṟarum, "pāṇaṉ kiyati, vaḷḷuyirttaṇṇumi pōla, uḷ yātum illatōr pōrvi yañ col1" eṉṟum, "poypoti koṭuñcol2" eṉṟum kuṟippatu kāṇka paṇimoḻi, paṇivip pulappaṭuttum coṟkaḷ. nuṇaṅ keḻil eṉṟaṟkuk kūrmiyiyuṭiya aḻaku eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iṟi - cantu, el - ilakkam.
talimakaḷatu maṉi nōkkippōnta pāṇaṉ talivaṉ pukaḻip pāṭik koṇṭum avaṉuṭiya kātaṉmiyi viyantukoṇṭum vantamiyiṉ taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ tūtoṭu vanta pāṇa eṉṟāḷ. talimakaṉ pukaḻum kātalaṉpum piṟar kūṟak kēṭpiṉ taliviyiṉ uḷḷattu vemmi nīṅkum eṉṉum karuttiṉa ṉātalit tāṉ uṇarntu koṇṭami tōṉṟat taṇṇan  tuṟivaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ poruṭṭu vāyil vēṇṭi vantamipaṟṟit tūtoṭu vanta pāṇa eṉṟum vitantu moḻintāḷ. atu kēṭṭut taṉ karuttu muṭiyum eṉṟa kiḷarcciyuṭaṉ pāṇaṉ, talivaṉ ciṟappukkaḷi mikavum puṉintu kūṟalum, nī kūṟuvaṉa kūṟiyāṅkuk kōṭaṟkuriya vāymiyuṭiya vallavē eṉpāḷāy, payaṉteri paṉuval eṉṟāḷ. payaṉ nōkki illatu peytu kūṟuvaṉa vākaliṉ, niṉ coṟkaḷ ārāyn tallatu koḷḷaṟ kuriyavalla eṉṟu tōḻi colvatāka eṇṇip pāṇaṉ ciṟitu uḷḷattē ayarcciyuṟṟāṉ; aḵtu avaṉ meyppaṭṭut tōṉṟutalum, tōḻi atu nōkkip pitaṟ pāṇa eṉṟāḷ. avaṭkut tāṉ kūṟuvaṉa aṉit tum poyyoṭu paṭāta meymmi yurikaḷē eṉap paṇintu kūṟiṉāṉāka, niṉ vāy poy kūṟip payiṉṟatu eṉṟaṟku niṉ vāy moḻi eṉṟum, ammoḻikaḷ niṉ paṇivip pulappaṭuttuvaṉavē yaṉṟit talimakaḷ uḷḷat til nimirntu niṟkum ūṭali taṇittaṟkuriyavalla eṉpāḷ. paṇimoḻi eṉṟum, kaḷiyā eṉṟum icittāḷ. ūṭal taṇintālaṉṟip pacappu nīṅkāmiyiṉ, tirunutal pāaya pacappuk kaḷiyā eṉpā ḷāyiṉaḷ. avvūṭal tāṉum nuṇaṅkeḻil iḻantamiyāṟ piṟanta teṉṟum, eḻil pēṇum nōkkam iḷamikkuc ciṟappātaliṉ, iṟiyēr elvaḷik kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟum. piṟipōlum oḷimikka tirunuta lātaliṉ. pacappu iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṟa teṉṟaṟkup piṟiyēr tirunutal eṉṟum vakutturittāḷ. ñāḻaliṉ putuppūvōṭu puṉṉiyiṉ malarkaḷ paravutalāl, kāṉal maṇam kamaḻum eṉṟataṉāl, talimakaṉnalam putiyarāya parattiyarōṭu talimakaḷiyum oppakkoṇṭu iṉpuṟak karutukiṉṟāṉ eṉat talivi nalaṉ iḻantu ūṭutaṟkuriya ētuvit tōḻi uḷḷuṟut turittalāl maṇaṅkamaḻ kāṉal nōkki nuṇaṅ keḻil iḻantaṉaḷ talivi eṉṟāḷ. itaṉāṟ payaṉ tōḻi pāṇaṟku vāyil maṟuppatu.
168. maturi aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ 
iḷavēṭṭaṉār
iḷavēṭṭaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr oruvar ittoki nūṟkaṇ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟār; āyiṉum ivar peyar aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ eṉṟa toṭarāṟ ciṟappikkappaṭutalāl, iruvarum vēṟu vēṟātal viḷaṅkukiṟatu. akanāṉūṟu ivari maturi aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ eṉṟu kuṟippatāl ivviḷavēṭṭaṉār maturi nakarkkaṇ iruntu aṟuvi vāṇikam purintava reṉpatu teḷitām. aṟuvi vāṇikam, uṭi vāṇikam, ivar pāṭiya vākap pala pāṭṭukkaḷ tokinūlkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. āṭavar peṇṭir eṉṟa iruvar uḷḷattum muḷitteḻunta kātali naṉku vaḷarttu iruvaruyirum ōruyirāy oṉṟi amiyumāṟu ceytu vāḻkkit tuṇiva rāvatutāṉ maṉiyaṟam purintu māṇpeytum neṟiyil tamiḻar kaṇṭa paṇṭi nāḷip paṇpāṭu. taṉittuk kaṇṭa talivipāl karuttic celuttiya talimakaṉ, maṟunāḷum avaḷi avviṭattē kaṇṭu muṉṉāḷ muḷitta kātali uṟuti ceytukoṇṭu, avaḷ taṉ kaṇṇāṟ kuṟittuk kāṭṭiya tōḻiyi aṟintukoḷvāṉ; piṉpu tōḻiyoṭu collāṭi avaḷuṭiya naṭpip peṟṟut taṉakkum talimakaṭkum uḷatākiya kātaṟṟoṭarpi vali yuṭiya tākkik koḷḷa muyalvaṉ. talimakaḷum tōḻiyum kuṟikku miṭaṅkaḷil pōntu talimakaḷik kaṇṭu taṉ uḷḷattuk kātaliṉ māṇpi uṇarttuvaṉ; pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ vantoḻukum avaṉatu toṭarpu puṟattārkkut teriyiṉ varum ētattukku añcit tōḻi avaṉi iraviṉkaṇ vantu taliyaḷikkumāṟu vēṇṭuvaḷ. talimakaḷ uṟiyum ūr malināṭātaliṉ, maliyum kāṭum kaṭantu vaḻiyil vaṉa vilaṅkukaḷāl viḷiyak kūṭiya tīṅkukaḷip poruḷceyyātu vantu pōkum talimakaṉ māṇpit talimakaḷ naṉkuṇarntu pēraṉpu ceyyu mukattāl avaḷ avaṉiyiṉṟi yamiyāta kātalaṉpu koḷḷukiṉṟāḷ. iravuppōtiṉum pakaṟpōtiṉum iruvarum taṉittuk kāṇpatāl taliviyiṉ māṇpukku mācu uṇṭākumō eṉṟa accam tōḻikku uṇṭākiṟatu. tali makaṭku avaṉik kūṭiya vaḻip piṟakkum iṉpam, pirivāl keṭuvatu peruntuyaric ceykiṟatu. atu kāṇum tōḻi talimakaṉik kaṇṭu taliviyi varintu koḷvatē iṉic ceyaṟpālatu eṉat terivikka virumpukiṉṟāḷ. taliviyiṉ kātalaṉpum kaṟpu nalamum talimakaṉ uṇarntaṉa vātaliṉ, aṟiyātāṉ pōlak karuti varintu koḷḷumāṟu aṟi vuṟuttuvatu tōḻikku aṟamākat tōṉṟukiṉṟilatu. pakaṟkuṟi varuṅkāl avaṉi maṟuttu iravukkuṟi varuka eṉṟum, iravu varuvāṉip pakal varuka eṉṟum, iraṇṭum ākā eṉa orō vaḻi maṟuttum tōḻi urinikaḻttuvaḷ. talimakaṉ uṇmi yuṇarntu taliviyi varintukoḷvaṉ. iṅṅaṉam nikaḻum kātaloḻukkattil orunāḷ iraviṉkaṇ, taliviyiṉ maṉip pakkattē kuṟitta iṭamoṉṟukkut talivaṉ pōntu taṉ varukiyik kuṟikaḷāl terivittāṉ. talimakaḷum tōḻiyum ceṉṟu avaṉik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟaṉar. appōtu tōḻi, avaṉi nōkki, "naṉmalināṭaṉē, niṉṉiyē kātalittu niṉporuṭṭē iṅku uṟikiṉṟāḷ eṉ talivi: niṉakkuc ciṟitu ūṟu nēriṉum uyirvāḻāta attuṇip peruṅkāta luṭiya ḷākaliṉ, avaḷ uyirip poruḷāka nī niṉik kiṉṟāyilli. koṭiya pāmpukaḷ iyaṅkum kāṭṭiṉ vaḻi yākak kiyil ēntiya vēloṉṟē tuṇiyākakkoṇṭu maliccāra liṭattu em ciṟukuṭikku nī iraviṉ kaṇ varu kiṉṟāy; itu naṟpaṇpuṭiya ceyal eṉac cāṉṟōr pārāṭ ṭum takutiyatākātu; itaṉi niṉ maṉaṅkoḷal vēṇṭum" eṉṟu muṟiyiṭṭāḷ.
tōḻiyatu ivvuriyiṉkaṇ, taliviyatu kātal māṇpum iravukkuṟi varum neṟiyiṉatu ētamum kūṟi, ivvoḻukkam paṇpuṭiyār mēṟkoḷḷum naṟceyalaṉmiyiṉ, nī virintu ceṉṟu uriyaṉa muyaṉṟu ivaḷi varintu kōṭalē niṉakku nēriya paṇpākum eṉpatum; atu kēṭṭu avaṉum makiḻntu varivu mēṟkōṭalum kaṇṭa maturi aṟuvi vāṇikarāṉa iḷavēṭṭaṉār avaṟṟi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
curumpuṇa virinta karuṅkāl vēṅkip
peruñciṉit toṭutta koḻuṅkaṇ 1iṟāal
puḷḷuṟṟuk kacinta tīntēṉ kallaḷik
kuṟakkuṟu mākkaḷ uṇṭa 2miccil
puṉṟali manti vaṉpaṟaḻ nakkum
naṉmali nāṭa paṇpeṉap paṭumō
niṉnayan tuṟivi iṉṉuyir uḷḷāy
aṇaṅkuṭi araviṉ āriruḷ3 naṭunāḷ
mipaṭu ciṟuneṟi eḵtuṇiyāka 
āram kamaḻum mārviṉi
cāraṟ ciṟukuṭi īṅkunī varalē
itu, tōḻi iravukkuṟi maṟuttatu.
uri
curumpuṇa virinta karuṅkāl vēṅki - vaṇṭukaḷ moyttut tēṉuṇṇumāṟu malarnta kariya aṭiyiṉi yuṭiya vēṅkimarattiṉ; peruñciṉit toṭutta koḻuṅkaṇiṟāal - periya kiḷiyiṉkaṇ īṭṭi vitta koḻuviya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya tēṉaṭiyil; puḷḷuṟṟuk kacinta tīntēṉ kallaḷi - tēṉīkkaḷ neruṅkutalāṟ kiḻintu kacinta tīviya tēṉ kaṟkuḻiyil paṭintataṉi; kuṟakkuṟumākkaḷ uṇṭa miccil - kuṟavarkaḷiṉ ciṟuvarkaḷ vaḻittuṇṇa eñciya miccili; puṉṟali manti vaṉpaṟaḻ nakkum - pulliya taliyiyuṭiya mantiyiṉ valiya kuṭṭi pōntu nakki yuṇṇum; naṉmali nāṭa - nalla malināṭṭukkut talivaṉē; paṇpu eṉappaṭumō - pāṭaṟintoḻukum paṇpuṭimi eṉṟu cāṉṟōrāl karutappaṭa māṭṭā taṉṟō; niṉ nayantuṟivi iṉ uyir uḷḷāy - niṉṉiyē kātalittu iṅkē uṟiyum talimakaḷiṉ iṉiya uyirkku uḷatākum tīṅkiṉi nī eṇṇukiṉṟāyilli; aṇaṅkuṭi araviṉ āriruḷ naṭunāḷ - accattiyuṭiya pāmpukaḷ iyaṅkum niṟinta iruḷ mikka naḷḷiravil; mipaṭu ciṟu neṟi - kuḷirmukil paṭinta ciṟuvaḻiyil; eḵku tuṇiyāka-kiyilēntiya vēliyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭu; āram kamaḻum mārpiṉi - cantaṉattiṉ maṇam nāṟum mārpiṉi yuṭiyiyāy; cāral ciṟukuṭi īṅku nī varal - maliccāraṟ puṟattiluḷḷa ciṟukuṭiyākiya iṅku nī varuvatu e.ṟu.
nāṭa, niṉṉayantu uṟivi iṉṉuyir uḷḷāy; naṭunāḷ, ciṟuneṟi eḵku tuṇiyāka mārpiṉi, nī ciṟukuṭi īṅku varal, paṇpu eṉappaṭumō, kāṇ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. pūkkaḷ itaḻ virital, vaṇṭiṉam iṉitu pukuntu tampāluḷḷa tēṉip peṟumāṟṟāl, karukkoṇṭu iṉam perukkum naṟpaṇi kuṟitta tāyiṉum, tēṉuṇṇal muṟpaṭak kaṇkūṭāka nikaḻtaliṉ ataṉiyē vitantu curumpuṇa virinta vēṅki eṉṟār. peruṅkiḷiyoṭu kūṭiya uyariya maramātaliṉ karuṅkāl vēṅki eṉṟār. karuṅkāl - parutta aṭimaram. koḻuṅkaṇ iṟāl - akaṉṟa koḻuviya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya tēṉ aṭi; akkaṇ tēṉīkkaḷ oṭuṅkiyuṟitaṟkum iṭa māmeṉa aṟika. paṟakkum ceyalpaṟṟi, tēṉī puḷḷeṉap paṭṭatu. 'uṟṟu', ceyteṉaccam kāraṇapporuṭṭu; maḻi peytu kuḷam niṟintatu eṉṟāṟ pōla. kallaḷi - kaṟkuḻi, kuṟumākkaḷ - iḷañciṟuvar. airōppiya nāṭṭavari airōppiyareṉṟum, āppirikka nāṭṭiṉari āppirikka reṉṟum, āntira nāṭṭāri āntirareṉṟum, kēraḷa nāṭṭārik kēraḷa reṉṟum kūṟuvatu pōlap paṇṭi yōr kuṟiñcinilattavarik kuṟavar, vēṭṭuvar eṉṟum, mullinilattāri āya reṉṟum, marutanilattavari uḻavar, vēḷāḷa reṉṟum, neytal nilattārip paratavar eṉṟum kuṟittu vaḻaṅkiṉar, viṭāppiṭi yuṭimi paṟṟik kuraṅkiṉ kuṭṭi vaṉpaṟaḻ eṉappaṭṭatu. paṇpu - avaravar pāṭaṟintu oḻukum naṟpaṇpu, uṟivi - uṟi pavaḷ. kaṇṭārkku accam taruvatu paṟṟi aṇaṅkuṭi yaravu eṉṟār; "pāmpeṉṟāl paṭiyum naṭuṅkum" eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. mi - kūtirkālak kuḷirmukil. eḵku - vēṟpaṭi.
uḷḷuṟiyāl notumalar varivu karuti muyalvataṉi urikkiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, veḷippaṭiyākat talimakaḷiṉ uḷḷak kātalip pulappaṭuttaṟku, niṉ nayantuṟivi eṉavum, tamar piṟarkku makaṭkoṭi nērvā rāyiṉ, ivaḷ uyirvāḻāḷ eṉpāḷ. iṉṉuyir uḷḷāy eṉavum, neṟiyarumi kūṟi iravukkuṟi maṟuttu varivu kaṭāvum karuttiṉa ḷātaliṉ, aṇaṅkuṭi araviṉ āriruḷ naṭu nāḷ mipaṭu ciṟuneṟi eṉavum, nī vēlēnti varuvatu kāṇiṉ ciṟu kuṭiyiṉar tīṅkiḻippa reṉpāḷ, cāraṟ ciṟukuṭi eṉavum. nī avarkaṭpulaṉākāmi variṉum niṉ mārpiṟ cāntam maṇam kamaḻntu niṉṉik kāṭṭiviṭum eṉṟaṟku, āram kamaḻum mārpiṉi eṉavum, iṉic cāṉṟōr tuṇiyāka varivoṭu varutal paṇpākumē yaṉṟi, eḵku tuṇiyāka varutal paṇpu eṉappaṭātu eṉpāḷ, eḵku tuṇiyāka īṅku nī varal paṇpeṉappaṭumō eṉavum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. tēṉ aṭiyiṟ kacinta tēṉik kuṟakkuṟumākkaḷ uṇṭu kaḻiya. miccili mantiyiṉ vaṉpaṟaḻ nakkum eṉṟatu, talimakaḷpāl katirttut tōṉṟum nalatti nī nukarntu kaḻiyavum notumalar variyak karutukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatām.
talimakaṉ eḵku tuṇiyāka, mārpiṟ cāntam kamaḻa variṉ, ciṟu kuṭiyōr tīṅkiḻippa reṉat tōḻi kūṟuvatu pōla, "kaṭampucūṭi uṭampiṭi yēnti, maṭanti poruṭṭāl varuvatu ivvūr, aṟumukamilli aṇimayi lilli. kuṟamakaḷ illi ceṟitōḷ illi. kaṭampūṇ teyva māka nērār, maṭavarmaṉṟic ciṟukuṭi yōrē" eṉap piṟāṇṭum tōḻiyorutti kūṟuvatu kāṇka.1
169. iṭikkāṭaṉār
kāṇappaṭum nam ulakattip paṇṭi nāḷilēyē nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, vicumpu eṉa aintu kūṟukaḷāka vakuttu avaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷi aṟiya aṟiñar muyaṉṟaṉar. "nilamtī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum. kalanta mayakkam ulakam" eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟuvatik kāṇalām. ivaṟṟōṭu kālam, uyir, kaṭavuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟum kūṭṭi eṭṭu eṉak kōṭalum oṉṟu. ivi eṇporuḷ eṉṟu ciṟappākak kūṟappaṭum. ulakiyaṟ poruḷiṉ iyalpukaḷi mēlum ārāyntavaḻi ovvoru poruḷiṉ kaṇṇum uḷḷīṭāka iruvakip paṇpukaḷ pulaṉāyiṉa; avi poruṇmip paṇpu, toḻiṟpaṇpu eṉpaṉa. poruṇmiyip poruḷ eṉavum, toḻiṉmiyi viṉi yeṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñar kuṟittaṉar. viṉi yeṉpatu poruḷiṉ nīṅkātu ataṉiyē iṭamākak koṇṭu ataṉ āṟṟal vaṭivāy iruppatu. poruḷ oṉṟu ōriṭattu iruttaṟkum, irātu iyaṅkutaṟkum ētuvāy uḷḷa āṟṟalē viṉiyāvatu. poruḷum, viṉiyumākiya iraṇṭiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē makkaḷ maṉattil eṇṇaṅkaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭivam collātal koṇṭē paḻantamiḻp perumakkaḷ, makkaḷatu pēcciṭi nilavum coṟkaḷ aṉittum, peyar viṉi eṉa iraṇṭē vakippaṭum eṉṟaṉar; "colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉ, ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟinticiṉōrē" eṉa āciriyar eṭutturippatu kāṇka.
ipporuḷ aḷavu, vaṭivu, niṟam, cuvi mutaliya kūṟukaḷākak kāṇappaṭum uruvuṭiyaṉavum, kāṇappaṭāmiyāl uru villaṉavum eṉa iruvakiyāka iyalum; eṉiṉum iv vuruvamum, uruviṉmiyum poruṭku uriyavi yalla; avaṟ ṟiṉ uḷḷīṭāy iyakkum viṉikkē uriyavi eṉpatu piṉpu kāṇappaṭṭatu. iṉṟiya viññāṉa neṟiyum poruḷkaḷi aṇu aṇuvāka nuṇukkic ceṉṟu muṭivil ettakiya nuṇ karuvikkum pulaṉākāta aḷavil nuṇukkiyavaḻi āṟṟalāy (Energy) viṉiyāy muṭivatik kaṇṭurikkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl poruḷiṉ ākkamum vaṭivum viṉiyē eṉpatu teḷivām. ātalāl poruḷ tōṉṟum pōtē viṉiyum uṭaṉ tōṉṟukiṟatu eṉpatu mukkālattum oppaniṟkum uṇmi yākiṟatu. makkaḷ tōṉṟum pōtē uyirum uṭaṉ tōṉṟuvatu pōla viṉiyum uṭaṉtōṉṟuvatālum, uyiruḷvaḻi uṭa liṭattē ceykiyum, uyir nīṅkiyavaḻic ceyaliṉmiyum kaṇṭōr uyirē ceyal eṉṟum, ceyali viṉi yeṉṉum vaḻakkuppaṟṟi uyirē viṉi eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. “viṉiyē āṭavarkku uyir” eṉṟu paṇṭi āciriyaṉmār kūṟiyatu ettuṇi uṇmiyuriyākiṟatu. kāṇmiṉ! itaṉāl uyirp poruḷ, uyirilporuḷ ākiya ellāvaṟṟiṟkum viṉi iyalpāka amintiruppatu iṉitu viḷaṅkutalāl, viṉi ceyvatē uyiroṭu kūṭiya uṭampiṉ ceyalum payaṉumātal teḷiyappaṭum. nalviṉi ceyvatu vāḻvum ceyyāmi tāḻvu mākaliṉ, vāḻvāṅku vāḻap piṟanta makkaṭku viṉi urimi āyiṟṟu.
ivvaṇṇam ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkum viṉikku iṭam, viṉiyiṉ uruvāy virintu parantu elliyaṟṟut tōṉṟum inta ulakamēyākum; ākavē viṉiyiṉ pērelliyum inta ulakap parappāyiṟṟu, nilatti akaḻntum, kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyum, vāṉattiṟ paṟantu ceṉṟu āṅkuk kāṇappaṭum viṇmīṉ, kōḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aḷantum eṇṇiyum avaṟṟōṭu vāḻkkit toṭarpu koḷḷa muyaṉṟum iṉṟiya makkaḷuyir arumpāṭu paṭuvatellām avaṟṟiṉ uḷḷīṭāka nilavum viṉiyiṉ iyakkamē yākum. ivviṉiyiṉ ciṟṟelli uyir niṟṟaṟku iṭamākiya uṭampallatu illi, uṭampiṉ nīṅkiya uyirkkō, uyiriṉ nīṅkiya uṭampukkō taṉiniliyil uruvuṭiya viṉiyiṉmiyiṉ, uṭampōṭu kūṭiya uyiri viṉi mutalākak koḷḷiṉ uṭampoṭu kūṭiya uyirkku viṉi ceytaṟkuriya ciṟṟelli atu vāḻum maṉiyē yāyiṟṟu. ākavē ciṟṟelliyākiya maṉiyiṉkaṇ iruntu viṉiceyyum uyirkku virintu parantu kāṇappaṭum ulakam pērelliyāy vāḻiṭamākiṟatu; itupaṟṟiyē nallaṟiñar “peritē ulakam pēṇunar palarē” eṉa itaṉ perumiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
viṉinikaḻccik kuriya ciṟṟelliyum pērelliyum kaṇṭapiṉ, viṉiyiṉ iyalpukaḷik kāṇṭal vēṇṭum. viṉiyāy, viṉimutalāy iyaṅkum uyirkku mutaṟkaṭaṉāka vēṇṭuvatu uyir niṟṟaṟkuriya uṭampu. “uṭampār aḻiyil uyirār aḻivar” eṉpar tirumūlar. uṭampoṭu kūṭiya uyir, uṭampip pēṇutaṟku uṇṭiyum uṟakkamum uṟiyuḷum nāṭuvatu mutalviṉi. uṇṭi viṉikkup poruḷum uṟakka viṉikku uṟiyuḷum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. uṭampuṭiyuyirkaḷ mutaṟpaṭiyākat tēṭikkoḷḷum uṇṭi uṟiyuḷukkup piṉ, āṇum peṇṇumāyk kūṭi vēṟu uṭampuṭi yuyir kaḷit tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟaṉa. ciṟṟellikkaṇ iruntu ceyyappaṭum mutalviṉiyiṉ payaṉāka vēṟu uṭampuṭi yuyir tarum viṉi tōṉṟukiṟatu. itaṉāl ataṉ iṉam perukutaliṉ iṉam perukkum viṉiyāy aḵtu amikiṟatu. ākavē uṇṭal, uṟaṅkal, iṉamperukkal eṉṟa mūvaki viṉikkum maṉiyakam ciṟṟelliyātal teḷivām. ivi ellāvuyirkkum potu. iṉic ciṟappuṭiya makka ḷuyirik kāṇpām.
uṇṭiviṉi kuṟittup poruḷceytalum, ataṉik kāttalum, taṉṉāṟ perukiya iṉattukku vaḻaṅkutalum eṉa varum ceyalvakikaḷāl makkaḷuyiriṉ viṉiyelli virintu ulakam muḻutum paravi niṟkiṟatu; mēlum, ulakiṉ parappu, ōriṭattuḷḷa poruḷ piṟitōriṭattiṉṟi iṭantōṟum vēṟuvēṟu vakiyil poruḷum viṉiyum nikaḻtaṟku iṭan taruvatu karuttāka amintirukkiṟatu. ataṉāl ulakil uḷḷa poruḷvaki aṉittum ōriṭattē kiṭippatu illi; āyiṉum aviyaṉittum ulaka muḻuvāḻvukku vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa. ellāvaṟṟiyum peṟutaṟku iṭamum kālamum piṟavum iyalā toḻiyiṉum orucila ariyavaṟṟik koṇṭu vāḻa muyalvatum ataṟkēṟṟa viṉiceyvatum makkaḷuyirkku iyalpu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ, ēṉi uyirkaḷiviṭa makkaḷuyiriṉ viṉiyelli ulakamuḻutum virintu parantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
ciṟṟellikkaṇ ṇiruntu avvappōtu vēṇṭuvaṉa tēṭik koṇṭamivatu makkaḷuyiriṉ kīḻnili uyir kaḷiṭattum, kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, mēṉmakkaḷ tamatu nalvāḻvu kuṟittum tamatu iṉattiṉ nalvāḻvu kuṟittum ulakiṉ pala pakutikaṭkum parantu ceṉṟu viṉi ceyyum pāṉmi yuṭiya rāyiṉar. nāṭu parappum ceyalvakikaḷāka nāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷ kūṟuvaṉa aṉittukkum aṭippaṭi makkaḷuyiriṉ kaṇ iyaṟkiyil aminta pērelli viṉittuṭippē eṉṟu uṇartal vēṇṭum. ittuṭippu makkaḷuyirpāl potuvāka iruppiṉum, kuṭimi, cūḻnili, aṟivu, āṇmik kūṟukaḷāl nalvāyppuṭiya makkaḷiṭattētāṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṟatu; avarkaḷē talimi naṉmakka ḷākiṉṟārkaḷ.
maṉittakka māṇpuṭiya maṅkiyāṉa talimakaḷi maṇantu vāḻvāṅku vāḻntu varum talimakaṉ viṉi kāraṇa māka maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic ceṉṟāṉ. pērelliyiṟ piṟaṅkum peruviṉit tuṭippāl pirintāṉāyiṉum, avaṉ uḷḷam, ciṟṟellikkaṇ iruntu ceyyum ciṟappuṭiya iṉpa viṉiyiṉkaṇ ūṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ, avaṉip piṉṉiṉṟu īrtta vaṇṇam iruntatu; eṉiṉum, ataṉip piṟpaṭutti mēṟ koṇṭa viṉi muṭiyuṅkāṟum maṉiyiṭatti maṟantu viṭuvatu viṉiyāṇmiyiṉ vīṟupāṭu. viṉimuṟṟiyatum, maṉattiṭpam vēṟupaṭṭu maṉivāḻvi niṉippittu viṭum. maṉiyaṟattāṟ piṟakkum iṉpavuṇarvu mēlōṅki avaṉi virintu mīḷutal vēṇṭum eṉat tūṇṭum. avvāṟu tūṇṭappaṭṭut tuṭikkum neñciṉi nōkkiṉāṉ viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉ. viṉi paṟṟiya eṇṇaṅkaḷē niṟintirunta uḷḷam. maṉivāḻum maṭantiyākiya kātaliyiṉ kātal niṉivukaḷ mikuntu niṉṟatu. taṉ pirivāl kātali eytiya varuttam maṉakkaṇṇil vayaṅkit tōṉṟiyatu. neñcē, nām mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyi muṭittuk koṇṭōm; ic ceyti iṉṉē nam talimakaṭkut teriyu māyiṉ, nam varavu nōkki varunti niṟkum avaḷuṭiya kavali nīṅkumaṉṟō; ciṟukuṭiyiluḷḷa namatu perumaṉikkaṇ vāḻum pallikaḷ avaḷ uṇarumāṟu namatu varukiyi aṟivikkum kollō?” eṉṟāṉ.
avaṉatu uriyiṉkaṇ, pērelliyiṟ piṟakkum. peru viṉikkaṇ tōyntirunta avaṉ maṉam viṉimuṟṟiyatum avvelli curuṅki maṉiyuṟiyum kātaliyiṉ niṉiviṭi oṉṟi niṉṟu taṉ varaviṉip palli collumō eṉap paṭarn tami kaṇṭu viyanta iṭikkāṭaṉār ippāṭṭip pāṭu kiṉṟār.
muṉṉiyatu muṭittaṉam āyiṉ naṉṉutal
1varukuvam eṉṉum paruvaral tīrap
paṭuṅkol vāḻi 2neṭuñcuvarp palli
paraṟṟali pōkiya ciraṟṟalik kaḷḷi
mīmicik kalitta 3naṟuvī mulli
āṭutalit turuviṉ tōṭutalip peyarkkum
vaṉki iṭiyaṉ ellip 4paṭīiyar
veṇpōḻ 5tatiiya alaṅkalan toṭali
maṟukuṭaṉ kamaḻum mālic
6ciṟukuṭi yāṅkaṇem perunaka rāṉē.
itu, viṉimuṟṟi maṟuttarāniṉṟāṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.
uri
muṉṉiyatu muṭittaṉam āyiṉ - mēṟkoṇṭu pōnta viṉimuṟṟi mīḷkiṉṟa mākaliṉ; naṇṇutal - nalla neṟṟi yiṉiyuṭiyaḷāṉa nam kātaliyāvāḷ; varukuvam eṉṉum paruvaral tīra - yām vantu cērvēm eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutta colvaḻi niṉṟu eytum varuttam nīṅkumāṟu; neṭuñcuvarp palli - neṭiya cuvariṉkaṇ vāḻum palli; paṭuṅkol - namatu varukiyi olittut terivikkuṅ kollō; paraltali pōkiya ciraltalik kaḷḷi - paraṟkaṟkaḷ niṟinta pāli nilattu vaḷarntu niṟkum cirappaṟaviyiṉ koṇṭi pōl pūttuḷḷa kaḷḷimarattiṉ; mīmicik kulitta naṟuvī mulli - mēlē paṭarntu taḻittup pūttirukkum naṟiya pūkkaḷi yuṭiya mulliyi; āṭutalit turuviṉ tōṭu talip peyarkkum - acikiṉṟa taliyiyuṭiya āṭukaḷiṉ tokutiyi mēyccaluḷḷa iṭam nōkkic celuttum; vaṉkiiṭiyaṉ - valiya kiyiyuṭiya iṭiyaṉ; ellip paṭīiyar - iraviṉkaṇ malarum poruṭṭu; veṇpōḻ tatiiya alaṅkalan toṭali - veṇmiyāṉa nārāl arumpukaḷ neruṅkat toṭukkappaṭṭa acikiṉṟa māli yiṉ maṇam; maṟukuṭaṉ kamaḻum māli - teruviṭa meṅkum kamaḻum antippōtil; ciṟukuṭi āṅkaṇ em perunakarāṉ - ciṟukuṭiyiṭattē yuḷḷa ematu periya maṉiyiṉ kaṇ e.ṟu.
muṉṉiyatu muṭittaṉam; āyiṉ, naṉṉutal paruvaral tīra. ciṟukuṭi āṅkaṇ emperunakarāṉ neṭuñcuvarp palli paṭuṅkōl vāḻi eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. muṉṉi yatu. viṉi muṭittal īṇṭu muṭittu mīḷutal mēṉiṉṟatu. āyiṉ - ākaliṉ, naṉṉutal eṉṟatu taliviyi. varukuvam eṉṉum paruvaral - varavu nōkkiyirukkum varuttam. paṭutal - olittal, paraṟṟali - paraṟkaṟkaḷ niṟinta pāliniṟam. ciral - ciccilip paṟavi. kol - aiyapporuṭṭu kaḷḷippūvukkuc ciraṟpaṟaviyiṉ koṇṭi uvamam. turu - āṭu; itu turuvi  eṉavum vaḻaṅkum, tōṭu - tokuti, paṉimaṭalip pōḻntu koṇṭa melliya veṇmiyāṉa nūl pōṉṟa nārip pōḻ eṉṟār. “pōnti yaravāy māmaṭal nārum pōḻum kiṇiyoṭu curukki1” eṉpatu kāṇka. paṉimaṭaliṉ pōḻ poṉṉiṟamum uṭpuṟam veṇṇiṟamum uṭiyatu; pōḻoṭu kūṭiya nārē pūttoṭuttaṟkuriya nīṭciyum vaṉmi peṟ ṟiruttal yāvarum naṉkaṟinta toṉṟu. alaṅkal, acital, toṭali - māli, mulliyarumpu anti māliyil malarva tākaliṉ. mālip pōtil teru vaḻiyē koṇarum pōtu malarntu maṇam kamaḻntaliṉ maṟukuṭaṉ kamaḻum māli eṉṟār.
viṉiyē uyirākak koṇṭa talimakaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyi muṭittallatu piṟitiyātum niṉippatu ilaṉā yiṉum, viṉimuṭivum kātaliyiṉ kātaṉiṉivum, iruḷ nīkkamum oḷiviḷakkamum pōla uṭaṉikaḻtaliṉ, viṉi muṭintavuṭaṉē kātaliyi nōkki mīḷvāṉāyiṉamiyiṉ, muṉṉiyatu muṭittaṉa māyiṉ eṉṟāṉ. viṇimuṭiviyē nōkki yirukkum kātalikku ataṉ muṭivi muṉṉar uṇarttip piṉṉarttaṉ varukiyi uṇarttaluṟiṉ kālakkaḻivum, talimakaṭku āṟṟāmiyum tōṉṟu meṉṟa karuttāl tāṉē virintu varumāṟu tōṉṟa, muṭittaṉam āyiṉ eṉṟāṉ eṉiṉum amiyum. taṉṉik kāṇum pōtellām mukam malarntu oḷi ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nutaluṭimi paṟṟi, naṉṉutal eṉṟāṉ. kāli vārā rāyiṉum māli vārā rallar eṉṟu varavunōkkip pirivuttuyarāl varuntutali niṉintu kūṟaliṉ, varukuvam eṉṉum paruvaral eṉṟāṉ. tūtu nōkkiyum palli col kēṭṭum pakaṟpōtiṉik kaḻittu varuntum taliviyiṉ ceyali avaḷum tōḻiyum collak kēṭṭuḷā ṉākaliṉ paṭuṅkol vāḻi neṭuñ cuvarp palli eṉṟāṉ. palli colliṟ payaṉuṇṭu eṉpatu paṟṟi vāḻi eṉal iyalpu. āṟṟātu varuntum talivikkup palliyiṉ col āṟṟu māṟu payattaliṉ vāḻi eṉṟāṉ eṉiṉumām. naṟuvī mulli elliyil malarumāṟu iṭiyaṉ toṭutta māli maṟukuṭaṉ kamaḻum eṉṟatu. tāṉ viṉimuṟṟi mīḷum ceytiyit talimakaṉ uṇarumāṟu palli collumāyiṉ avaḷ maṉikalantu viruntum ciṟappum ceytu makiḻvāḷ eṉat talimakaṉ kuṟippittavāṟākak koḷka.
“karaṇattiṉamintu muṭinta kāli1” eṉṟa nūṟpāviṉ kaṇ varum. “avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇum” eṉṟa taṟku itaṉik kāṭṭi, muṉṉiyatu muṭittaṉa māyiṉ eṉṉum naṟṟiṇiyuḷ potuppaṭac ciṟappum kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
170. paraṇar
caṅkakālat tamiḻvāḻvil aracarkaḷiṭiyē nikaḻnta pōrkaḷil pārppaṉarum, mikka mutiyavarum, ciṟuvarkaḷum, peṇṭirum, makappeṟātōrum, nōyuṟṟōrum vilakkap paṭṭaṉar. pōril iṟantō rellām kaṭṭāṇmi mikka āṭavarkaḷē; itaṉāl nāṭṭil āṇmakkaḷiṉum  peṇ makkaḷ kūṭṭam mikuntiruntatu. āṭavarum peṇṭirum tattamak kuriya vāḻkkit tuṇiyit tāmē tērntu koḷvatutāṉ annāḷiya maṇamuṟi. āṇum peṇṇumākat tām peṟṟa makkaṭku uriya vāḻkkittuṇiyip peṟṟōr tērn teṭuttu maṇam ceyyum iṉṟiya muṟi tamiḻvāḻvil iṭikkālattē pukunta putuneṟi. naṉmakkaṭ pēṟṟi eṭutturitta tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār, tammiṉum tammakkaḷ aṟivuṭiyarātal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tammakkaḷic cāṉṟōr kūṭiya nallaviyil kalvi kēḷvikaḷil ciṟantu muntiyiruppac ceytal tantikkuk kaṭaṉ eṉṟum, avar meytīṇṭal uṭaṟkiṉpam eṉṟum, avar coṟkēṭṭal cevikku iṉpa meṉṟum: avarkaḷē peṟṟōr peṟum pēṟukaḷuḷ tali mippēṟu eṉṟum. tam makkaḷiyē tamakkup poruḷākap peṟṟōr karutuva reṉṟum viriyak kūṟiṉavar. yāṇṭum ammakkaṭkut takka vāḻkkit tuṇiyip peṟṟōr tēṭi maṇam ceytal vēṇṭu meṉṟu kūṟavē yilli. ataṉāl makkaṭku vāḻkkittuṇiyit tēṭippuṇarttal peṟṟōr kaṭaṉ eṉpatu avar kālavaḻakkiṉmi teḷiyappaṭum. mēlum, avar tām vakutta kāmattuppālil āṭavarum makaḷirum tamakku otta vāḻkkit tuṇiyit tērntu koḷḷum kaḷavi yaliyum piṉṉar maṇam purintukoṇṭu oruvark koruvar tuṇivarāy maṉiyaṟam puritaṟkuriya kaṟpiyaliyum  viriyak kūṟuvatu mēlē kaṇṭa muṭipiyē vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, piṉvanta cilappatikārattil peṟṟōr vāḻkkit tuṇiyit tēṭip puṇarkkum maṇamuṟi kāṇappaṭutalāl, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ mutaliya cāṉṟōr kālattilēyē tamiḻ vāḻvu koṇṭirunta maṇamuṟiyuṭaṉ peṟṟōr puṇarttum maṇamuṟiyum uṭaṉnilaviṉami peṟappaṭukiṟatu.
paḻantamiḻ muṟiyil oruvaṉukku oruttiyum oruttikku oruvaṉum vāḻkkittuṇiyāyiṉar. mutal maṉivikku makappēṟu illiyāyiṉ vēṟoruttiyi maṇakkum urimi āṭavaṉukku uṇṭu; iraṇṭāmavaṭkup piṉ muṟiyākiya perumporuḷ vatuvit toṉmuṟi maṉivi eṉṟu peyar kūṟuvar. iruvarkku mēlum uḷarāyiṉ avar kāmakkiḻattiyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivvāṟu palar oruvaṉukku uriyavarāka variyappaṭiṉ, avarkaḷ urimi makaḷir eṉappaṭuvārāyiṉar. āṉāl irumaṇam ceytu kōṭal makaḷirkku iyalpaṉṟu.
āṭavartoki kuṟintum makaḷirtoki mikuntum iruntamiyiṉ, maṉiviyākavō kāmakkiḻattiyākavō urimi makaḷākavō variyappaṭāta peṇkaḷ varivil makaḷir eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvar. oruvariṉ mēlum parakkum maṉa muṭiyavar parattar; avaruḷ āṭavarip parattar eṉṟum peṇṭirip parattiyar eṉṟum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvar. parattiyarik kūṭi yoḻukuvatu parattimi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
maṇamākāta āṭavarkaḷ iravil taṅkuvataṟku oru potu viṭam vakuttu aṅkē uṟaṅkac ceyvatu paṇṭināḷil iruntatoru vaḻakkāṟu; aḵtu ippōtu nāṭṭuppuṟaṅ kaḷilum malimukaṭukaḷil vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṭiyum iruntu varukiṟatu. avvāṟē āṭava rillāmiyāl vāḻkkit tuṇiyāka varintu koḷḷappaṭāta makaḷir parattimi pūṇṭamiyāl, parattiyeṉa otukkap peṟṟami yiṉ, avarkaṭku ūrppuṟattē vīṭukaḷ amittu vāḻac ceytaṉar; avar uṟiyumiṭam parattiyar cēri eṉa vaḻaṅki vantatu. varivil makaḷir, āṭal pāṭal aḻakukaḷāl tammi uyarttip poruḷīṭṭit tam vāḻkkiyi naṭattiṉar. nāṭu purakkum talivarkaḷ avarkaḷ nalattip pēṇi vāḻac ceyvatu puravukkaṭaṉākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. vēṟu nāṭukaḷil vēnta riṭiyē pōr nikaḻumāyiṉ, veṉṟa vēntarkkup poṉṉum poruḷum pōla nūṟṟukkaṇakkilum āyirakkaṇakkilum makaḷirit tiṟiyākak koṭukkum vaḻakkam iruntatu; itaṉi eraṭōṭṭas mutaliyōr eḻutiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷilum parakkak kāṇalām.
ellā vuyirkkum iṉpanāṭṭam iyalpilē iruppa toṉṟu; parattiyar itaṟku vilakkalla rākaliṉ, avarkaḷ tamakkiṉiya āṭavar maṉattit tampāl īrttaṟkuriya ceyal vakikaḷāka āṭal pāṭal mutaliya palavaṟṟi mēṟkoṇ ṭiruntaṉar. tām peṟum iṉpam nilipēṟum vaḷamum eytutaṟkup poruḷ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ, avarkaḷ, poruḷ mikka celvattalivarkaḷit tampāl īṭupaṭumāṟu muyalvārāyiṉar. attuṟiyil avarkaṭkup pāṇar eṉpār tuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. pāṇmakaḷiri viṟaliyar eṉpatum uṇṭu. avarkaḷ āṭal pāṭal aḻakukaḷil vallunarāy, aracar, celvar mutaliyōruṭiya maṟamāṇpukaḷiyum aṟac ceyalkaḷiyum iciyilum kūttilum amittup pukaḻntu avartarum paricil peṟṟu vāḻvar. avvaḻakkāṟu iṭik kālac cōḻavēntaṉ iraṇṭāṅkulōttuṅkaṉ kālattum iruntataṉik1 kalveṭṭukkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvaṇṇam iyaṉṟu vanta parattimi vāḻvil tali makkaḷ kalantukoṇṭu oḻukuvārāyiṉ avaratu oḻukkam puṟattoḻukkam eṉappaṭum eṉpatip piṟitōriṭattiṟ kūṟiṉām. orukāl talimakaṉ puṟattoḻukkam mēṟkoṇṭu ciṟappuṭiya parattiyoruttipāl toṭarpu peṟṟāṉ. ciṉṉāṭkuppiṉ vēṟoru parattiyiṉ naṭpu avaṉukku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl muṉṉip parattikkup poṟāmi yuṇṭāyiṟṟu. avaḷpāl avaṉ ceṉṟa pōtu avaḷ peritum pulantu avaṉukku vāyil maṟuttāḷ. ataṉāl, avaṉ, viṟaliyoruttiyi apparatti maṉikkut taṉporuṭṭut tūtu ceṉṟu vāyil peṟumāṟu muṉṉar viṭuttu, piṉṉar avaḷ maṉiyil naṭanta viḻāvoṉṟukku vantāṉ. viṟali varakkaṇṭa parattiyiṉ tōḻi vekuṇṭu ēṉi makaḷiri nōkki, “tōḻimīr, eḻumiṉ; itō vantu niṟkum ivviṟali nam talivaṉip piṟamakaḷirpāl koṇṭu puṇarkkum ceyaliṉaḷ; ivaḷ ippōtu nām ceyyum ivvāṭal viḻaviṉuḷ van tuḷḷā ḷākaliṉ, nam talivaṉi nām ippōtē cūḻntu kāttal vēṇṭum. illiyēl nām palarāyiruntum paya ṉiṉṟāy muṭiyum” eṉṟāḷ.
parattit tōḻiyiṉ ivvurikkaṇ, makkaḷ palariyum pēṇip purakkum takkavaṉākiya viṭattum, puṟattoḻukkam pūṇṭataṉāl, parattimakaḷir cūḻntu ciṟikkum eḷimi talimakaṟ keytuvatu kaṇṭu viyanta āciriyar paraṇar ip pāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
maṭakkaṇ takarak kūntaṟ paṇittōḷ
vārnta vāleyiṟṟuc cērntuceṟi kuṟaṅkiṉ 
piṇiyal antaḻit 1tiit tuṇiyilaḷ
viḻavukkaḷam poliya vantuniṉ ṟōḷē
eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉnam 2koḻunaṟ kākkuvam
āriyar tuvaṉṟiya pērici muḷḷūrp
palaruṭaṉ kaḻitta 3oḷvāḷ maliyaṉa
toruvēṟ kōṭi yāṅkunam
4perumiya tevaṉōivaḷ naṉmitalip paṭiṉē.
itu, tōḻi viṟalikku vāyiṉmaṟuttatu.
uri
maṭakkaṇ takarak kūntal paṇittōḷ - maṭappatti yuṭiya kaṇkaḷum mayirccān taṇinta kūntalum parutta tōḷkaḷum; vārnta vāleyiṟṟuc cērntu ceṟikuṟaṅ kiṉ - niralpaṭa oḻukiya veṇmiyāṉa paṟkaḷum tiraṇṭu neruṅkiya tuṭikaḷu muṭiyaḷāy; piṇiyal antaḻi tii - mālikaḷālum taḻiyuṭiyālum taṉṉi oppaṉi ceytukoṇṭu; tuṇiyilaḷ -  tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittu ivviṟali viḻavukkaḷam poliya vantu niṉṟāḷ - namatu ivviḻākkaḷam eṅkum taṉ poṟpu viḷaṅka vantu niṟ kiṉṟāḷ; eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉ nam koḻunaṉ kākkuvam - iṉṉē eḻuka, eḻuntu ceṉṟu nam koḻunaṉic cūḻa niṉṟu kāppēmāka; āriyar tuvaṉṟiya pērici muḷḷūr - āriya nāṭṭiṉar pōntu niṟintu koṇṭa periya pukaḻiyuṭiya muḷḷūrp pōril; palaruṭaṉ cuḻitta oḷvāḷ - palarāy etirniṉṟu uṟikaḻittēntiya oḷḷiya vāṭpaṭiyiṉarāṉa āriyamaṟavar; maliyaṉatu oruvēṟku ōṭiyāṅku - maliya māṉ ēntic ceṉṟa oru vēṟpaṭiyik kaṇṭamāttiriyē añci ōṭiyatu pōla; nam perumiyatu evaṉō - palarāyk kūṭiyuḷḷa namatu perumi oru payaṉu millatākum; ivaḷ naṉmi talippaṭiṉ - ivviṟali taṉ āṭalpāṭalkaḷāl naṉmi eytuva ḷāyiṉ e-ṟu.
viṟali, tuṇiyilaḷ vantu niṉṟāḷ; ākaliṉ, eḻumiṉ eḻumiṉ nam koḻunaṉik kākkuvam; ivaḷ naṉmi talip paṭiṉ, nam perumi evaṉō, orupayaṉu miṉṟām eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kāmak kuṟippillāta veḷḷi nōkkam uṭimi paṟṟi maṭakkaṇ eṉṟār. takaram - kūntaṟkaṇiyum oruvakic cānteṇṇey, paṇi - perumi; mūṅkilumām. cērtal, tiraṭci piṇiyal, māli. iḷampeṇṇātaliṉ taḻiyuṭi kūṟiṉār; “iḷiya mākat taḻiyā yiṉavē1” eṉpatu kāṇka. eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉ eṉṟatu virivu tōṉṟaṟku. caṅkakālac cēra nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapulam āriyanāṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, an nāṭṭavar āriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. mēṉāṭṭu yavaṉa āciriya rāṉa tālami mutaliyōr cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkilirunta nāṭṭi āriyakē2 (āriyakam) eṉṟē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar. āriyarkkum cērarkkum oru kālattē muḷḷūreṉṉum ūriṭattē kaṭumpōr naṭantatu; cērarkkut tuṇiyāka maliyamāṉ ēntiya vēṟpaṭikku etir niṟka āṟṟātu uṭintu ōṭiṟṟu. tamiḻ vēntarkku vēṇṭumiṭattu utavi purintu veṟṟi peṟuvippatil, maliyamāṉ pukaḻ annāḷil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu; “valvēl maliya ṉalla ṉāyiṉ, nallamark kaṭattal eḷitumaṉ namakkeṉat tōṟṟōṉ tāṉum niṟkūṟummē, toliiyōṉ ivaṉeṉa3” eṉpata ṉāl iḵtu uṇarappaṭum. inta muḷḷūr ippōtu kōyam puttūr māvaṭṭattuk koḷḷēkāl vaṭṭattil uḷatu; itaṉi aṅkuḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ muḷḷūrkkōṭṭi4 eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. perumi - paṉmipaṟṟi vantatu. ‘evaṉ’, viṉāppeyar naṉmi - karutiyatu muṭittal. perumiyatu ‘atu’ eṉṉum cuṭṭu. cāttaṉavaṉ vantāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla vantatu.
iḷamic cevvi kuṉṟātu veḷḷi nōkkattōṭu paratti maṉiyiṉkaṇ nikaḻum viḻākkaṇṭu niṟkum viṟaliyi viyantu, maṭakkaṇ eṉṟāḷ. maṭami, maṭappam; aḵtāvatu iḷamic cevvi, muṭiyiṭumāṟu vaḷarum kūntali yuṭiya petumpip paruvam eytāmi tōṉṟat takarak kūntal eṉṟum, viṟalpaṭap pāṭi yāṭum payiṟciyāl tōḷ vaṉmiyum perumiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkutaliṉ paṇittōḷ eṉṟum, vārnta vāleyiṟṟuc cērntu ceṟikuṟaṅkiṉ eṉṟum ciṟappittāḷ. viḻāk kāṇa varutaliṉ piṇiyalum taḻiyuṭiyum aṇintu kāṇpōr kaṇkaḷik kavarum vaṇṇam vaṉintu vanta vaṉappuṭimi cuṭṭi, piṇiyal attaḻi tii eṉavum, iḷiya ḷāyiṉum, tāṉ mēṟkoḷḷum paṇiyi veṉṟiyuṟa muṭikkum vīṟuṭiyaḷ eṉa viyantu kūṟuvāḷ tuṇiyilaḷ vantu niṉṟōḷ eṉavum parattit tōḻi viṟaliyiṉ mēṉinalatti ivvāṟu pārit turittāḷ. celva mikka āṭavarit tampāl īrttup poruḷīṭṭamum, iṉpa nukarcciyum peṟuvatē parattiyar toḻilātaliṉ, āṭal viḻā, pāṭalviḻā, araṅkēṟṟu viḻā eṉap palavēṟu viḻākkaḷ parattiyar cērikkaṇ aṭikkaṭi nikaḻum; celva mikka talimakkaḷ ceṉṟu ovvoṉṟukkum muṉṉiṉṟu talikki tantu naṭattuvar, atupaṟṟiyē talimakaṉ paratti maṉikkaṇ nikaḻum viḻāvukku vantiruntā ṉeṉa uṇarka, taṉporuṭṭu vāyil peṟaṟku viṟaliyi avaṉ viṭut tiruntamiyiṉ, avaḷatu varavu viḻākkaḷattē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṉamipaṟṟi tuṇiyilaḷ viḻavukkaḷam poliya vantu niṉṟōḷē eṉṟāḷ. parattittalivipāl vāyil vēṇṭi avviṟali vantiruppati uṇarāmiyāl, piṟa ḷoruttipāl talimakaṉik koṇṭu puṇarttaṟku van tuḷāḷ eṉap piṟaḻa uṇarntu eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉ eṉat taṉ tōḻiyarkku urippāḷāyiṉaḷ. atu kaṇṭa viṟali yiṉ muṟuvaṟkuṟippum talivaṉ makiḻcciyum tōḻiyiṉ karutti vaṟpuṟuttavē, nam koḻunaṟ kākkuvam eṉṟum, talimakaṉ viṟaliyi viyantu nōkkiyatu kaṇṭa tōḻi, viṟali talimakaṉit taṉṉuṭaṉ koṇṭu cēṟalil veṟṟi peṟuvāḷ eṉat tuṇintami tōṉṟa, ivaḷ naṉmi talippaṭiṉ nam periya tiraḷ payaṉiṉṟām eṉṟaṟku nam perumiya tevaṉō eṉṟum kūṟi viṟali parattit taliviyiṉ maṉipukāvāṟu vāyilmaṟuttā ḷāyiṟṟu.
“poḻutum āṟum uṭkuvarat tōṉṟi1” eṉṟa nūṟpā vuriyiṉkaṇ itaṉik kāṭṭi, iḵtu iṭiccurattuk kuṟumpiṉuḷḷār. ivarik kaṇṭu kōḷiḻippuṟṟārkku avar peṇṭir kūṟiyatu” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
171. ceṅkaṇṇār
makkaḷ uṭampiṉkaṇ vaḷarum uyir, piḷḷimip paruvat til uṭaluṇarcci vaḻiniṉṟu ataṉ ākkattiṟkum vaḷarccikkum toḻilceytu vāḻum; piṉṉar aḵtu ulakiyalil vāḻntu peṟum iyaṟkiyaṟivālum, kalvi kēḷvikaḷāṟ piṟakkum ceyaṟkiyaṟivālum vaṉmi peṟṟu, uṭampiṉ ceyalit taṉakku aṭippaṭutti ataṉit taṉ vaḻiniṟuttaṟku muyalum. itu nikaḻuṅkālam iḷamic cevvi. akkāli, uṭampu tāṉ peṟṟa ākkattuk kēṟpak kaviṉum kaṇkavarcciyum eyti, āṇuṭampu peṇṇiyum, peṇṇuṭampu āṇiyum īrttu, iraṇṭaṉ uyiriyum kātalāl oṉṟupaṭutti iṉamperukkum perum paṇiyil īṭupaṭuttum. inniliyil uyiraṟivu vaṉmi mik kiruppiṉ, uṭampiṉ ceyalākiya kātaṟkāmavuṇarvik kīḻ paṭuttik, kaṭamiyākiya viṉiceyaluṇarvu mēmpaṭac ceyyum. appōḻtu, kaṭamivaḻi niṟpavaṉ poruḷ, pukaḻ, mutaliyaṉa peṟṟu mēṉmiyum; kātaṟkāma vaḻiniṟpavaṉ vēṇṭuvaṉa nirampāta nirappiṭumpi eytik kīḻmiyum eytuvatu iyalpu. iṉṟiya makkaḷ vāḻvil kātaṟkāmattukku ākkamākum niḻaṟpaṭak kāṭcikaḷum, vēṟu piṟa nikaḻccikaḷum palki, makkaḷiṉ uyiraṟivi melivittaliṉ iṉapperukkamum vaṟumittuṉpamum perumpālāriṭam nilavukiṉṟaṉa. caṅkakāla vāḻvil makkaḷiṉattiṉ toki kuṟitaṟ kētuvākiya pōrkaḷ aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntamiyiṉ iṉapperukkam ulakiyal vāḻvukku mikka iṭiyūṟu ceyyavilli. poruḷīṭṭattukku vēṇṭum nilapparappu mikuntu miruntatu. āyiṉum tamiḻt talimakkaḷ kātaṟkāma vuṇarvik kīḻppaṭuttu oṭukkik kaṭamiyuṇarvukkut talimi tantu ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. ataṉāl toḻilum poruḷum, vāṇikamum, perukip poṉviḷiyum nāṭu eṉa mēṉāṭukaḷ pōṟṟum aḷavil namatu nāṭu pukaḻmikkup polintatu.
annāḷil maṉiyiṉkaṇ iruntu aṟam ceytu pōnta talimakaṉ viṉiyāṇmi kuṟittup pirintu cellak karuti ṉāṉ. taṉṉi iṉṟiyamiyātu uṟiyum taṉ kātalikkut taṉ karutti aṟivikkumāṟu tōḻik kurittāṉ. kātaṟkāma nukarcciyi neṟippaṭuttik kaṭamivāḻvu ciṟappuṟa naṭattaṟkut tuṇiyāvatu, māṇpuṭiya maṉiviyiṉ kaṭaṉ; āyiṉum avaḷuṭiya cūḻnili kātaluṇarccikkē ciṟappiṭam tantami yiṉ kātalaṉ pirivu avaḷip peritum varuttuvatu iyalpā yiruntatu. anta niliyil nīr cellum vaḻiyiṟ cāyntu koṭuttu, nīrpperukkuk kuṉṟumiṭattu nimirntu niṟkum pullippōla, avaṭkut tuṇiyākum tōḻi; avaḷ kātal vaḻiyē mutaṟkaṇ cāyntuniṉṟu ataṉ kotippuc ciṟitu taṇiyum pōtu kaṭami yuṇarviṉ ciṟappi eṭuttu moḻintu vaṟpuṟuttuvaḷ. aḵtu avaṭku aṟamātaliṉ; “tōḻi, nam kātalar ariya curattik kaṭantu viṉi kāraṇamākap pirintu cellak karutukiṉṟār; avar pirinta viṭattu, arumaṇiyi iḻantu tēṭupavar pōla avar varavi yeṇṇiyeṇṇi varunta vēṇṭiya nili uṇṭātalāl; nām eṅṅaṉam uṟaṅkap pōkiṉṟōm?” eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉāl, “puṇarvum pirivum kaṭamivāḻviṉ iyaṟki nikaḻcci yākaliṉ, nām aṟivu melintu uḷḷam alamarutal nalamaṉṟu; eṉat talimakaḷ tēṟit teḷivu eytuva ḷeṉa urikkum tōḻiyiṉ matinuṭpam kaṇṭa ceṅkaṇṇa ṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
nīrnacik kūkkiya uyaval yāṉi
vēṉiṟ kuṉṟattu vevvarik kavāaṉ
1iṭañcelac cellāk kayantalik kuḻavi
cēriyam 2peṇṭiriṉ añcit tīriya
ūrāṉ kaṉṟoṭu pukutarum nāṭaṉ
paṉmali aruñcuram iṟappiṉ1 emmō
ṭiyāṅku valluna maṟṟō ñāṅkar
viṉippūṇ teṇmaṇi 2vīḻttaṉar nikarppak
kaḻutukāl koḷḷum poḻutukoḷ pāṉāḷ
ārva neñcamo ṭaḷii
mararpuṟap 3paṭutal marīiya kaṇṇē
itu pirivuṇarttappaṭṭa tōḻi talimakaṭku urittatu.
uri
nīr nacikku ūkkiya uyaval yāṉi - nīr vēṭki mikkuṟutalāl varuntutaliyuṭiya piṭiyāṉi; vēṉiṟ kuṉṟattu vevvarik kavāaṉ - vēṉilatu vemmiyi yuṭiya kuṉṟiṉ vevvitākiya pakkamaliyiṉ kaṇ; iṭam cela - ōriṭattukkuc ceṉṟatāka; cellāk kayantalik kuḻavi - uṭaṉcellamāṭṭāta periya taliyiyuṭiya ataṉ kaṉṟu; cēriyam peṇṭiriṉ añci - puṟañcēriyil vāḻum makaḷir akanakarkkaṇ pukkavaḻi añci alamarutal pōla añci; tīriya - avviṭattuniṉṟum nīṅkutaṟ poruṭṭu; ūrāṉ kaṉṟoṭu pukutarum nāṭaṉ - ūrāṉ kaṉṟukaḷōṭu kūṭi ūrkkuṭ pōtarum nāṭaṉākiya talimakaṉ; paṉmali aruñcuram iṟappiṉ - pala malikaḷi yuṭi ariya curaṅ kaḷik kaṭantu celkuva rāyiṉ; emmōṭu yāṅku valluna - nammuṭaṉē evvāṟu āṟṟiyirukka vallaṉavām; ñāṅkar viṉippūṇ teṇmaṇi - mēlē nalla vēlippā ṭaminta teḷḷiya pūṇiṭattu aminta maṇiyi; vīḻttaṉar nikarppa - keṭuttu vīḻttavar uṟaṅkātu varuntuvatu pōla; kaḻutu kālkoḷḷum poḻutukoḷ pāṉāḷ - pēyiṉaṅkaḷ kālūṉṟi vaḻaṅkum poḻutākiya naṭuviyāmattē; ārva neñcamoṭu aḷii - aṉpuṭiya neñcam tammil kalattalāl; mārpuṟappaṭutal marīiya kaṇ - avaruṭiya mārpakam poruntap pullit tuyilutalip poruntiya nam kaṇkaḷ e-ṟu.
nāṭaṉ, aruñcuram iṟappiṉ, mārpuṟap paṭutal marīiya kaṇyāṅka valluna kollō, vīḻttaṉar nikarppap paṭutal māṭṭāvām eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. nīrnaci - nīrvēṭki, ūkkutal - mēṉmēlum tēṭikkoṇṭē pōtal, uyaval - varuttam, kayantalik kuḻaviyik kūṟaliṉ -  yāṉi piṭiyāyiṟṟu. vēṉil - vemmiyāl paciyaṟṟut tōṉṟum kuṉṟiṉi vēṉiṟ kuṉṟam eṉṟār. kuṉṟiṉ ucciyiṉum malippakkam vemmi mikavuṭiyatākaliṉ, vevvarik kavāaṉ eṉṟār. kavāaṉ malippakkam. iṭam eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟaliṉ, kaṉṟu kāṇamāṭṭāta iṭamāyiṟṟu. vēṭki ūkkutalāl piṭiyum taṉ kaṉṟiṉi maṟantu pōyiṟ ṟeṉka. piṭiyāṉiyip pētuṟuvitta vēṉilvemmikku ataṉ kuḻavi vilakkaṉmiyiṉ, atuvum vemmiyālvāṭi appiṭi ceṉṟa iṭam nāṭic cella māṭṭā tāyiṉamiyiṉ, cellāk kayantalik kuḻavi eṉṟār. kaya - perumi, yāṉikkaṉṟiṉik kuḻaviyeṉṟa lum marapu; “kuñcaram peṟumē kuḻavip peyarkkoṭi1” eṉpatu kāṇka cēri - nakarppuṟattē ataṉic cēra irukkum teru, puṟañcēriyil vāḻum peṇṭir akanakarkkuṭ celliṉ, terukkaḷiṉ nerukkamum,  perumaṉikaḷiṉ perukkamum, cantiyum, catukkamum, māṭamum, maṟukum kaṇṭu maṉamaruṭci koṇṭu, cellum neṟi teḷiyātu tikip puṟutal iyalpātaliṉ cēriyam peṇṭiriṉ añci eṉṟār. tāyip pirinta yāṉikkaṉṟu, ceṉṉeṟi kāṇātu tikittu añciyataṟkup peṇṭir añcuvatu uvamamāyiṟṟu; yāṅku valluna eṉṟatu māṭṭāmi vaṟ puṟutti niṉṟatu. ñāṅkar - mēliṭam; “ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē2” eṉṟāṟ pōla. poṉṉiṉum ciṟappuṭiya tākaliṉ, maṇiyiṉi yiḻantōr kalakkamikuti yāl kaṇṇuṟakka miṉṟi varuntutal ulakaṟinta uṇmi yātaliṉ, kātalaṉip pirintuṟiyum makaḷir kaṇṇuṟakka miṉṟi varuntuvataṟku uvamamākkiṉār. kaḻutu -pēy, pēykaḷ naṭuviyāmattē ulavum eṉpatu paṇṭiyōr koḷki. “kaḻutuvaḻaṅkari nāḷ kāvalar maṭiya3” eṉavum, “kaḻutu kālkiḷara ūr maṭintaṉṟē4” eṉavum cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. kātalaṉ mārpil tuyilkōṭaṟku makaḷir maṉam peritum virumpu meṉpar; “maṇittuṟi yūraṉ mārpē, paṉittuyil ceyyum iṉcāyaṟṟē1” eṉap piṟarum kūṟuvar.
piṭiyāṉiyōṭu cella māṭṭāta kaṉṟu ūrāṉkaṉ ṟoṭu kūṭi ūrkkuṭ pukutum nāṭaṉ eṉṟataṉāl, tali makaṉōṭu cellutaṟkākāta nām cuṟṟam cūḻa maṉikkaṇ iruntoḻitalē ceyaṟpālateṉak kūṟukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, nāṭaṉ paṉmali aruñcuram iṟappiṉ eṉṟāḷ; talimakaṉ piriyak karutiyatallatu iṉṉum piriyāmiyiṉ; iṟappiṉ eṉṟā ḷeṉka. ataṉāl talimakaṉuṭiya pirivuk kuṟippuṇarntu talimakaḷ pētuṟṟamiyiṉ, avaḷvaḻi niṉṟu teruṭṭaluṟum tōḻi, māṭṭāmi tōṉṟa, mārpuṟap paṭutal marīiya kaṇyāṅku valluna kollō eṉṟāḷ. kātalaṉoṭu kūṭiyuṟiyum maṉivāḻvu, viṉip pūṇ teṇmaṇi pōlavum, ataṉkaṇ avaṉ teṇmaṇi pōlavum, avaṉip pirintuṟiyum talivi vīḻttaṉar pōlavum koḷka. vīḻttavar kalaṅkañar uṟṟuk kaṇ ṇuṟakkam peṟātu varuntuvatu pōlattaliviyum taṉimit tuyaruṟṟuk kaṇṇuṟakkam peṟātu varuntuvaḷ eṉattōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. iṅkut talivaṉ mārpiṉi uṟutalāl uṟakkam peṟutaluṭiya kaṇkaḷ eṉṟatu. yāṅku vallunakollō eṉṟaṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu niṉ kaṇkaḷ uṟaṅkutal māṭṭāvāyiṉ, yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ eṉat tōḻi taṉ āṟṟāmi yurittavaḻi, “iṉpamum iṭumpiyum puṇarvum pirivum, naṉpakal amiyamum iravum pōla2” iyaluvaṉa vākaliṉ yāṉ āṟṟuval eṉpatupaṭat talimakaḷ takuvaṉa urippatu payaṉ eṉa uṇarka.
172. nakkīraṉār
kaṇkavar vaṉappum, kaṟpuc ciṟappum, poṟpuṟa aminta taliviyik kaṇṭu kātaluṟavu koṇṭāṉ taliminalam peṟṟa tamiḻk kāḷi. orumuṟik kirumuṟi avaḷit taṉit tukkaṇṭu kātaluṟuti peṟṟavaṉ, avaḷuṭiya uyirt tōḻiyi aṟintu avaḷuṭiya naṭpup peṟṟā laṉṟit talimakaḷpāl koṇṭa kātaluṟavu vaḷarntu, ciṟantu vāḻkkittuṇiyākum vaḷam payavātu eṉpati naṉku uṇarntava ṉākaliṉ; paṉmuṟiyum viṭātu muyaṉṟu tōḻiyi matiyuṭampaṭuttu, avaḷ vāyilākat talimakaḷik kāṇum nalam peṟṟāṉ. piṉṉart taṉṉip pakaṟpōtil kāṇṭaṟ keṉat taṉittatōr iṭam kuṟikkumāṟu avaṉ talimakaḷi vēṇṭiṉāṉ. ciṟanta iṭamoṉṟu tōḻiyāl cuṭṭik kāṭṭap paṭṭatu. appakaṟkuṟikkaṇ avaṉ nāṭōṟum pōntu tali makaḷit talippeytu aḷavaḷāvi iṉpuṟṟoḻukiṉāṉ. itaṉiivvaṇṇam nīḷaviṭiṉ, piṟar aṟintu ētam viḷippa reṉṟu tōḻi añciṉāḷ. taṉṉip pōlavē talimakaṉum eṇṇuvā ṉākaliṉ, avaṉukkut tāṉ muṟpaṭṭu varintu koḷḷumāṟu veḷippaṭiyāka vurippatu muṟiyaṉṟu, eṉa avaḷ eṇṇik kuṟippāka avaṉē uyttuṇarntu koḷḷum vakiyil uriyāṭa luṟṟāḷ. ataṉāl, avaṉ peritum virumpi vantu koṇṭirukkum pakaṟkuṟi varavi maṟukkat talippaṭṭāḷ. iṉpam nilipēṟiṉṟic ciṟitu pōtil kaḻiyum iyalpiṟ ṟākaliṉ,  ataṉi nukarpavar ataṉ peruk kattiyē peritum viḻivatum muyalvatum ceyvar. tali makaṉum tāṉ nukarum iṉpam iṭiyīṭum, iṭiyaṟavum eyti aḻivuṟātu, perukuvatiyē virumpi niṟkiṉṟamiyiṉ; tōḻi kūṟṟu ataṟkut taṭiyātaliṉ, avaṉ aḻivil iṉpam vēṇṭi varitalit teruṇṭu mēṟkoḷvaṉ eṉpatu karuttu. pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ talimakaṉ varavu kaṇṭa tōḻi, orunāḷ, avaṉi nērpaṭṭu. “tuṟiva, nām kūṭum ip puṉṉip potumparil ippuṉṉiyiṉ nīḻalil nummoṭu kūṭi makiḻvatu emakku nāṇut tarukiṉṟatu; nāṅkaḷ ciṟumikaḷāka iṅkē viḷiyāṭiya pōtu puṉṉiyiṉ vitiyoṉṟi immaṇaṟkaṇ putittuc ceṉṟēm; ciṉṉāṭkaḷilatu muḷittu varavē, ataṟku nīrum pālum peytu nāṅkaḷ vaḷarttu vantēm; ataṉik kaṇṭa em aṉṉi, itu numpōṟ ciṟantatu; numakkut taṅkiyā meṉa moḻintaṉaḷ; itaṉ nīḻalilē yiruntu nummik kāṇpatu naṉṟaṉṟu; nī emmit talippeytu taliyaḷi ceyvatāyiṉ ataṟku nīḻaliṭaṅkaḷ vēṟu ettuṇiyō uḷavē; āṅkuc cellalā maṉṟō” eṉṟāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ ikkūṟṟut tāṉ taṭi ciṟitum iṉṟi, nēriṟ pōntu kaṇṭu peṟum iṉpattukku iṭiyūṟu ceytaliyum, vēṟṟiṭam tēṭik kāṇiṉum, atuvum orukāl iṭiyūṟā mākaliṉ, varintu kōṭa loṉṟē aḻivil kūṭṭattukku amivatiyum talimakaṉ teruḷumāṟu uṇarttuvatiyum kaṇṭa nakkīraṉār, ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaṟṟit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
viḷiyā ṭāyamoṭu 1veṇmaṇal aḻutti
maṟantaṉam tuṟanta kāḻmuḷi2 akiya
neypey tīmpāl 3peytaṉam 4vaḷarppa
nummiṟ5 ciṟantatu nuvvi yākumeṉ
ṟaṉṉi kūṟiṉaḷ puṉṉiyatu 6ciṟappē
amma nāṇutum nummoṭu nakiyē 
viruntiṟ pāṇar 7tiruntici kaṭuppa
valampuri vāṉkōṭu naralum ilaṅkunīrt
tuṟikeḻu koṇkanī nalkiṉ
8iṟipaṭu nīḻal piṟavumā ruḷavē,
itu, pakaṟkuṟi vanta talimakaṉit tōḻi varivu kaṭāyatu; kuṟipeyart tīṭumām.
uri
viḷiyāṭu āyamoṭu veṇmaṇal aḻutti-viḷi yāṭum āyamakaḷiruṭaṉ veṇmiyāṉa maṇaliṉkaṇ putittu; maṟantaṉam tuṟanta kāḻmuḷi akiya - yām maṟantu ceṉṟa puṉṉiyiṉ vitimuḷittuvara; neypey tīmpāl peytaṉam vaḷarppa - tēṉ kalanta tīviyapālip peytu yām vaḷarttēmāka; nummiṟ ciṟantatu nuvvi yākum eṉṟu aṉṉi kūṟiṉaḷ - numpōl ciṟantu vaḷarntiruttaliṉ itu numakkut taṅkiyākum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷāḷ. puṉṉiyatu ciṟappu - ippuṉṉimarattiṉ varalāṟu itu; amma-; num moṭu naki nāṇutum - itaṉ kīḻiruntu nummōṭu nakiyāṭi makiḻtali nāṇukiṉṟōm; viruntiṉ pāṇar tirun tici kaṭuppa - putiyarāy vanta pāṇar icittaṟku eṭutta tiruntiya icipōla; valampuri vāṉkōṭu naralum - valampuri yākiya veṇcaṅkukaḷ olikkum; ilaṅkunīrt tuṟikeḻu koṇka - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīrttuṟiyiyuṭiya talivaṉē; nī nalkiṉ - nī emakku ittaliyaḷiyic ceyvatāyiṉ; iṟipaṭu nīḻal piṟavumār uḷa-taṅkutaṟ kaminta maranīḻalkaḷ piṟavum uḷḷaṉa kāṇ e-ṟu.
koṇka. aḻutti maṟantaṉam, tuṟanta kāḻmuki yakiya, tīmpāl peytaṉam vaḷarppa. nummiṟ ciṟantatu nuvvi yākum eṉṟu aṉṉi puṉṉiyatu ciṟappuk kūṟiṉaḷ; itaṉ niḻalil nummoṭu naki nāṇutum amma; nīnalkiṉ, iṟipaṭu nīḻal piṟavu muḷa, kāṇ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka, viḷi yāṭaṟkut tuṇiyāy aminta iḷañciṟumiyar eṉṟaṟku viḷiyāṭā yam eṉṟār. kāḻ - vittu, muḷi akital - muḷikoṇṭu vaḷartal, neypaṭu tīmpāl eṉṉātu neypey tīmpāl eṉṟamiyiṉ ney - tēṉey yāyiṟṟu; “tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya1” eṉpatu kāṇka. tēṉi tēṉey eṉṟalum vaḻakku; “tēṉey yoṭu kiḻaṅku māṟiyōr, mīṉeyyoṭu naṟavu maṟukavum2” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar. nuvavi - num taṅki. avvi, tavvi eṉṟāṟ pōṟaliṉ. nuvvi numakku muṉpiṟantā ḷeṉap poruḷ tarumāyiṉum, īṇṭup piṉtōṉṟiṉami paṟṟi num taṅki eṉakkūṟal vēṇṭiṟṟu, viruntu - putumi. tiruntici - ciṟanta muṟiyil icikkak karuti yeṭukkum ici, itaṉi in nāḷil curuti yeṉa vaḻaṅkupa; paṇṭināḷil, itaṉi iyakkam eṉak kuṟittu mūvakip paṭutturippa; mūvaki yiyakkamum muṟiyuḷik kaḻippi3” eṉpatu kāṇka. iṟiniḻal, taṅkutaṟ kamintaniḻal; “ōṅkunili viyaṉmaṉi iṟiniḻal4” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar, piṟavumār eṉṟa viṭattu ‘ār’, acinili; “acinilik kiḷavi yākuvaḻi yaṟital”5.
puṉṉip potumparuḷ oru puṉṉimarattiṉ kīḻp pakaṟpōtil talimakaḷik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvi iṉpuṟum talimakaṉōṭu collāṭaluṟum tōḻi, ippuṉṉiyi yām pakaṟkuṟik kaḷaṉākak kuṟīttamikkuk kāraṇam, iḵtu emmāl vaḷarkkappaṭṭa teṉpāḷ, viḷiyā ṭāyamoṭu veṇmaṇal aḻutti maṟantaṉam tuṟanta kāḻmuḷi akiya neypey tīmpāl peytaṉam vaḷarppa eṉṟāḷ. orukāl āya makaḷiroṭu kūṭi iṅkē yām viḷiyāṭ ṭayarkiyil emmāl maṇaliṉkaṇ mitippuṇṭu putinta puṉṉi vittu eṉṟaṟku, viḷiyā ṭāyamoṭu eṉṟum, veṇmaṇal aḻuttit tuṟanta kāḻ eṉṟum, yām vēṇṭu meṉṟē putittaṉṟu eṉṟaṟku maṟantaṉam tuṟanta kāḻ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. ciṉṉāṭkup piṉṉar, atu muḷi viṭṭu vaḷarak kaṇṭatum, ataṉi yām uṇṭa pālip peytu vaḷarttaṉam eṉpāḷ, neypey tīmpāl pey taṉam vaḷarppa itu vaḷarntatu eṉṟum, ataṉ vaṉappiṉi yām kāṭṭak kaṇṭa em aṉṉi makiḻntu, numpōl taḻittu aḻakuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟamiyiṉ, itu numakkut taṅkiyām eṉa emakkum itaṟkum uṟavumuṟi kaṟpit taṉaḷ eṉpāḷ. nummiṟ ciṟantatu nuvvi yākum eṉṟu aṉṉi kūṟiṉaḷ eṉṟāḷ. ēṉip puṉṉikaḷiṭi iḵtum oṉṟāyiṉum emakkut taṅkiyām taṉicciṟappu itaṟku uṇṭu eṉṟaṟkup puṉṉiyatu ciṟappu eṉṟum, atu kāṇa, ataṉ nīḻalilē iruntu yām ikkaḷaviṉi mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukutaṟku em uḷḷam nāṇattālcuruṅki maṟukkiṉṟa teṉpāḷ, amma nāṇutum nummoṭu nakiyē eṉṟum urittāḷ. atu kēṭṭa māttiriyē, talimakaṉ uḷḷattil cūḻcciyum, mēṉiyil acivum, tōṉṟak kaṇṭa tōḻi, itaṉāl emmi veṟātu aruḷuvatu umatu karut tāyiṉ itu ceyka eṉpāḷ, koṇka nī nalkiṉ eṉṟum, iṟipaṭu nīḻal piṟavumār uḷavē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. tām vaḷartta puṉṉiyiṉ nīḻaṟkaṇ kūṭutaṟku nāṇupavaḷ, pakaṟkuṟi vantoḻukum itu piṟarkkup pulaṉāyiṉ peru nāṇattāl iṟantupaṭuvaḷ; eṉavē, ivvoḻukkattik kiviṭṭu ivaḷi varintukōṭalē ceyaṟpālatu eṉṟu talimakaṉ teruṇṭu varivu mēṟkoḷḷumāṟu iṟipaṭu nīḻal piṟavumā ruḷavē eṉṟāḷ. iṟipaṭu nīḻal piṟavum uḷavē eṉṟatu, talimakaṉatu neṭumaṉiyiyum uḷap paṭuttu niṉṟatu. aṉṉi kūṟiṉaḷ eṉṟatu, aṉṉi mutaliya tamar aṟivareṉa añciyavāṟu uṇarttiṟṟu. valampuri vāṉ kōṭu pāṇaratu tiruntici pōla naralum eṉṟataṉāl, nī ivaṭkuc ceyyum aruḷ em kuṭikku mikka ciṟappuc cey vatām eṉṟu uḷḷuṟi koḷka.
“uṭaṉuṟi yuvamam cuṭṭu naki1”eṉṉum nūṟpā vuriyil ippāṭṭik kāṭṭi, “itaṉuḷ puṉṉikku nāṇutum eṉavē, avvaḻit tāṉ vaḷartta puṉṉi yeṉṟum, palkālum aṉṉi varuvaḷ eṉṟum uṭaṉuṟi kūṟi vilakkiyavāṟu” eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. iṉi, “maṟin tavaṟ kāṇṭal2” eṉṉum nūṟpāviṉkaṇ “nāṇumika variṉum” eṉṟaṟku itaṉik kāṭṭi, “itaṉuḷ amma nāṇutum eṉap putitu vantatōr nāṇumikuti tōṉṟa maṟutturittaliṉ taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum kūṟiṉāḷ” eṉṟum, “uṭaṉuṟi yuvamam cuṭṭu3” eṉṟa nūṟpāvuriyil, “itaṉuṭ puṉṉiyi aṉṉi nuvviyāku meṉṟataṉāl ivaḷetir nummi nakiyāṭuta lañcutum eṉa nakiyāṭip pakaṟkuṟi etirē koḷḷāmik kuṟippiṉāl maṟittuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka; itaṉic cevvaṉam kūṟāmiyiṉ amittār” eṉṟum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. “māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal4” eṉṟa nūṟpāvuriyil, “puṉṉimarattiṉi nuvvi yeṉṟal marapu aṉmiyiṉ vaḻakkiṉuḷ māṟṟutaṟku uriyavām eṉpatu karuttu” eṉpar pērāciriyar.
173. piramaṉ kāri
perumuyaṟci ceytu palavaki iṭarppāṭukaḷik kaḷintu aritiṟ peṟum poruḷiṭattu mikka aṉpum. ataṉ pēṟṟiṉ kaṇ eytum iṉpattiṉpāl perunayappum uṇṭāvatu āṇmi mikka āṭavarkku iyalpu; attakiya paṇpiṉa ṉāṉa talimakaṉ kaḷavu neṟikkaṇ talimakaḷi eytip peṟum kātaliṉpattiyē virumpi avaḷi varintukoḷḷum muyaṟciyi nīṭṭittu vantāṉ. tōḻiyum taliviyum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟiyākiya ellā neṟiyiṉum maṟimukamākavum, veḷip paṭiyākavum avaṉi varivukaṭāvi vantaṉar. taliviyiṉ kātaṉmāṇpum, avaḷi eytutaṟkaṇ uḷavākum iṭi yūṟukaḷi vilakkaluṟum viṉimāṇpum, nalkum iṉpacciṟappu avaṉi anta neṟiyilēyē uṟittu niṟkac ceytatu. āyiṉum, atu tōḻiyiṉ uḷḷattil pēraccattiyum, peruṅ kavaliyiyum viḷittatu. taṉ poruṭṭut talimakaṉ eytum iṭarkaḷi eṇṇiyum, avaṉik kūṭip peṟalākum aḻivil kūṭṭatti nayantum, talivi maṉattuyar mikku mēṉi vēṟu paṭalāṉāḷ. putumiyāka narappukkāl tōṟum paravi uṭal muḻutum vetuppik katirttu niṟkum kātal vēṭki taṇiyum vāyil peṟāmiyāl, neruppu nīṟupūppatu pōla mēṉiyil viḷarppum, nutaliṟ pacappum, eyti nuṭaṅkuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. makaṭkeytiya vēṟupāṭṭiṉ uṇmi yaṟiyāta maṭavōḷākiya aṉṉi, maliyuṟiyum murukaṉāl ivaṭku ivvēṟupāṭu eytiyirukkumeṉa eṇṇi maliccuṉiyil malarnta pūvik koṇarntu cūṭiyum, māli toṭuttaṇintum murukaṟkuriya ceṅkāntaṭ kaṇṇiyit tantum amiti peṟāḷāy vēlaṉik koṇṭu, murukaṉukku veṟiyāṭ ṭayarva  tallatu piṟitiyātum ivaḷ nōy taṇikkum vāyilākā  teṉa uṇarntu, ataṟku āvaṉa muyalvā ḷāyiṉaḷ. an niliyil talimakaṉ, talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattē piṟar aṟiyalākāta ōriṭattē vantu niṉṟāṉ; ataṉit tōḻi uṇarntu avaṉ ceviyiṟ kēṭkumaḷavukkut taliviyik koṇṭu ceṉṟu niṟutti, avaḷi nōkki, “toṭiyuṭiyāy, niṉ vēṟupāṭṭut tuyarip pōkkutaṟku veṟiyāṭalē vāyppuṭiyateṉa aṉṉi muyalkiṉṟāḷ; ammurukavēḷ tāṉum, avaḷ kaṇ kāṇa vātal, kaṉavilātal tōṉṟi, uṇmiyi uṇarttukiṉṟāṉilliyē; itaṟku eṉ cey vatu? avaḷ kaṇmuṉṉē taṉ uṇmivaṭivu tōṉṟāvaki maṟittu vēṟṟuruk koṇṭēṉum, kaṉaviṉkaṇ nērpaṭat tōṉṟiyēṉum, ivaṭku innōy eṉṉāl vanta taṉṟu; itaṉi uṟuvittavaṉ anta malikiḻavaṉāṉa talimakaṉē eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl; avaṉukku ētam uṇṭō? oṉṟum aṟiyāmiyāl niṉṉiyē viṉavukiṟēṉ. aṉpukūrntu kūṟuka” eṉṟu viṉaviṉāḷ.  
ciṟippuṟattē niṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭirunta talimakaṉ naṉku eṇṇi, talivipāl uḷatākiya mēṉi vēṟupāṭṭiṉ mikutiyiyum, aṉṉi veṟiyeṭukku māṟṟāl, atu peritum ciṟantu nāṭu muḻutum paravit talivikku uṇṭākkum ētatti yum teruṇṭu, varivu mēṟkoḷḷac ceyyum cīrmi ik kūṟṟiṉkaṇ amintami kaṇṭa piramaṉ kāriyār ip pāṭṭil ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
cuṉippūk kuṟṟum toṭali tiiyum
malicceṅ kāntaṭ kaṇṇi tantum
taṉvaḻip paṭūum nannayan taruḷi 
veṟiyeṉa uyaṅkum aḷiyaḷ aṉṉiyik1
kaṇṇiṉum kaṉaviṉum kāṭṭi innōy
eṉṉiṉum vārātu maṇiyiṉ tōṉṟum
ammali kiḻavōṉ ceytaṉaṉ ituveṉil 
paṭuvaṇ ṭārkkum pintār mārpiṉ
neṭuvēṭ kēta muṭittō
toṭiyōy kūṟumati viṉavuval yāṉē
itu, tōḻi talivik kurippāḷāyc ciṟippuṟamākac colliyatu;  veṟi accuṟīit tōḻi aṟattōṭu nili payappitta tūumām.
uri
cuṉippūk kuṟṟum - cuṉiyil malarnta pūkkaḷik koytu aṇintum; toṭali tiiyum - māli toṭuttu namakkuc cūṭiyum; malic ceṅkāntaḷ kaṇṇi tantum - maliyiṭattu malarnta ceṅkāntaṭ pūvāl kaṇṇi toṭuttut tantum; taṉvaḻip paṭūum - taṉ karuttiṉpaṭiyē naṭakkum; nam nayantaruḷi - nampāl mikka aṉpuṟṟu; veṟiyeṉa uyaṅkum aḷiyaḷ aṉṉiyi - nāmuṟṟa vēṟupāṭṭukku maruntu veṟiyayartalallatu piṟitu yātum illiyeṉa eṇṇi ataṟku āvaṉa muyalum aḷikkat takkavaḷākiya aṉṉikku; kaṇṇiṉum kaṉaviṉum kāṭṭi - naṉavil kaṇ kāṇak kuṟippākavum kaṉavil veḷippaṭiyākavum taṉ vaṭivik kāṭṭi; innōy - niṉmakaḷ vēṟupāṭṭuk kuriya innōy; eṉṉiṉ vārātu - eṉṉāl vantataṉṟu; maṇi yiṉtōṉṟum ammali kiḻavōṉ itu ceytaṉaṉ  eṉiṉ - cēymikkaṇ nīlamaṇi pōlat tōṉṟum anta malikkiḻavōṉākiya talimakaṉ uṟuvikka vantatu eṉṟu coṉṉāl, acceyal; paṭuvaṇṭu ārkkum pintār mārpiṉ - olikkiṉṟa vaṇṭu moyttu āravārikkum pacumiyāṉa māli yaṇinta mārpiṉiyuṭiya; neṭuvēṭku ētam uṭittō-neṭuvēḷākiya murukaṉukku ētam taruva tāmō; toṭiyōy-toṭi yaṇinta tōḻi; yāṉ viṉavu val kūṟumati - yāṉ aṟiyātu viṉavukiṉṟēṉ, collu vāyāka e-ṟu.
toṭiyōy, kuṟṟum, tiiyum, tantum, nayantaruḷi uyaṅkum aṉṉiyik kāṭṭi, nōy eṉṉiṉ vārātu, kiḻavōṉ ceytaṉaṉ itu eṉil, neṭuvēṭku ētam uṭittō? yāṉ aṟiyātu viṉavuval, kūṟumati eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka; cuṉi - malikaḷil kāṇappaṭum nīrnili, toṭali - māli, kaṇṇi - taliyiṟ cūṭum māli; veṟi - veṟiyāṭal, uyaṅkutal - varuntutal; īṇṭu veṟiyāṭaṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa tēṭi varuntutal. aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkavaḷ. aṉṉiyik kāṭṭi eṉa iyiyum; urupumayakkam; “ceyyavaḷ tavviyik kāṭṭi viṭum1” eṉṟāṟ pōla, eṉṉiṉum, ummi, iciniṟi, cēymik kaṇ tōṉṟum mali nīlamaṇiyiṉ niṟam koṇṭu tōṉṟu vatu paṟṟi maṇiyiṉ tōṉṟum ammali eṉṟār. toṭi yōy eṉṟatu taliviyi.
talimakaḷ mēṉi vēṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟum nōykkuriya kāraṇatti uḷḷavāṟu aṟiyātu murukaṉāl uṇṭāya teṉap piṟaḻa uṇarntu, avaṉ uṟiyum maliyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa cuṉiyiṭattē malarnta pūkkoytu, viḷiyāṭiya ñāṉṟu aṇaṅkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟa karuttāl, ac cuṉippūvik koytu vantu talimakaṭku aṇintami tōṉṟac cuṉippūk kuṟṟum eṉṟum, mutumakaḷir cilar, cuṉippūvi veṟitē aṇitaliṉum māliyākat toṭuttuc cūṭu eṉṟamiyiṉ toṭali tiiyum eṉṟum, cilar murukaṉ maliyiṭattu malarntu vaṇṭu mūcāta māṇpuṭiya ceṅkāntaḷ murukaṉukku viruppamāṉa pūvā taliṉ ataṉik koṇarntu taruka eṉṟamiyiṉ malic ceṅkāntaḷ kaṇṇi tantu eṉṟum, atu murukaṟkē ciṟappāka vuriya tākaliṉ piṟar cūṭāmipaṟṟit tantu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “curumpu mūcāc cūṭarppūṅ kāntaṭ, peruntaṇ kaṇṇi milinta ceṉṉiyaṉ1” eṉpatu kāṇka. pūvum, toṭaliyum, kaṇṇiyum, murukaṉālō piṟarālō aṇiyappaṭāmiyiṉ, avaṟṟik koṇarntu cūṭum pōtum, aṇiyum pōtum, tarum pōtum talimakaḷ ciṟitum maṟāmal ēṟṟukkoṇṭami tōṉṟa, taṉ vaḻippaṭūum eṉavum, ataṉāl tāymaṉam makaḷpāl pēraṉpu curantu niṉṟami viḷaṅka nannayan taruḷi eṉavum iyampiṉāḷ. iṅṅaṉam mutupeṇṭirum piṟarum pōntu talimakaḷik kaṇṭu kaḻuvāyākac colliyaṉavellām ceytum, vēṟupāṭu nīṅkāmiyāṉ; vēlaṉik koṇṭu veṟiyayartalē iṉic ceyaṟpāṟ ṟeṉat tuṇintamiyum, aḵtu oru viḻāppōla nikaḻva tākaliṉ ataṟkāvaṉa tēṭum muyaṟciyāl meyvaruntiṉamiyum kaṇṭu kūṟaliṉ, veṟi yeṉa uyaṅkum eṉṟum, tāyatu aṟiyāmikku iraṅkik kūṟaliṉ aḷiyaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.  talimakaḷ eytiya vēṟupāṭṭuk kuriya kāraṇatti murukaṉāl itu viḷinta tāka makaḷir palarum vēlaṉum kūṟaliṉ, taṉpāl paḻiyiṉmi kāṭṭaṟku avaṉē uṇmi kūṟaṟ kuriyavaṉ eṉpatu paṟṟi avaṉic cuṭṭit tōḻi, avaṉ kaṭavu ḷātaliṉ, naṉavil teyvamuṟṟāṭum makkaḷic cārntum, vēṟu tippiya nikaḻcci kāṭṭiyum kuṟippāl tāṉ aṇaṅkāmiyik kāṭṭaṟ kuriyavaṉ; aḵtu iṉṟēl, tāyiṉ kaṉaviṭi uṇmivaṭivu kāṭṭi innōy taṉṉāl vārāmi kāṭṭalām; kāṭṭiya vaḻi, nōy ceytāṉ yāvaṉ eṉak kēṭṭavaḻi malikiḻavō ṉākiya talivaṉik kāṭṭalāmē; ataṉit tāṉum murukavēḷ kāṭṭātoḻikiṉṟāṉ; kāṭṭiṉ, avaṟku yātu kuṟṟam varum; yāṉ oṉṟum aṟikilēṉ eṉpāḷ, kaṇṇi ṉum kaṉaviṉum kāṭṭi innōy eṉṉiṉum vārātu maṇiyiṉ tōṉṟum ammalikkiḻavōṉ ceytaṉaṉ ituveṉil neṭuvēṭku ētamuṭittō eṉavum, toṭiyōy kūṟumati eṉavum, viṉavuval yāṉē eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷ. kaṇṇiṉum kaṉaviṉum eṉṟatu, kāṭṭaṟkuriya vāyil kūṟiyavāṟu. taṉmēl ēṟṟik kūṟappaṭum paḻiyiṉ nīṅkutaṟku murukaṉ, innōy eṉṉiṉum vārātu eṉṟal vēṇṭum; kaṭavuḷātaliṉ avaṉ aṟiyātatu oṉṟu milli eṉṟaṟku ammalikkiḻavōṉ ceytaṉaṉ itu eṉṟu kūṟalām eṉṟum, ivvāṟu kūṟalāl neṭuvēṭkup paḻi nīṅkumē yaṉṟik kuṟṟam yātum illi eṉṟaṟku, ētam uṭittō eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. atu kēṭkum talimakaḷ, “itaṉi neṭuvēḷ kūṟiṉ atu vēṇṭākūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟamām;
